### Code

```bash
  1 | #!/bin/bash
  2 | # Legacy clang batch pipeline plus manager-friendly single-project mode.
  3 | set -euo pipefail
  4 | 
  5 | DATA_DIR="${1:-/home/dave/data/cpp_raw}"
  6 | OUTPUT_DIR="${2:-/home/dave/data}"
  7 | WORKERS="${3:-48}"
  8 | 
  9 | SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
 10 | REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
 11 | INDEXER="${INDEXER:-$REPO_ROOT/tools/clang_indexer/index_project.py}"
 12 | PYTHON="${PYTHON:-$HOME/venv/bin/python3}"
 13 | PARQUET_SCRIPT="${PARQUET_SCRIPT:-$REPO_ROOT/scripts/data/stream_jsonl_to_parquet.py}"
 14 | LOCAL_CONVERTER="${LOCAL_CONVERTER:-$REPO_ROOT/scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py}"
 15 | TOKENIZER_PATH="${TOKENIZER_PATH:-$REPO_ROOT/tokenizer.json}"
 16 | 
 17 | MODE="${CLANG_PIPELINE_MODE:-batch}"
 18 | MAX_TOKENS="${MAX_TOKENS:-16384}"
 19 | MAX_DEP_DEPTH="${MAX_DEP_DEPTH:-5}"
 20 | SIZE_LABEL="${SIZE_LABEL:-4k}"
 21 | OVERFLOW_POLICY="${OVERFLOW_POLICY:-drop}"
 22 | MATERIALIZE_TOKENIZED_ENRICHED="${MATERIALIZE_TOKENIZED_ENRICHED:-1}"
 23 | 
 24 | echo "=== Clang Cross-File Dependency Pipeline ==="
 25 | echo "Mode: $MODE"
 26 | echo "Data dir: $DATA_DIR"
 27 | echo "Output dir: $OUTPUT_DIR"
 28 | echo "Workers per project: $WORKERS"
 29 | echo "Max tokens: $MAX_TOKENS"
 30 | echo "Start time: $(date)"
 31 | 
 32 | if [ ! -f "$INDEXER" ]; then
 33 |     echo "ERROR: Indexer not found at $INDEXER"
 34 |     exit 1
 35 | fi
 36 | if [ ! -f "$LOCAL_CONVERTER" ]; then
 37 |     echo "ERROR: Converter not found at $LOCAL_CONVERTER"
 38 |     exit 1
 39 | fi
 40 | if [ ! -d "$DATA_DIR" ]; then
 41 |     echo "ERROR: Data directory not found at $DATA_DIR"
 42 |     exit 1
 43 | fi
 44 | 
 45 | if [ "$MODE" = "single-project" ]; then
 46 |     PROJECT_DIR="$DATA_DIR"
 47 |     PROJECT_NAME="$(basename "$PROJECT_DIR")"
 48 |     OUTPUT_JSONL="${SINGLE_PROJECT_JSONL:-$OUTPUT_DIR/$PROJECT_NAME.jsonl}"
 49 |     OUTPUT_PARQUET="${SINGLE_PROJECT_PARQUET:-$OUTPUT_DIR/$PROJECT_NAME.parquet}"
 50 | 
 51 |     mkdir -p "$(dirname "$OUTPUT_JSONL")" "$(dirname "$OUTPUT_PARQUET")"
 52 | 
 53 |     echo "=== Single-project enriched mode ==="
 54 |     echo "Project: $PROJECT_DIR"
 55 |     echo "JSONL: $OUTPUT_JSONL"
 56 |     echo "Parquet: $OUTPUT_PARQUET"
 57 |     echo "Overflow policy: $OVERFLOW_POLICY"
 58 | 
 59 |     rm -f "$OUTPUT_JSONL" "$OUTPUT_PARQUET"
 60 | 
 61 |     "$PYTHON" "$INDEXER" \
 62 |         --project-dir "$PROJECT_DIR" \
 63 |         --output "$OUTPUT_JSONL" \
 64 |         --max-tokens "$MAX_TOKENS" \
 65 |         --max-dep-depth "$MAX_DEP_DEPTH" \
 66 |         --workers 1 \
 67 |         --parse-workers "$WORKERS" \
 68 |         --enriched
 69 | 
 70 |     if [ ! -s "$OUTPUT_JSONL" ]; then
 71 |         echo "empty jsonl"
 72 |         exit 0
 73 |     fi
 74 | 
 75 |     CONVERTER_ARGS=(
 76 |         --size "$SIZE_LABEL"
 77 |         --input-file "$OUTPUT_JSONL"
 78 |         --output-file "$OUTPUT_PARQUET"
 79 |         --overflow-policy "$OVERFLOW_POLICY"
 80 |         --tokenizer-path "$TOKENIZER_PATH"
 81 |     )
 82 |     if [ "$MATERIALIZE_TOKENIZED_ENRICHED" != "0" ]; then
 83 |         CONVERTER_ARGS+=(--materialize-tokenized-enriched)
 84 |     fi
 85 | 
 86 |     "$PYTHON" "$LOCAL_CONVERTER" "${CONVERTER_ARGS[@]}"
 87 | 
 88 |     if [ ! -s "$OUTPUT_PARQUET" ]; then
 89 |         echo "empty parquet"
 90 |         exit 0
 91 |     fi
 92 | 
 93 |     echo "Single-project pipeline complete"
 94 |     echo "Parquet: $OUTPUT_PARQUET"
 95 |     exit 0
 96 | fi
 97 | 
 98 | generate_compile_db() {
 99 |     local project_dir="$1"
100 |     local project_name
101 |     project_name=$(basename "$project_dir")
102 | 
103 |     if [ -f "$project_dir/CMakeLists.txt" ]; then
104 |         local build_dir="$project_dir/build_cc"
105 |         if [ ! -f "$project_dir/compile_commands.json" ]; then
106 |             echo "  Generating compile_commands.json for $project_name..."
107 |             mkdir -p "$build_dir"
108 |             cmake -S "$project_dir" -B "$build_dir" \
109 |                 -DCMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS=ON \
110 |                 -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
111 |                 > /dev/null 2>&1 && \
112 |                 cp "$build_dir/compile_commands.json" "$project_dir/" 2>/dev/null && \
113 |                 echo "  compile_commands.json generated for $project_name" || \
114 |                 echo "  WARN: cmake failed for $project_name, using fallback mode"
115 |             rm -rf "$build_dir"
116 |         fi
117 |     fi
118 | }
119 | 
120 | PROJECTS=()
121 | for d in "$DATA_DIR"/*/; do
122 |     [ -d "$d" ] && PROJECTS+=("${d%/}")
123 | done
124 | 
125 | echo "Found ${#PROJECTS[@]} projects"
126 | echo ""
127 | 
128 | echo "=== Phase 1: Generating compile databases ==="
129 | PIDS=()
130 | for project in "${PROJECTS[@]}"; do
131 |     generate_compile_db "$project" &
132 |     PIDS+=($!)
133 |     if [ ${#PIDS[@]} -ge 8 ]; then
134 |         wait "${PIDS[0]}" 2>/dev/null || true
135 |         PIDS=("${PIDS[@]:1}")
136 |     fi
137 | done
138 | wait 2>/dev/null || true
139 | echo "Phase 1 complete: $(date)"
140 | 
141 | echo ""
142 | echo "=== Phase 2: Clang semantic indexing ==="
143 | OUTPUT_JSONL="$OUTPUT_DIR/cpp_clang_crossfile_16k.jsonl"
144 | rm -f "$OUTPUT_JSONL"
145 | 
146 | "$PYTHON" "$INDEXER" \
147 |     --projects-dir "$DATA_DIR" \
148 |     --output "$OUTPUT_JSONL" \
149 |     --max-tokens "$MAX_TOKENS" \
150 |     --max-dep-depth "$MAX_DEP_DEPTH" \
151 |     --workers 1 \
152 |     --parse-workers "$WORKERS"
153 | 
154 | echo ""
155 | echo "Phase 2 complete: $(date)"
156 | echo "Output: $OUTPUT_JSONL"
157 | ls -lh "$OUTPUT_JSONL"
158 | wc -l "$OUTPUT_JSONL"
159 | 
160 | echo ""
161 | echo "=== Phase 3: Converting to parquet ==="
162 | "$PYTHON" "$PARQUET_SCRIPT" \
163 |     --input "$OUTPUT_JSONL" \
164 |     --output-dir "$OUTPUT_DIR/parquet/cpp_clang_crossfile_16k" \
165 |     --batch-size 50000
166 | 
167 | echo ""
168 | echo "=== Pipeline complete ==="
169 | echo "End time: $(date)"
170 | ls -lh "$OUTPUT_DIR/parquet/cpp_clang_crossfile_16k/"
```

### Code

```python
   1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2 | """Convert Clang-enriched clang-indexer JSONL to hard-budgeted parquet.
   3 | 
   4 | Reads gs://nanochat-training-data-2026/v5_enriched/*.jsonl.gz, applies:
   5 |   1. Dead-platform filter (removes __SYMBIAN32__, _MSDOS, __VXWORKS__ blocks)
   6 |   2. Platform header prepend (x86_64-linux-gnu / g++ / c++17 default)
   7 |   3. tokenizer-aware hard-budget handling using chunk boundaries
   8 | 
   9 | Writes parquet shards to:
  10 |   gs://nanochat-training-data-2026/data/parquet/clang_enriched_<size>/
  11 | 
  12 | Usage:
  13 |     python scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py --size 4k [--dry-run] [--shard-size 10000]
  14 |     python scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py --input-file repo.jsonl --output-file repo.parquet --overflow-policy drop
  15 |     python scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py --help
  16 | """
  17 | 
  18 | import argparse
  19 | import gzip
  20 | import json
  21 | import logging
  22 | import os
  23 | import re
  24 | import subprocess
  25 | import sys
  26 | import tempfile
  27 | from pathlib import Path
  28 | 
  29 | import pyarrow as pa
  30 | import pyarrow.parquet as pq
  31 | 
  32 | from nanochat.language_info import language_info_to_prefix
  33 | from nanochat.tokenized_enriched import (
  34 |     PLATFORM_IDS_COLUMN,
  35 |     TOKEN_AST_DEPTH_COLUMN,
  36 |     TOKEN_AST_NODE_TYPE_COLUMN,
  37 |     TOKEN_CALL_EDGES_COLUMN,
  38 |     TOKEN_CHUNK_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN,
  39 |     TOKEN_CHUNK_ENDS_COLUMN,
  40 |     TOKEN_CHUNK_KINDS_COLUMN,
  41 |     TOKEN_CHUNK_STARTS_COLUMN,
  42 |     TOKEN_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN,
  43 |     TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN,
  44 |     TOKEN_SIBLING_INDEX_COLUMN,
  45 |     TOKEN_STRUCTURE_IDS_COLUMN,
  46 |     TOKEN_TYPE_EDGES_COLUMN,
  47 |     materialize_tokenized_enriched_batch,
  48 | )
  49 | from nanochat.tokenized_enriched_schema import (
  50 |     AUTHOR_TIMESTAMP_COLUMN,
  51 |     CHANGED_CHUNK_IDS_COLUMN,
  52 |     CHANGED_CHUNK_SPANS_COLUMN,
  53 |     COMMIT_HASH_COLUMN,
  54 |     COMMIT_TIMESTAMP_COLUMN,
  55 |     EDIT_OP_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN,
  56 |     FILEPATH_COLUMN,
  57 |     FILEPATH_STABLE_ID_COLUMN,
  58 |     FILE_LOCAL_COMMIT_INDEX_COLUMN,
  59 |     HAS_AMBIGUOUS_RECONSTRUCTION_COLUMN,
  60 |     HAS_RENAME_AMBIGUITY_COLUMN,
  61 |     HUNK_ID_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN,
  62 |     IS_MERGE_COMMIT_COLUMN,
  63 |     PARENT_COUNT_COLUMN,
  64 |     PARENT_HASHES_COLUMN,
  65 |     REPO_COLUMN,
  66 |     REPO_STABLE_ID_COLUMN,
  67 |     TIMESTAMP_COLUMN,
  68 |     TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_POST_COLUMN,
  69 |     TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_PRE_COLUMN,
  70 | )
  71 | from scripts.data.token_budget import (
  72 |     chunk_enriched_document,
  73 |     count_tokens,
  74 |     load_tokenizer,
  75 |     size_label_to_tokens,
  76 | )
  77 | 
  78 | logging.basicConfig(
  79 |     level=logging.INFO,
  80 |     format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s",
  81 | )
  82 | log = logging.getLogger("clang_enriched_to_4k")
  83 | 
  84 | GCS_BUCKET = "nanochat-training-data-2026"
  85 | GCS_INPUT_PREFIX = "v5_enriched"
  86 | GCS_OUTPUT_PREFIX_TEMPLATE = "data/parquet/clang_enriched_{size}"
  87 | _TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_TOKENIZER = None
  88 | _OVERFLOW_POLICIES = ("split", "drop")
  89 | 
  90 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  91 | # Platform header (default — enriched docs are already processed, no repo_dir)
  92 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  93 | 
  94 | _DEFAULT_PLATFORM_INFO = {
  95 |     "platform": "x86_64-linux-gnu",
  96 |     "compiler": "g++",
  97 |     "standard": "c++17",
  98 |     "arch": "x86_64",
  99 |     "mode": "user",
 100 | }
 101 | 
 102 | 
 103 | def build_platform_header(platform_info: dict) -> str:
 104 |     """Build a platform context comment header."""
 105 |     platform = platform_info.get("platform", "x86_64-linux-gnu")
 106 |     compiler = platform_info.get("compiler", "g++")
 107 |     standard = platform_info.get("standard", "c++17")
 108 |     arch = platform_info.get("arch", "x86_64")
 109 |     mode = platform_info.get("mode", "user")
 110 |     lines = [
 111 |         "// <BOS>",
 112 |         f"// platform: {platform}",
 113 |         f"// compiler: {compiler}",
 114 |         f"// standard: {standard}",
 115 |         f"// arch: {arch}",
 116 |         f"// mode: {mode}",
 117 |         "",
 118 |     ]
 119 |     return "\n".join(lines)
 120 | 
 121 | 
 122 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 123 | # Dead-platform filter
 124 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 125 | 
 126 | _DEAD_PLATFORM_MARKERS = [
 127 |     "__SYMBIAN32__",
 128 |     "_MSDOS",
 129 |     "__VXWORKS__",
 130 | ]
 131 | 
 132 | 
 133 | def filter_dead_platforms(text: str) -> str:
 134 |     """Remove dead-platform #ifdef blocks from C++ source text.
 135 | 
 136 |     Handles nested #ifdef/#endif correctly by tracking depth.
 137 |     """
 138 |     if not any(marker in text for marker in _DEAD_PLATFORM_MARKERS):
 139 |         return text
 140 | 
 141 |     lines = text.split("\n")
 142 |     result_lines = []
 143 |     skip_depth = 0
 144 |     overall_depth = 0
 145 | 
 146 |     i = 0
 147 |     while i < len(lines):
 148 |         line = lines[i]
 149 |         stripped = line.strip()
 150 | 
 151 |         if skip_depth == 0:
 152 |             is_dead = False
 153 |             for marker in _DEAD_PLATFORM_MARKERS:
 154 |                 if re.match(r'^\s*#\s*ifdef\s+' + re.escape(marker) + r'\b', line):
 155 |                     is_dead = True
 156 |                     break
 157 |                 if re.match(r'^\s*#\s*if\s+defined\s*\(\s*' + re.escape(marker) + r'\s*\)', line):
 158 |                     is_dead = True
 159 |                     break
 160 | 
 161 |             if is_dead:
 162 |                 skip_depth = 1
 163 |                 overall_depth += 1
 164 |                 i += 1
 165 |                 continue
 166 | 
 167 |             if re.match(r'^\s*#\s*(?:ifdef|ifndef|if)\b', stripped):
 168 |                 overall_depth += 1
 169 |             elif re.match(r'^\s*#\s*endif\b', stripped):
 170 |                 overall_depth = max(0, overall_depth - 1)
 171 | 
 172 |             result_lines.append(line)
 173 |         else:
 174 |             if re.match(r'^\s*#\s*(?:ifdef|ifndef|if)\b', stripped):
 175 |                 skip_depth += 1
 176 |             elif re.match(r'^\s*#\s*endif\b', stripped):
 177 |                 skip_depth -= 1
 178 |                 if skip_depth == 0:
 179 |                     i += 1
 180 |                     continue
 181 | 
 182 |         i += 1
 183 | 
 184 |     return "\n".join(result_lines)
 185 | 
 186 | 
 187 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 188 | # Token-budgeted chunker
 189 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 190 | 
 191 | PREAMBLE_KIND = 1
 192 | 
 193 | 
 194 | def chunk_document_exact(doc: dict, tokenizer, max_tokens: int) -> list:
 195 |     """Chunk an enriched document into exact-token-budgeted sub-documents."""
 196 |     def sort_key(cb):
 197 |         is_preamble = (
 198 |             cb.get("dep_level", 0) == 0 and cb.get("kind", 0) == PREAMBLE_KIND
 199 |         )
 200 |         return (0 if is_preamble else 1, cb.get("dep_level", 0), cb.get("start", 0))
 201 | 
 202 |     return chunk_enriched_document(
 203 |         doc,
 204 |         max_tokens,
 205 |         tokenizer,
 206 |         boundary_sort_key=lambda item: sort_key(item[1]),
 207 |     )
 208 | 
 209 | 
 210 | def maybe_keep_document_exact(doc: dict, tokenizer, max_tokens: int) -> list[dict]:
 211 |     """Emit the whole document only when it already fits the exact budget."""
 212 |     exact_total = count_tokens(doc.get("text", ""), tokenizer)
 213 |     if exact_total > max_tokens:
 214 |         return []
 215 |     out = dict(doc)
 216 |     out["actual_token_count"] = exact_total
 217 |     return [out]
 218 | 
 219 | 
 220 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 221 | # Parquet schema
 222 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 223 | 
 224 | _SCHEMA = pa.schema([
 225 |     pa.field("text", pa.string()),
 226 |     pa.field("actual_token_count", pa.int32()),
 227 |     pa.field("structure_ids", pa.list_(pa.int8())),
 228 |     pa.field("chunk_boundaries", pa.list_(pa.struct([
 229 |         pa.field("start", pa.int32()),
 230 |         pa.field("end", pa.int32()),
 231 |         pa.field("kind", pa.int8()),
 232 |         pa.field("dep_level", pa.int32()),
 233 |         pa.field("name", pa.string()),
 234 |     ]))),
 235 |     pa.field("call_edges", pa.list_(pa.struct([
 236 |         pa.field("from", pa.int32()),
 237 |         pa.field("to", pa.int32()),
 238 |     ]))),
 239 |     pa.field("type_edges", pa.list_(pa.struct([
 240 |         pa.field("from", pa.int32()),
 241 |         pa.field("to", pa.int32()),
 242 |     ]))),
 243 |     pa.field("platform_info", pa.string()),
 244 |     pa.field("language_info", pa.string()),
 245 |     pa.field("build_info", pa.string()),
 246 |     pa.field("constituent_provenance", pa.list_(pa.struct([
 247 |         pa.field("filepath", pa.string()),
 248 |         pa.field("language_info", pa.string()),
 249 |         pa.field("build_info", pa.string()),
 250 |     ]))),
 251 |     pa.field("constituent_provenance_json", pa.string()),
 252 |     pa.field(REPO_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 253 |     pa.field(FILEPATH_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 254 |     pa.field(COMMIT_HASH_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 255 |     pa.field(TIMESTAMP_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 256 |     pa.field(PARENT_HASHES_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.string())),
 257 |     pa.field(PARENT_COUNT_COLUMN, pa.int32()),
 258 |     pa.field(IS_MERGE_COMMIT_COLUMN, pa.bool_()),
 259 |     pa.field(AUTHOR_TIMESTAMP_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 260 |     pa.field(COMMIT_TIMESTAMP_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 261 |     pa.field(REPO_STABLE_ID_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 262 |     pa.field(FILEPATH_STABLE_ID_COLUMN, pa.string()),
 263 |     pa.field(FILE_LOCAL_COMMIT_INDEX_COLUMN, pa.int32()),
 264 |     pa.field(HAS_AMBIGUOUS_RECONSTRUCTION_COLUMN, pa.bool_()),
 265 |     pa.field(HAS_RENAME_AMBIGUITY_COLUMN, pa.bool_()),
 266 |     pa.field(TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint32())),
 267 |     pa.field(PLATFORM_IDS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint16())),
 268 |     pa.field(TOKEN_STRUCTURE_IDS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint8())),
 269 |     pa.field(TOKEN_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint16())),
 270 |     pa.field(TOKEN_AST_DEPTH_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint16())),
 271 |     pa.field(TOKEN_SIBLING_INDEX_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint16())),
 272 |     pa.field(TOKEN_AST_NODE_TYPE_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint16())),
 273 |     pa.field(TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_PRE_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint8())),
 274 |     pa.field(TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_POST_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint8())),
 275 |     pa.field(HUNK_ID_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.int32())),
 276 |     pa.field(EDIT_OP_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint8())),
 277 |     pa.field(TOKEN_CHUNK_STARTS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint32())),
 278 |     pa.field(TOKEN_CHUNK_ENDS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint32())),
 279 |     pa.field(TOKEN_CHUNK_KINDS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint8())),
 280 |     pa.field(TOKEN_CHUNK_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint16())),
 281 |     pa.field(CHANGED_CHUNK_IDS_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.uint32())),
 282 |     pa.field(
 283 |         CHANGED_CHUNK_SPANS_COLUMN,
 284 |         pa.list_(
 285 |             pa.struct(
 286 |                 [
 287 |                     pa.field("start", pa.uint32()),
 288 |                     pa.field("end", pa.uint32()),
 289 |                 ]
 290 |             )
 291 |         ),
 292 |     ),
 293 |     pa.field(TOKEN_CALL_EDGES_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.struct([
 294 |         pa.field("from", pa.uint16()),
 295 |         pa.field("to", pa.uint16()),
 296 |     ]))),
 297 |     pa.field(TOKEN_TYPE_EDGES_COLUMN, pa.list_(pa.struct([
 298 |         pa.field("from", pa.uint16()),
 299 |         pa.field("to", pa.uint16()),
 300 |     ]))),
 301 | ])
 302 | 
 303 | 
 304 | def rows_to_table(rows: list, *, tokenized_rows: list[dict] | None = None) -> pa.Table:
 305 |     """Convert a list of doc dicts to a PyArrow table."""
 306 |     tokenized_rows = tokenized_rows or [{} for _ in rows]
 307 |     texts = []
 308 |     token_counts = []
 309 |     structure_ids_col = []
 310 |     chunk_boundaries_col = []
 311 |     call_edges_col = []
 312 |     type_edges_col = []
 313 |     platform_info_col = []
 314 |     language_info_col = []
 315 |     build_info_col = []
 316 |     constituent_provenance_col = []
 317 |     constituent_provenance_json_col = []
 318 |     repos = []
 319 |     filepaths = []
 320 |     commits = []
 321 |     timestamps = []
 322 |     parent_hashes = []
 323 |     parent_counts = []
 324 |     is_merge_commits = []
 325 |     author_timestamps = []
 326 |     commit_timestamps = []
 327 |     repo_stable_ids = []
 328 |     filepath_stable_ids = []
 329 |     file_local_commit_indices = []
 330 |     ambiguous_reconstruction = []
 331 |     rename_ambiguity = []
 332 |     token_ids_col = []
 333 |     platform_ids_col = []
 334 |     token_structure_ids_col = []
 335 |     token_dep_levels_col = []
 336 |     token_ast_depth_col = []
 337 |     token_sibling_index_col = []
 338 |     token_ast_node_type_col = []
 339 |     token_change_mask_pre_col = []
 340 |     token_change_mask_post_col = []
 341 |     hunk_id_per_token_col = []
 342 |     edit_op_per_token_col = []
 343 |     token_chunk_starts_col = []
 344 |     token_chunk_ends_col = []
 345 |     token_chunk_kinds_col = []
 346 |     token_chunk_dep_levels_col = []
 347 |     changed_chunk_ids_col = []
 348 |     changed_chunk_spans_col = []
 349 |     token_call_edges_col = []
 350 |     token_type_edges_col = []
 351 | 
 352 |     for row, tokenized in zip(rows, tokenized_rows):
 353 |         texts.append(row.get("text", ""))
 354 |         token_counts.append(int(row.get("actual_token_count", 0)))
 355 |         structure_ids_col.append(row.get("structure_ids", []))
 356 |         # Normalize chunk boundaries
 357 |         cbs = row.get("chunk_boundaries", [])
 358 |         chunk_boundaries_col.append([
 359 |             {
 360 |                 "start": int(cb.get("start", 0)),
 361 |                 "end": int(cb.get("end", 0)),
 362 |                 "kind": int(cb.get("kind", 0)),
 363 |                 "dep_level": int(cb.get("dep_level", 0)),
 364 |                 "name": str(cb.get("name", "")),
 365 |             }
 366 |             for cb in cbs
 367 |         ])
 368 |         call_edges_col.append([
 369 |             {"from": int(e.get("from", 0)), "to": int(e.get("to", 0))}
 370 |             for e in row.get("call_edges", [])
 371 |         ])
 372 |         type_edges_col.append([
 373 |             {"from": int(e.get("from", 0)), "to": int(e.get("to", 0))}
 374 |             for e in row.get("type_edges", [])
 375 |         ])
 376 |         pi = row.get("platform_info")
 377 |         platform_info_col.append(json.dumps(pi) if pi else None)
 378 |         li = row.get("language_info")
 379 |         language_info_col.append(json.dumps(li) if li else None)
 380 |         bi = row.get("build_info")
 381 |         build_info_col.append(json.dumps(bi) if bi else None)
 382 |         raw_constituents = row.get("constituent_provenance")
 383 |         if raw_constituents is None and row.get("constituent_provenance_json"):
 384 |             try:
 385 |                 raw_constituents = json.loads(row["constituent_provenance_json"])
 386 |             except (TypeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
 387 |                 raw_constituents = None
 388 |         normalized_constituents = []
 389 |         if isinstance(raw_constituents, list):
 390 |             for item in raw_constituents:
 391 |                 if not isinstance(item, dict):
 392 |                     continue
 393 |                 normalized_constituents.append(
 394 |                     {
 395 |                         "filepath": item.get("filepath"),
 396 |                         "language_info": (
 397 |                             json.dumps(item["language_info"])
 398 |                             if item.get("language_info") is not None
 399 |                             else None
 400 |                         ),
 401 |                         "build_info": (
 402 |                             json.dumps(item["build_info"])
 403 |                             if item.get("build_info") is not None
 404 |                             else None
 405 |                         ),
 406 |                     }
 407 |                 )
 408 |         constituent_provenance_col.append(normalized_constituents)
 409 |         constituent_provenance_json_col.append(row.get("constituent_provenance_json"))
 410 |         repos.append(row.get(REPO_COLUMN, ""))
 411 |         filepaths.append(row.get(FILEPATH_COLUMN, ""))
 412 |         commits.append(row.get(COMMIT_HASH_COLUMN, row.get("commit", "")))
 413 |         timestamps.append(row.get(TIMESTAMP_COLUMN, ""))
 414 |         parent_hashes.append(row.get(PARENT_HASHES_COLUMN, []))
 415 |         parent_counts.append(row.get(PARENT_COUNT_COLUMN))
 416 |         is_merge_commits.append(row.get(IS_MERGE_COMMIT_COLUMN))
 417 |         author_timestamps.append(row.get(AUTHOR_TIMESTAMP_COLUMN))
 418 |         commit_timestamps.append(row.get(COMMIT_TIMESTAMP_COLUMN))
 419 |         repo_stable_ids.append(row.get(REPO_STABLE_ID_COLUMN))
 420 |         filepath_stable_ids.append(row.get(FILEPATH_STABLE_ID_COLUMN))
 421 |         file_local_commit_indices.append(row.get(FILE_LOCAL_COMMIT_INDEX_COLUMN))
 422 |         ambiguous_reconstruction.append(
 423 |             row.get(HAS_AMBIGUOUS_RECONSTRUCTION_COLUMN, False)
 424 |         )
 425 |         rename_ambiguity.append(row.get(HAS_RENAME_AMBIGUITY_COLUMN, False))
 426 |         token_ids_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN, []))
 427 |         platform_ids_col.append(tokenized.get(PLATFORM_IDS_COLUMN, []))
 428 |         token_structure_ids_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_STRUCTURE_IDS_COLUMN, []))
 429 |         token_dep_levels_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN, []))
 430 |         token_ast_depth_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_AST_DEPTH_COLUMN, []))
 431 |         token_sibling_index_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_SIBLING_INDEX_COLUMN, []))
 432 |         token_ast_node_type_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_AST_NODE_TYPE_COLUMN, []))
 433 |         token_change_mask_pre_col.append(
 434 |             tokenized.get(TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_PRE_COLUMN, row.get(TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_PRE_COLUMN, []))
 435 |         )
 436 |         token_change_mask_post_col.append(
 437 |             tokenized.get(TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_POST_COLUMN, row.get(TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_POST_COLUMN, []))
 438 |         )
 439 |         hunk_id_per_token_col.append(
 440 |             tokenized.get(HUNK_ID_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN, row.get(HUNK_ID_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN, []))
 441 |         )
 442 |         edit_op_per_token_col.append(
 443 |             tokenized.get(EDIT_OP_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN, row.get(EDIT_OP_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN, []))
 444 |         )
 445 |         token_chunk_starts_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_CHUNK_STARTS_COLUMN, []))
 446 |         token_chunk_ends_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_CHUNK_ENDS_COLUMN, []))
 447 |         token_chunk_kinds_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_CHUNK_KINDS_COLUMN, []))
 448 |         token_chunk_dep_levels_col.append(
 449 |             tokenized.get(TOKEN_CHUNK_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN, [])
 450 |         )
 451 |         changed_chunk_ids_col.append(
 452 |             tokenized.get(CHANGED_CHUNK_IDS_COLUMN, row.get(CHANGED_CHUNK_IDS_COLUMN, []))
 453 |         )
 454 |         changed_chunk_spans_col.append(
 455 |             tokenized.get(CHANGED_CHUNK_SPANS_COLUMN, row.get(CHANGED_CHUNK_SPANS_COLUMN, []))
 456 |         )
 457 |         token_call_edges_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_CALL_EDGES_COLUMN, []))
 458 |         token_type_edges_col.append(tokenized.get(TOKEN_TYPE_EDGES_COLUMN, []))
 459 | 
 460 |     return pa.table(
 461 |         {
 462 |             "text": pa.array(texts, type=_SCHEMA.field("text").type),
 463 |             "actual_token_count": pa.array(
 464 |                 token_counts, type=_SCHEMA.field("actual_token_count").type
 465 |             ),
 466 |             "structure_ids": pa.array(
 467 |                 structure_ids_col, type=_SCHEMA.field("structure_ids").type
 468 |             ),
 469 |             "chunk_boundaries": pa.array(
 470 |                 chunk_boundaries_col, type=_SCHEMA.field("chunk_boundaries").type
 471 |             ),
 472 |             "call_edges": pa.array(
 473 |                 call_edges_col, type=_SCHEMA.field("call_edges").type
 474 |             ),
 475 |             "type_edges": pa.array(
 476 |                 type_edges_col, type=_SCHEMA.field("type_edges").type
 477 |             ),
 478 |             "platform_info": pa.array(
 479 |                 platform_info_col, type=_SCHEMA.field("platform_info").type
 480 |             ),
 481 |             "language_info": pa.array(
 482 |                 language_info_col, type=_SCHEMA.field("language_info").type
 483 |             ),
 484 |             "build_info": pa.array(
 485 |                 build_info_col, type=_SCHEMA.field("build_info").type
 486 |             ),
 487 |             "constituent_provenance": pa.array(
 488 |                 constituent_provenance_col,

 493 |                 type=_SCHEMA.field("constituent_provenance_json").type,

 495 |             REPO_COLUMN: pa.array(repos, type=_SCHEMA.field(REPO_COLUMN).type),

 497 |             COMMIT_HASH_COLUMN: pa.array(commits, type=_SCHEMA.field(COMMIT_HASH_COLUMN).type),
 498 |             TIMESTAMP_COLUMN: pa.array(timestamps, type=_SCHEMA.field(TIMESTAMP_COLUMN).type),
 499 |             PARENT_HASHES_COLUMN: pa.array(parent_hashes, type=_SCHEMA.field(PARENT_HASHES_COLUMN).type),

 504 |             REPO_STABLE_ID_COLUMN: pa.array(repo_stable_ids, type=_SCHEMA.field(REPO_STABLE_ID_COLUMN).type),
 505 |             FILEPATH_STABLE_ID_COLUMN: pa.array(filepath_stable_ids, type=_SCHEMA.field(FILEPATH_STABLE_ID_COLUMN).type),

 564 |             ),

 566 |                 token_chunk_kinds_col, type=_SCHEMA.field(TOKEN_CHUNK_KINDS_COLUMN).type

 579 |             ),

 581 |                 token_call_edges_col, type=_SCHEMA.field(TOKEN_CALL_EDGES_COLUMN).type
 582 |             ),

 591 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 592 | # GCS helpers
 593 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 594 | 
 595 | def gcs_list_files(prefix: str) -> list:
 596 |     """List GCS files under a prefix using gcloud storage."""
 597 |     uri = f"gs://{GCS_BUCKET}/{prefix}/"
 598 |     result = subprocess.run(
 599 |         ["gcloud", "storage", "ls", uri],
 600 |         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60,
 601 |     )
 602 |     if result.returncode != 0:
 603 |         log.error("gcloud storage ls failed: %s", result.stderr)
 604 |         return []
 605 |     return [line.strip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
 606 | 
 607 | 
 608 | def gcs_download(gcs_uri: str, local_path: str):
 609 |     """Download a GCS file to local path."""
 610 |     subprocess.run(
 611 |         ["gcloud", "storage", "cp", gcs_uri, local_path],
 612 |         check=True, timeout=600,
 613 |     )
 614 | 
 615 | 
 616 | def gcs_upload(local_path: str, gcs_uri: str):
 617 |     """Upload a local file to GCS."""
 618 |     subprocess.run(
 619 |         ["gcloud", "storage", "cp", local_path, gcs_uri],
 620 |         check=True, timeout=600,
 621 |     )
 622 | 
 623 | 
 624 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 625 | # Main pipeline
 626 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 627 | 
 628 | _PLATFORM_HEADER = build_platform_header(_DEFAULT_PLATFORM_INFO)
 629 | _HEADER_LEN = len(_PLATFORM_HEADER)
 630 | 
 631 | 
 632 | def _align_structure_ids(values: list, text_len: int) -> list[int]:
 633 |     """Return per-char structure IDs aligned to the emitted text length."""
 634 |     if not values:
 635 |         return [0] * text_len
 636 |     aligned = [int(v) for v in values[:text_len]]
 637 |     if len(aligned) < text_len:
 638 |         aligned.extend([0] * (text_len - len(aligned)))
 639 |     return aligned
 640 | 
 641 | 
 642 | def process_record(record: dict, tokenizer, max_tokens: int) -> list:
 643 |     """Apply full processing pipeline to one enriched JSONL record."""
 644 |     return process_record_with_policy(
 645 |         record,
 646 |         tokenizer,
 647 |         max_tokens,
 648 |         overflow_policy="split",
 649 |     )
 650 | 
 651 | 
 652 | def process_record_with_policy(
 653 |     record: dict,
 654 |     tokenizer,
 655 |     max_tokens: int,
 656 |     *,
 657 |     overflow_policy: str = "split",
 658 | ) -> list[dict]:
 659 |     """Apply full processing pipeline with explicit handling for oversized docs."""
 660 |     if overflow_policy not in _OVERFLOW_POLICIES:
 661 |         raise ValueError(
 662 |             f"Unsupported overflow_policy={overflow_policy!r}; "
 663 |             f"expected one of {_OVERFLOW_POLICIES}"
 664 |         )
 665 | 
 666 |     text = record.get("text", "")
 667 |     structure_ids = record.get("structure_ids", [])
 668 |     chunk_boundaries = record.get("chunk_boundaries", [])
 669 | 
 670 |     # 1. Dead-platform filter
 671 |     filtered_text = filter_dead_platforms(text)
 672 |     metadata_stale = filtered_text != text
 673 | 
 674 |     # 2. Prepend metadata headers; adjust structure_ids + chunk offsets
 675 |     language_prefix = language_info_to_prefix(record.get("language_info"))
 676 |     header_prefix = language_prefix + _PLATFORM_HEADER
 677 |     header_len = len(header_prefix)
 678 |     full_text = header_prefix + filtered_text
 679 |     if metadata_stale:
 680 |         filtered_structure_ids = [0] * len(filtered_text)
 681 |         filtered_chunk_boundaries = []
 682 |         filtered_call_edges = []
 683 |         filtered_type_edges = []
 684 |     else:
 685 |         filtered_structure_ids = _align_structure_ids(structure_ids, len(filtered_text))
 686 |         filtered_chunk_boundaries = chunk_boundaries
 687 |         filtered_call_edges = record.get("call_edges", [])
 688 |         filtered_type_edges = record.get("type_edges", [])
 689 | 
 690 |     full_sids = [0] * header_len + filtered_structure_ids
 691 | 
 692 |     adjusted_chunks = [
 693 |         {
 694 |             "start": cb.get("start", 0) + header_len,
 695 |             "end": cb.get("end", 0) + header_len,
 696 |             "kind": cb.get("kind", 0),
 697 |             "dep_level": cb.get("dep_level", 0),
 698 |             "name": cb.get("name", ""),
 699 |         }
 700 |         for cb in filtered_chunk_boundaries
 701 |     ]
 702 | 
 703 |     combined = {
 704 |         **{k: v for k, v in record.items()
 705 |            if k not in ("text", "structure_ids", "chunk_boundaries",
 706 |                         "call_edges", "type_edges", "actual_token_count")},
 707 |         "text": full_text,
 708 |         "structure_ids": full_sids,
 709 |         "chunk_boundaries": adjusted_chunks,
 710 |         "call_edges": filtered_call_edges,
 711 |         "type_edges": filtered_type_edges,
 712 |     }
 713 | 
 714 |     if overflow_policy == "drop":
 715 |         docs = maybe_keep_document_exact(combined, tokenizer, max_tokens=max_tokens)
 716 |         if not docs:
 717 |             log.info(
 718 |                 "drop overflow record repo=%s filepath=%s actual_token_count>%d",
 719 |                 record.get("repo", ""),
 720 |                 record.get("filepath", ""),
 721 |                 max_tokens,
 722 |             )
 723 |         return docs
 724 | 
 725 |     return chunk_document_exact(combined, tokenizer, max_tokens=max_tokens)
 726 | 
 727 | 
 728 | def _open_jsonl(path: str | os.PathLike[str]):
 729 |     target = Path(path)
 730 |     if target.suffix == ".gz":
 731 |         return gzip.open(target, "rt", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
 732 |     return open(target, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
 733 | 
 734 | 
 735 | def convert_local_jsonl_to_parquet(
 736 |     input_path: str | os.PathLike[str],
 737 |     output_path: str | os.PathLike[str],
 738 |     *,
 739 |     tokenizer,
 740 |     max_tokens: int,
 741 |     overflow_policy: str = "split",
 742 |     dry_run: bool = False,
 743 |     materialize_tokenized_enriched: bool = False,
 744 | ) -> dict[str, int]:
 745 |     """Convert a local clang JSONL/JSONL.GZ file into one parquet file."""
 746 |     if overflow_policy not in _OVERFLOW_POLICIES:
 747 |         raise ValueError(
 748 |             f"Unsupported overflow_policy={overflow_policy!r}; "
 749 |             f"expected one of {_OVERFLOW_POLICIES}"
 750 |         )
 751 | 
 752 |     source = Path(input_path)
 753 |     target = Path(output_path)
 754 |     docs_in = 0
 755 |     docs_out = 0
 756 |     rows: list[dict] = []
 757 | 
 758 |     with _open_jsonl(source) as handle:
 759 |         for line in handle:
 760 |             line = line.strip()
 761 |             if not line:
 762 |                 continue

 764 |             docs_in += 1

 767 |                 tokenizer,
 768 |                 max_tokens,
 769 |                 overflow_policy=overflow_policy,
 770 |             )

 772 |             docs_out += len(sub_docs)
 773 | 
 774 |     if dry_run:
 775 |         log.info(
 776 |             "[DRY RUN] local convert %s -> %s docs_in=%d docs_out=%d policy=%s",
 777 |             source,
 778 |             target,
 779 |             docs_in,
 780 |             docs_out,
 781 |             overflow_policy,
 782 |         )
 783 |         return {"docs_in": docs_in, "docs_out": docs_out}

 786 |         target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
 787 |         log.info("empty parquet")
 788 |         return {"docs_in": docs_in, "docs_out": docs_out}

 792 |     if materialize_tokenized_enriched:
 793 |         tokenized_rows = materialize_tokenized_enriched_batch(rows, tokenizer)

 798 |         target,
 799 |         docs_in,
 800 |         docs_out,
 801 |         overflow_policy,
 802 |     )
 803 |     return {"docs_in": docs_in, "docs_out": docs_out}

 805 | 
 806 | def process_input_file(gcs_uri: str, tmpdir: str,
 807 |                        dry_run: bool, shard_size: int,
 808 |                        shard_counter: list, output_rows: list,
 809 |                        output_prefix_local: str,
 810 |                        output_prefix_gcs: str,
 811 |                        tokenizer,
 812 |                        max_tokens: int,
 813 |                        overflow_policy: str = "split") -> tuple:
 814 |     """Download, decompress, and process one .jsonl.gz file.
 815 | 
 816 |     Returns (docs_in, docs_out) counts.
 817 |     """
 818 |     fname = gcs_uri.split("/")[-1]
 819 |     local_gz = os.path.join(tmpdir, fname)
 820 | 
 821 |     log.info("Downloading %s ...", gcs_uri)
 822 |     if not dry_run:
 823 |         gcs_download(gcs_uri, local_gz)
 824 |     else:
 825 |         log.info("[DRY RUN] Would download %s", gcs_uri)
 826 |         return 0, 0
 827 | 
 828 |     docs_in = 0
 829 |     docs_out = 0
 830 | 
 831 |     with gzip.open(local_gz, "rt", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
 832 |         for line in f:
 833 |             line = line.strip()
 834 |             if not line:
 835 |                 continue
 836 |             try:
 837 |                 record = json.loads(line)
 838 |             except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
 839 |                 log.warning("JSON decode error in %s: %s", fname, e)
 840 |                 continue
 841 | 
 842 |             docs_in += 1
 843 |             if overflow_policy == "split":
 844 |                 sub_docs = process_record(record, tokenizer, max_tokens)
 845 |             else:
 846 |                 sub_docs = process_record_with_policy(
 847 |                     record,
 848 |                     tokenizer,
 849 |                     max_tokens,
 850 |                     overflow_policy=overflow_policy,
 851 |                 )
 852 |             output_rows.extend(sub_docs)
 853 |             docs_out += len(sub_docs)
 854 | 
 855 |             # Flush shard when we hit shard_size
 856 |             while len(output_rows) >= shard_size:
 857 |                 shard_rows = output_rows[:shard_size]
 858 |                 output_rows[:] = output_rows[shard_size:]
 859 |                 _flush_shard(
 860 |                     shard_rows,
 861 |                     shard_counter,
 862 |                     dry_run,
 863 |                     output_prefix_local,
 864 |                     output_prefix_gcs,
 865 |                 )
 866 | 
 867 |     os.unlink(local_gz)
 868 |     return docs_in, docs_out
 869 | 
 870 | 
 871 | def _flush_shard(
 872 |     rows: list,
 873 |     counter: list,
 874 |     dry_run: bool,
 875 |     output_prefix_local: str,
 876 |     output_prefix_gcs: str,
 877 | ):
 878 |     """Write rows to a parquet file and upload to GCS."""
 879 |     shard_idx = counter[0]
 880 |     counter[0] += 1
 881 |     fname = f"train_{shard_idx:05d}.parquet"
 882 |     local_path = os.path.join(output_prefix_local, fname)
 883 |     gcs_uri = f"gs://{GCS_BUCKET}/{output_prefix_gcs}/{fname}"
 884 | 
 885 |     log.info("Writing shard %d (%d rows) -> %s", shard_idx, len(rows), gcs_uri)
 886 |     if dry_run:
 887 |         log.info("[DRY RUN] Would write %d rows to %s", len(rows), gcs_uri)
 888 |         return
 889 | 
 890 |     table = rows_to_table(
 891 |         rows,
 892 |         tokenized_rows=(
 893 |             materialize_tokenized_enriched_batch(rows, _TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_TOKENIZER)
 894 |             if _TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_TOKENIZER is not None

 897 |     )
 898 |     pq.write_table(table, local_path, compression="snappy")
 899 |     gcs_upload(local_path, gcs_uri)
 900 |     os.unlink(local_path)
 901 |     log.info("Uploaded shard %d (%d rows, %.1f MB)",
 902 |              shard_idx, len(rows),
 903 |              os.path.getsize(local_path) / 1e6 if os.path.exists(local_path) else 0)
 904 |
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/nanochat/scripts/data/reconcat_clang_4k.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 5524 bytes
- Created: 2026-04-08 01:34:11
- Modified: 2026-04-08 01:34:11

### Code

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Reconcat clang_semantic_4k_v10 small docs into properly-filled 4K chunks.
  3 | 
  4 | Problem: clang_semantic_4k_v10 has avg 369 tokens/doc (median 165) with 82% < 500 tokens.
  5 | Each doc starts with ~100 token boilerplate header (language, platform, compiler).
  6 | At max_seq_len=4096, we waste ~90% of each training sample.
  7 | 
  8 | Solution: concat same-project docs into larger chunks, dedup boilerplate headers.
  9 | Keep the first doc's header, strip headers from subsequent docs in the same chunk.
 10 | 
 11 | Usage:
 12 |     python scripts/data/reconcat_clang_4k.py \
 13 |         --input /path/to/clang_semantic_4k_v10/ \
 14 |         --output /path/to/clang_semantic_4k_v10_concat/ \
 15 |         --max_tokens 4096
 16 | """
 17 | import argparse
 18 | import glob
 19 | import os
 20 | import re
 21 | import sys
 22 | 
 23 | import pyarrow as pa
 24 | import pyarrow.parquet as pq
 25 | 
 26 | 
 27 | BOILERPLATE_RE = re.compile(
 28 |     r"^(//\s*(language|platform|compiler|standard|arch|<BOS>|build|confidence|primary).*\n)+",
 29 |     re.MULTILINE,
 30 | )
 31 | 
 32 | # Rough chars-per-token for C++ code
 33 | CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 3.5
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | def strip_boilerplate(text: str) -> str:
 37 |     """Remove the header boilerplate from a doc (keep for first doc in chunk)."""
 38 |     return BOILERPLATE_RE.sub("", text).lstrip("\n")
 39 | 
 40 | 
 41 | def estimate_tokens(text: str) -> int:
 42 |     return int(len(text) / CHARS_PER_TOKEN)
 43 | 
 44 | 
 45 | def extract_project(text: str) -> str:
 46 |     """Try to extract project name from boilerplate or first include."""
 47 |     # Look for filepath or project hints
 48 |     for line in text.split("\n")[:20]:
 49 |         if "// filepath:" in line.lower():
 50 |             parts = line.split("/")
 51 |             if len(parts) >= 2:
 52 |                 return parts[1] if parts[0].endswith(":") else parts[0]
 53 |         if "#include" in line and '"' in line:
 54 |             return "includes"
 55 |     return "unknown"
 56 | 
 57 | 
 58 | def process_shard(input_path: str, max_tokens: int = 4096) -> pa.Table:
 59 |     """Read a parquet shard and reconcat small docs into larger chunks."""
 60 |     table = pq.read_table(input_path, columns=["text"])
 61 |     texts = table.column("text").to_pylist()
 62 | 
 63 |     chunks = []
 64 |     current_chunk = ""
 65 |     current_tokens = 0
 66 | 
 67 |     for text in texts:
 68 |         tok = estimate_tokens(text)
 69 | 
 70 |         if tok >= max_tokens * 0.8:
 71 |             # Large doc — keep as-is
 72 |             if current_chunk:
 73 |                 chunks.append(current_chunk)
 74 |                 current_chunk = ""
 75 |                 current_tokens = 0
 76 |             chunks.append(text)
 77 |             continue
 78 | 
 79 |         if current_tokens + tok > max_tokens:
 80 |             # Flush current chunk
 81 |             if current_chunk:
 82 |                 chunks.append(current_chunk)
 83 |             current_chunk = text
 84 |             current_tokens = tok
 85 |         else:
 86 |             if current_chunk:
 87 |                 # Append with separator, strip boilerplate from non-first doc
 88 |                 stripped = strip_boilerplate(text)
 89 |                 current_chunk += "\n\n// ---\n\n" + stripped
 90 |                 current_tokens += estimate_tokens(stripped) + 3  # separator
 91 |             else:
 92 |                 current_chunk = text
 93 |                 current_tokens = tok
 94 | 
 95 |     if current_chunk:
 96 |         chunks.append(current_chunk)
 97 | 
 98 |     return pa.table({"text": chunks})
 99 | 
100 | 
101 | def main():
102 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Reconcat small clang docs into 4K chunks")
103 |     parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input parquet dir")
104 |     parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output parquet dir")
105 |     parser.add_argument("--max_tokens", type=int, default=4096)
106 |     args = parser.parse_args()
107 | 
108 |     os.makedirs(args.output, exist_ok=True)
109 |     shards = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(args.input, "shard_*.parquet")))
110 | 
111 |     if not shards:
112 |         print(f"No shards found in {args.input}")
113 |         sys.exit(1)
114 | 
115 |     total_input_docs = 0
116 |     total_output_docs = 0
117 |     total_input_tokens = 0
118 |     total_output_tokens = 0
119 | 
120 |     for i, shard_path in enumerate(shards):
121 |         print(f"Processing {os.path.basename(shard_path)} ({i+1}/{len(shards)})...", end=" ")
122 | 
123 |         input_table = pq.read_table(shard_path, columns=["text"])
124 |         input_texts = input_table.column("text").to_pylist()
125 |         input_docs = len(input_texts)
126 |         input_tokens = sum(estimate_tokens(t) for t in input_texts)
127 | 
128 |         output_table = process_shard(shard_path, args.max_tokens)
129 |         output_texts = output_table.column("text").to_pylist()
130 |         output_docs = len(output_texts)
131 |         output_tokens = sum(estimate_tokens(t) for t in output_texts)
132 | 
133 |         out_path = os.path.join(args.output, os.path.basename(shard_path))
134 |         pq.write_table(output_table, out_path)
135 | 
136 |         total_input_docs += input_docs
137 |         total_output_docs += output_docs
138 |         total_input_tokens += input_tokens
139 |         total_output_tokens += output_tokens
140 | 
141 |         avg_in = input_tokens / input_docs if input_docs else 0
142 |         avg_out = output_tokens / output_docs if output_docs else 0
143 |         print(f"{input_docs} → {output_docs} docs, avg {avg_in:.0f} → {avg_out:.0f} tok/doc")
144 | 
145 |     # Write completion sentinel
146 |     open(os.path.join(args.output, "_COMPLETE"), "w").close()
147 | 
148 |     print("\n=== SUMMARY ===")
149 |     print(f"Input:  {total_input_docs:,} docs, {total_input_tokens/1e9:.2f}B tokens, avg {total_input_tokens/total_input_docs:.0f} tok/doc")
150 |     print(f"Output: {total_output_docs:,} docs, {total_output_tokens/1e9:.2f}B tokens, avg {total_output_tokens/total_output_docs:.0f} tok/doc")
151 |     print(f"Compression: {total_input_docs/total_output_docs:.1f}x fewer docs, {total_output_tokens/total_input_tokens:.2f}x token retention")
152 | 
153 | 
154 | if __name__ == "__main__":
155 |     main()
```

- Language: python

### Code

```python
  1 | """
  2 | Extract and normalize C++ files from downloaded source repos.
  3 | Produces a single JSONL file suitable for tokenizer training.
  4 | 
  5 | Usage:
  6 |     python -m scripts.data.extract_cpp [--raw_dir data/cpp_raw] [--output data/cpp_clean.jsonl] [--max_gb 5]
  7 | """
  8 | import os
  9 | import re
 10 | import json
 11 | import argparse
 12 | from pathlib import Path
 13 | 
 14 | CPP_EXTENSIONS = {'.cpp', '.cc', '.cxx', '.c', '.h', '.hpp', '.hxx', '.cu', '.cuh'}
 15 | 
 16 | # Skip these directories (build artifacts, not source code)
 17 | # NOTE: We intentionally include third_party/vendor/external because they contain
 18 | # real C++ source code useful for tokenizer training.
 19 | SKIP_DIRS = {
 20 |     '.git', 'build', 'cmake-build',
 21 |     'node_modules', '.deps',
 22 |     'testdata', 'benchmarks/data',
 23 | }
 24 | 
 25 | # Auto-generated file markers
 26 | GENERATED_MARKERS = [
 27 |     'DO NOT EDIT', 'Generated by', 'AUTO-GENERATED', 'GENERATED FILE',
 28 |     'This file was generated', 'machine generated', 'auto-generated',
 29 |     '@generated', 'Code generated by',
 30 | ]
 31 | 
 32 | def is_generated(content: str, max_check: int = 500) -> bool:
 33 |     header = content[:max_check].upper()
 34 |     return any(m.upper() in header for m in GENERATED_MARKERS)
 35 | 
 36 | def strip_non_ascii_lines(code: str) -> str:
 37 |     """Remove lines containing non-ASCII characters.
 38 | 
 39 |     After comment translation, remaining non-ASCII is in string literals,
 40 |     identifiers, or test data (CJK text, box-drawing chars, Unicode test
 41 |     vectors).  These don't teach C++ reasoning so we drop the entire line
 42 |     to keep the corpus ASCII-clean.
 43 | 
 44 |     Lines that are part of a multi-line string literal or block comment may
 45 |     leave orphaned delimiters — the quality filter downstream will catch files
 46 |     that become too small or malformed.
 47 |     """
 48 |     lines = code.split('\n')
 49 |     cleaned = []
 50 |     for line in lines:
 51 |         try:
 52 |             line.encode('ascii')
 53 |             cleaned.append(line)
 54 |         except UnicodeEncodeError:
 55 |             # Drop the line — it contains non-ASCII characters
 56 |             continue
 57 |     return '\n'.join(cleaned)
 58 | 
 59 | 
 60 | def normalize_cpp(code: str) -> str:
 61 |     """Normalize C++ code: preserve indentation, compress blank lines, strip trailing whitespace.
 62 | 
 63 |     We keep indentation because it carries structural information and contributes
 64 |     meaningful tokens for a code model.  Tabs are converted to 4 spaces for
 65 |     consistency.  Consecutive blank lines are collapsed to one.
 66 |     """
 67 |     lines = code.split('\n')
 68 |     result = []
 69 |     prev_blank = False
 70 |     for line in lines:
 71 |         line = line.replace('\t', '    ').rstrip()
 72 |         if not line:
 73 |             if not prev_blank:
 74 |                 result.append('')
 75 |                 prev_blank = True
 76 |             continue
 77 |         prev_blank = False
 78 |         result.append(line)
 79 |     return '\n'.join(result)
 80 | 
 81 | def strip_license_header(code: str) -> str:
 82 |     """Remove license/copyright block comment at the top of file."""
 83 |     # Match /* ... */ block at very start (possibly after blank lines)
 84 |     m = re.match(r'^\s*/\*.*?\*/\s*\n?', code, re.DOTALL)
 85 |     if m:
 86 |         block = m.group()
 87 |         # Only strip if it looks like license (contains copyright/license keywords)
 88 |         block_lower = block.lower()
 89 |         if any(kw in block_lower for kw in ['copyright', 'license', 'permission is hereby granted',
 90 |                                               'redistribution', 'all rights reserved', 'spdx']):
 91 |             return code[m.end():]
 92 |     # Also match // comment block at top
 93 |     lines = code.split('\n')
 94 |     i = 0
 95 |     while i < len(lines) and i < 30:
 96 |         stripped = lines[i].strip()
 97 |         if stripped.startswith('//'):
 98 |             i += 1
 99 |         elif stripped == '':
100 |             i += 1
101 |         else:
102 |             break
103 |     if i > 3:
104 |         header = '\n'.join(lines[:i]).lower()
105 |         if any(kw in header for kw in ['copyright', 'license', 'permission is hereby granted']):
106 |             return '\n'.join(lines[i:])
107 |     return code
108 | 
109 | def quality_filter(content: str) -> bool:
110 |     """Return True if file passes quality checks."""
111 |     # Size checks
112 |     if len(content) < 100:
113 |         return False
114 |     if len(content) > 2_000_000:
115 |         return False
116 |     # Line length check
117 |     lines = content.split('\n')
118 |     if any(len(line) > 2000 for line in lines[:100]):
119 |         return False
120 |     # Unique lines ratio
121 |     if len(lines) > 10:
122 |         unique = len(set(lines))
123 |         if unique / len(lines) < 0.3:
124 |             return False
125 |     # Not auto-generated
126 |     if is_generated(content):
127 |         return False
128 |     return True
129 | 
130 | def should_skip_dir(dirpath: str) -> bool:
131 |     parts = Path(dirpath).parts
132 |     return any(p in SKIP_DIRS for p in parts)
133 | 
134 | def extract_files(raw_dir: str, output_path: str, max_bytes: int):
135 |     """Walk raw_dir, extract C++ files, normalize, write JSONL."""
136 |     total_bytes = 0
137 |     total_files = 0
138 |     skipped = 0
139 | 
140 |     with open(output_path, 'w') as out:
141 |         for root, dirs, files in os.walk(raw_dir):
142 |             # Prune skip dirs
143 |             dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in SKIP_DIRS and not d.startswith('.')]
144 | 
145 |             if should_skip_dir(root):
146 |                 continue
147 | 
148 |             for fname in files:
149 |                 ext = os.path.splitext(fname)[1].lower()
150 |                 if ext not in CPP_EXTENSIONS:
151 |                     continue
152 | 
153 |                 fpath = os.path.join(root, fname)
154 |                 try:
155 |                     with open(fpath, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
156 |                         content = f.read()
157 |                 except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
158 |                     skipped += 1
159 |                     continue
160 | 
161 |                 if not quality_filter(content):
162 |                     skipped += 1
163 |                     continue
164 | 
165 |                 # Process
166 |                 content = strip_license_header(content)
167 |                 content = strip_non_ascii_lines(content)
168 |                 content = normalize_cpp(content)
169 | 
170 |                 if len(content) < 50:
171 |                     skipped += 1
172 |                     continue
173 | 
174 |                 # Relative path for metadata
175 |                 rel_path = os.path.relpath(fpath, raw_dir)
176 |                 record = {"text": content, "path": rel_path}
177 |                 line = json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=True)
178 |                 out.write(line + '\n')
179 | 
180 |                 total_bytes += len(content)
181 |                 total_files += 1
182 | 
183 |                 if total_files % 10000 == 0:
184 |                     print(f"  {total_files:,} files, {total_bytes/1e9:.2f} GB")
185 | 
186 |                 if total_bytes >= max_bytes:
187 |                     print(f"Reached {max_bytes/1e9:.1f} GB limit")
188 |                     break
189 |             else:
190 |                 continue
191 |             break
192 | 
193 |     print(f"\nDone: {total_files:,} files, {total_bytes/1e9:.2f} GB text")
194 |     print(f"Skipped: {skipped:,} files")
195 |     print(f"Output: {output_path}")
196 | 
197 | 
198 | if __name__ == '__main__':
199 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
200 |     parser.add_argument('--raw_dir', default='data/cpp_raw')
201 |     parser.add_argument('--output', default='data/cpp_clean.jsonl')
202 |     parser.add_argument('--max_gb', type=float, default=5.0)
203 |     args = parser.parse_args()
204 | 
205 |     raw_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', args.raw_dir)
206 |     raw_dir = os.path.abspath(raw_dir)
207 |     output = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', args.output)
208 |     output = os.path.abspath(output)
209 |     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output), exist_ok=True)
210 | 
211 |     print(f"Extracting C++ from: {raw_dir}")
212 |     print(f"Output: {output}")
213 |     print(f"Max size: {args.max_gb} GB")
214 |     extract_files(raw_dir, output, int(args.max_gb * 1e9))
```

### Code

```python
  1 | """
  2 | Prepare C++ data for nanochat training pipeline.
  3 | 
  4 | 1. Convert cpp_clean.jsonl → parquet files (with 'text' column)
  5 | 2. Install our C++ tokenizer as the nanochat tokenizer
  6 | 3. Generate token_bytes.pt for BPB evaluation
  7 | 
  8 | Usage:
  9 |     python -m scripts.data.setup_cpp_training [--input data/cpp_clean.jsonl] [--tokenizer_dir data/cpp_tokenizer]
 10 | """
 11 | import os
 12 | import sys
 13 | import json
 14 | import argparse
 15 | import random
 16 | 
 17 | import pyarrow as pa
 18 | import pyarrow.parquet as pq
 19 | import torch
 20 | 
 21 | sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..'))
 22 | from nanochat.common import get_base_dir
 23 | from nanochat.cpp_tokenizer import CppTokenizer
 24 | 
 25 | 
 26 | def jsonl_to_parquets(input_path: str, output_dir: str, rows_per_file: int = 50_000,
 27 |                       val_fraction: float = 0.01):
 28 |     """Convert JSONL to parquet files with train/val split."""
 29 |     os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
 30 | 
 31 |     # Read all texts
 32 |     print(f"Reading {input_path}...")
 33 |     texts = []
 34 |     with open(input_path, 'r') as f:
 35 |         for line in f:
 36 |             if line.strip():
 37 |                 record = json.loads(line)
 38 |                 texts.append(record['text'])
 39 | 
 40 |     print(f"Total documents: {len(texts):,}")
 41 | 
 42 |     # Shuffle for randomness
 43 |     random.seed(42)
 44 |     random.shuffle(texts)
 45 | 
 46 |     # Split train/val
 47 |     val_count = max(1, int(len(texts) * val_fraction))
 48 |     val_texts = texts[-val_count:]
 49 |     train_texts = texts[:-val_count]
 50 |     print(f"Train: {len(train_texts):,} docs, Val: {len(val_texts):,} docs")
 51 | 
 52 |     # Write train shards
 53 |     shard_idx = 0
 54 |     for start in range(0, len(train_texts), rows_per_file):
 55 |         batch = train_texts[start:start + rows_per_file]
 56 |         table = pa.table({'text': batch})
 57 |         path = os.path.join(output_dir, f"shard_{shard_idx:05d}.parquet")
 58 |         pq.write_table(table, path, row_group_size=1024)
 59 |         shard_idx += 1
 60 | 
 61 |     # Write val as the last shard (nanochat convention: last parquet = val)
 62 |     val_table = pa.table({'text': val_texts})
 63 |     val_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f"shard_{shard_idx:05d}.parquet")
 64 |     pq.write_table(val_table, val_path, row_group_size=1024)
 65 | 
 66 |     print(f"Written {shard_idx} train shards + 1 val shard to {output_dir}")
 67 |     return shard_idx + 1
 68 | 
 69 | 
 70 | def install_tokenizer(tokenizer_dir: str, base_dir: str):
 71 |     """Copy our tokenizer to nanochat's expected location and generate token_bytes.pt."""
 72 |     target_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "tokenizer")
 73 |     os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
 74 | 
 75 |     # Load our tokenizer
 76 |     tok = CppTokenizer(tokenizer_dir)
 77 |     print(f"Loaded C++ tokenizer: {tok.vocab_size} tokens")
 78 | 
 79 |     # Save as HuggingFace format (tokenizer.json) — it's already in this format
 80 |     src_path = os.path.join(tokenizer_dir, "tokenizer.json")
 81 |     dst_path = os.path.join(target_dir, "tokenizer.json")
 82 |     import shutil
 83 |     shutil.copy2(src_path, dst_path)
 84 |     print(f"Copied tokenizer.json to {dst_path}")
 85 | 
 86 |     # Also copy the fixed_vocab for reference
 87 |     fixed_src = os.path.join(tokenizer_dir, "fixed_vocab.json")
 88 |     if os.path.exists(fixed_src):
 89 |         shutil.copy2(fixed_src, os.path.join(target_dir, "fixed_vocab.json"))
 90 | 
 91 |     # Generate token_bytes.pt
 92 |     # For each token, count the number of UTF-8 bytes it represents.
 93 |     # Special tokens (angle bracket format) get 0 bytes.
 94 |     id_to_token = tok._id_to_token
 95 |     vocab_size = tok.vocab_size
 96 | 
 97 |     token_bytes = []
 98 |     for tid in range(vocab_size):
 99 |         token_str = id_to_token.get(tid, "")
100 |         # Special/reserved tokens: 0 bytes
101 |         if token_str.startswith("<") and token_str.endswith(">"):
102 |             token_bytes.append(0)
103 |         elif not token_str:
104 |             token_bytes.append(0)
105 |         else:
106 |             token_bytes.append(len(token_str.encode("utf-8")))
107 | 
108 |     token_bytes_tensor = torch.tensor(token_bytes, dtype=torch.int32)
109 |     token_bytes_path = os.path.join(target_dir, "token_bytes.pt")
110 |     torch.save(token_bytes_tensor, token_bytes_path)
111 |     print(f"Saved token_bytes.pt ({vocab_size} entries) to {token_bytes_path}")
112 | 
113 |     # Stats
114 |     nonzero = token_bytes_tensor[token_bytes_tensor > 0].float()
115 |     print(f"  Non-special tokens: {len(nonzero)}")
116 |     print(f"  Bytes/token: mean={nonzero.mean():.2f}, min={nonzero.min()}, max={nonzero.max()}")
117 | 
118 |     # Generate a pickle version for RustBPETokenizer compatibility
119 |     # Actually, the training uses get_tokenizer() which returns RustBPETokenizer.
120 |     # We need to make get_tokenizer() return our CppTokenizer instead.
121 |     # For now, let's create a wrapper pickle file.
122 |     print("\nNOTE: You need to modify nanochat/tokenizer.py get_tokenizer() to use CppTokenizer")
123 |     print("  or set NANOCHAT_TOKENIZER=cpp environment variable")
124 | 
125 | 
126 | def main():
127 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
128 |     parser.add_argument('--input', default='data/cpp_clean.jsonl')
129 |     parser.add_argument('--tokenizer_dir', default='data/cpp_tokenizer')
130 |     parser.add_argument('--rows_per_file', type=int, default=50_000)
131 |     parser.add_argument('--val_fraction', type=float, default=0.01)
132 |     args = parser.parse_args()
133 | 
134 |     project_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..'))
135 |     input_path = os.path.join(project_root, args.input)
136 |     tokenizer_dir = os.path.join(project_root, args.tokenizer_dir)
137 | 
138 |     base_dir = get_base_dir()
139 |     data_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "base_data")
140 |     print(f"nanochat base dir: {base_dir}")
141 |     print(f"Data output: {data_dir}")
142 |     print()
143 | 
144 |     # Step 1: Convert JSONL → parquet
145 |     print("=== Step 1: Convert JSONL to Parquet ===")
146 |     jsonl_to_parquets(input_path, data_dir, args.rows_per_file, args.val_fraction)
147 |     print()
148 | 
149 |     # Step 2: Install tokenizer
150 |     print("=== Step 2: Install C++ Tokenizer ===")
151 |     install_tokenizer(tokenizer_dir, base_dir)
152 |     print()
153 | 
154 |     print("=== Setup Complete ===")
155 |     print(f"Data: {data_dir}")
156 |     print(f"Tokenizer: {os.path.join(base_dir, 'tokenizer')}")
157 |     print()
158 |     print("Next: run training with:")
159 |     print("  python -m scripts.base_train --depth=4 --num_iterations=100 --device_batch_size=4")
160 | 
161 | 
162 | if __name__ == '__main__':
163 |     main()
```

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | # ruff: noqa: E402
  3 | """Migrate clang_commits_4k_v1 parquet files to v12 schema.
  4 | 
  5 | Reads existing v1 parquet shards, adds missing v12 columns with appropriate
  6 | null/empty defaults, and writes upgraded v12 parquet files.
  7 | 
  8 | The v12 schema is defined in ``scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py``
  9 | and corresponds to the unified clang semantic family that covers both static
 10 | code and temporal commit data.

185 | # Map of column name -> default value factory for missing columns.

187 | _EMPTY_LIST_COLUMNS: dict[str, pa.DataType] = {}
188 | _SCALAR_DEFAULTS: dict[str, tuple[pa.DataType, object]] = {}
189 | 
190 | for field in V12_SCHEMA:
191 |     if pa.types.is_list(field.type):
192 |         _EMPTY_LIST_COLUMNS[field.name] = field.type
193 |     elif pa.types.is_string(field.type):
194 |         _SCALAR_DEFAULTS[field.name] = (field.type, None)
195 |     elif pa.types.is_boolean(field.type):
196 |         _SCALAR_DEFAULTS[field.name] = (field.type, False)
197 |     elif pa.types.is_integer(field.type):
198 |         _SCALAR_DEFAULTS[field.name] = (field.type, None)
199 | 
200 | 
201 | def _make_null_column(
202 |     name: str, pa_type: pa.DataType, num_rows: int
203 | ) -> pa.Array:
204 |     """Create a column of nulls or empty lists for the given type."""
205 |     if pa.types.is_list(pa_type):
206 |         # Empty list for each row
207 |         return pa.array([[] for _ in range(num_rows)], type=pa_type)
208 |     if pa.types.is_boolean(pa_type):
209 |         return pa.array([False] * num_rows, type=pa_type)
210 |     # Null for string/int scalars
211 |     return pa.array([None] * num_rows, type=pa_type)
212 | 
213 | 
214 | def migrate_table(table: pa.Table) -> pa.Table:
215 |     """Add missing v12 columns to a table, preserving existing data.
216 | 
217 |     Returns a new table with the full v12 schema. Existing columns are
218 |     kept unchanged; missing columns are filled with appropriate defaults.
219 |     Extra columns not in v12 schema are preserved at the end.
220 |     """
221 |     existing_names = set(table.column_names)
222 |     num_rows = table.num_rows
223 | 
224 |     # Build output columns in v12 schema order
225 |     columns: dict[str, pa.Array] = {}
226 |     added_columns: list[str] = []
227 | 
228 |     for field in V12_SCHEMA:
229 |         if field.name in existing_names:
230 |             # Keep existing column, cast if needed
231 |             col = table.column(field.name)
232 |             try:
233 |                 if col.type != field.type:
234 |                     col = col.cast(field.type, safe=False)
235 |             except (pa.ArrowInvalid, pa.ArrowNotImplementedError):
236 |                 # If cast fails, keep the original type
237 |                 pass
238 |             columns[field.name] = col
239 |         else:
240 |             # Add missing column with defaults
241 |             columns[field.name] = _make_null_column(
242 |                 field.name, field.type, num_rows
243 |             )
244 |             added_columns.append(field.name)
245 | 
246 |     # Preserve extra columns that exist in source but not in v12 schema
247 |     # (e.g., changed_symbol_ids, ripple_candidates from v1 raw output)
248 |     extra_columns: dict[str, pa.Array] = {}
249 |     for name in table.column_names:
250 |         if name not in {f.name for f in V12_SCHEMA}:
251 |             extra_columns[name] = table.column(name)
252 | 
253 |     if added_columns:
254 |         log.info("  Added %d missing columns: %s", len(added_columns), added_columns)
255 | 
256 |     if extra_columns:
257 |         log.info(
258 |             "  Preserved %d extra v1 columns: %s",
259 |             len(extra_columns),
260 |             list(extra_columns.keys()),
261 |         )
262 | 
263 |     # Build the output table: v12 columns first, then extras
264 |     all_names = list(columns.keys()) + list(extra_columns.keys())
265 |     all_arrays = list(columns.values()) + list(extra_columns.values())
266 |     return pa.table(dict(zip(all_names, all_arrays)))
267 | 
268 | 
269 | def migrate_shard(
270 |     input_path: Path,
271 |     output_path: Path,
272 |     *,
273 |     dry_run: bool = False,
274 |     row_group_size: int = 1024,
275 | ) -> dict:
276 |     """Migrate a single parquet shard from v1 to v12.
277 | 
278 |     Returns a summary dict with stats about the migration.
279 |     """
280 |     pf = pq.ParquetFile(str(input_path))
281 |     table = pf.read()
282 |     existing_columns = set(table.column_names)
283 |     v12_columns = {f.name for f in V12_SCHEMA}
284 |     missing = sorted(v12_columns - existing_columns)
285 |     extra = sorted(existing_columns - v12_columns)
286 | 
287 |     stats = {
288 |         "input_path": str(input_path),
289 |         "output_path": str(output_path),
290 |         "num_rows": table.num_rows,
291 |         "num_row_groups": pf.num_row_groups,
292 |         "existing_columns": len(existing_columns),
293 |         "v12_columns": len(v12_columns),
294 |         "missing_columns": missing,
295 |         "extra_columns": extra,
296 |         "dry_run": dry_run,
297 |     }
298 | 
299 |     if dry_run:
300 |         log.info(
301 |             "  [DRY RUN] %s: %d rows, %d missing columns, %d extra columns",
302 |             input_path.name,
303 |             table.num_rows,
304 |             len(missing),
305 |             len(extra),
306 |         )
307 |         return stats
308 | 
309 |     if not missing:
310 |         log.info("  %s: already v12, no migration needed", input_path.name)
311 |         if input_path != output_path:
312 |             # Copy as-is with extras preserved
313 |             migrated = migrate_table(table)
314 |             pq.write_table(migrated, str(output_path), row_group_size=row_group_size)
315 |         stats["action"] = "copied" if input_path != output_path else "skipped"
316 |         return stats
317 | 
318 |     migrated = migrate_table(table)
319 | 
320 |     # Write to output (may be same path for in-place migration)
321 |     pq.write_table(migrated, str(output_path), row_group_size=row_group_size)
322 | 
323 |     # Validate written file
324 |     check = pq.ParquetFile(str(output_path))
325 |     assert check.num_row_groups > 0, f"Empty parquet: {output_path}"
326 |     check.read_row_group(0)
327 | 
328 |     log.info(
329 |         "  %s: migrated %d rows, added %d columns",
330 |         input_path.name,
331 |         table.num_rows,
332 |         len(missing),
333 |     )
334 |     stats["action"] = "migrated"
335 |     return stats
336 | 
337 | 
338 | def migrate_directory(
339 |     input_dir: Path,
340 |     output_dir: Path,
341 |     *,
342 |     dry_run: bool = False,
343 |     row_group_size: int = 1024,
344 | ) -> list[dict]:
345 |     """Migrate all parquet shards in a directory from v1 to v12.
346 | 
347 |     Returns a list of per-shard stats dicts.
348 |     """
349 |     input_dir = Path(input_dir)
350 |     output_dir = Path(output_dir)
351 | 
352 |     if not input_dir.is_dir():
353 |         raise FileNotFoundError(f"Input directory not found: {input_dir}")
354 | 
355 |     # Collect parquet files (train shards + val shard)
356 |     parquet_files = sorted(input_dir.glob("*.parquet"))
357 |     if not parquet_files:
358 |         raise FileNotFoundError(f"No .parquet files found in {input_dir}")
359 | 
360 |     log.info(
361 |         "Found %d parquet files in %s", len(parquet_files), input_dir
362 |     )
363 | 
364 |     if not dry_run:
365 |         output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
366 | 
367 |     all_stats = []
368 |     total_rows = 0
369 |     total_missing = 0
370 | 
371 |     for pf_path in parquet_files:
372 |         out_path = output_dir / pf_path.name
373 |         stats = migrate_shard(
374 |             pf_path,
375 |             out_path,
376 |             dry_run=dry_run,
377 |             row_group_size=row_group_size,
378 |         )
379 |         all_stats.append(stats)
380 |         total_rows += stats["num_rows"]
381 |         total_missing += len(stats["missing_columns"])
382 | 
383 |     # Copy non-parquet files (e.g., _COMPLETE sentinel)
384 |     if not dry_run:
385 |         for item in input_dir.iterdir():
386 |             if item.is_file() and not item.name.endswith(".parquet"):
387 |                 dest = output_dir / item.name
388 |                 if item != dest:
389 |                     dest.write_bytes(item.read_bytes())
390 | 
391 |         # Write _COMPLETE sentinel for the output directory
392 |         sentinel = output_dir / "_COMPLETE"
393 |         sentinel.write_text(
394 |             f"Migrated from {input_dir.name} to v12 schema.\n"
395 |             f"{len(parquet_files)} shards, {total_rows} total rows.\n"
396 |         )
397 | 
398 |     log.info(
399 |         "Migration %s: %d shards, %d total rows",
400 |         "preview" if dry_run else "complete",
401 |         len(parquet_files),
402 |         total_rows,
403 |     )
404 | 
405 |     return all_stats
406 | 
407 | 
408 | def main() -> None:
409 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
410 |         description="Migrate clang_commits_4k_v1 parquet to v12 schema"
411 |     )
412 |     parser.add_argument(
413 |         "--input_dir",
414 |         required=True,
415 |         help="Path to v1 parquet directory (e.g., clang_commits_4k_v1)",
416 |     )
417 |     parser.add_argument(
418 |         "--output_dir",
419 |         required=True,
420 |         help="Path to output v12 directory (may equal input_dir for in-place)",
421 |     )
422 |     parser.add_argument(
423 |         "--dry_run",
424 |         action="store_true",
425 |         help="Report missing columns without writing",
426 |     )
427 |     parser.add_argument(
428 |         "--row_group_size",
429 |         type=int,
430 |         default=1024,
431 |         help="Row group size for output parquet (default: 1024)",
432 |     )
433 |     args = parser.parse_args()
434 | 
435 |     t0 = time.time()
436 |     stats = migrate_directory(
437 |         Path(args.input_dir),
438 |         Path(args.output_dir),
439 |         dry_run=args.dry_run,
440 |         row_group_size=args.row_group_size,
441 |     )
442 |     elapsed = time.time() - t0
443 | 
444 |     # Print summary
445 |     all_missing = set()
446 |     all_extra = set()
447 |     for s in stats:
448 |         all_missing.update(s["missing_columns"])
449 |         all_extra.update(s["extra_columns"])
450 | 
451 |     if all_missing:
452 |         log.info("Columns added across all shards: %s", sorted(all_missing))
453 |     if all_extra:
454 |         log.info("Extra v1 columns preserved: %s", sorted(all_extra))
455 |     log.info("Elapsed: %.1f seconds", elapsed)
456 | 
457 | 
458 | if __name__ == "__main__":
459 |     main()
```

```python
   1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2 | # ruff: noqa: E402
   3 | """
   4 | Clang-based cross-file dependency indexer for C++ training data preparation.
   5 | 
   6 | Uses libclang to parse C++ translation units with full semantic analysis,
   7 | building a cross-file call graph and generating bottom-up training documents.
   8 | 
   9 | Architecture:
  10 |   1. Walk project directory, find all .cpp/.cc/.cxx/.c files
  11 |   2. Parse each with libclang (optionally using compile_commands.json)
  12 |   3. Extract functions, classes, and cross-file call references
  13 |   4. Build global call graph
  14 |   5. Topological sort: HAL/system → drivers → subsystems → API
  15 |   6. Generate 16K-token training documents with bottom-up dependency ordering
  16 | 
  17 | Usage:
  18 |   # With compile_commands.json (best quality):
  19 |   python index_project.py --project-dir /path/to/project --output chunks.jsonl
  20 | 
  21 |   # Without build system (fallback mode):
  22 |   python index_project.py --project-dir /path/to/project --output chunks.jsonl --no-compile-db
  23 | 
  24 |   # Process multiple projects in parallel:
  25 |   python index_project.py --projects-list projects.txt --output chunks.jsonl --workers 48
  26 | """

 141 | def _iter_bundled_libclang_candidates() -> Iterator[str]:
 142 |     module_path = getattr(clang_cindex_module, "__file__", "")
 143 |     if not module_path:
 144 |         return
 145 |     native_dir = Path(module_path).resolve().parent / "native"
 146 |     if not native_dir.is_dir():
 147 |         return
 148 |     for pattern in ("libclang.so*", "libclang.dylib*", "libclang.dll*"):
 149 |         for candidate in sorted(native_dir.glob(pattern)):
 150 |             if candidate.is_file():
 151 |                 yield str(candidate)
 152 | 
 153 | 
 154 | def _iter_libclang_candidates(explicit_path: str | None = None) -> Iterator[str]:
 155 |     seen: set[str] = set()
 156 | 
 157 |     def add(value: str | None) -> str | None:
 158 |         if not value:
 159 |             return None
 160 |         candidate = value.strip()
 161 |         if not candidate or candidate in seen:
 162 |             return None
 163 |         seen.add(candidate)
 164 |         return candidate
 165 | 
 166 |     candidate = add(explicit_path)
 167 |     if candidate:
 168 |         yield candidate
 169 |     for env_name in (
 170 |         "NANOCHAT_LIBCLANG_PATH",
 171 |         "LIBCLANG_PATH",
 172 |         "LIBCLANG_FILE",
 173 |         "CLANG_LIBRARY_FILE",
 174 |     ):
 175 |         candidate = add(os.environ.get(env_name))
 176 |         if candidate:
 177 |             yield candidate
 178 |     for candidate in _iter_bundled_libclang_candidates():
 179 |         normalized = add(candidate)
 180 |         if normalized:
 181 |             yield normalized
 182 |     for candidate in _COMMON_LIBCLANG_CANDIDATES:
 183 |         if os.path.exists(candidate):
 184 |             normalized = add(candidate)
 185 |             if normalized:
 186 |                 yield normalized
 187 |     detected = add(ctypes.util.find_library("clang"))
 188 |     if detected:
 189 |         yield detected
 190 |     for pattern in (
 191 |         "/usr/lib*/**/libclang*.so*",
 192 |         "/usr/local/lib*/**/libclang*.so*",
 193 |         "/lib*/**/libclang*.so*",
 194 |     ):
 195 |         for candidate in glob.iglob(pattern, recursive=True):
 196 |             if os.path.isfile(candidate):
 197 |                 normalized = add(candidate)
 198 |                 if normalized:
 199 |                     yield normalized

 215 |         os.environ["NANOCHAT_LIBCLANG_PATH"] = candidate

 221 |     except Exception as exc:

 251 | CPP_EXTENSIONS = {'.cpp', '.cc', '.cxx', '.c', '.c++', '.cp'}
 252 | HEADER_EXTENSIONS = {'.h', '.hpp', '.hxx', '.hh', '.h++', '.inl', '.inc'}

 256 |     'std::', 'boost::', '__builtin', '__', 'operator', 'printf', 'fprintf',
```
