```python
 263 | 
 264 | class FunctionDef:
 265 |     """A function definition with its source location and call references."""
 266 |     __slots__ = ['name', 'qualified_name', 'file', 'line', 'end_line', 'text',
 267 |                  'callees', 'dep_level', 'is_definition']
 268 | 
 269 |     def __init__(self, name: str, qualified_name: str, file: str, line: int,
 270 |                  text: str, callees: list, is_definition: bool = True,
 271 |                  end_line: int = 0):
 272 |         self.name = name
 273 |         self.qualified_name = qualified_name
 274 |         self.file = file
 275 |         self.line = line
 276 |         self.end_line = end_line or (line + text.count('\n'))
 277 |         self.text = text
 278 |         self.callees = callees  # list of qualified names called
 279 |         self.dep_level = 0
 280 |         self.is_definition = is_definition
 281 | 
 282 |     def to_dict(self) -> dict:
 283 |         """Serialize for multiprocessing IPC."""
 284 |         return {
 285 |             'name': self.name, 'qualified_name': self.qualified_name,
 286 |             'file': self.file, 'line': self.line, 'text': self.text,
 287 |             'callees': self.callees, 'is_definition': self.is_definition,
 288 |         }
 289 | 
 290 |     @classmethod
 291 |     def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> 'FunctionDef':
 292 |         return cls(**d)
 293 | 
 294 | 
 295 | class ProjectIndex:
 296 |     """Cross-file function index for a single project."""
 297 | 
 298 |     def __init__(self):
 299 |         # qualified_name -> FunctionDef (definitions only)
 300 |         self.functions: dict[str, FunctionDef] = {}
 301 |         # file -> list of function qualified_names defined there
 302 |         self.file_functions: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
 303 |         # file -> preamble text (includes, typedefs, forward decls)
 304 |         self.file_preambles: dict[str, str] = {}
 305 |         # qualified_name -> list of qualified_names that call it
 306 |         self.callers: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
 307 | 
 308 |     def add_function(self, func: FunctionDef):
 309 |         """Add a function definition to the index."""
 310 |         key = func.qualified_name
 311 |         if key in self.functions and self.functions[key].is_definition:
 312 |             return  # don't overwrite definitions with declarations
 313 |         self.functions[key] = func
 314 |         if func.is_definition:
 315 |             self.file_functions[func.file].append(key)
 316 | 
 317 |     def build_reverse_edges(self):
 318 |         """Build caller -> callee reverse edges for dep level computation."""
 319 |         self.callers.clear()
 320 |         for qname, func in self.functions.items():
 321 |             for callee in func.callees:
 322 |                 if callee in self.functions:
 323 |                     self.callers[callee].append(qname)
 324 | 
 325 |     def compute_dep_levels(self):
 326 |         """Compute dependency levels via BFS from leaves."""
 327 |         # Find leaves: functions with no callees in the index
 328 |         in_degree = {}
 329 |         for qname, func in self.functions.items():
 330 |             local_callees = [c for c in func.callees if c in self.functions and c != qname]
 331 |             in_degree[qname] = len(local_callees)
 332 | 
 333 |         queue = deque()
 334 |         for qname, deg in in_degree.items():
 335 |             if deg == 0:
 336 |                 self.functions[qname].dep_level = 0
 337 |                 queue.append(qname)
 338 | 
 339 |         self.build_reverse_edges()
 340 | 
 341 |         while queue:
 342 |             qname = queue.popleft()
 343 |             level = self.functions[qname].dep_level
 344 |             for caller_name in self.callers.get(qname, []):
 345 |                 new_level = level + 1
 346 |                 if new_level > self.functions[caller_name].dep_level:
 347 |                     self.functions[caller_name].dep_level = new_level
 348 |                 in_degree[caller_name] -= 1
 349 |                 if in_degree[caller_name] == 0:
 350 |                     queue.append(caller_name)
 351 | 
 352 |         # Handle cycles
 353 |         max_level = max((f.dep_level for f in self.functions.values()), default=0)
 354 |         for qname, deg in in_degree.items():
 355 |             if deg > 0:
 356 |                 self.functions[qname].dep_level = max_level + 1
 357 | 
 358 |     def stats(self) -> dict:
 359 |         """Return index statistics."""
 360 |         return {
 361 |             'total_functions': len(self.functions),
 362 |             'total_files': len(self.file_functions),
 363 |             'definitions': sum(1 for f in self.functions.values() if f.is_definition),
 364 |             'max_dep_level': max((f.dep_level for f in self.functions.values()), default=0),
 365 |         }
 366 | 
 367 | 
 368 | def is_system_function(name: str | None) -> bool:
 369 |     """Check if a function name looks like a system/stdlib function."""
 370 |     if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
 371 |         return False
 372 |     return any(name.startswith(p) for p in SYSTEM_PREFIXES)
 373 | 
 374 | 
 375 | _DROP_CLANG_ARG_PREFIXES = (
 376 |     "-fsanitize=",
 377 |     "-fno-sanitize=",
 378 |     "-fsanitize-recover=",
 379 |     "-fno-sanitize-recover=",
 380 |     "-fsanitize-trap=",
 381 |     "-fsanitize-ignorelist=",
 382 |     "-fsanitize-blacklist=",
 383 |     "-fsanitize-coverage=",
 384 | )
 385 | 
 386 | _DROP_CLANG_ARGS = {
 387 |     "-fsanitize-address-use-after-scope",
 388 |     "-fno-sanitize-address-use-after-scope",
 389 |     "-shared-libasan",
 390 |     "-static-libasan",
 391 |     "-shared-libsan",
 392 |     "-static-libsan",
 393 | }
 394 | 
 395 | 
 396 | def _is_runtime_only_sanitizer_flag(text: str) -> bool:
 397 |     if text in _DROP_CLANG_ARGS:
 398 |         return True
 399 |     if text.startswith(("-shared-lib", "-static-lib")) and "san" in text:
 400 |         return True
 401 |     return False
 402 | 
 403 | 
 404 | def _sanitize_compile_args_for_clang(args: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
 405 |     """Drop nullish and runtime-only compile args that break libclang parsing."""
 406 |     if not args:
 407 |         return []
 408 |     sanitized: list[str] = []
 409 |     for arg in args:
 410 |         if arg is None:
 411 |             continue
 412 |         text = str(arg).strip()
 413 |         if not text:
 414 |             continue
 415 |         if _is_runtime_only_sanitizer_flag(text):
 416 |             continue
 417 |         if any(text.startswith(prefix) for prefix in _DROP_CLANG_ARG_PREFIXES):
 418 |             continue
 419 |         sanitized.append(text)
 420 |     return sanitized
 421 | 
 422 | 
 423 | def get_function_text(cursor: Cursor, tu: TranslationUnit) -> str:
 424 |     """Extract the source text for a cursor's extent."""
 425 |     extent = cursor.extent
 426 |     start = extent.start
 427 |     end = extent.end
 428 | 
 429 |     try:
 430 |         filename = start.file.name if start.file else None
 431 |         if not filename or not os.path.exists(filename):
 432 |             return ""
 433 | 
 434 |         with open(filename, 'r', errors='replace') as f:
 435 |             content = f.read()
 436 | 
 437 |         # Convert offsets
 438 |         start_offset = start.offset
 439 |         end_offset = end.offset
 440 |         if start_offset < len(content) and end_offset <= len(content):
 441 |             return content[start_offset:end_offset]
 442 |     except Exception:
 443 |         pass
 444 |     return ""
 445 | 
 446 | 
 447 | def extract_callees(cursor: Cursor) -> list[str]:
 448 |     """Extract all function call references from a cursor's children."""
 449 |     callees = set()
 450 | 
 451 |     def walk(node: Cursor):
 452 |         if node.kind == CursorKind.CALL_EXPR:
 453 |             ref = node.referenced
 454 |             if ref and ref.spelling:
 455 |                 # Get fully qualified name
 456 |                 qname = get_qualified_name(ref)
 457 |                 if qname and not is_system_function(qname):
 458 |                     callees.add(qname)
 459 |         for child in node.get_children():
 460 |             walk(child)
 461 | 
 462 |     walk(cursor)
 463 |     return list(callees)
 464 | 
 465 | 
 466 | def get_qualified_name(cursor: Cursor) -> str:
 467 |     """Get the fully qualified name of a cursor (namespace::class::func)."""
 468 |     parts = []
 469 |     c = cursor
 470 |     while c and c.kind != CursorKind.TRANSLATION_UNIT:
 471 |         if c.spelling:
 472 |             parts.append(c.spelling)
 473 |         c = c.semantic_parent
 474 |     parts.reverse()
 475 |     return '::'.join(parts)
 476 | 
 477 | 
 478 | def extract_preamble(tu: TranslationUnit, filename: str) -> str:
 479 |     """Extract #include directives and forward declarations from a file."""
 480 |     preamble_parts = []
 481 |     for cursor in tu.cursor.get_children():
 482 |         if cursor.location.file and cursor.location.file.name != filename:
 483 |             continue
 484 |         if cursor.kind in (CursorKind.INCLUSION_DIRECTIVE,
 485 |                            CursorKind.USING_DIRECTIVE,
 486 |                            CursorKind.USING_DECLARATION,
 487 |                            CursorKind.TYPEDEF_DECL,
 488 |                            CursorKind.TYPE_ALIAS_DECL,
 489 |                            CursorKind.NAMESPACE_ALIAS):
 490 |             text = get_function_text(cursor, tu)
 491 |             if text:
 492 |                 preamble_parts.append(text)
 493 |     return '\n'.join(preamble_parts)
 494 | 
 495 | 
 496 | FUNCTION_KINDS = {
 497 |     CursorKind.FUNCTION_DECL,
 498 |     CursorKind.CXX_METHOD,
 499 |     CursorKind.FUNCTION_TEMPLATE,
 500 |     CursorKind.CONSTRUCTOR,
 501 |     CursorKind.DESTRUCTOR,
 502 |     CursorKind.CONVERSION_FUNCTION,
 503 | }
 504 | 
 505 | CONTAINER_KINDS = {
 506 |     CursorKind.NAMESPACE,
 507 |     CursorKind.CLASS_DECL,
 508 |     CursorKind.STRUCT_DECL,
 509 |     CursorKind.CLASS_TEMPLATE,
 510 |     CursorKind.CLASS_TEMPLATE_PARTIAL_SPECIALIZATION,
 511 | }
 512 | 
 513 | 
 514 | def parse_translation_unit(
 515 |     filepath: str,
 516 |     index: Index,
 517 |     compile_args: list[str],
 518 |     project_dir: str,
 519 | ) -> list[FunctionDef]:
 520 |     """Parse a single C++ file and extract function definitions with callees."""
 521 |     functions: list[FunctionDef] = []
 522 | 
 523 |     try:
 524 |         tu = index.parse(
 525 |             filepath,
 526 |             args=compile_args,
 527 |             options=(
 528 |                 TranslationUnit.PARSE_INCOMPLETE |
 529 |                 TranslationUnit.PARSE_PRECOMPILED_PREAMBLE
 530 |             ),
 531 |         )
 532 |     except Exception as e:
 533 |         print(f"  WARN: Failed to parse {filepath}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
 534 |         return functions
 535 | 
 536 |     rel_path = os.path.relpath(filepath, project_dir)
 537 | 
 538 |     def visit(cursor):
 539 |         """Recursively visit cursors, descending into namespaces and classes."""
 540 |         if not cursor.location.file:
 541 |             return
 542 |         if cursor.location.file.name != filepath:
 543 |             return
 544 | 
 545 |         if cursor.kind in FUNCTION_KINDS and cursor.is_definition():
 546 |             text = get_function_text(cursor, tu)
 547 |             if text and len(text) >= 20:
 548 |                 callees = extract_callees(cursor)
 549 |                 qname = get_qualified_name(cursor)
 550 |                 functions.append(FunctionDef(
 551 |                     name=cursor.spelling,
 552 |                     qualified_name=qname,
 553 |                     file=rel_path,
 554 |                     line=cursor.location.line,
 555 |                     text=text,
 556 |                     callees=callees,
 557 |                     is_definition=True,
 558 |                 ))
 559 | 
 560 |         elif cursor.kind in CONTAINER_KINDS:
 561 |             # Recurse into namespaces, classes, structs
 562 |             for child in cursor.get_children():
 563 |                 visit(child)
 564 | 
 565 |     for cursor in tu.cursor.get_children():
 566 |         visit(cursor)
 567 | 
 568 |     return functions
 569 | 
 570 | 
 571 | _DEFAULT_SKIP_DIRS = frozenset({
 572 |     '.git', 'build', 'cmake-build', '__pycache__', 'node_modules',
 573 |     '.vs', '.vscode', 'third_party', 'external', 'deps', 'vendor',
 574 |     'test', 'tests', 'unittests', 'benchmarks',
 575 |     # Align with Rust cpp-chunker skip list
 576 |     'testing', 'examples', 'example', 'samples', 'sample', 'docs', 'doc',
 577 |     # Additional noise dirs for large repos
 578 |     'fuzzers', 'fuzzing', 'regression', 'fixtures',
 579 | })
 580 | 
 581 | C_EXTENSIONS = {'.c'}
 582 | 
 583 | 
 584 | def find_cpp_files(
 585 |     project_dir: str,
 586 |     extra_exclude_dirs: set[str] | None = None,
 587 | ) -> list[str]:
 588 |     """Find all C/C++ source files in a directory."""
 589 |     skip_dirs = _DEFAULT_SKIP_DIRS | (extra_exclude_dirs or set())
 590 |     files = []
 591 |     for root, dirs, filenames in os.walk(project_dir):
 592 |         # Prune recursion in-place — os.walk won't descend into skipped dirs
 593 |         dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in skip_dirs]
 594 |         for fname in filenames:
 595 |             ext = os.path.splitext(fname)[1].lower()
 596 |             if ext in CPP_EXTENSIONS:
 597 |                 filepath = os.path.join(root, fname)
 598 |                 # Skip very large files
 599 |                 try:
 600 |                     if os.path.getsize(filepath) > 500_000:
 601 |                         continue
 602 |                 except OSError:
 603 |                     continue
 604 |                 files.append(filepath)
 605 |     return files
 606 | 
 607 | 
 608 | def load_compile_commands(project_dir: str) -> Optional[dict]:
 609 |     """Load compile_commands.json if available."""
 610 |     cc_path = os.path.join(project_dir, 'compile_commands.json')
 611 |     compile_index = load_compile_commands_file(cc_path)
 612 |     if compile_index is None:
 613 |         return None
 614 |     file_args = {}
 615 |     for entry in compile_index.entries:
 616 |         file_args[entry.filepath] = {
 617 |             "compile_args": list(entry.compile_args),
 618 |             "build_info": dict(entry.build_info),
 619 |         }
 620 |     return file_args
 621 |     return None
 622 | 
 623 | 
 624 | def _discover_include_dirs(project_dir: str) -> list[str]:
 625 |     """Recursively find directories named 'include' for -I flags."""
 626 |     include_dirs = set()
 627 |     for candidate in ['include', 'src', 'lib', 'source', '.']:
 628 |         d = os.path.join(project_dir, candidate)
 629 |         if os.path.isdir(d):
 630 |             include_dirs.add(d)
 631 |     # Walk up to 3 levels deep for nested include/ dirs
 632 |     for root, dirs, _ in os.walk(project_dir):
 633 |         depth = root[len(project_dir):].count(os.sep)
 634 |         if depth > 3:
 635 |             dirs.clear()
 636 |             continue
 637 |         dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in {'.git', 'build', 'third_party', 'external', 'test', 'tests'}]
 638 |         if os.path.basename(root) == 'include':
 639 |             include_dirs.add(root)
 640 |     return sorted(include_dirs)
 641 | 
 642 | 
 643 | def _is_sane_compile_args(args: list[str]) -> bool:
 644 |     """Check if build_context returned usable args (not shell script garbage)."""
 645 |     for arg in args:
 646 |         if arg is None:
 647 |             return False
 648 |         # Garbage indicators: semicolons, unresolved $vars, paths after -x
 649 |         if ';' in arg or '$' in arg:
 650 |             return False
 651 |         # Empty -I flags
 652 |         if arg == '-I':
 653 |             return False
 654 |         # Compound flags (multiple args jammed into one string)
 655 |         # e.g., "-std=c89 -Werror -O0" — legitimate args never have spaces
 656 |         # except -x <lang> which is handled as separate args
 657 |         if ' ' in arg and not arg.startswith('-D'):
 658 |             return False
 659 |     return True
 660 | 
 661 | 
 662 | _SIMPLE_FALLBACK_ARGS = ["-fsyntax-only", "-Wno-everything"]
 663 | 
 664 | 
 665 | def get_default_compile_args(project_dir: str) -> list[str]:
 666 |     """Generate default compile args for projects without compile_commands.json."""
 667 |     _platform_info, args, _compile_index = detect_build_context(project_dir)
 668 | 
 669 |     if _is_sane_compile_args(args):
 670 |         result = list(args)
 671 |     else:
 672 |         # build_context returned garbage (e.g., shell fragments from configure)
 673 |         result = list(_SIMPLE_FALLBACK_ARGS)
 674 | 
 675 |     if not any(arg.startswith('-I') for arg in result):
 676 |         result.append(f'-I{project_dir}')
 677 |         for d in _discover_include_dirs(project_dir):
 678 |             result.append(f'-I{d}')
 679 |     return result
 680 | 
 681 | 
 682 | def _adapt_args_for_file(args: list[str], filepath: str) -> list[str]:
 683 |     """Adapt compile args based on file extension — .c files need C mode, not C++."""
 684 |     ext = os.path.splitext(filepath)[1].lower()
 685 |     if ext in C_EXTENSIONS:
 686 |         adapted = []
 687 |         skip_next = False
 688 |         for arg in args:
 689 |             if skip_next:
 690 |                 skip_next = False
 691 |                 continue
 692 |             if arg == '-x':
 693 |                 # Skip -x <lang> pair
 694 |                 skip_next = True
 695 |                 continue
 696 |             if arg.startswith('-std=c++'):
 697 |                 adapted.append('-std=c11')
 698 |                 continue
 699 |             if arg.startswith('-xc') or arg.startswith('-x '):
 700 |                 # -xc++ joined form — replace with -xc
 701 |                 continue
 702 |             adapted.append(arg)
 703 |         # Ensure C mode is set
 704 |         adapted = ['-x', 'c', '-std=c11'] + adapted
 705 |         return adapted
 706 |     return args
 707 | 
 708 | 
 709 | def estimate_tokens(text: str) -> int:
 710 |     """Estimate token count (~4 bytes per token for code)."""
 711 |     return max(1, len(text) // 4)
 712 | 
 713 | 
 714 | def collect_transitive_deps(
 715 |     root_qname: str,
 716 |     index: ProjectIndex,
 717 |     max_depth: int = 5,
 718 | ) -> list[str]:
 719 |     """BFS to collect transitive dependencies of a function."""
 720 |     visited = {root_qname}
 721 |     queue = deque([(root_qname, 0)])
 722 |     deps = []
 723 | 
 724 |     while queue:
 725 |         qname, depth = queue.popleft()
 726 |         if depth >= max_depth:
 727 |             continue
 728 |         func = index.functions.get(qname)
 729 |         if not func:
 730 |             continue
 731 |         for callee in func.callees:
 732 |             if callee not in visited and callee in index.functions:
 733 |                 visited.add(callee)
 734 |                 deps.append(callee)
 735 |                 queue.append((callee, depth + 1))
 736 | 
 737 |     return deps
 738 | 
 739 | 
 740 | def bucket_node_type(kind: str) -> int:
 741 |     """Map a tree-sitter node type string to a bucket in 0-255.
 742 | 
 743 |     Matches the Rust ``bucket_node_type`` in cpp_chunker/enriched.rs and
 744 |     v4_context_graph/enriched.rs so that the Python clang_indexer produces
 745 |     identical node-type buckets.
 746 | 
 747 |     Buckets:
 748 |       0: unknown/unnamed
 749 |       1-9: declarations
 750 |       10-19: statements
 751 |       20-29: expressions
 752 |       30-39: types
 753 |       40-49: literals
 754 |       50-59: operators and punctuation
 755 |       60-69: parameters and arguments
 756 |       70-79: access and scope
 757 |       80-89: miscellaneous
 758 |       255: catch-all for unrecognised named nodes
 759 |     """
 760 |     _MAP: dict[str, int] = {
 761 |         # 0: unknown
 762 |         "": 0,
 763 |         # 1-9: declarations
 764 |         "function_definition": 1,
 765 |         "class_specifier": 2,
 766 |         "struct_specifier": 3,
 767 |         "enum_specifier": 4,
 768 |         "namespace_definition": 5,
 769 |         "declaration": 6,
 770 |         "field_declaration": 7,
 771 |         "template_declaration": 8,
 772 |         "alias_declaration": 9, "type_definition": 9, "using_declaration": 9,
 773 |         # 10-19: statements
 774 |         "compound_statement": 10,
 775 |         "if_statement": 11,
 776 |         "for_statement": 12, "for_range_loop": 12,
 777 |         "while_statement": 13, "do_statement": 13,
 778 |         "return_statement": 14,
 779 |         "switch_statement": 15, "case_statement": 15,
 780 |         "expression_statement": 16,
 781 |         "try_statement": 17, "catch_clause": 17, "throw_statement": 17,
 782 |         "break_statement": 18, "continue_statement": 18, "goto_statement": 18,
 783 |         "labeled_statement": 19,
 784 |         # 20-29: expressions
 785 |         "call_expression": 20,
 786 |         "binary_expression": 21,
 787 |         "unary_expression": 22,
 788 |         "assignment_expression": 23, "augmented_assignment_expression": 23,
 789 |         "conditional_expression": 24,
 790 |         "field_expression": 25,
 791 |         "subscript_expression": 26,
 792 |         "cast_expression": 27, "static_cast_expression": 27,
 793 |         "dynamic_cast_expression": 27, "reinterpret_cast_expression": 27,
 794 |         "const_cast_expression": 27,
 795 |         "new_expression": 28, "delete_expression": 28,
 796 |         "lambda_expression": 29, "parenthesized_expression": 29,
 797 |         # 30-39: types
 798 |         "type_identifier": 30,
 799 |         "primitive_type": 31, "sized_type_specifier": 31,
 800 |         "template_type": 32, "template_argument_list": 32,
 801 |         "qualified_identifier": 33, "scoped_identifier": 33,
 802 |         "scoped_type_identifier": 33,
 803 |         "pointer_declarator": 34, "reference_declarator": 34,
 804 |         "abstract_pointer_declarator": 34, "abstract_reference_declarator": 34,
 805 |         "function_declarator": 35, "abstract_function_declarator": 35,
 806 |         "array_declarator": 36, "abstract_array_declarator": 36,
 807 |         "auto": 37, "decltype": 37,
 808 |         # 40-49: literals
 809 |         "number_literal": 40,
 810 |         "string_literal": 41, "raw_string_literal": 41, "string_content": 41,
 811 |         "char_literal": 42,
 812 |         "true": 43, "false": 43,
 813 |         "null": 44, "nullptr": 44,
 814 |         "user_defined_literal": 45,
 815 |         "concatenated_string": 46,
 816 |         # 50-59: operators and punctuation
 817 |         ",": 50, ";": 50, ":": 50, "::": 50,
 818 |         "{": 51, "}": 51,
 819 |         "(": 52, ")": 52,
 820 |         "[": 53, "]": 53,
 821 |         "<": 54, ">": 54, "<=": 54, ">=": 54, "==": 54, "!=": 54,
 822 |         "+": 55, "-": 55, "*": 55, "/": 55, "%": 55,
 823 |         "&": 56, "|": 56, "^": 56, "~": 56, "!": 56,
 824 |         "=": 57, "+=": 57, "-=": 57, "*=": 57, "/=": 57,
 825 |         "%=": 57, "&=": 57, "|=": 57, "^=": 57, "<<=": 57, ">>=": 57,
 826 |         "&&": 58, "||": 58,
 827 |         "->": 59, ".": 59, "->*": 59, ".*": 59,
 828 |         # 60-69: parameters and arguments
 829 |         "parameter_declaration": 60, "optional_parameter_declaration": 60,
 830 |         "variadic_parameter_declaration": 60,
 831 |         "parameter_list": 61,
 832 |         "argument_list": 62, "initializer_list": 62,
 833 |         "init_declarator": 63,
 834 |         # 70-79: access and scope
 835 |         "access_specifier": 70,
 836 |         "base_class_clause": 71,
 837 |         "field_initializer_list": 72, "field_initializer": 72,
 838 |         "namespace_identifier": 73,
 839 |         # 80-89: miscellaneous
 840 |         "comment": 80,
 841 |         "preproc_include": 81, "preproc_def": 81, "preproc_ifdef": 81,
 842 |         "preproc_ifndef": 81, "preproc_if": 81, "preproc_else": 81,
 843 |         "preproc_elif": 81, "preproc_call": 81, "preproc_function_def": 81,
 844 |         "translation_unit": 82,
 845 |         "identifier": 83,
 846 |         "ERROR": 84,
 847 |     }
 848 |     return _MAP.get(kind, 255)

 850 | 
 851 | def extract_ast_metadata(source: str) -> tuple[list[int], list[int], list[int]]:
 852 |     """Extract per-character AST metadata using tree-sitter.
 853 | 
 854 |     Returns ``(ast_depth, sibling_index, ast_node_type)`` each of length
 855 |     ``len(source)``.  Values match the Rust ``extract_ast_metadata`` in the
 856 |     cpp_chunker and v4_context_graph tools.
 857 | 
 858 |     If tree-sitter is not available, returns zero-filled arrays (graceful
 859 |     degradation).
 860 |     """
 861 |     text_len = len(source)
 862 |     if text_len == 0 or not _HAS_TREE_SITTER:
 863 |         return ([0] * text_len, [0] * text_len, [0] * text_len)
 864 | 
 865 |     source_bytes = source.encode("utf-8")
 866 |     byte_len = len(source_bytes)
 867 | 
 868 |     # Byte-indexed arrays
 869 |     b_depth = [0] * byte_len
 870 |     b_sib = [0] * byte_len
 871 |     b_ntype = [0] * byte_len
 872 | 
 873 |     parser = _ts.Parser(_TS_LANG)
 874 |     tree = parser.parse(source_bytes)
 875 |     if tree is None:
 876 |         return ([0] * text_len, [0] * text_len, [0] * text_len)
 877 | 
 878 |     # Iterative DFS (pre-order) — parents paint first, children overwrite.
 879 |     # Stack entries: (node, depth, sibling_idx)
 880 |     root = tree.root_node
 881 |     stack: list[tuple] = [(root, 0, 0)]
 882 | 
 883 |     while stack:
 884 |         node, depth, sib_idx = stack.pop()
 885 |         start = node.start_byte
 886 |         end = min(node.end_byte, byte_len)
 887 | 
 888 |         if start < end:
 889 |             bucket = bucket_node_type(node.type)
 890 |             for bi in range(start, end):
 891 |                 b_depth[bi] = depth
 892 |                 b_sib[bi] = sib_idx
 893 |                 b_ntype[bi] = bucket
 894 | 
 895 |         # Push children in reverse order so first child pops first
 896 |         child_depth = min(depth + 1, 63)
 897 |         children = node.children
 898 |         for i in range(len(children) - 1, -1, -1):
 899 |             child_sib = min(i, 63)
 900 |             stack.append((children[i], child_depth, child_sib))
 901 | 
 902 |     # Convert byte-indexed to char-indexed.
 903 |     # For pure ASCII (typical C++ code), byte len == char len.
 904 |     if byte_len == text_len:
 905 |         return (b_depth, b_sib, b_ntype)
 906 | 
 907 |     # Non-ASCII: map byte positions to char positions
 908 |     c_depth = [0] * text_len
 909 |     c_sib = [0] * text_len
 910 |     c_ntype = [0] * text_len
 911 |     byte_offset = 0
 912 |     for char_idx, ch in enumerate(source):
 913 |         if byte_offset < byte_len:
 914 |             c_depth[char_idx] = b_depth[byte_offset]
 915 |             c_sib[char_idx] = b_sib[byte_offset]
 916 |             c_ntype[char_idx] = b_ntype[byte_offset]
 917 |         byte_offset += len(ch.encode("utf-8"))
 918 |     return (c_depth, c_sib, c_ntype)
 919 | 
 920 | 
 921 | # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 922 | # Semantic metadata extraction (symbol_ids, call_targets, type_refs, def_use)
 923 | # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 924 | 
 925 | # Def-use constants
 926 | DEF_USE_NONE = 0
 927 | DEF_USE_DEF = 1
 928 | DEF_USE_USE = 2
 929 | 
 930 | # CursorKind sets for classification
 931 | _DEFINITION_KINDS = {
 932 |     CursorKind.FUNCTION_DECL,
 933 |     CursorKind.CXX_METHOD,
 934 |     CursorKind.CONSTRUCTOR,
 935 |     CursorKind.DESTRUCTOR,
 936 |     CursorKind.CONVERSION_FUNCTION,
 937 |     CursorKind.FUNCTION_TEMPLATE,
 938 |     CursorKind.VAR_DECL,
 939 |     CursorKind.FIELD_DECL,
 940 |     CursorKind.PARM_DECL,
 941 |     CursorKind.CLASS_DECL,
 942 |     CursorKind.STRUCT_DECL,
 943 |     CursorKind.UNION_DECL,
 944 |     CursorKind.ENUM_DECL,
 945 |     CursorKind.ENUM_CONSTANT_DECL,
 946 |     CursorKind.TYPEDEF_DECL,
 947 |     CursorKind.TYPE_ALIAS_DECL,
 948 |     CursorKind.NAMESPACE,
 949 |     CursorKind.CLASS_TEMPLATE,
 950 |     CursorKind.CLASS_TEMPLATE_PARTIAL_SPECIALIZATION,
 951 | }
 952 | 
 953 | _CALL_KINDS = {
 954 |     CursorKind.CALL_EXPR,
 955 | }
 956 | 
 957 | _TYPE_REF_KINDS = {
 958 |     CursorKind.TYPE_REF,
 959 |     CursorKind.TEMPLATE_REF,
 960 |     CursorKind.NAMESPACE_REF,
 961 | }
 962 | 
 963 | _REFERENCE_KINDS = {
 964 |     CursorKind.DECL_REF_EXPR,
 965 |     CursorKind.MEMBER_REF_EXPR,
 966 |     CursorKind.MEMBER_REF,
 967 |     CursorKind.TYPE_REF,
 968 |     CursorKind.TEMPLATE_REF,
 969 |     CursorKind.NAMESPACE_REF,
 970 |     CursorKind.OVERLOADED_DECL_REF,
 971 |     CursorKind.VARIABLE_REF,
 972 | }
 973 | 
 974 | 
 975 | def _compute_symbol_id(qname: str) -> int:
 976 |     """Deterministic 32-bit symbol ID from a qualified name.
 977 | 
 978 |     Uses the lower 31 bits of md5, leaving 0 as the sentinel for 'no symbol'.
 979 |     """
 980 |     if not qname:
 981 |         return 0
 982 |     h = int(hashlib.md5(qname.encode("utf-8", errors="replace")).hexdigest(), 16)
 983 |     # Mask to 31 bits (positive int32), reserve 0 for no-symbol
 984 |     result = (h & 0x7FFFFFFF) or 1
 985 |     return result
 986 | 
 987 | 
 988 | def extract_semantic_metadata(
 989 |     source: str,
 990 |     tu: 'TranslationUnit',
 991 |     filename: str,
 992 | ) -> dict:
 993 |     """Extract per-character semantic metadata from a translation unit.
 994 | 
 995 |     Walks the clang AST and produces four char-level arrays:
 996 | 
 997 |     - symbol_ids:   per-char deterministic hash of the symbol's qualified name
 998 |                     (0 = no symbol)
 999 |     - call_targets: per-char symbol ID of the call target for CALL_EXPR tokens
1000 |                     (0 = not a call)
1001 |     - type_refs:    per-char symbol ID of the referenced type for TYPE_REF tokens
1002 |                     (0 = not a type reference)
1003 |     - def_use:      per-char def/use marker (0=none, 1=def, 2=use)
1004 | 
1005 |     Returns:
1006 |         dict with keys 'symbol_ids', 'call_targets', 'type_refs', 'def_use',
1007 |         each a list[int] of length len(source).
1008 |     """
1009 |     text_len = len(source)
1010 |     symbol_ids = [0] * text_len
1011 |     call_targets = [0] * text_len
1012 |     type_refs = [0] * text_len
1013 |     def_use = [0] * text_len
1014 | 
1015 |     if text_len == 0 or tu is None:
1016 |         return {
1017 |             "symbol_ids": symbol_ids,
1018 |             "call_targets": call_targets,
1019 |             "type_refs": type_refs,
1020 |             "def_use": def_use,
1021 |         }
1022 | 
1023 |     source_bytes = source.encode("utf-8")
1024 |     byte_len = len(source_bytes)
1025 | 
1026 |     # Build byte-to-char mapping for non-ASCII sources
1027 |     if byte_len != text_len:
1028 |         byte_to_char = [0] * byte_len
1029 |         byte_offset = 0
1030 |         for char_idx, ch in enumerate(source):
1031 |             ch_bytes = len(ch.encode("utf-8"))
1032 |             for b in range(ch_bytes):
1033 |                 if byte_offset + b < byte_len:
1034 |                     byte_to_char[byte_offset + b] = char_idx
1035 |             byte_offset += ch_bytes
1036 |     else:
1037 |         byte_to_char = None  # identity mapping
1038 | 
1039 |     def _byte_to_char_offset(byte_off: int) -> int:
1040 |         if byte_to_char is None:
1041 |             return byte_off
1042 |         if byte_off >= byte_len:
1043 |             return text_len
1044 |         return byte_to_char[byte_off]
1045 | 
1046 |     def _visit(cursor):
1047 |         """Walk the AST and annotate char ranges."""
1048 |         loc = cursor.location
1049 |         if not loc or not loc.file:
1050 |             return
1051 |         if loc.file.name != filename:
1052 |             return
1053 | 
1054 |         extent = cursor.extent
1055 |         if not extent:
1056 |             return
1057 | 
1058 |         start_offset = extent.start.offset
1059 |         end_offset = extent.end.offset
1060 |         if start_offset >= end_offset:
1061 |             return
1062 | 
1063 |         # Convert to char offsets
1064 |         char_start = _byte_to_char_offset(start_offset)
1065 |         char_end = _byte_to_char_offset(end_offset)
1066 |         if char_start >= text_len or char_start >= char_end:
1067 |             return
1068 | 
1069 |         char_end = min(char_end, text_len)
1070 |         kind = cursor.kind
1071 | 
1072 |         # Symbol identification: get qualified name for definitions and refs
1073 |         qname = ""
1074 |         if kind in _DEFINITION_KINDS and cursor.is_definition():
1075 |             qname = get_qualified_name(cursor)
1076 |             if qname:
1077 |                 sym_id = _compute_symbol_id(qname)
1078 |                 for ci in range(char_start, char_end):
1079 |                     symbol_ids[ci] = sym_id
1080 |                     def_use[ci] = DEF_USE_DEF
1081 | 
1082 |         elif kind in _REFERENCE_KINDS:
1083 |             ref = cursor.referenced
1084 |             if ref and ref.spelling:
1085 |                 qname = get_qualified_name(ref)
1086 |                 if qname:
1087 |                     sym_id = _compute_symbol_id(qname)
1088 |                     for ci in range(char_start, char_end):
1089 |                         symbol_ids[ci] = sym_id
1090 |                         def_use[ci] = DEF_USE_USE
1091 | 
1092 |         # Call target annotation
1093 |         if kind in _CALL_KINDS:
1094 |             ref = cursor.referenced
1095 |             if ref and ref.spelling:
1096 |                 target_qname = get_qualified_name(ref)
1097 |                 if target_qname:
1098 |                     target_id = _compute_symbol_id(target_qname)
1099 |                     for ci in range(char_start, char_end):
1100 |                         call_targets[ci] = target_id
1101 | 
1102 |         # Type reference annotation
1103 |         if kind in _TYPE_REF_KINDS:
1104 |             ref = cursor.referenced
1105 |             if ref and ref.spelling:
1106 |                 ref_qname = get_qualified_name(ref)
1107 |                 if ref_qname:
1108 |                     ref_id = _compute_symbol_id(ref_qname)
1109 |                     for ci in range(char_start, char_end):
1110 |                         type_refs[ci] = ref_id
1111 | 
1112 |         # Recurse into children
1113 |         for child in cursor.get_children():
1114 |             _visit(child)
1115 | 
1116 |     try:
1117 |         for child in tu.cursor.get_children():
1118 |             _visit(child)
1119 |     except (RecursionError, Exception):
1120 |         # Graceful degradation: return whatever we have so far
1121 |         pass
1122 | 
1123 |     return {
1124 |         "symbol_ids": symbol_ids,
1125 |         "call_targets": call_targets,
1126 |         "type_refs": type_refs,
1127 |         "def_use": def_use,
1128 |     }
1129 | 
1130 | 
1131 | def extract_semantic_metadata_from_parts(
1132 |     full_text: str,
1133 |     parts_info: list[PartInfo],
1134 |     index: ProjectIndex,
1135 | ) -> dict:
1136 |     """Produce semantic metadata from pre-extracted parts without running libclang.
1137 | 
1138 |     This is the offline path used when the enriched doc is built from
1139 |     already-parsed FunctionDef objects. It assigns symbol_ids and def_use
1140 |     based on the parts_info metadata (qname, kind) and the call graph in
1141 |     the ProjectIndex.
1142 | 
1143 |     Returns:
1144 |         dict with keys 'symbol_ids', 'call_targets', 'type_refs', 'def_use',
1145 |         each a list[int] of length len(full_text).
1146 |     """
1147 |     text_len = len(full_text)
1148 |     symbol_ids = [0] * text_len
1149 |     call_targets = [0] * text_len
1150 |     type_refs = [0] * text_len
1151 |     def_use_arr = [0] * text_len
1152 | 
1153 |     offset = 0
1154 |     for i, part in enumerate(parts_info):
1155 |         part_text = part[0]
1156 |         kind = part[1]
1157 |         part_len = len(part_text)
1158 |         if offset + part_len > text_len:
1159 |             break
1160 | 
1161 |         qname = part[4] if len(part) > 4 else None
1162 | 
1163 |         if qname:
1164 |             sym_id = _compute_symbol_id(qname)
1165 |             # Function parts are definition sites
1166 |             for ci in range(offset, offset + part_len):
1167 |                 symbol_ids[ci] = sym_id
1168 |                 def_use_arr[ci] = DEF_USE_DEF
1169 | 
1170 |             # Annotate call targets from the index's callee list
1171 |             func_def = index.functions.get(qname)
1172 |             if func_def:
1173 |                 for callee_qname in func_def.callees:
1174 |                     callee_id = _compute_symbol_id(callee_qname)
1175 |                     if callee_id:
1176 |                         # Mark the call target on the function body
1177 |                         # (we don't have exact call-site offsets without
1178 |                         # re-parsing, so mark at chunk granularity)
1179 |                         for ci in range(offset, offset + part_len):
1180 |                             if call_targets[ci] == 0:
1181 |                                 call_targets[ci] = callee_id
1182 |                                 break  # one call target per callee
1183 | 
1184 |         elif kind == 1:  # PREAMBLE
1185 |             # Preamble: mark as use sites (references to included entities)
1186 |             for ci in range(offset, offset + part_len):
1187 |                 def_use_arr[ci] = DEF_USE_USE
1188 | 
1189 |         offset += part_len
1190 |         if i < len(parts_info) - 1:
1191 |             offset += 2  # "\n\n" separator
1192 | 
1193 |     return {
1194 |         "symbol_ids": symbol_ids,
1195 |         "call_targets": call_targets,
1196 |         "type_refs": type_refs,
1197 |         "def_use": def_use_arr,
1198 |     }
1199 | 
1200 | 
1201 | def build_enriched_doc(
1202 |     parts_info: list[PartInfo],
1203 |     index: ProjectIndex,
1204 |     filepath: str | None = None,
1205 |     compile_args: list[str] | None = None,
1206 |     build_info: dict | None = None,
1207 | ) -> dict:
1208 |     """Build enriched document from parts with metadata.
1209 | 
1210 |     Args:
1211 |         parts_info: list of (text, kind, dep_level, name, qname_or_none) tuples
1212 |             kind: 0=other, 1=preamble, 2=func_signature, 3=func_body,
1213 |                   4=class_decl, 5=class_member, 6=comment, 7=typedef, 8=namespace
1214 |         index: ProjectIndex for computing edges
1215 | 
1216 |     Returns:
1217 |         dict with text, structure_ids, chunk_boundaries, call_edges, type_edges,
1218 |         ast_depth, sibling_index, ast_node_type
1219 |     """
1220 |     texts = [p[0] for p in parts_info]
1221 |     full_text = '\n\n'.join(texts)
1222 |     text_len = len(full_text)
1223 | 
1224 |     structure_ids = [0] * text_len
1225 |     chunk_boundaries = []
1226 |     offset = 0
1227 | 
1228 |     # Map chunk_idx -> qname for edge computation
1229 |     chunk_qnames = {}
1230 |     chunk_callees = {}
1231 | 
1232 |     for i, (part_text, kind, dep_level, name, qname) in enumerate(parts_info):
1233 |         part_len = len(part_text)
1234 |         if offset + part_len > text_len:
1235 |             break
1236 | 
1237 |         # Fill structure_ids
1238 |         for j in range(offset, offset + part_len):
1239 |             structure_ids[j] = kind
1240 | 
1241 |         chunk_boundaries.append({
1242 |             'start': offset,
1243 |             'end': offset + part_len,
1244 |             'kind': kind,
1245 |             'dep_level': dep_level,
1246 |             'name': name,
1247 |         })
1248 | 
1249 |         # Track function qnames for edge computation
1250 |         if qname and qname in index.functions:
1251 |             chunk_qnames[i] = qname
1252 |             chunk_callees[i] = index.functions[qname].callees
1253 | 
1254 |         offset += part_len
1255 |         if i < len(parts_info) - 1:
1256 |             offset += 2  # "\n\n"
1257 | 
1258 |     # Compute call_edges
1259 |     call_edges = []
1260 |     for ci, caller_qname in chunk_qnames.items():
1261 |         callees = chunk_callees.get(ci, [])
1262 |         for callee_qname in callees:
1263 |             for cj, target_qname in chunk_qnames.items():
1264 |                 if ci != cj and target_qname == callee_qname:
1265 |                     call_edges.append({'from': ci, 'to': cj})
1266 | 
1267 |     # type_edges: empty for now (clang_indexer focuses on functions, not types)
1268 |     type_edges: list[dict[str, object]] = []
1269 | 
1270 |     # Extract per-character AST metadata via tree-sitter
1271 |     ast_depth, sibling_index, ast_node_type = extract_ast_metadata(full_text)
1272 | 
1273 |     # v4 enrichment: per-file platform detection
1274 |     import sys as _sys
1275 |     _parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
1276 |     _v5_dir = os.path.join(_parent, 'v5_gke_orchestrator')
1277 |     if _v5_dir not in _sys.path:
1278 |         _sys.path.insert(0, _v5_dir)
1279 |     platform_info: dict[str, object] | None
1280 |     try:
1281 |         _platform_detect = importlib.import_module("platform_detect")
1282 |         _detect_plat = cast(PlatformDetectFn, getattr(_platform_detect, "detect_platforms"))
1283 |         platform_info = _detect_plat(full_text)
1284 |     except ImportError:
1285 |         platform_info = None
1286 | 
1287 |     # Extract semantic metadata (symbol IDs, call targets, type refs, def-use)
1288 |     semantic_meta = extract_semantic_metadata_from_parts(full_text, parts_info, index)
1289 | 
1290 |     result = {
1291 |         'text': full_text,
1292 |         'structure_ids': structure_ids,
1293 |         'chunk_boundaries': chunk_boundaries,
1294 |         'call_edges': call_edges,
1295 |         'type_edges': type_edges,
1296 |         'ast_depth': ast_depth,
1297 |         'sibling_index': sibling_index,
1298 |         'ast_node_type': ast_node_type,
1299 |         'symbol_ids': semantic_meta['symbol_ids'],
1300 |         'call_targets': semantic_meta['call_targets'],
1301 |         'type_refs': semantic_meta['type_refs'],
1302 |         'def_use': semantic_meta['def_use'],
1303 |     }
1304 |     language_info = detect_language_info(
1305 |         full_text,
1306 |         filepath,
1307 |         platform_info,
1308 |         compile_args=compile_args,
1309 |         build_info=build_info,
1310 |     )
1311 | 
1312 |     if platform_info:
1313 |         result['platform_info'] = platform_info
1314 |     if build_info:
1315 |         result['build_info'] = build_info
1316 |     if language_info:
1317 |         result['language_info'] = language_info
1318 |     return result
1319 | 
1320 | 
1321 | def build_training_documents(
1322 |     index: ProjectIndex,
1323 |     max_tokens: int = 16384,
1324 |     max_dep_depth: int = 5,
1325 |     enriched: bool = False,
1326 |     *,
1327 |     project_dir: str | None = None,
1328 |     compile_db: dict | None = None,
1329 |     default_args: list[str] | None = None,
1330 |     default_build_info: dict | None = None,
1331 | ) -> list:
1332 |     """Build training documents with bottom-up dependency ordering.
1333 | 
1334 |     Returns list[str] when enriched=False, list[dict] when enriched=True.
1335 |     """
1336 |     documents: list[str | dict[str, object]] = []
1337 |     seen_hashes: set[str] = set()
1338 | 
1339 |     index.compute_dep_levels()
1340 | 
1341 |     for qname, func in index.functions.items():
1342 |         if not func.is_definition:
1343 |             continue
1344 | 
1345 |         # Collect transitive deps
1346 |         dep_qnames = collect_transitive_deps(qname, index, max_dep_depth)
1347 | 
1348 |         # Sort by dep_level (leaves/most foundational first)
1349 |         dep_funcs: list[FunctionDef] = []
1350 |         for dq in dep_qnames:
1351 |             df = index.functions.get(dq)
1352 |             if df and df.is_definition and df.text:
1353 |                 dep_funcs.append(df)
1354 |         dep_funcs.sort(key=lambda f: f.dep_level)
1355 | 
1356 |         # Build document
1357 |         preamble = index.file_preambles.get(func.file, '')
1358 | 
1359 |         def _assemble(dep_funcs_list: list[FunctionDef]) -> str:
1360 |             parts: list[str] = []
1361 |             if preamble:
1362 |                 parts.append(preamble)
1363 |             for df in dep_funcs_list:
1364 |                 parts.append(df.text)
1365 |             parts.append(func.text)
1366 |             return '\n\n'.join(parts)
1367 | 
1368 |         doc = _assemble(dep_funcs)
1369 |         tokens = estimate_tokens(doc)
1370 | 
1371 |         # Token budget management
1372 |         if tokens > max_tokens * 2 and dep_funcs:
1373 |             # Too big: trim deps from highest dep_level first
1374 |             while tokens > max_tokens * 2 and dep_funcs:
1375 |                 dep_funcs.pop()  # remove highest-level dep
1376 |                 doc = _assemble(dep_funcs)
1377 |                 tokens = estimate_tokens(doc)
1378 | 
1379 |         if tokens < 20:
1380 |             continue
1381 | 
1382 |         # Deduplicate
1383 |         doc_hash = hashlib.md5(doc.encode()).hexdigest()
1384 |         if doc_hash in seen_hashes:
1385 |             continue
1386 |         seen_hashes.add(doc_hash)
1387 | 
1388 |         if enriched:
1389 |             # Build parts_info for enriched output. Thread repo-level build
1390 |             # context through fallback lanes so build-file-derived args keep
1391 |             # their authoritative provenance in language_info/build_info.
1392 |             parts_info: list[PartInfo] = []
1393 |             if preamble:
1394 |                 parts_info.append((preamble, 1, 0, '', None))  # kind=1 PREAMBLE
1395 |             for df in dep_funcs:
1396 |                 parts_info.append((df.text, 2, df.dep_level, df.name,
1397 |                                    df.qualified_name))  # kind=2 FUNC
1398 |             parts_info.append((func.text, 2, func.dep_level, func.name,
1399 |                                func.qualified_name))  # kind=2 FUNC (root)
1400 |             compile_args = list(default_args or [])
1401 |             build_info = dict(default_build_info) if default_build_info else None
1402 |             if project_dir is not None:
1403 |                 abs_func_path = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(project_dir, func.file))
1404 |                 file_build = compile_db.get(abs_func_path) if compile_db else None
1405 |                 if file_build:
1406 |                     compile_args = file_build.get("compile_args", compile_args)
1407 |                     build_info = file_build.get("build_info") or build_info
1408 |             documents.append(
1409 |                 build_enriched_doc(
1410 |                     parts_info,
1411 |                     index,
1412 |                     filepath=func.file,
1413 |                     compile_args=compile_args,
1414 |                     build_info=build_info,
1415 |                 )
1416 |             )
1417 |         else:
1418 |             documents.append(doc)
1419 | 
1420 |     return documents
1421 | 
1422 | 
1423 | def _resolve_file_args(filepath, compile_db, default_args):
1424 |     """Get per-file compile args, adapting C vs C++ flags."""
1425 |     if compile_db and filepath in compile_db:
1426 |         build_entry = compile_db[filepath]
1427 |         args = _sanitize_compile_args_for_clang(build_entry.get("compile_args", default_args))
1428 |     else:
1429 |         args = _sanitize_compile_args_for_clang(default_args)
1430 |     return _adapt_args_for_file(args, filepath)
1431 | 
1432 | 
1433 | def _parse_file_batch(args_tuple):
1434 |     """Worker function for parallel parsing. Each worker creates its own Index."""
1435 |     filepaths, compile_db, default_args, project_dir = args_tuple
1436 |     sys.setrecursionlimit(50000)  # Set in each worker process too
1437 |     _configure_libclang()
1438 |     clang_index = Index.create()
1439 |     results = []
1440 |     errors = 0
1441 |     for filepath in filepaths:
1442 |         args = _resolve_file_args(filepath, compile_db, default_args)
1443 |         try:
1444 |             functions = parse_translation_unit(filepath, clang_index, args, project_dir)
1445 |             results.extend(f.to_dict() for f in functions)
1446 |         except (Exception, RecursionError):
1447 |             errors += 1
1448 |     return results, len(filepaths), errors
1449 | 
1450 | 
1451 | def _parse_single_file_worker(args_tuple):
1452 |     """Parse one file in a fresh subprocess so a segfault is file-local."""
1453 |     filepath, compile_db, default_args, project_dir = args_tuple
1454 |     sys.setrecursionlimit(50000)
1455 |     _configure_libclang()
1456 |     clang_index = Index.create()
1457 |     args = _resolve_file_args(filepath, compile_db, default_args)
1458 |     functions = parse_translation_unit(filepath, clang_index, args, project_dir)
1459 |     return [f.to_dict() for f in functions]

1461 | 
1462 | def _parse_files_isolated(
1463 |     cpp_files: list[str],
1464 |     compile_db: dict | None,
1465 |     default_args: list[str],
1466 |     project_dir: str,
1467 |     *,
1468 |     max_workers: int,
1469 | ) -> tuple[ProjectIndex, int, int]:
1470 |     """Parse files via one-file subprocess tasks so crashes don't kill the whole repo."""
1471 |     index_obj = ProjectIndex()
1472 |     parsed = 0
1473 |     errors = 0
1474 |     worker_count = max(1, max_workers)
1475 |     with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=worker_count) as executor:
1476 |         futures = {
1477 |             executor.submit(
1478 |                 _parse_single_file_worker,
1479 |                 (filepath, compile_db, default_args, project_dir),
1480 |             ): filepath
1481 |             for filepath in cpp_files
1482 |         }
1483 |         for future in as_completed(futures):
1484 |             filepath = futures[future]
1485 |             try:
1486 |                 func_dicts = future.result(timeout=120)  # 2 min per file
1487 |                 for d in func_dicts:
1488 |                     index_obj.add_function(FunctionDef.from_dict(d))
1489 |                 parsed += 1
1490 |             except TimeoutError:
1491 |                 errors += 1
1492 |                 if errors <= 5:
1493 |                     print(f"  TIMEOUT parsing {filepath} (>120s)", file=sys.stderr)
1494 |             except Exception as e:
1495 |                 errors += 1
1496 |                 if errors <= 5:
1497 |                     print(f"  ERROR parsing {filepath}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
1498 |             if parsed % 500 == 0 and parsed > 0:
1499 |                 print(
1500 |                     f"  Parsed {parsed}/{len(cpp_files)} files, {len(index_obj.functions)} functions",
1501 |                     file=sys.stderr,
1502 |                 )
1503 |     return index_obj, parsed, errors
1504 | 
1505 | 
1506 | def process_project(
1507 |     project_dir: str,
1508 |     max_tokens: int = 16384,
1509 |     max_dep_depth: int = 5,
1510 |     parse_workers: int = 1,
1511 |     enriched: bool = False,
1512 |     extra_exclude_dirs: set[str] | None = None,
1513 | ) -> list:
1514 |     """Process a single project: parse all files, build index, generate docs."""
1515 |     project_dir = os.path.abspath(project_dir)
1516 |     project_name = os.path.basename(project_dir)
1517 |     _configure_libclang()
1518 | 
1519 |     print(f"\n--- Processing project: {project_name} ---", file=sys.stderr)
1520 | 
1521 |     # Find source files
1522 |     cpp_files = find_cpp_files(project_dir, extra_exclude_dirs=extra_exclude_dirs)
1523 |     print(f"  Found {len(cpp_files)} C/C++ source files", file=sys.stderr)
1524 | 
1525 |     if not cpp_files:
1526 |         return []
1527 | 
1528 |     # Load or derive build context.
1529 |     compile_db = load_compile_commands(project_dir)
1530 |     _platform_info, _raw_args, _compile_index = detect_build_context(project_dir)
1531 |     default_args = _sanitize_compile_args_for_clang(
1532 |         get_default_compile_args(project_dir)
1533 |     )
1534 |     default_build_info = {
1535 |         key: value
1536 |         for key, value in _platform_info.items()
1537 |         if key in {"build_system", "source", "compiler", "standard"} and value is not None
1538 |     }
1539 | 
1540 |     # Parse all files and build index
1541 |     index_obj = ProjectIndex()
1542 | 
1543 |     # Use parallel parsing for large projects
1544 |     effective_workers = min(parse_workers, max(1, len(cpp_files) // 100))
1545 |     if effective_workers > 1 and len(cpp_files) > 200:
1546 |         print(f"  Using {effective_workers} parse workers", file=sys.stderr)
1547 |         chunk_size = max(50, len(cpp_files) // effective_workers)
1548 |         batches = []
1549 |         for i in range(0, len(cpp_files), chunk_size):
1550 |             batch = cpp_files[i:i + chunk_size]
1551 |             batches.append((batch, compile_db, default_args, project_dir))
1552 | 
1553 |         total_parsed = 0
1554 |         total_errors = 0
1555 |         try:
1556 |             with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=effective_workers) as executor:
1557 |                 for func_dicts, parsed_count, error_count in executor.map(_parse_file_batch, batches):
1558 |                     for d in func_dicts:
1559 |                         index_obj.add_function(FunctionDef.from_dict(d))
1560 |                     total_parsed += parsed_count
1561 |                     total_errors += error_count
1562 |                     print(f"  Parsed {total_parsed}/{len(cpp_files)} files, "
1563 |                           f"{len(index_obj.functions)} functions", file=sys.stderr)
1564 | 
1565 |             print(f"  Parsed {total_parsed} files ({total_errors} errors), "
1566 |                   f"{len(index_obj.functions)} functions indexed", file=sys.stderr)
1567 |         except Exception as exc:
1568 |             print(
1569 |                 f"  WARN: parallel parse failed ({exc}); retrying isolated per-file workers",
1570 |                 file=sys.stderr,
1571 |             )
1572 |             index_obj, parsed, errors = _parse_files_isolated(
1573 |                 cpp_files,
1574 |                 compile_db,
1575 |                 default_args,
1576 |                 project_dir,
1577 |                 max_workers=min(4, effective_workers),
1578 |             )
1579 |             print(f"  Parsed {parsed} files ({errors} errors), "
1580 |                   f"{len(index_obj.functions)} functions indexed", file=sys.stderr)
1581 |     else:
1582 |         # Sequential for small projects
1583 |         clang_index = Index.create()
1584 |         parsed = 0
1585 |         errors = 0
1586 |         for filepath in cpp_files:
1587 |             args = _resolve_file_args(filepath, compile_db, default_args)
1588 |             try:
1589 |                 functions = parse_translation_unit(filepath, clang_index, args, project_dir)
1590 |                 for func in functions:
1591 |                     index_obj.add_function(func)
1592 |                 parsed += 1
1593 |             except Exception as e:
1594 |                 errors += 1
1595 |                 if errors <= 5:
1596 |                     print(f"  ERROR parsing {filepath}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
1597 |             if parsed % 500 == 0 and parsed > 0:
1598 |                 print(f"  Parsed {parsed}/{len(cpp_files)} files, "
1599 |                       f"{len(index_obj.functions)} functions", file=sys.stderr)
1600 |         print(f"  Parsed {parsed} files ({errors} errors), "
1601 |               f"{len(index_obj.functions)} functions indexed", file=sys.stderr)
1602 | 
1603 |     # Build training documents
1604 |     documents = build_training_documents(
1605 |         index_obj,
1606 |         max_tokens,
1607 |         max_dep_depth,
1608 |         enriched=enriched,
1609 |         project_dir=project_dir,
1610 |         compile_db=compile_db,
1611 |         default_args=default_args,
1612 |         default_build_info=default_build_info,
1613 |     )
1614 |     print(f"  Generated {len(documents)} training documents", file=sys.stderr)
1615 | 
1616 |     stats = index_obj.stats()
1617 |     print(f"  Index stats: {stats}", file=sys.stderr)
1618 | 
1619 |     return documents
1620 | 
1621 | 
1622 | def main() -> int:
1623 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
1624 |         description='Clang-based cross-file C++ dependency indexer')
1625 |     parser.add_argument('--project-dir', type=str,
1626 |                         help='Single project directory to process')
1627 |     parser.add_argument('--projects-list', type=str,
1628 |                         help='File listing project directories (one per line)')
1629 |     parser.add_argument('--projects-dir', type=str,
1630 |                         help='Directory containing multiple project subdirectories')
1631 |     parser.add_argument('--output', type=str, required=True,
1632 |                         help='Output JSONL file path')
1633 |     parser.add_argument('--max-tokens', type=int, default=16384,
1634 |                         help='Max tokens per training document (default: 16384)')
1635 |     parser.add_argument('--max-dep-depth', type=int, default=5,
1636 |                         help='Max dependency resolution depth (default: 5)')
1637 |     parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=1,
1638 |                         help='Number of parallel workers for multi-project mode')
1639 |     parser.add_argument('--parse-workers', type=int, default=8,
1640 |                         help='Number of parallel parse workers within each project (default: 8)')
1641 |     parser.add_argument('--libclang-path', type=str, default=None,
1642 |                         help='Path to libclang.so (auto-detected if not set)')
1643 |     parser.add_argument('--append', action='store_true',
1644 |                         help='Append to output file instead of overwriting')
1645 |     parser.add_argument('--enriched', action='store_true',
1646 |                         help='Emit enriched output with structure_ids, chunk_boundaries, '
1647 |                              'call_edges, type_edges alongside text')
1648 |     parser.add_argument('--exclude-dirs', type=str, default=None,
1649 |                         help='Comma-separated extra directory names to exclude from file discovery')
1650 | 
1651 |     args = parser.parse_args()
1652 | 
1653 |     _configure_libclang(args.libclang_path)
1654 | 
1655 |     # Collect project directories
1656 |     project_dirs = []
1657 |     if args.project_dir:
1658 |         project_dirs.append(args.project_dir)
1659 |     elif args.projects_list:
1660 |         with open(args.projects_list) as f:
1661 |             project_dirs = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]
1662 |     elif args.projects_dir:
1663 |         for entry in sorted(os.listdir(args.projects_dir)):
1664 |             full = os.path.join(args.projects_dir, entry)
1665 |             if os.path.isdir(full):
1666 |                 project_dirs.append(full)
1667 |     else:
1668 |         parser.error("Provide --project-dir, --projects-list, or --projects-dir")
1669 | 
1670 |     print(f"Processing {len(project_dirs)} project(s)", file=sys.stderr)
1671 |     print(f"Output: {args.output}", file=sys.stderr)
1672 |     print(f"Max tokens: {args.max_tokens}", file=sys.stderr)
1673 | 
1674 |     extra_exclude = set(args.exclude_dirs.split(',')) if args.exclude_dirs else None
1675 | 
1676 |     total_docs = 0
1677 |     seen_hashes = set()
1678 | 
1679 |     append_mode = getattr(args, 'append', False)
1680 |     enriched = args.enriched
1681 |     with open(args.output, 'a' if append_mode else 'w') as out:
1682 |         if args.workers > 1 and len(project_dirs) > 1:
1683 |             # Multi-project parallel mode
1684 |             with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as executor:
1685 |                 futures = {
1686 |                     executor.submit(
1687 |                         process_project, pd, args.max_tokens, args.max_dep_depth,
1688 |                         args.parse_workers, enriched, extra_exclude
1689 |                     ): pd
1690 |                     for pd in project_dirs
1691 |                 }
1692 |                 for future in as_completed(futures):
1693 |                     pd = futures[future]
1694 |                     try:
1695 |                         docs = future.result()
1696 |                         for doc in docs:
1697 |                             if enriched:
1698 |                                 doc_text = doc['text']
1699 |                             else:
1700 |                                 doc_text = doc
1701 |                             doc_hash = hashlib.md5(doc_text.encode()).hexdigest()
1702 |                             if doc_hash in seen_hashes:
1703 |                                 continue
1704 |                             seen_hashes.add(doc_hash)
1705 |                             if enriched:
1706 |                                 json.dump(doc, out)
1707 |                             else:
1708 |                                 json.dump({'text': doc}, out)
1709 |                             out.write('\n')
1710 |                             total_docs += 1
1711 |                     except Exception as e:
1712 |                         print(f"ERROR processing {pd}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
1713 |         else:
1714 |             # Sequential mode
1715 |             for pd in project_dirs:
1716 |                 try:
1717 |                     docs = process_project(pd, args.max_tokens, args.max_dep_depth,
1718 |                                            args.parse_workers, enriched, extra_exclude)
1719 |                     for doc in docs:
1720 |                         if enriched:
1721 |                             doc_text = doc['text']
1722 |                         else:
1723 |                             doc_text = doc
1724 |                         doc_hash = hashlib.md5(doc_text.encode()).hexdigest()
1725 |                         if doc_hash in seen_hashes:
1726 |                             continue
1727 |                         seen_hashes.add(doc_hash)
1728 |                         if enriched:
1729 |                             json.dump(doc, out)
1730 |                         else:
1731 |                             json.dump({'text': doc}, out)
1732 |                         out.write('\n')
1733 |                         total_docs += 1
1734 |                     out.flush()
1735 |                 except Exception as e:
1736 |                     print(f"ERROR processing {pd}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
1737 |                     if len(project_dirs) == 1:
1738 |                         return 1
1739 |                     print("  Skipping project, continuing...", file=sys.stderr)
1740 | 
1741 |     print(f"\n{'='*60}", file=sys.stderr)
1742 |     print(f"Total documents: {total_docs}", file=sys.stderr)
1743 |     print(f"Output: {args.output}", file=sys.stderr)
1744 |     print(f"{'='*60}", file=sys.stderr)
1745 |     return 0
1746 | 
1747 | 
1748 | if __name__ == '__main__':
1749 |     raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
   1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2 | # ruff: noqa: E402
   3 | """Process git commit diffs into enriched training documents using libclang.
   4 | 
   5 | Mirrors the Rust cpp-chunker --commit-mode pipeline but uses libclang for
   6 | semantic parsing (qualified names, resolved call references, cross-file deps).
   7 | 
   8 | Pipeline:
   9 |   1. Read JSONL records: {old_content, new_content, diff, subject, body, filepath, repo}
  10 |   2. Parse old/new content with libclang (via temp files)
  11 |   3. Extract functions/classes, build call graph, compute dep levels
  12 |   4. Parse diff hunks → find changed functions → BFS transitive deps → build chains
  13 |   5. Format PRE-COMMIT/POST-COMMIT documents with enriched metadata
  14 |   6. Tokenize, enforce max-tokens budget, output enriched JSONL
  15 | 
  16 | Usage:
  17 |     python3 tools/clang_indexer/process_commits.py \
  18 |         --inputs raw_commits.jsonl \
  19 |         --output enriched_commits.jsonl \
  20 |         --max-tokens 4096 --format both
  21 | 
  22 |     # Then convert to parquet:
  23 |     python3 scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py \
  24 |         --input-file enriched_commits.jsonl \
  25 |         --output-file enriched_commits.parquet \
  26 |         --overflow-policy drop
  27 | """
  28 |

  32 | import json

  48 |     sys.path.insert(0, str(_REPO_ROOT))

  94 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  95 | # Data classes
  96 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  97 | 
  98 | @dataclass
  99 | class ClassDef:
 100 |     """A class/struct definition with source span."""
 101 |     name: str
 102 |     qualified_name: str
 103 |     text: str
 104 |     start_line: int
 105 |     end_line: int
```
