```python
210 | 
211 | # ─── C++ Comment Scanner (ported from scanner.rs) ─────────────────────────────
212 | 
213 | @dataclass
214 | class CommentSpan:
215 |     """A comment found in source code with non-ASCII content."""
216 |     byte_start: int       # offset of // or /* in the source bytes
217 |     byte_end: int         # exclusive end
218 |     comment_type: str     # "line" or "block"
219 |     original_text: str    # full text including markers
220 |     content: str          # content without markers
221 |     non_ascii_count: int
222 | 
223 | 
224 | def scan_comments(source: bytes) -> list[CommentSpan]:
225 |     """Scan C++ source bytes for comments containing non-ASCII characters.
226 | 
227 |     Mirrors the Rust scanner logic: skips string literals, char literals,
228 |     and raw string literals. Returns only comments with at least one
229 |     non-ASCII byte.
230 |     """
231 |     comments = []
232 |     n = len(source)
233 |     i = 0
234 | 
235 |     while i < n:
236 |         b = source[i]
237 | 
238 |         # Line comment
239 |         if b == 0x2F and i + 1 < n and source[i + 1] == 0x2F:  # //
240 |             start = i
241 |             i += 2
242 |             content_start = i
243 |             while i < n and source[i] != 0x0A:  # \n
244 |                 i += 1
245 |             content_end = i
246 |             content_bytes = source[content_start:content_end]
247 |             non_ascii = sum(1 for x in content_bytes if x > 127)
248 |             if non_ascii > 0:
249 |                 try:
250 |                     original = source[start:content_end].decode("utf-8")
251 |                     content = content_bytes.decode("utf-8")
252 |                     comments.append(CommentSpan(
253 |                         byte_start=start,
254 |                         byte_end=content_end,
255 |                         comment_type="line",
256 |                         original_text=original,
257 |                         content=content,
258 |                         non_ascii_count=non_ascii,
259 |                     ))
260 |                 except UnicodeDecodeError:
261 |                     pass
262 |             continue
263 | 
264 |         # Block comment
265 |         if b == 0x2F and i + 1 < n and source[i + 1] == 0x2A:  # /*
266 |             start = i
267 |             i += 2
268 |             content_start = i
269 |             while i + 1 < n and not (source[i] == 0x2A and source[i + 1] == 0x2F):
270 |                 i += 1
271 |             content_end = i
272 |             if i + 1 < n:
273 |                 i += 2  # skip */
274 |             end = i
275 | 
276 |             content_bytes = source[content_start:content_end]
277 |             non_ascii = sum(1 for x in content_bytes if x > 127)
278 |             if non_ascii > 0:
279 |                 try:
280 |                     original = source[start:end].decode("utf-8")
281 |                     content = content_bytes.decode("utf-8")
282 |                     comments.append(CommentSpan(
283 |                         byte_start=start,
284 |                         byte_end=end,
285 |                         comment_type="block",
286 |                         original_text=original,
287 |                         content=content,
288 |                         non_ascii_count=non_ascii,
289 |                     ))
290 |                 except UnicodeDecodeError:
291 |                     pass
292 |             continue
293 | 
294 |         # Raw string literal: R"delim(...)delim"
295 |         if b == 0x52 and i + 1 < n and source[i + 1] == 0x22:  # R"
296 |             i += 2
297 |             delim_start = i
298 |             while i < n and source[i] != 0x28:  # (
299 |                 i += 1
300 |             delimiter = source[delim_start:i]
301 |             if i < n:
302 |                 i += 1  # skip (
303 |             # Find )delimiter"
304 |             while i < n:
305 |                 if source[i] == 0x29:  # )
306 |                     after = i + 1
307 |                     dlen = len(delimiter)
308 |                     if (after + dlen < n
309 |                             and source[after:after + dlen] == delimiter
310 |                             and source[after + dlen] == 0x22):
311 |                         i = after + dlen + 1
312 |                         break
313 |                 i += 1
314 |             continue
315 | 
316 |         # String literal
317 |         if b == 0x22:  # "
318 |             i += 1
319 |             while i < n and source[i] != 0x22:
320 |                 if source[i] == 0x5C and i + 1 < n:  # backslash
321 |                     i += 1
322 |                 i += 1
323 |             if i < n:
324 |                 i += 1
325 |             continue
326 | 
327 |         # Char literal
328 |         if b == 0x27:  # '
329 |             i += 1
330 |             if i < n and source[i] == 0x5C and i + 1 < n:
331 |                 i += 1
332 |             if i < n:
333 |                 i += 1
334 |             if i < n and source[i] == 0x27:
335 |                 i += 1
336 |             continue
337 | 
338 |         i += 1
339 | 
340 |     return comments
341 | 
342 | 
343 | # ─── Classification and Replacement ──────────────────────────────────────────
344 | 
345 | def try_ascii_replace(content: str) -> Optional[str]:
346 |     """Try to replace all non-ASCII chars with ASCII equivalents.
347 |     Returns None if any char has no known mapping (needs translation).
348 |     """
349 |     result = []
350 |     for c in content:
351 |         if c.isascii():
352 |             result.append(c)
353 |         elif c in UNICODE_TO_ASCII:
354 |             result.append(UNICODE_TO_ASCII[c])
355 |         else:
356 |             return None
357 |     return "".join(result)
358 | 
359 | 
360 | def apply_replacements_to_source(source: bytes, replacements: list[tuple[int, int, str]]) -> bytes:
361 |     """Apply byte-range replacements to source, working end-to-start.
362 |     Each replacement is (byte_start, byte_end, new_text_str).
363 |     """
364 |     result = bytearray(source)
365 |     # Sort descending by start offset to avoid drift
366 |     for start, end, new_text in sorted(replacements, key=lambda r: r[0], reverse=True):
367 |         new_bytes = new_text.encode("utf-8")
368 |         result[start:end] = new_bytes
369 |     return bytes(result)
370 | 
371 | 
372 | def process_content_field(source_str: str) -> tuple[str | None, list[_CommentRecord]]:
373 |     """Process a single content field (old_content or new_content).
374 | 
375 |     Returns:
376 |         (cleaned_source, needs_translation_list)
377 |         cleaned_source has symbol replacements applied.
378 |         needs_translation_list contains dicts with comment info for Gemini.
379 |         Returns (None, []) if source has untranslatable comments that make
380 |         processing impossible.
381 |     """
382 |     if not source_str:
383 |         return source_str, []
384 | 
385 |     # Quick check: any non-ASCII at all?
386 |     if source_str.isascii():
387 |         return source_str, []
388 | 
389 |     source_bytes = source_str.encode("utf-8")
390 |     comments = scan_comments(source_bytes)
391 | 
392 |     if not comments:
393 |         # Non-ASCII exists but not in comments — could be in strings/code.
394 |         # Check if ALL non-ASCII is within string literals or code identifiers.
395 |         # We pass through as-is; the training data can handle non-ASCII in
396 |         # strings (they're not comments).
397 |         return source_str, []
398 | 
399 |     symbol_replacements: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []
400 |     needs_translation: list[_CommentRecord] = []
401 | 
402 |     for comment in comments:
403 |         replacement = try_ascii_replace(comment.content)
404 |         if replacement is not None:
405 |             # Can do symbol replacement
406 |             if comment.comment_type == "line":
407 |                 new_text = f"//{replacement}"
408 |             else:
409 |                 new_text = f"/*{replacement}*/"
410 |             symbol_replacements.append((comment.byte_start, comment.byte_end, new_text))
411 |         else:
412 |             needs_translation.append({
413 |                 "byte_start": comment.byte_start,
414 |                 "byte_end": comment.byte_end,
415 |                 "comment_type": comment.comment_type,
416 |                 "original_text": comment.original_text,
417 |                 "content": comment.content,
418 |             })
419 | 
420 |     # Apply symbol replacements immediately
421 |     if symbol_replacements:
422 |         source_bytes = apply_replacements_to_source(source_bytes, symbol_replacements)
423 |         try:
424 |             source_str = source_bytes.decode("utf-8")
425 |         except UnicodeDecodeError:
426 |             # Symbol replacement broke encoding — shouldn't happen but be safe
427 |             return None, []
428 | 
429 |         # Re-scan to get updated byte offsets for remaining comments
430 |         if needs_translation:
431 |             updated_comments = scan_comments(source_bytes)
432 |             # Match by content to update offsets
433 |             new_needs: list[_CommentRecord] = []
434 |             for nt in needs_translation:
435 |                 matched = False
436 |                 for uc in updated_comments:
437 |                     if uc.content == nt["content"] and uc.comment_type == nt["comment_type"]:
438 |                         new_needs.append({
439 |                             "byte_start": uc.byte_start,
440 |                             "byte_end": uc.byte_end,
441 |                             "comment_type": uc.comment_type,
442 |                             "original_text": uc.original_text,
443 |                             "content": uc.content,
444 |                         })
445 |                         matched = True
446 |                         break
447 |                 if not matched:
448 |                     # Comment vanished after symbol replacement — skip it
449 |                     pass
450 |             needs_translation = new_needs
451 | 
452 |     return source_str, needs_translation
453 | 
454 | 
455 | # ─── Gemini Translation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
456 | 
457 | class TranslatedComment(BaseModel):
458 |     id: str = Field(description="Comment ID from input")
459 |     translated: str = Field(description="English translation (ASCII only)")
460 |     source_language: str = Field(description="Detected source language ISO 639-1 code")
461 | 
462 | 
463 | class TranslationBatch(BaseModel):
464 |     comments: list[TranslatedComment]
465 | 
466 | 
467 | SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are a C++ code comment translator. Translate non-English comments to English.
468 | 
469 | Rules:
470 | 1. Translate ONLY the comment text content. Do NOT include comment markers (// or /* */).
471 | 2. Output MUST be pure ASCII - no Unicode characters at all.
472 | 3. Preserve code identifiers, function names, variable names exactly as-is.
473 | 4. Keep technical terminology in English.
474 | 5. Preserve whitespace structure (leading spaces, newlines in block comments).
475 | 6. If the comment mixes English and another language, translate only the non-English parts.
476 | 7. For author names with accents, transliterate to ASCII.
477 | """
478 | 
479 | 
480 | def estimate_tokens(text: str) -> int:
481 |     non_ascii = sum(1 for c in text if ord(c) > 127)
482 |     ascii_chars = len(text) - non_ascii
483 |     return non_ascii + ascii_chars // 4
484 | 
485 | 
486 | @dataclass
487 | class TranslationRequest:
488 |     """A batch of comments from one or more records needing translation."""
489 |     record_idx: int          # index into the pending list
490 |     field: str               # "old_content" or "new_content"
491 |     comments: list[dict]     # comment dicts with byte_start, content, etc.
492 | 
493 | 
494 | def build_translation_prompt(items: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> str:
495 |     """Build prompt from list of (id, content) pairs."""
496 |     lines = []
497 |     for cid, content in items:
498 |         lines.append(f"ID: {cid}")
499 |         lines.append(f"Content: {content}")
500 |         lines.append("")
501 |     return (
502 |         f"Translate the following {len(items)} non-English C++ comment texts to English.\n"
503 |         f"Return a JSON object with a 'comments' array.\n\n" + "\n".join(lines)
504 |     )
505 | 
506 | 
507 | def translate_batch_gemini(
508 |     client,
509 |     items: list[tuple[str, str]],
510 |     model_name: str,
511 |     worker_id: int,
512 |     max_retries: int = 3,
513 | ) -> Optional[dict[str, str]]:
514 |     """Translate a batch of (id, content) pairs. Returns {id: translated} or None."""
515 |     prompt = build_translation_prompt(items)
516 | 
517 |     for attempt in range(max_retries):
518 |         try:
519 |             response = client.models.generate_content(
520 |                 model=model_name,
521 |                 contents=prompt,
522 |                 config={
523 |                     "system_instruction": SYSTEM_PROMPT,
524 |                     "response_mime_type": "application/json",
525 |                     "response_schema": TranslationBatch,
526 |                 },
527 |             )
528 | 
529 |             if hasattr(response, "parsed") and response.parsed is not None:
530 |                 batch = response.parsed
531 |             else:
532 |                 batch = TranslationBatch.model_validate_json(response.text)
533 | 
534 |             result = {}
535 |             for t in batch.comments:
536 |                 text = t.translated
537 |                 if not text.isascii():
538 |                     text = text.encode("ascii", errors="replace").decode("ascii")
539 |                 result[t.id] = text
540 |             return result
541 | 
542 |         except Exception as e:
543 |             wait = 2 ** (attempt + 1) + worker_id * 0.5
544 |             print(f"  [W{worker_id}] Attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries} failed: {e}",
545 |                   flush=True)
546 |             if attempt < max_retries - 1:
547 |                 time.sleep(wait)
548 |             else:
549 |                 return None
550 |     return None
551 | 
552 | 
553 | # ─── Record Processing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
554 | 
555 | @dataclass
556 | class PendingRecord:
557 |     """A commit record that needs Gemini translation for some comments."""
558 |     line_idx: int
559 |     record: dict
560 |     # After symbol replacement, the partially-cleaned content
561 |     old_content_clean: Optional[str]
562 |     new_content_clean: Optional[str]
563 |     # Comments needing translation: list of (field, comment_dict)
564 |     translation_items: list[tuple[str, _CommentRecord]] = field(default_factory=list)
565 | 
566 | 
567 | @dataclass
568 | class Stats:
569 |     total_read: int = 0
570 |     total_ascii_only: int = 0           # records with no non-ASCII in comments
571 |     total_symbol_replaced: int = 0      # records fixed by symbol replacement alone
572 |     total_need_translation: int = 0     # records sent to Gemini
573 |     total_translated: int = 0           # records successfully translated
574 |     total_dropped: int = 0              # records dropped (translation failed)
575 |     total_written: int = 0
576 |     comments_symbol_replaced: int = 0   # individual comments replaced
577 |     comments_translated: int = 0        # individual comments translated
578 | 
579 | 
580 | def process_record_phase1(record: dict) -> tuple[Optional[dict], Optional[PendingRecord]]:
581 |     """Phase 1: scan comments, apply symbol replacements.
582 | 
583 |     Returns:
584 |         (clean_record, None) — if no translation needed, record is ready
585 |         (None, pending) — if translation is needed
586 |         (None, None) — if record should be dropped (e.g., broken encoding)
587 |     """
588 |     old_content = record.get("old_content", "")
589 |     new_content = record.get("new_content", "")
590 | 
591 |     # Process both fields
592 |     old_clean, old_needs = process_content_field(old_content)
593 |     if old_clean is None:
594 |         return None, None
595 | 
596 |     new_clean, new_needs = process_content_field(new_content)
597 |     if new_clean is None:
598 |         return None, None
599 | 
600 |     if not old_needs and not new_needs:
601 |         # No translation needed — record is clean
602 |         out = dict(record)
603 |         out["old_content"] = old_clean
604 |         out["new_content"] = new_clean
605 |         return out, None
606 | 
607 |     # Need translation
608 |     pending = PendingRecord(
609 |         line_idx=0,  # will be set by caller
610 |         record=record,
611 |         old_content_clean=old_clean,
612 |         new_content_clean=new_clean,
613 |     )
614 |     for comment in old_needs:
615 |         pending.translation_items.append(("old_content", comment))
616 |     for comment in new_needs:
617 |         pending.translation_items.append(("new_content", comment))
618 | 
619 |     return None, pending
620 | 
621 | 
622 | def apply_translations_to_record(
623 |     pending: PendingRecord,
624 |     translations: dict[str, str],
625 | ) -> Optional[dict]:
626 |     """Apply translations back to the record's content fields.
627 | 
628 |     Returns the clean record, or None if any comment couldn't be translated.
629 |     """
630 |     # Group translations by field
631 |     old_replacements: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []
632 |     new_replacements: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = []
633 | 
634 |     for field_name, comment in pending.translation_items:
635 |         cid = f"{field_name}:{comment['byte_start']}"
636 |         translated = translations.get(cid)
637 |         if translated is None:
638 |             return None  # Drop: missing translation
639 | 
640 |         if not translated.strip():
641 |             return None  # Drop: empty translation
642 | 
643 |         # Reconstruct with markers
644 |         if comment["comment_type"] == "line":
645 |             new_text = f"//{translated}"
646 |         else:
647 |             new_text = f"/*{translated}*/"
648 | 
649 |         if field_name == "old_content":
650 |             old_replacements.append((comment["byte_start"], comment["byte_end"], new_text))
651 |         else:
652 |             new_replacements.append((comment["byte_start"], comment["byte_end"], new_text))
653 | 
654 |     # Apply to old_content
655 |     old_str = pending.old_content_clean
656 |     if old_replacements:
657 |         if old_str is None:
658 |             return None
659 |         old_bytes = old_str.encode("utf-8")
660 |         old_bytes = apply_replacements_to_source(old_bytes, old_replacements)
661 |         try:
662 |             old_str = old_bytes.decode("utf-8")
663 |         except UnicodeDecodeError:
664 |             return None
665 | 
666 |     # Apply to new_content
667 |     new_str = pending.new_content_clean
668 |     if new_replacements:
669 |         if new_str is None:
670 |             return None
671 |         new_bytes = new_str.encode("utf-8")
672 |         new_bytes = apply_replacements_to_source(new_bytes, new_replacements)
673 |         try:
674 |             new_str = new_bytes.decode("utf-8")
675 |         except UnicodeDecodeError:
676 |             return None
677 | 
678 |     # Verify all ASCII in comments now
679 |     out = dict(pending.record)
680 |     out["old_content"] = old_str
681 |     out["new_content"] = new_str
682 |     return out
683 | 
684 | 
685 | # ─── Streaming Pipeline ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
686 | 
687 | def chunk_pending_records(
688 |     pending: list[PendingRecord],
689 |     max_tokens: int = 150_000,
690 |     max_comments: int = 80,
691 | ) -> list[list[_TranslationChunkItem]]:
692 |     """Group pending records into translation chunks that fit API limits.
693 | 
694 |     Each chunk is a list of (PendingRecord, items_in_this_chunk).
695 |     A single PendingRecord may be split across chunks if it has many comments.
696 |     """
697 |     chunks: list[list[_TranslationChunkItem]] = []
698 |     current_chunk: list[_TranslationChunkItem] = []
699 |     current_tokens = 0
700 |     current_count = 0
701 | 
702 |     for pr in pending:
703 |         for field_name, comment in pr.translation_items:
704 |             tokens = estimate_tokens(comment["content"]) + 20
705 | 
706 |             if current_chunk and (
707 |                 current_tokens + tokens > max_tokens or current_count >= max_comments
708 |             ):
709 |                 chunks.append(current_chunk)
710 |                 current_chunk = []
711 |                 current_tokens = 0
712 |                 current_count = 0
713 | 
714 |             current_chunk.append((pr, field_name, comment))
715 |             current_tokens += tokens
716 |             current_count += 1
717 | 
718 |     if current_chunk:
719 |         chunks.append(current_chunk)
720 | 
721 |     return chunks
722 | 
723 | 
724 | def process_translation_chunk(
725 |     chunk_idx: int,
726 |     chunk: list[_TranslationChunkItem],
727 |     client,
728 |     model_name: str,
729 |     worker_id: int,
730 | ) -> Optional[dict[str, str]]:
731 |     """Translate one chunk. Returns {comment_id: translated_text} or None."""
732 |     items: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
733 |     for pr, field_name, comment in chunk:
734 |         cid = f"{field_name}:{comment['byte_start']}"
735 |         items.append((cid, comment["content"]))
736 | 
737 |     return translate_batch_gemini(client, items, model_name, worker_id)
738 | 
739 | 
740 | def main():
741 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
742 |         description="Translate non-English comments in CommitPack JSONL"
743 |     )
744 |     parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input JSONL file")
745 |     parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output JSONL file")
746 |     parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=8, help="Parallel Gemini workers")
747 |     parser.add_argument("--model", default="gemini-2.5-flash", help="Gemini model")
748 |     parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=5000,
749 |                         help="Records to buffer before flushing translations")
750 |     parser.add_argument("--delay", type=float, default=0.5,
751 |                         help="Seconds between API calls per worker")
752 |     parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
753 |                         help="Scan and report stats without calling Gemini")
754 |     parser.add_argument("--max-records", type=int, default=0,
755 |                         help="Stop after N records (0 = no limit)")
756 |     args = parser.parse_args()
757 | 
758 |     api_key = os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY")
759 |     if not api_key and not args.dry_run:
760 |         print("ERROR: GEMINI_API_KEY not set", file=sys.stderr)
761 |         sys.exit(1)
762 | 
763 |     clients = []
764 |     if not args.dry_run:
765 |         genai = _load_google_genai()
766 |         clients = [genai.Client(api_key=api_key) for _ in range(args.workers)]
767 | 
768 |     stats = Stats()
769 |     start_time = time.time()
770 | 
771 |     outf = open(args.output, "w", buffering=1024 * 1024)
772 | 
773 |     write_lock = threading.Lock()
774 | 
775 |     def write_record(record: dict):
776 |         line = json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
777 |         with write_lock:
778 |             outf.write(line)
779 |             stats.total_written += 1
780 | 
781 |     def flush_pending_batch(pending_batch: list[PendingRecord]):
782 |         """Send a batch of pending records through Gemini and write results."""
783 |         if not pending_batch:
784 |             return
785 | 
786 |         if args.dry_run:
787 |             stats.total_dropped += len(pending_batch)
788 |             return
789 | 
790 |         chunks = chunk_pending_records(pending_batch)
791 |         # Collect all translations
792 |         all_translations: dict[str, str] = {}
793 |         failed_chunks = 0
794 | 
795 |         with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as executor:
796 |             futures = {}
797 |             for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
798 |                 worker_id = i % args.workers
799 |                 future = executor.submit(
800 |                     process_translation_chunk,
801 |                     i, chunk, clients[worker_id], args.model, worker_id,
802 |                 )
803 |                 futures[future] = i
804 | 
805 |             for future in as_completed(futures):
806 |                 chunk_idx = futures[future]
807 |                 try:
808 |                     result = future.result()
809 |                     if result is not None:
810 |                         all_translations.update(result)
811 |                         stats.comments_translated += len(result)
812 |                     else:
813 |                         failed_chunks += 1
814 |                 except Exception as e:
815 |                     print(f"  Chunk {chunk_idx} exception: {e}", flush=True)
816 |                     failed_chunks += 1
817 | 
818 |                 time.sleep(args.delay)
819 | 
820 |         # Apply translations to each pending record
821 |         for pr in pending_batch:
822 |             clean_record = apply_translations_to_record(pr, all_translations)
823 |             if clean_record is not None:
824 |                 write_record(clean_record)
825 |                 stats.total_translated += 1
826 |             else:
827 |                 stats.total_dropped += 1
828 | 
829 |     # ─── Main streaming loop ──────────────────────────────────────────────
830 | 
831 |     pending_batch: list[PendingRecord] = []
832 | 
833 |     with open(args.input, "r", buffering=16 * 1024 * 1024) as inf:
834 |         for line_idx, line in enumerate(inf):
835 |             line = line.strip()
836 |             if not line:
837 |                 continue
838 | 
839 |             try:
840 |                 record = json.loads(line)
841 |             except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
842 |                 print(f"  Line {line_idx}: JSON error: {e}", flush=True)
843 |                 stats.total_dropped += 1
844 |                 continue
845 | 
846 |             stats.total_read += 1
847 | 
848 |             if args.max_records and stats.total_read > args.max_records:
849 |                 break
850 | 
851 |             # Phase 1: scan + symbol replace
852 |             clean_record, pending = process_record_phase1(record)
853 | 
854 |             if clean_record is not None:
855 |                 # No translation needed
856 |                 has_non_ascii_comments = (
857 |                     record.get("old_content", "") != clean_record.get("old_content", "")
858 |                     or record.get("new_content", "") != clean_record.get("new_content", "")
859 |                 )
860 |                 if has_non_ascii_comments:
861 |                     stats.total_symbol_replaced += 1
862 |                     stats.comments_symbol_replaced += 1  # approximate
863 |                 else:
864 |                     stats.total_ascii_only += 1
865 |                 write_record(clean_record)
866 |             elif pending is not None:
867 |                 pending.line_idx = line_idx
868 |                 stats.total_need_translation += 1
869 |                 pending_batch.append(pending)
870 |             else:
871 |                 # Broken record
872 |                 stats.total_dropped += 1
873 | 
874 |             # Flush pending batch when large enough
875 |             if len(pending_batch) >= args.batch_size:
876 |                 elapsed = time.time() - start_time
877 |                 rate = stats.total_read / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0
878 |                 print(
879 |                     f"  [{stats.total_read:,} read] "
880 |                     f"ascii={stats.total_ascii_only:,} "
881 |                     f"sym={stats.total_symbol_replaced:,} "
882 |                     f"pending={len(pending_batch):,} "
883 |                     f"translated={stats.total_translated:,} "
884 |                     f"dropped={stats.total_dropped:,} "
885 |                     f"({rate:.0f} rec/s, {elapsed:.0f}s)",
886 |                     flush=True,
887 |                 )
888 |                 flush_pending_batch(pending_batch)
889 |                 pending_batch = []
890 | 
891 |     # Final flush
892 |     if pending_batch:
893 |         print(f"  Flushing final batch of {len(pending_batch):,} records...", flush=True)
894 |         flush_pending_batch(pending_batch)
895 | 
896 |     outf.close()
897 | 
898 |     elapsed = time.time() - start_time
899 | 
900 |     # ─── Report ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
901 | 
902 |     print(f"\n{'='*60}")
903 |     print(f"Translation complete in {elapsed:.1f}s")
904 |     print(f"{'='*60}")
905 |     print(f"  Total records read:        {stats.total_read:>10,}")
906 |     print(f"  Already ASCII (no-op):     {stats.total_ascii_only:>10,}")
907 |     print(f"  Symbol-replaced only:      {stats.total_symbol_replaced:>10,}")
908 |     print(f"  Sent to Gemini:            {stats.total_need_translation:>10,}")
909 |     print(f"  Successfully translated:   {stats.total_translated:>10,}")
910 |     print(f"  Dropped (failed/broken):   {stats.total_dropped:>10,}")
911 |     print("  ────────────────────────────────────")
912 |     print(f"  Total written to output:   {stats.total_written:>10,}")
913 |     print(f"  Comments symbol-replaced:  {stats.comments_symbol_replaced:>10,}")
914 |     print(f"  Comments Gemini-translated:{stats.comments_translated:>10,}")
915 |     if stats.total_read > 0:
916 |         pct_clean = (stats.total_ascii_only / stats.total_read) * 100
917 |         pct_sym = (stats.total_symbol_replaced / stats.total_read) * 100
918 |         pct_trans = (stats.total_need_translation / stats.total_read) * 100
919 |         pct_drop = (stats.total_dropped / stats.total_read) * 100
920 |         print(f"\n  Breakdown: {pct_clean:.1f}% clean, {pct_sym:.1f}% symbol, "
921 |               f"{pct_trans:.1f}% translated, {pct_drop:.1f}% dropped")
922 |         rate = stats.total_read / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0
923 |         print(f"  Throughput: {rate:.0f} records/sec")
924 |     print(f"\n  Output: {args.output}")
925 | 
926 | 
927 | if __name__ == "__main__":
928 |     main()
```

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Raw commit slicer — Phase 1b of staged training.
  3 | 
  4 | Reads raw commit JSONL files (pre→post diffs) and slices them into
  5 | 4K-token training documents. Each commit doc contains:
  6 |   - Commit metadata header (repo, hash, message)
  7 |   - File diffs (unified diff format)
  8 | 
  9 | Handles multiple input formats:
 10 |   1. Per-repo JSONL (blender_raw.jsonl, etc.) — each line is a commit
 11 |   2. Chain JSONL (commitpack_chains.jsonl, etc.) — each line is a commit chain
 12 |   3. GPU/NPU commit JSONL — same format as per-repo
 13 | 
 14 | Usage:
 15 |     python scripts/data/raw_commit_slicer.py \
 16 |         --input_dirs /mnt/nvme/nanochat_data/raw_commits \
 17 |                      /mnt/nvme/commit_chains \
 18 |                      /mnt/nvme/nanochat_data/gpu_npu_commits \
 19 |         --output /mnt/nvme/raw_commits_4k.jsonl \
 20 |         --max_tokens 4096
 21 | """
 22 | import argparse
 23 | import glob
 24 | import json
 25 | import os
 26 | import sys
 27 | 
 28 | CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 3.5
 29 | 
 30 | 
 31 | def estimate_tokens(text: str) -> int:
 32 |     return max(1, int(len(text) / CHARS_PER_TOKEN))
 33 | 
 34 | 
 35 | def split_text(text: str, max_tokens: int) -> list[str]:
 36 |     """Split text into chunks of at most max_tokens."""
 37 |     if estimate_tokens(text) <= max_tokens:
 38 |         return [text]
 39 | 
 40 |     lines = text.split("\n")
 41 |     chunks = []
 42 |     current_lines: list[str] = []
 43 |     current_tok = 0
 44 | 
 45 |     for line in lines:
 46 |         line_tok = estimate_tokens(line)
 47 |         if current_tok + line_tok > max_tokens and current_lines:
 48 |             chunks.append("\n".join(current_lines))
 49 |             current_lines = [line]
 50 |             current_tok = line_tok
 51 |         else:
 52 |             current_lines.append(line)
 53 |             current_tok += line_tok
 54 | 
 55 |     if current_lines:
 56 |         chunk = "\n".join(current_lines)
 57 |         if estimate_tokens(chunk) >= 30:
 58 |             chunks.append(chunk)
 59 | 
 60 |     return chunks
 61 | 
 62 | 
 63 | def process_commit_doc(doc: dict, max_tokens: int) -> list[dict]:
 64 |     """Process a single commit document into training chunks."""
 65 |     # Try different field names used across our formats
 66 |     text = doc.get("text", "")
 67 |     if not text:
 68 |         # Chain format: may have pre/post or diff fields
 69 |         pre = doc.get("pre", "")
 70 |         post = doc.get("post", "")
 71 |         diff = doc.get("diff", "")
 72 |         message = doc.get("message", doc.get("commit_message", ""))
 73 |         repo = doc.get("repo", doc.get("repository", ""))
 74 |         sha = doc.get("sha", doc.get("hash", doc.get("commit_hash", "")))
 75 | 
 76 |         if diff:
 77 |             text = diff
 78 |         elif pre and post:
 79 |             text = f"// pre:\n{pre}\n\n// post:\n{post}"
 80 |         else:
 81 |             # Try content field
 82 |             text = doc.get("content", "")
 83 | 
 84 |         if not text:
 85 |             return []
 86 | 
 87 |         # Add header if we have metadata
 88 |         header_parts = []
 89 |         if repo:
 90 |             header_parts.append(f"// repo: {repo}")
 91 |         if sha:
 92 |             header_parts.append(f"// commit: {sha[:12]}")
 93 |         if message:
 94 |             msg_short = message.split("\n")[0][:200]
 95 |             header_parts.append(f"// message: {msg_short}")
 96 |         if header_parts:
 97 |             text = "\n".join(header_parts) + "\n\n" + text
 98 | 
 99 |     if not text or estimate_tokens(text) < 30:
100 |         return []
101 | 
102 |     chunks = split_text(text, max_tokens)
103 |     return [{"text": c} for c in chunks if estimate_tokens(c) >= 30]
104 | 
105 | 
106 | def process_jsonl_file(filepath: str, max_tokens: int, out_f) -> tuple[int, int]:
107 |     """Process a JSONL file, write docs to output. Returns (docs, tokens)."""
108 |     total_docs = 0
109 |     total_tokens = 0
110 | 
111 |     try:
112 |         with open(filepath, "r", errors="replace") as f:
113 |             for line_no, line in enumerate(f):
114 |                 line = line.strip()
115 |                 if not line:
116 |                     continue
117 |                 try:
118 |                     doc = json.loads(line)
119 |                 except json.JSONDecodeError:
120 |                     continue
121 | 
122 |                 chunks = process_commit_doc(doc, max_tokens)
123 |                 for chunk in chunks:
124 |                     json.dump(chunk, out_f)
125 |                     out_f.write("\n")
126 |                     total_docs += 1
127 |                     total_tokens += estimate_tokens(chunk["text"])
128 | 
129 |     except Exception as e:
130 |         print(f"  ERROR processing {filepath}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
131 | 
132 |     return total_docs, total_tokens
133 | 
134 | 
135 | def main():
136 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Raw commit slicer for Phase 1b")
137 |     parser.add_argument(
138 |         "--input_dirs",
139 |         nargs="+",
140 |         required=True,
141 |         help="Directories containing JSONL commit files",
142 |     )
143 |     parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output JSONL file")
144 |     parser.add_argument("--max_tokens", type=int, default=4096)
145 |     args = parser.parse_args()
146 | 
147 |     # Collect all JSONL files
148 |     jsonl_files = []
149 |     for input_dir in args.input_dirs:
150 |         if not os.path.isdir(input_dir):
151 |             print(f"WARNING: {input_dir} not found, skipping")
152 |             continue
153 |         files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(input_dir, "*.jsonl")))
154 |         jsonl_files.extend(files)
155 |         print(f"  {input_dir}: {len(files)} JSONL files")
156 | 
157 |     print(f"Total: {len(jsonl_files)} JSONL files to process")
158 | 
159 |     grand_docs = 0
160 |     grand_tokens = 0
161 | 
162 |     with open(args.output, "w") as out_f:
163 |         for i, fpath in enumerate(jsonl_files):
164 |             fname = os.path.basename(fpath)
165 |             fsize = os.path.getsize(fpath) / 1e9
166 | 
167 |             docs, tokens = process_jsonl_file(fpath, args.max_tokens, out_f)
168 |             grand_docs += docs
169 |             grand_tokens += tokens
170 | 
171 |             print(
172 |                 f"  [{i+1}/{len(jsonl_files)}] {fname} ({fsize:.1f}GB): "
173 |                 f"{docs:,} docs, {tokens/1e6:.1f}M tok | "
174 |                 f"Total: {grand_docs:,} docs, {grand_tokens/1e9:.2f}B tok",
175 |                 flush=True,
176 |             )
177 | 
178 |     print("\n=== SUMMARY ===")
179 |     print(f"Files processed: {len(jsonl_files)}")
180 |     print(f"Documents: {grand_docs:,}")
181 |     print(f"Total tokens: {grand_tokens/1e9:.2f}B")
182 |     if grand_docs > 0:
183 |         print(f"Avg tokens/doc: {grand_tokens/grand_docs:.0f}")
184 |     print(f"Output: {args.output} ({os.path.getsize(args.output)/1e9:.1f} GB)")
185 | 
186 | 
187 | if __name__ == "__main__":
188 |     main()
```

### Code

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Extract C/C++ commit chains from a git repository.
  3 | 
  4 | For each commit that modifies C/C++ files, extracts:
  5 | - Pre-commit file content (parent version)
  6 | - Post-commit file content (current version)
  7 | - Unified diff
  8 | - Commit message
  9 | - Repository name
 10 | - File path
 11 | 
 12 | Outputs JSONL in "Applied" format compatible with transform_commits.py:
 13 |   {"text": "/** @brief <message>\n * Repository: <repo>\n * File: <path>\n */\n<post_code>\n\n// Applied fix:\n<diff>"}
 14 | 
 15 | Usage:
 16 |   python extract_git_commits.py --repo-path /path/to/bare/repo --repo-name linux --output /path/to/output.jsonl [--workers 4]
 17 | """
 18 | 
 19 | import argparse
 20 | import hashlib
 21 | import json
 22 | import subprocess
 23 | import unicodedata
 24 | from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed
 25 | from pathlib import Path
 26 | from typing import TypedDict
 27 | 
 28 | CPP_EXTENSIONS = frozenset({
 29 |     '.c', '.cc', '.cpp', '.cxx', '.c++',
 30 |     '.h', '.hh', '.hpp', '.hxx', '.h++',
 31 |     '.inl', '.ipp', '.tcc', '.tpp',
 32 | })
 33 | 
 34 | # Max file size to extract (skip huge generated files)
 35 | MAX_FILE_SIZE = 512 * 1024  # 512 KB
 36 | 
 37 | 
 38 | class _CommitMetadata(TypedDict):
 39 |     parent_hashes: list[str]
 40 |     parent_count: int
 41 |     is_merge_commit: bool
 42 |     author_timestamp: str | None
 43 |     commit_timestamp: str | None
 44 |     timestamp: str | None
 45 | 
 46 | 
 47 | def stable_repo_id(repo_name: str) -> str:
 48 |     return hashlib.sha1(repo_name.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
 49 | 
 50 | 
 51 | def stable_filepath_id(repo_name: str, filepath: str) -> str:
 52 |     key = f"{repo_name}\0{filepath}"
 53 |     return hashlib.sha1(key.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
 54 | 
 55 | 
 56 | def get_commit_metadata(
 57 |     repo_path: str, commit_hash: str
 58 | ) -> _CommitMetadata | None:
 59 |     """Return chronology metadata needed for deterministic local sequencing."""
 60 |     try:
 61 |         output = git_cmd(
 62 |             repo_path,
 63 |             "show",
 64 |             "-s",
 65 |             "--format=%P%n%aI%n%cI",
 66 |             commit_hash,
 67 |         )
 68 |     except Exception:
 69 |         return None
 70 |     lines = output.splitlines()
 71 |     if len(lines) < 3:
 72 |         return None
 73 |     parent_hashes = [item for item in lines[0].strip().split() if item]
 74 |     author_timestamp = lines[1].strip() or None
 75 |     commit_timestamp = lines[2].strip() or None
 76 |     return {
 77 |         "parent_hashes": parent_hashes,
 78 |         "parent_count": len(parent_hashes),
 79 |         "is_merge_commit": len(parent_hashes) > 1,
 80 |         "author_timestamp": author_timestamp,
 81 |         "commit_timestamp": commit_timestamp,
 82 |         "timestamp": commit_timestamp or author_timestamp,
 83 |     }
 84 | 
 85 | 
 86 | def compute_file_local_commit_indices(
 87 |     repo_path: str,
 88 |     commit_hashes: list[str],
 89 | ) -> dict[tuple[str, str], int]:
 90 |     """Assign a deterministic chronology index for each (commit, filepath) pair."""
 91 |     counters: dict[str, int] = {}
 92 |     indices: dict[tuple[str, str], int] = {}
 93 |     for commit_hash in reversed(commit_hashes):
 94 |         for filepath in get_changed_cpp_files(repo_path, commit_hash):
 95 |             next_index = counters.get(filepath, 0)
 96 |             indices[(commit_hash, filepath)] = next_index
 97 |             counters[filepath] = next_index + 1
 98 |     return indices
 99 | 
100 | 
101 | def to_ascii(text: str) -> str:
102 |     """Convert Unicode text to ASCII-safe version."""
103 |     # Common replacements
104 |     replacements = {
105 |         '\u2018': "'", '\u2019': "'", '\u201c': '"', '\u201d': '"',
106 |         '\u2013': '-', '\u2014': '--', '\u2026': '...',
107 |         '\u00a0': ' ', '\u200b': '', '\u200c': '', '\u200d': '',
108 |         '\ufeff': '', '\u00ab': '"', '\u00bb': '"',
109 |         '\u2022': '*', '\u2023': '*', '\u25cf': '*',
110 |         '\u2192': '->', '\u2190': '<-', '\u21d2': '=>',
111 |         '\u2264': '<=', '\u2265': '>=', '\u2260': '!=',
112 |         '\u00b1': '+-', '\u00d7': 'x', '\u00f7': '/',
113 |         '\u00a9': '(c)', '\u00ae': '(R)', '\u2122': '(TM)',
114 |         '\u00b0': ' degrees',
115 |     }
116 |     for old, new in replacements.items():
117 |         text = text.replace(old, new)
118 | 
119 |     # NFD decompose then strip non-ASCII
120 |     text = unicodedata.normalize('NFD', text)
121 |     result = []
122 |     for ch in text:
123 |         if ord(ch) < 128:
124 |             result.append(ch)
125 |         elif unicodedata.category(ch) not in ('Mn', 'Mc', 'Me'):
126 |             # Non-combining mark, non-ASCII — replace with ?
127 |             result.append('?')
128 |     return ''.join(result)
129 | 
130 | 
131 | def is_cpp_file(path: str) -> bool:
132 |     """Check if a file path has a C/C++ extension."""
133 |     return Path(path).suffix.lower() in CPP_EXTENSIONS
134 | 
135 | 
136 | def git_cmd(repo_path: str, *args, timeout: int = 60) -> str:
137 |     """Run a git command and return stdout."""
138 |     cmd = ['git', '-C', repo_path] + list(args)
139 |     result = subprocess.run(
140 |         cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout,
141 |         errors='replace'
142 |     )
143 |     if result.returncode != 0:
144 |         raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(result.returncode, cmd, result.stdout, result.stderr)
145 |     return result.stdout
146 | 
147 | 
148 | def get_all_commits(repo_path: str) -> list[str]:
149 |     """Get all commit hashes in reverse chronological order."""
150 |     output = git_cmd(repo_path, 'rev-list', '--all', timeout=300)
151 |     return output.strip().split('\n') if output.strip() else []
152 | 
153 | 
154 | def get_commit_message(repo_path: str, commit_hash: str) -> str:
155 |     """Get the commit message for a commit."""
156 |     try:
157 |         return git_cmd(repo_path, 'log', '-1', '--format=%s', commit_hash).strip()
158 |     except Exception:
159 |         return ''
160 | 
161 | 
162 | def get_changed_cpp_files(repo_path: str, commit_hash: str) -> list[str]:
163 |     """Get list of C/C++ files modified in a commit."""
164 |     try:
165 |         # --diff-filter=AM: Added or Modified (skip Deleted, Renamed source, etc.)
166 |         output = git_cmd(
167 |             repo_path, 'diff-tree', '--no-commit-id', '-r',
168 |             '--diff-filter=AM', '--name-only', commit_hash,
169 |             timeout=30
170 |         )
171 |         files = output.strip().split('\n') if output.strip() else []
172 |         return [f for f in files if is_cpp_file(f)]
173 |     except Exception:
174 |         return []
175 | 
176 | 
177 | def get_file_content(repo_path: str, tree_ish: str, filepath: str) -> str | None:
178 |     """Get file content at a specific commit."""
179 |     try:
180 |         content = git_cmd(repo_path, 'show', f'{tree_ish}:{filepath}', timeout=30)
181 |         if len(content) > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
182 |             return None
183 |         return content
184 |     except Exception:
185 |         return None
186 | 
187 | 
188 | def get_unified_diff(repo_path: str, commit_hash: str, filepath: str) -> str | None:
189 |     """Get unified diff for a file in a commit."""
190 |     try:
191 |         diff = git_cmd(
192 |             repo_path, 'diff', f'{commit_hash}~1', commit_hash,
193 |             '--', filepath, timeout=30
194 |         )
195 |         return diff if diff.strip() else None
196 |     except Exception:
197 |         return None
198 | 
199 | 
200 | def format_applied(message: str, repo_name: str, filepath: str,
201 |                    post_code: str, diff: str, commit_hash: str = "") -> str:
202 |     """Format as 'Applied' format for transform_commits.py compatibility.
203 | 
204 |     Output format:
205 |     /** @brief <commit message>
206 |      * Repository: <repo_name>
207 |      * File: <filepath>
208 |      * @commit <commit_hash>
209 |      */
210 |     <post_code>
211 | 
212 |     // Applied fix:
213 |     <diff>
214 |     """
215 |     # Clean the message
216 |     message = message.replace('*/', '* /')  # Prevent closing comment
217 |     message = message[:500]  # Truncate very long messages
218 | 
219 |     header = f'/** @brief {message}\n * Repository: {repo_name}\n * File: {filepath}\n * @commit {commit_hash}\n */'
220 |     return f'{header}\n{post_code}\n\n// Applied fix:\n{diff}'
221 | 
222 | 
223 | def process_commit_batch(args: tuple) -> list[dict]:
224 |     """Process a batch of commits for a single repo.
225 | 
226 |     Args:
227 |         args: (repo_path, repo_name, commit_hashes)
228 | 
229 |     Returns:
230 |         List of extracted records with chronology metadata.
231 |     """
232 |     repo_path, repo_name, commit_hashes, file_local_indices = args
233 |     results = []
234 | 
235 |     for commit_hash in commit_hashes:
236 |         try:
237 |             cpp_files = get_changed_cpp_files(repo_path, commit_hash)
238 |             if not cpp_files:
239 |                 continue
240 | 
241 |             message = get_commit_message(repo_path, commit_hash)
242 |             if not message:
243 |                 continue
244 |             commit_meta = get_commit_metadata(repo_path, commit_hash)
245 |             if commit_meta is None:
246 |                 continue
247 | 
248 |             for filepath in cpp_files:
249 |                 try:
250 |                     # Get post-commit content
251 |                     post_code = get_file_content(repo_path, commit_hash, filepath)
252 |                     if not post_code:
253 |                         continue
254 | 
255 |                     # Get diff
256 |                     diff = get_unified_diff(repo_path, commit_hash, filepath)
257 |                     if not diff:
258 |                         continue
259 | 
260 |                     # Format as Applied
261 |                     text = format_applied(message, repo_name, filepath, post_code, diff, commit_hash=commit_hash)
262 | 
263 |                     # ASCII-ify
264 |                     text = to_ascii(text)
265 | 
266 |                     results.append(
267 |                         {
268 |                             "text": text,
269 |                             "repo": repo_name,
270 |                             "filepath": filepath,
271 |                             "commit_hash": commit_hash,
272 |                             "timestamp": commit_meta["timestamp"],
273 |                             "parent_hashes": list(commit_meta["parent_hashes"]),
274 |                             "parent_count": int(commit_meta["parent_count"]),
275 |                             "is_merge_commit": bool(commit_meta["is_merge_commit"]),
276 |                             "author_timestamp": commit_meta["author_timestamp"],
277 |                             "commit_timestamp": commit_meta["commit_timestamp"],
278 |                             "repo_stable_id": stable_repo_id(repo_name),
279 |                             "filepath_stable_id": stable_filepath_id(repo_name, filepath),
280 |                             "file_local_commit_index": int(
281 |                                 file_local_indices[(commit_hash, filepath)]
282 |                             ),
283 |                             "has_ambiguous_reconstruction": False,
284 |                             "has_rename_ambiguity": False,
285 |                         }
286 |                     )
287 | 
288 |                 except Exception:
289 |                     continue
290 | 
291 |         except Exception:
292 |             continue
293 | 
294 |     return results
295 | 
296 | 
297 | def extract_repo(
298 |     repo_path: str,
299 |     repo_name: str,
300 |     output_path: str,
301 |     workers: int = 4,
302 |     batch_size: int = 200,
303 | ) -> dict[str, int | str]:
304 |     """Extract all C/C++ commit chains from a repository.
305 | 
306 |     Returns stats dict.
307 |     """
308 |     print(f'[{repo_name}] Getting commit list...', flush=True)
309 |     commits = get_all_commits(repo_path)
310 |     total_commits = len(commits)
311 |     print(f'[{repo_name}] Found {total_commits} commits', flush=True)
312 | 
313 |     if not commits:
314 |         return {'repo': repo_name, 'commits': 0, 'docs': 0}
315 | 
316 |     file_local_indices = compute_file_local_commit_indices(repo_path, commits)
317 | 
318 |     # Split into batches
319 |     batches = []
320 |     for i in range(0, len(commits), batch_size):
321 |         batch = commits[i:i + batch_size]
322 |         batches.append((repo_path, repo_name, batch, file_local_indices))
323 | 
324 |     print(f'[{repo_name}] Processing {len(batches)} batches with {workers} workers...', flush=True)
325 | 
326 |     total_docs = 0
327 |     with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
328 |         with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as executor:
329 |             futures = {executor.submit(process_commit_batch, b): i
330 |                        for i, b in enumerate(batches)}
331 | 
332 |             done = 0
333 |             for future in as_completed(futures):
334 |                 done += 1
335 |                 try:
336 |                     results = future.result()
337 |                     for doc in results:
338 |                         f.write(json.dumps(doc, ensure_ascii=True) + '\n')
339 |                     total_docs += len(results)
340 | 
341 |                     if done % 50 == 0 or done == len(batches):
342 |                         pct = done / len(batches) * 100
343 |                         print(f'[{repo_name}] {done}/{len(batches)} batches ({pct:.1f}%), '
344 |                               f'{total_docs} docs so far', flush=True)
345 |                 except Exception as e:
346 |                     print(f'[{repo_name}] Batch error: {e}', flush=True)
347 | 
348 |     print(f'[{repo_name}] Done: {total_docs} docs from {total_commits} commits', flush=True)
349 |     return {'repo': repo_name, 'commits': total_commits, 'docs': total_docs}
350 | 
351 | 
352 | def main():
353 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Extract C/C++ commits from a git repo')
354 |     parser.add_argument('--repo-path', required=True, help='Path to bare git repo')
355 |     parser.add_argument('--repo-name', required=True, help='Repository name (e.g. linux)')
356 |     parser.add_argument('--output', required=True, help='Output JSONL path')
357 |     parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=4, help='Parallel workers (default: 4)')
358 |     parser.add_argument('--batch-size', type=int, default=200, help='Commits per batch (default: 200)')
359 |     args = parser.parse_args()
360 | 
361 |     stats = extract_repo(
362 |         repo_path=args.repo_path,
363 |         repo_name=args.repo_name,
364 |         output_path=args.output,
365 |         workers=args.workers,
366 |         batch_size=args.batch_size,
367 |     )
368 | 
369 |     print(f'\nFinal stats: {json.dumps(stats)}')
370 | 
371 | 
372 | if __name__ == '__main__':
373 |     main()
```

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """
  3 | Pipeline: JSONL(.gz) -> Parquet -> Pretokenize for clean commit chain datasets.
  4 | 
  5 | Reads per-repo gzipped JSONL files (e.g., linux_v2_doxygen.jsonl.gz),
  6 | aggregates by variant, deduplicates by content hash, converts to sharded
  7 | parquet, then pretokenizes with tokenizer v3.
  8 | 
  9 | Supports two input formats:
 10 |   - Per-repo: {repo}_{variant}.jsonl.gz (from Rust transform)
 11 |   - Single-file: commit_chains_{variant}.jsonl (old format)
 12 | 
 13 | Usage:
 14 |     python pipeline_commit_chains.py \
 15 |         --input-dir /path/to/commit_chains_new \
 16 |         --output-dir /mnt/nvme/commit_chains_v2_new \
 17 |         --tokenizer /mnt/nvme/tokenizer_v3_output/tokenizer_v3.json \
 18 |         --workers 12 \
 19 |         --parquet-shard-size 100000 \
 20 |         --dedup-against /mnt/nvme/commit_chains_clean
 21 | """
 22 | 
 23 | import argparse
 24 | import gzip
 25 | import hashlib
 26 | import json
 27 | import os
 28 | import sys
 29 | import subprocess
 30 | import time
 31 | import glob
 32 | from pathlib import Path
 33 | from multiprocessing import Pool
 34 | 
 35 | import pyarrow as pa
 36 | import pyarrow.parquet as pq
 37 | 
 38 | # Force unbuffered output for real-time logging
 39 | import functools
 40 | print = functools.partial(print, flush=True)
 41 | 
 42 | 
 43 | VARIANTS = ["v2_doxygen", "v2_simple", "v3_doxygen", "v3_simple"]
 44 | VariantTask = tuple[str, str, str, str, int, int, set[str] | None]
 45 | VariantResult = tuple[str, bool, int]
 46 | 
 47 | 
 48 | def load_dedup_hashes(dedup_dirs: list[str]) -> set[str]:
 49 |     """Load content hashes from existing datasets for deduplication."""
 50 |     hashes: set[str] = set()
 51 |     for d in dedup_dirs:
 52 |         for pattern in ["*.jsonl", "*.jsonl.gz"]:
 53 |             for fpath in glob.glob(os.path.join(d, pattern)):
 54 |                 opener = gzip.open if fpath.endswith('.gz') else open
 55 |                 with opener(fpath, 'rt', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace') as f:
 56 |                     for line in f:
 57 |                         try:
 58 |                             doc = json.loads(line)
 59 |                             h = hashlib.sha256(doc["text"].encode('utf-8', errors='replace')).hexdigest()[:16]
 60 |                             hashes.add(h)
 61 |                         except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError):
 62 |                             continue
 63 |                 print(f"  Loaded hashes from {fpath}, total: {len(hashes):,}")
 64 |     return hashes
 65 | 
 66 | 
 67 | def find_variant_files(input_dir: str, variant: str) -> list[str]:
 68 |     """Find all input files for a given variant.
 69 |     
 70 |     Matches patterns:
 71 |       - {repo}_{variant}.jsonl.gz (per-repo from Rust transform)
 72 |       - commit_chains_{variant}.jsonl (single-file old format)
 73 |     """
 74 |     files = []
 75 |     
 76 |     # Per-repo gzipped files: *_{variant}.jsonl.gz
 77 |     gz_pattern = os.path.join(input_dir, f"*_{variant}.jsonl.gz")
 78 |     files.extend(sorted(glob.glob(gz_pattern)))
 79 |     
 80 |     # Single-file old format
 81 |     single = os.path.join(input_dir, f"commit_chains_{variant}.jsonl")
 82 |     if os.path.exists(single):
 83 |         files.append(single)
 84 |     
 85 |     return files
 86 | 
 87 | 
 88 | def jsonl_files_to_parquet(
 89 |     input_files: list[str],
 90 |     parquet_dir: str,
 91 |     shard_size: int,
 92 |     dedup_hashes: set[str] | None = None,
 93 | ) -> tuple[int, int]:
 94 |     """Convert multiple JSONL/JSONL.gz files to sharded parquet.
 95 |     Returns (total_docs, dedup_skipped)."""
 96 |     os.makedirs(parquet_dir, exist_ok=True)
 97 |     
 98 |     texts: list[str] = []
 99 |     shard_idx = 0
100 |     total = 0
101 |     skipped = 0
102 |     
103 |     for fpath in input_files:
104 |         opener = gzip.open if fpath.endswith('.gz') else open
105 |         with opener(fpath, 'rt', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace') as f:
106 |             for line in f:
107 |                 try:
108 |                     doc = json.loads(line)
109 |                     text = doc["text"]
110 |                 except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError):
111 |                     continue
112 |                 
113 |                 # Dedup check
114 |                 if dedup_hashes is not None:
115 |                     h = hashlib.sha256(text.encode('utf-8', errors='replace')).hexdigest()[:16]
116 |                     if h in dedup_hashes:
117 |                         skipped += 1
118 |                         continue
119 |                     dedup_hashes.add(h)
120 |                 
121 |                 texts.append(text)
122 |                 total += 1
123 |                 
124 |                 if len(texts) >= shard_size:
125 |                     table = pa.table({"text": texts})
126 |                     out_path = os.path.join(parquet_dir, f"shard_{shard_idx:04d}.parquet")
127 |                     pq.write_table(table, out_path, compression="snappy")
128 |                     shard_idx += 1
129 |                     texts = []
130 |                     
131 |                     if total % 500_000 == 0:
132 |                         print(f"    Progress: {total:,} docs, {skipped:,} dedup skipped, {shard_idx} shards")
133 |     
134 |     # Write remaining
135 |     if texts:
136 |         table = pa.table({"text": texts})
137 |         out_path = os.path.join(parquet_dir, f"shard_{shard_idx:04d}.parquet")
138 |         pq.write_table(table, out_path, compression="snappy")
139 |         shard_idx += 1
140 |     
141 |     print(f"  Wrote {shard_idx} parquet shards ({total:,} docs, {skipped:,} dedup skipped) to {parquet_dir}")
142 |     return total, skipped
143 | 
144 | 
145 | def pretokenize(parquet_dir: str, pretok_dir: str, tokenizer_path: str, workers: int):
146 |     """Run pretokenize_v3.py on a parquet directory."""
147 |     script = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "pretokenize_v3.py")
148 |     cmd = [
149 |         sys.executable, script,
150 |         "--input", parquet_dir,
151 |         "--output", pretok_dir,
152 |         "--tokenizer", tokenizer_path,
153 |         "--num_workers", str(workers),
154 |     ]
155 |     print(f"  Running: {' '.join(cmd)}")
156 |     result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
157 |     if result.returncode != 0:
158 |         print(f"  FAILED: {result.stderr[-500:]}")
159 |         return False
160 |     # Print last few lines of stdout
161 |     lines = result.stdout.strip().split("\n")
162 |     for line in lines[-5:]:
163 |         print(f"    {line}")
164 |     return True
165 | 
166 | 
167 | def process_variant(args: VariantTask) -> VariantResult:
168 |     """Process a single variant: JSONL(.gz) files -> Parquet -> Pretokenize."""
169 |     variant, input_dir, output_dir, tokenizer_path, workers, shard_size, dedup_hashes = args
170 |     
171 |     parquet_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{variant}_parquet")
172 |     pretok_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{variant}_pretok")
173 |     
174 |     print(f"\n[{variant}] Starting pipeline...")
175 |     
176 |     # Find all input files for this variant
177 |     input_files = find_variant_files(input_dir, variant)
178 |     if not input_files:
179 |         print(f"[{variant}] ERROR: No input files found for variant '{variant}' in {input_dir}")
180 |         return variant, False, 0
181 |     
182 |     print(f"[{variant}] Found {len(input_files)} input files")
183 |     
184 |     # Step 1: JSONL -> Parquet (with dedup)
185 |     t0 = time.time()
186 |     print(f"[{variant}] Step 1: JSONL -> Parquet (dedup={'ON' if dedup_hashes is not None else 'OFF'})")
187 |     doc_count, dedup_skipped = jsonl_files_to_parquet(
188 |         input_files, parquet_dir, shard_size, dedup_hashes
189 |     )
190 |     t1 = time.time()
191 |     print(f"[{variant}] Parquet done in {t1 - t0:.1f}s ({doc_count:,} docs, {dedup_skipped:,} deduped)")
192 |     
193 |     # Step 2: Pretokenize
194 |     print(f"[{variant}] Step 2: Pretokenize")
195 |     ok = pretokenize(parquet_dir, pretok_dir, tokenizer_path, workers)
196 |     t2 = time.time()
197 |     print(f"[{variant}] Pretokenize done in {t2 - t1:.1f}s")
198 |     
199 |     # Count output bins
200 |     bin_count = len(list(Path(pretok_dir).glob("*.bin"))) if os.path.exists(pretok_dir) else 0
201 |     
202 |     print(f"[{variant}] COMPLETE: {doc_count:,} docs -> {bin_count} bin shards ({t2 - t0:.1f}s total)")
203 |     return variant, ok, doc_count
204 | 
205 | 
206 | def main():
207 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Pipeline: JSONL(.gz) -> Parquet -> Pretokenize")
208 |     parser.add_argument("--input-dir", required=True,
209 |                         help="Directory with *_{variant}.jsonl.gz or commit_chains_{variant}.jsonl files")
210 |     parser.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True, help="Output directory for parquet and pretok")
211 |     parser.add_argument("--tokenizer", required=True, help="Path to tokenizer_v3.json")
212 |     parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=6, help="Workers per pretokenize job")
213 |     parser.add_argument("--parquet-shard-size", type=int, default=100000, help="Docs per parquet shard")
214 |     parser.add_argument("--variants", nargs="+", default=VARIANTS, help="Which variants to process")
215 |     parser.add_argument("--sequential", action="store_true", help="Run variants sequentially (less memory)")
216 |     parser.add_argument("--dedup-against", nargs="*", default=None,
217 |                         help="Directories with existing JSONL/JSONL.gz to deduplicate against")
218 |     args = parser.parse_args()
219 |     
220 |     os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
221 |     
222 |     # Load dedup hashes if requested
223 |     dedup_hashes = None
224 |     if args.dedup_against:
225 |         print("Loading deduplication hashes from existing datasets...")
226 |         t0 = time.time()
227 |         dedup_hashes = load_dedup_hashes(args.dedup_against)
228 |         print(f"Loaded {len(dedup_hashes):,} dedup hashes in {time.time() - t0:.1f}s")
229 |     
230 |     tasks = [
231 |         (v, args.input_dir, args.output_dir, args.tokenizer, args.workers,
232 |          args.parquet_shard_size, dedup_hashes)
233 |         for v in args.variants
234 |     ]
235 |     
236 |     t_start = time.time()
237 |     results: list[VariantResult] = []
238 |     
239 |     if args.sequential:
240 |         for task in tasks:
241 |             results.append(process_variant(task))
242 |     else:
243 |         if dedup_hashes is not None:
244 |             # Give each variant its own copy of the dedup set.
245 |             # Cross-variant dedup is unnecessary (variants produce different text).
246 |             # Within-variant dedup against old data uses read-only checks.
247 |             print(f"Cloning dedup hashes for {len(tasks)} parallel variants...")
248 |             for i in range(len(tasks)):
249 |                 v, inp, out, tok, w, ss, _ = tasks[i]
250 |                 tasks[i] = (v, inp, out, tok, w, ss, set(dedup_hashes))
251 |         # Run all 4 variants in parallel processes
252 |         with Pool(len(tasks)) as pool:
253 |             results = pool.map(process_variant, tasks)
254 |     
255 |     t_total = time.time() - t_start
256 |     
257 |     print("\n" + "=" * 60)
258 |     print("PIPELINE SUMMARY")
259 |     print("=" * 60)
260 |     for variant, ok, doc_count in results:
261 |         status = "OK" if ok else "FAILED"
262 |         print(f"  {variant}: {doc_count:>10,} docs  [{status}]")
263 |     print(f"\nTotal time: {t_total:.1f}s")
264 | 
265 | 
266 | if __name__ == "__main__":
267 |     main()
```

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """
  3 | Transform commit chain documents into clean C++ training data.
  4 | 
  5 | Produces 4 output datasets (2 variants x 2 comment styles):
  6 |   Variant 2 (full reconstruction):
  7 |     - v2_doxygen: Doxygen-style docstring comments with before/after code
  8 |     - v2_simple:  Simple // comments with before/after code
  9 |   Variant 3 (structured diff context):
 10 |     - v3_doxygen: Doxygen-style with changed sections + full post-commit code
 11 |     - v3_simple:  Simple // comments with changed sections + full post-commit code
 12 | 
 13 | Input formats handled:
 14 |   - "Applied" format (79.7%): full post-commit code + unified diff appended
 15 |     -> reverse-apply diff to reconstruct pre-commit code
 16 |   - "Pre/Post" format (20.3%): both full versions present
 17 |     -> extract directly
 18 | 
 19 | All output is pure C++ (no diff markers), ASCII-only.
 20 | Non-ASCII characters are stripped or transliterated.
 21 | 
 22 | Usage:
 23 |     python transform_commits.py --input /path/to/commit_chains_64k.jsonl \
 24 |         --output-dir /path/to/output/ --workers 16
 25 | """
 26 | 
 27 | import argparse
 28 | import json
 29 | import os
 30 | import re
 31 | import unicodedata
 32 | from multiprocessing import Pool
 33 | from typing import Literal, Optional, TypedDict
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | class ParsedHunk(TypedDict):
 37 |     old_start: int
 38 |     old_count: int
 39 |     new_start: int
 40 |     new_count: int
 41 |     lines: list[tuple[Literal["+", "-", " "], str]]
 42 | 
 43 | 
 44 | class ParsedDocument(TypedDict):
 45 |     format: Literal["applied", "pre_post", "unknown"] | None
 46 |     brief: str
 47 |     repository: str
 48 |     filepath: str
 49 |     description: str
 50 |     pre_code: str | None
 51 |     post_code: str | None
 52 |     diff_text: str | None
 53 |     context_hints: list[str]
 54 |     fix_description: str
 55 | 
 56 | 
 57 | class BatchStats(TypedDict):
 58 |     total: int
 59 |     applied: int
 60 |     pre_post: int
 61 |     unknown: int
 62 |     pre_reconstructed: int
 63 |     failed: int
 64 |     non_ascii_cleaned: int
```
