```python
 73 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 74 | # ASCII / English cleanup
 75 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 76 | 
 77 | def to_ascii(text: str) -> str:
 78 |     """Replace non-ASCII characters with ASCII equivalents or strip them."""
 79 |     # Common Unicode -> ASCII mappings
 80 |     replacements = {
 81 |         '\u2018': "'", '\u2019': "'",  # smart quotes
 82 |         '\u201c': '"', '\u201d': '"',
 83 |         '\u2013': '-', '\u2014': '--',  # en/em dash
 84 |         '\u2026': '...',               # ellipsis
 85 |         '\u00e9': 'e', '\u00e8': 'e', '\u00ea': 'e',  # accented e
 86 |         '\u00e0': 'a', '\u00e1': 'a', '\u00e2': 'a', '\u00e3': 'a',
 87 |         '\u00f3': 'o', '\u00f4': 'o', '\u00f5': 'o',
 88 |         '\u00ed': 'i', '\u00ec': 'i',
 89 |         '\u00fa': 'u', '\u00fc': 'u',
 90 |         '\u00e7': 'c',  # cedilla
 91 |         '\u00f1': 'n',  # tilde n
 92 |         '\u00df': 'ss',  # eszett
 93 |         '\u00c9': 'E', '\u00c0': 'A',
 94 |         '\u0410': 'A', '\u0411': 'B', '\u0412': 'V', '\u0413': 'G',
 95 |         '\u0414': 'D', '\u0415': 'E', '\u0416': 'Zh', '\u0417': 'Z',
 96 |         '\u0418': 'I', '\u0419': 'Y', '\u041a': 'K', '\u041b': 'L',
 97 |         '\u041c': 'M', '\u041d': 'N', '\u041e': 'O', '\u041f': 'P',
 98 |         '\u0420': 'R', '\u0421': 'S', '\u0422': 'T', '\u0423': 'U',
 99 |         '\u0424': 'F', '\u0425': 'Kh', '\u0426': 'Ts', '\u0427': 'Ch',
100 |         '\u0428': 'Sh', '\u0429': 'Shch', '\u042a': '', '\u042b': 'Y',
101 |         '\u042c': '', '\u042d': 'E', '\u042e': 'Yu', '\u042f': 'Ya',
102 |         '\u0430': 'a', '\u0431': 'b', '\u0432': 'v', '\u0433': 'g',
103 |         '\u0434': 'd', '\u0435': 'e', '\u0436': 'zh', '\u0437': 'z',
104 |         '\u0438': 'i', '\u0439': 'y', '\u043a': 'k', '\u043b': 'l',
105 |         '\u043c': 'm', '\u043d': 'n', '\u043e': 'o', '\u043f': 'p',
106 |         '\u0440': 'r', '\u0441': 's', '\u0442': 't', '\u0443': 'u',
107 |         '\u0444': 'f', '\u0445': 'kh', '\u0446': 'ts', '\u0447': 'ch',
108 |         '\u0448': 'sh', '\u0449': 'shch', '\u044a': '', '\u044b': 'y',
109 |         '\u044c': '', '\u044d': 'e', '\u044e': 'yu', '\u044f': 'ya',
110 |     }
111 |     result = []
112 |     for ch in text:
113 |         if ord(ch) <= 127:
114 |             result.append(ch)
115 |         elif ch in replacements:
116 |             result.append(replacements[ch])
117 |         else:
118 |             # Try NFKD decomposition
119 |             decomposed = unicodedata.normalize('NFKD', ch)
120 |             ascii_part = decomposed.encode('ascii', 'ignore').decode('ascii')
121 |             if ascii_part:
122 |                 result.append(ascii_part)
123 |             # else: drop the character
124 |     return ''.join(result)
125 | 
126 | 
127 | _DIFF_LINE_RE = re.compile(r'^(@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+\d+(?:,\d+)? @@|--- a/|--- /dev/null|\+\+\+ b/|\+\+\+ /dev/null|diff --git a/)')
128 | 
129 | def strip_diff_artifacts(code: str) -> str:
130 |     """Remove any unified-diff artifacts that leaked into reconstructed code.
131 |     
132 |     Strips lines that match diff hunk headers, file markers, or +/- prefixed
133 |     diff lines that appear in blocks (consecutive +/- lines = diff fragment).
134 |     """
135 |     if not code:
136 |         return code
137 |     
138 |     lines = code.split('\n')
139 |     clean = []
140 |     i = 0
141 |     while i < len(lines):
142 |         line = lines[i]
143 |         # Remove diff header/hunk lines
144 |         if _DIFF_LINE_RE.match(line):
145 |             # Skip this line and any following diff content lines
146 |             i += 1
147 |             while i < len(lines):
148 |                 next_line = lines[i]
149 |                 if (next_line.startswith('+') or next_line.startswith('-') or
150 |                     next_line.startswith(' ') or next_line.startswith('\\') or
151 |                     _DIFF_LINE_RE.match(next_line)):
152 |                     i += 1
153 |                 else:
154 |                     break
155 |             continue
156 |         clean.append(line)
157 |         i += 1
158 |     
159 |     return '\n'.join(clean)
160 | 
161 | 
162 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
163 | # Diff parsing and reverse-application
164 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
165 | 
166 | def parse_unified_diff(diff_text: str) -> list[ParsedHunk]:
167 |     """Parse unified diff into hunks with line numbers and changes."""
168 |     hunks: list[ParsedHunk] = []
169 |     current_hunk: ParsedHunk | None = None
170 |     
171 |     for line in diff_text.split('\n'):
172 |         # Hunk header: @@ -old_start,old_count +new_start,new_count @@
173 |         m = re.match(r'^@@ -(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? \+(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? @@', line)
174 |         if m:
175 |             if current_hunk:
176 |                 hunks.append(current_hunk)
177 |             current_hunk = {
178 |                 'old_start': int(m.group(1)),
179 |                 'old_count': int(m.group(2) or 1),
180 |                 'new_start': int(m.group(3)),
181 |                 'new_count': int(m.group(4) or 1),
182 |                 'lines': []
183 |             }
184 |             continue
185 |         
186 |         if current_hunk is not None:
187 |             if line.startswith('+'):
188 |                 current_hunk['lines'].append(('+', line[1:]))
189 |             elif line.startswith('-'):
190 |                 current_hunk['lines'].append(('-', line[1:]))
191 |             elif line.startswith(' '):
192 |                 current_hunk['lines'].append((' ', line[1:]))
193 |             elif line.startswith('\\'):
194 |                 pass  # "\ No newline at end of file"
195 |     
196 |     if current_hunk:
197 |         hunks.append(current_hunk)
198 |     
199 |     return hunks
200 | 
201 | 
202 | def reverse_apply_diff(post_code: str, diff_text: str) -> Optional[str]:
203 |     """Reverse-apply a unified diff to post-commit code to get pre-commit code.
204 |     
205 |     The diff describes old->new. We have new (post_code) and want old (pre_code).
206 |     Strategy: walk through the new file, using the diff hunks to undo changes.
207 |     """
208 |     hunks = parse_unified_diff(diff_text)
209 |     if not hunks:
210 |         return None
211 |     
212 |     post_lines = post_code.split('\n')
213 |     pre_lines = []
214 |     
215 |     # Sort hunks by new_start
216 |     hunks.sort(key=lambda h: h['new_start'])
217 |     
218 |     post_idx = 0  # 0-based index into post_lines
219 |     
220 |     for hunk in hunks:
221 |         new_start_0 = hunk['new_start'] - 1  # convert to 0-based
222 |         
223 |         # Copy unchanged lines before this hunk
224 |         while post_idx < new_start_0 and post_idx < len(post_lines):
225 |             pre_lines.append(post_lines[post_idx])
226 |             post_idx += 1
227 |         
228 |         # Process hunk lines: reverse the diff
229 |         # In forward diff: '+' = added line (in new), '-' = removed line (was in old)
230 |         # To reverse: skip '+' lines (they're new, not in old), keep '-' lines (they were in old)
231 |         for op, content in hunk['lines']:
232 |             if op == '+':
233 |                 # This line was added in new version - skip it (not in old)
234 |                 post_idx += 1
235 |             elif op == '-':
236 |                 # This line was removed from old version - add it back
237 |                 pre_lines.append(content)
238 |             elif op == ' ':
239 |                 # Context line - keep it
240 |                 pre_lines.append(content)
241 |                 post_idx += 1
242 |     
243 |     # Copy remaining lines after last hunk
244 |     while post_idx < len(post_lines):
245 |         pre_lines.append(post_lines[post_idx])
246 |         post_idx += 1
247 |     
248 |     return '\n'.join(pre_lines)
249 | 
250 | 
251 | def extract_diff_changes(diff_text: str) -> list[dict]:
252 |     """Extract the changed sections from a diff for variant 3 output."""
253 |     hunks = parse_unified_diff(diff_text)
254 |     changes = []
255 |     
256 |     for hunk in hunks:
257 |         removed = [content for op, content in hunk['lines'] if op == '-']
258 |         added = [content for op, content in hunk['lines'] if op == '+']
259 |         context = [content for op, content in hunk['lines'] if op == ' ']
260 |         
261 |         if removed or added:
262 |             changes.append({
263 |                 'line': hunk['new_start'],
264 |                 'removed': removed,
265 |                 'added': added,
266 |                 'context_before': context[:2] if context else [],
267 |                 'context_after': context[-2:] if len(context) > 2 else [],
268 |             })
269 |     
270 |     return changes
271 | 
272 | 
273 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
274 | # Document parsing
275 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
276 | 
277 | def parse_document(text: str) -> ParsedDocument:
278 |     """Parse a commit chain document into structured components."""
279 |     result: ParsedDocument = {
280 |         'format': None,          # 'applied' or 'pre_post'
281 |         'brief': '',
282 |         'repository': '',
283 |         'filepath': '',
284 |         'description': '',
285 |         'pre_code': None,        # full code before change (if available)
286 |         'post_code': None,       # full code after change
287 |         'diff_text': None,       # raw unified diff (if present)
288 |         'context_hints': [],     # // Context: ... lines
289 |         'fix_description': '',   # from @brief or Applied fix description
290 |     }
291 |     
292 |     lines = text.split('\n')
293 |     
294 |     # Extract doxygen header
295 |     for line in lines:
296 |         line_stripped = line.strip()
297 |         if line_stripped.startswith('* @brief'):
298 |             result['brief'] = line_stripped.replace('* @brief', '').strip()
299 |             result['fix_description'] = result['brief']
300 |         elif line_stripped.startswith('* @repository') or line_stripped.startswith('* Repository:'):
301 |             result['repository'] = line_stripped.split(':', 1)[-1].strip() if ':' in line_stripped else ''
302 |         elif line_stripped.startswith('* @file') or line_stripped.startswith('* File:'):
303 |             result['filepath'] = line_stripped.split(':', 1)[-1].strip() if ':' in line_stripped else ''
304 |     
305 |     # Detect format and extract code sections
306 |     if '// Pre-version' in text and '// Post-version' in text:
307 |         result['format'] = 'pre_post'
308 |         _parse_pre_post(text, result)
309 |     elif '// Applied fix' in text or '// Applied change' in text or '// Applied patch' in text:
310 |         result['format'] = 'applied'
311 |         _parse_applied(text, result)
312 |     else:
313 |         # Unknown format - try to extract any code
314 |         result['format'] = 'unknown'
315 |     
316 |     return result
317 | 
318 | 
319 | def _parse_pre_post(text: str, result: ParsedDocument) -> None:
320 |     """Parse Pre-version / Post-version format."""
321 |     lines = text.split('\n')
322 |     
323 |     state = 'header'
324 |     pre_lines = []
325 |     post_lines = []
326 |     
327 |     for line in lines:
328 |         stripped = line.strip()
329 |         
330 |         if '// Pre-version' in stripped:
331 |             state = 'pre'
332 |             continue
333 |         elif '// Post-version' in stripped:
334 |             state = 'post'
335 |             continue
336 |         elif stripped.startswith('/**') and state == 'pre':
337 |             # Doxygen block between pre and post - switch to looking for post
338 |             state = 'between'
339 |             continue
340 |         elif state == 'between':
341 |             if '// Post-version' in stripped:
342 |                 state = 'post'
343 |             continue
344 |         
345 |         if state == 'pre':
346 |             pre_lines.append(line)
347 |         elif state == 'post':
348 |             post_lines.append(line)
349 |     
350 |     result['pre_code'] = strip_diff_artifacts('\n'.join(pre_lines).strip())
351 |     result['post_code'] = strip_diff_artifacts('\n'.join(post_lines).strip())
352 | 
353 | 
354 | def _parse_applied(text: str, result: ParsedDocument) -> None:
355 |     """Parse Applied fix format: extract post-commit code and trailing diff."""
356 |     lines = text.split('\n')
357 |     
358 |     # Collect context hints
359 |     for line in lines:
360 |         stripped = line.strip()
361 |         if stripped.startswith('// Context:'):
362 |             result['context_hints'].append(stripped)
363 |     
364 |     # Find the "// Applied fix" marker
365 |     applied_idx = None
366 |     for i, line in enumerate(lines):
367 |         if re.match(r'\s*//\s*Applied\s+(fix|change|patch)', line):
368 |             applied_idx = i
369 |             break
370 |     
371 |     if applied_idx is None:
372 |         return
373 |     
374 |     # Everything after "// Applied fix" until diff starts is the post-commit code
375 |     code_lines = []
376 |     diff_lines = []
377 |     in_diff = False
378 |     
379 |     for i in range(applied_idx + 1, len(lines)):
380 |         line = lines[i]
381 |         
382 |         # Detect start of unified diff section
383 |         # Matches: "--- a/", "diff --git", or "@@ -N,N +N,N @@" hunk headers
384 |         if not in_diff and (
385 |             line.startswith('--- a/') or
386 |             line.startswith('diff --git') or
387 |             re.match(r'^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+\d+(?:,\d+)? @@', line)
388 |         ):
389 |             in_diff = True
390 |         
391 |         if in_diff:
392 |             diff_lines.append(line)
393 |         else:
394 |             code_lines.append(line)
395 |     
396 |     result['post_code'] = '\n'.join(code_lines).strip()
397 |     result['diff_text'] = '\n'.join(diff_lines).strip() if diff_lines else None
398 |     
399 |     # Try to reconstruct pre-commit code by reverse-applying the diff
400 |     if result['diff_text'] and result['post_code']:
401 |         try:
402 |             pre = reverse_apply_diff(result['post_code'], result['diff_text'])
403 |             if pre and len(pre) > 10:
404 |                 result['pre_code'] = strip_diff_artifacts(pre)
405 |         except Exception:
406 |             pass  # If reverse-apply fails, we just won't have pre_code
407 | 
408 | 
409 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
410 | # Output formatters
411 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
412 | 
413 | def format_v2_doxygen(doc: ParsedDocument) -> Optional[str]:
414 |     """Variant 2 + Doxygen: Full before/after reconstruction with Doxygen docstrings."""
415 |     if not doc['post_code']:
416 |         return None
417 |     
418 |     parts = []
419 |     
420 |     # Doxygen header
421 |     parts.append('/**')
422 |     parts.append(' * @file ' + (doc['filepath'] or 'unknown'))
423 |     if doc['fix_description']:
424 |         parts.append(' * @brief Code evolution: ' + doc['fix_description'])
425 |     if doc['repository']:
426 |         parts.append(' * @repository ' + doc['repository'])
427 |     parts.append(' *')
428 |     
429 |     if doc['pre_code']:
430 |         parts.append(' * @section before Original version before the change')
431 |         parts.append(' * @section after  Modified version after applying the fix')
432 |         if doc['fix_description']:
433 |             parts.append(' * @details ' + doc['fix_description'])
434 |     else:
435 |         parts.append(' * @section code Final version after the change was applied')
436 |     
437 |     parts.append(' */')
438 |     parts.append('')
439 |     
440 |     if doc['pre_code']:
441 |         parts.append('// === ORIGINAL VERSION ===')
442 |         parts.append(doc['pre_code'])
443 |         parts.append('')
444 |         parts.append('// === MODIFIED VERSION ===')
445 |         parts.append(doc['post_code'])
446 |     else:
447 |         parts.append('// === CODE ===')
448 |         parts.append(doc['post_code'])
449 |     
450 |     return '\n'.join(parts)
451 | 
452 | 
453 | def format_v2_simple(doc: ParsedDocument) -> Optional[str]:
454 |     """Variant 2 + Simple: Full before/after reconstruction with // comments."""
455 |     if not doc['post_code']:
456 |         return None
457 |     
458 |     parts = []
459 |     
460 |     # Simple header
461 |     if doc['filepath']:
462 |         parts.append('// File: ' + doc['filepath'])
463 |     if doc['fix_description']:
464 |         parts.append('// Change: ' + doc['fix_description'])
465 |     if doc['repository']:
466 |         parts.append('// Repository: ' + doc['repository'])
467 |     parts.append('//')
468 |     
469 |     if doc['pre_code']:
470 |         parts.append('// This file was modified. The original and updated versions follow.')
471 |         parts.append('')
472 |         parts.append('// --- Original version ---')
473 |         parts.append(doc['pre_code'])
474 |         parts.append('')
475 |         parts.append('// --- Updated version ---')
476 |         parts.append(doc['post_code'])
477 |     else:
478 |         parts.append('// Final version of the file after the change.')
479 |         parts.append('')
480 |         parts.append(doc['post_code'])
481 |     
482 |     return '\n'.join(parts)
483 | 
484 | 
485 | def format_v3_doxygen(doc: ParsedDocument) -> Optional[str]:
486 |     """Variant 3 + Doxygen: Structured diff context + full post-commit code."""
487 |     if not doc['post_code']:
488 |         return None
489 |     
490 |     parts = []
491 |     
492 |     # Doxygen header
493 |     parts.append('/**')
494 |     parts.append(' * @file ' + (doc['filepath'] or 'unknown'))
495 |     if doc['fix_description']:
496 |         parts.append(' * @brief ' + doc['fix_description'])
497 |     if doc['repository']:
498 |         parts.append(' * @repository ' + doc['repository'])
499 |     
500 |     # If we have diff info, summarize the changes
501 |     changes = []
502 |     if doc['diff_text']:
503 |         changes = extract_diff_changes(doc['diff_text'])
504 |         if changes:
505 |             total_removed = sum(len(c['removed']) for c in changes)
506 |             total_added = sum(len(c['added']) for c in changes)
507 |             parts.append(' *')
508 |             parts.append(' * @section change_summary')
509 |             parts.append(' * Lines removed: ' + str(total_removed) + ', Lines added: ' + str(total_added))
510 |             parts.append(' * Hunks: ' + str(len(changes)))
511 |     
512 |     parts.append(' */')
513 |     parts.append('')
514 |     
515 |     # Show each changed section
516 |     if changes:
517 |         for i, change in enumerate(changes):
518 |             parts.append('// === Change ' + str(i + 1) + ' (around line ' + str(change['line']) + ') ===')
519 |             if change['removed']:
520 |                 parts.append('// Removed:')
521 |                 for rline in change['removed']:
522 |                     parts.append('//   ' + rline)
523 |             if change['added']:
524 |                 parts.append('// Added:')
525 |                 for aline in change['added']:
526 |                     parts.append('//   ' + aline)
527 |             parts.append('')
528 |     elif doc['pre_code']:
529 |         # Pre/post format - show as a single change block
530 |         parts.append('// === Changes applied ===')
531 |         parts.append('// See the full file below for the updated version.')
532 |         parts.append('')
533 |     
534 |     # Full post-commit code
535 |     parts.append('// === Full file after changes ===')
536 |     parts.append(doc['post_code'])
537 |     
538 |     return '\n'.join(parts)
539 | 
540 | 
541 | def format_v3_simple(doc: ParsedDocument) -> Optional[str]:
542 |     """Variant 3 + Simple: Structured diff context + full post-commit code."""
543 |     if not doc['post_code']:
544 |         return None
545 |     
546 |     parts = []
547 |     
548 |     # Simple header
549 |     if doc['filepath']:
550 |         parts.append('// File: ' + doc['filepath'])
551 |     if doc['fix_description']:
552 |         parts.append('// Change: ' + doc['fix_description'])
553 |     if doc['repository']:
554 |         parts.append('// Repository: ' + doc['repository'])
555 |     
556 |     changes = []
557 |     if doc['diff_text']:
558 |         changes = extract_diff_changes(doc['diff_text'])
559 |         if changes:
560 |             total_removed = sum(len(c['removed']) for c in changes)
561 |             total_added = sum(len(c['added']) for c in changes)
562 |             parts.append('// Lines removed: ' + str(total_removed) + ', Lines added: ' + str(total_added))
563 |     
564 |     parts.append('//')
565 |     
566 |     # Show changed sections
567 |     if changes:
568 |         for i, change in enumerate(changes):
569 |             parts.append('// Change ' + str(i + 1) + ' (line ' + str(change['line']) + '):')
570 |             if change['removed']:
571 |                 parts.append('// Before:')
572 |                 for rline in change['removed']:
573 |                     parts.append('//   ' + rline)
574 |             if change['added']:
575 |                 parts.append('// After:')
576 |                 for aline in change['added']:
577 |                     parts.append('//   ' + aline)
578 |             parts.append('//')
579 |     
580 |     parts.append('')
581 |     parts.append('// Full file:')
582 |     parts.append(doc['post_code'])
583 |     
584 |     return '\n'.join(parts)
585 | 
586 | 
587 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
588 | # Worker function
589 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
590 | 
591 | FORMATTERS = {
592 |     'v2_doxygen': format_v2_doxygen,
593 |     'v2_simple': format_v2_simple,
594 |     'v3_doxygen': format_v3_doxygen,
595 |     'v3_simple': format_v3_simple,
596 | }
597 | 
598 | 
599 | def process_batch(args: tuple[list[str], int]) -> BatchResult:
600 |     """Process a batch of JSONL lines and return results for all 4 formats."""
601 |     batch_lines, batch_id = args
602 |     
603 |     results: dict[str, list[str]] = {fmt: [] for fmt in FORMATTERS}
604 |     stats: BatchStats = {
605 |         'total': 0,
606 |         'applied': 0,
607 |         'pre_post': 0,
608 |         'unknown': 0,
609 |         'pre_reconstructed': 0,
610 |         'failed': 0,
611 |         'non_ascii_cleaned': 0,
612 |     }
613 |     
614 |     for line in batch_lines:
615 |         try:
616 |             doc_raw = json.loads(line)
617 |             text = doc_raw.get('text', '')
618 |             if not text:
619 |                 stats['failed'] += 1
620 |                 continue
621 |             
622 |             stats['total'] += 1
623 |             
624 |             # Check and clean non-ASCII
625 |             if any(ord(c) > 127 for c in text):
626 |                 text = to_ascii(text)
627 |                 stats['non_ascii_cleaned'] += 1
628 |             
629 |             # Parse the document
630 |             parsed = parse_document(text)
631 |             
632 |             if parsed['format'] == 'applied':
633 |                 stats['applied'] += 1
634 |                 if parsed['pre_code']:
635 |                     stats['pre_reconstructed'] += 1
636 |             elif parsed['format'] == 'pre_post':
637 |                 stats['pre_post'] += 1
638 |             else:
639 |                 stats['unknown'] += 1
640 |                 continue
641 |             
642 |             # Generate all 4 output formats
643 |             for fmt_name, formatter in FORMATTERS.items():
644 |                 output = formatter(parsed)
645 |                 if output:
646 |                     # Final ASCII enforcement
647 |                     output = to_ascii(output)
648 |                     results[fmt_name].append(output)
649 |         
650 |         except Exception:
651 |             stats['failed'] += 1
652 |     
653 |     return {"results": results, "stats": stats, "batch_id": batch_id}
654 | 
655 | 
656 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
657 | # Main
658 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
659 | 
660 | def iter_jsonl_lines(path_or_dir, limit=0):
661 |     """Stream lines from a single JSONL file or all JSONL files in a directory."""
662 |     import glob as globmod
663 | 
664 |     if os.path.isdir(path_or_dir):
665 |         files = sorted(globmod.glob(os.path.join(path_or_dir, '*.jsonl')))
666 |         print(f"Found {len(files)} JSONL files in {path_or_dir}")
667 |     else:
668 |         files = [path_or_dir]
669 | 
670 |     count = 0
671 |     for fpath in files:
672 |         with open(fpath) as f:
673 |             for line in f:
674 |                 line = line.strip()
675 |                 if not line:
676 |                     continue
677 |                 yield line
678 |                 count += 1
679 |                 if limit and count >= limit:
680 |                     return
681 | 
682 | 
683 | def main():
684 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Transform commit chains into clean C++ training data')
685 |     parser.add_argument('--input', required=True,
686 |                         help='Input JSONL file OR directory of JSONL files')
687 |     parser.add_argument('--output-dir', required=True, help='Output directory')
688 |     parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=16, help='Number of parallel workers')
689 |     parser.add_argument('--batch-size', type=int, default=1000, help='Documents per batch')
690 |     parser.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=0, help='Limit number of documents (0=all)')
691 |     args = parser.parse_args()
692 | 
693 |     os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
694 | 
695 |     # Open output files
696 |     output_files = {}
697 |     for fmt in FORMATTERS:
698 |         path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f'commit_chains_{fmt}.jsonl')
699 |         output_files[fmt] = open(path, 'w')
700 | 
701 |     # Process in parallel — stream batches from input
702 |     total_stats = {
703 |         'total': 0, 'applied': 0, 'pre_post': 0, 'unknown': 0,
704 |         'pre_reconstructed': 0, 'failed': 0, 'non_ascii_cleaned': 0,
705 |     }
706 |     doc_counts = {fmt: 0 for fmt in FORMATTERS}
707 |     total_docs = 0
708 |     batch_idx = 0
709 |     batch = []
710 | 
711 |     print(f"Streaming from {args.input}...")
712 |     print(f"Processing with {args.workers} workers, batch size {args.batch_size}")
713 | 
714 |     with Pool(args.workers) as pool:
715 |         pending = []
716 | 
717 |         for line in iter_jsonl_lines(args.input, args.limit):
718 |             batch.append(line)
719 |             if len(batch) >= args.batch_size:
720 |                 pending.append(pool.apply_async(process_batch, [(batch, batch_idx)]))
721 |                 batch = []
722 |                 batch_idx += 1
723 | 
724 |                 # Drain completed futures periodically
725 |                 if len(pending) >= args.workers * 4:
726 |                     new_pending = []
727 |                     for fut in pending:
728 |                         if fut.ready():
729 |                             result = fut.get()
730 |                             for fmt in FORMATTERS:
731 |                                 for text in result['results'][fmt]:
732 |                                     json.dump({'text': text}, output_files[fmt])
733 |                                     output_files[fmt].write('\n')
734 |                                     doc_counts[fmt] += 1
735 |                             for key in total_stats:
736 |                                 total_stats[key] += result['stats'][key]
737 |                             total_docs += result['stats']['total']
738 |                         else:
739 |                             new_pending.append(fut)
740 |                     pending = new_pending
741 | 
742 |                     if batch_idx % 500 == 0:
743 |                         print(f"  [batch {batch_idx:>8,}] ~{total_docs:>12,} docs processed  "
744 |                               f"applied={total_stats['applied']:,} "
745 |                               f"v2_dox={doc_counts.get('v2_doxygen',0):,} "
746 |                               f"v3_sim={doc_counts.get('v3_simple',0):,}",
747 |                               flush=True)
748 | 
749 |         # Submit final partial batch
750 |         if batch:
751 |             pending.append(pool.apply_async(process_batch, [(batch, batch_idx)]))
752 | 
753 |         # Drain all remaining futures
754 |         for fut in pending:
755 |             result = fut.get()
756 |             for fmt in FORMATTERS:
757 |                 for text in result['results'][fmt]:
758 |                     json.dump({'text': text}, output_files[fmt])
759 |                     output_files[fmt].write('\n')
760 |                     doc_counts[fmt] += 1
761 |             for key in total_stats:
762 |                 total_stats[key] += result['stats'][key]
763 |             total_docs += result['stats']['total']
764 | 
765 |     # Close files
766 |     for f in output_files.values():
767 |         f.close()
768 | 
769 |     # Print summary
770 |     print("\n" + "=" * 60)
771 |     print("TRANSFORM COMPLETE")
772 |     print("=" * 60)
773 |     print(f"Total documents:     {total_stats['total']:>10,}")
774 |     print(f"  Applied format:    {total_stats['applied']:>10,}")
775 |     print(f"  Pre/Post format:   {total_stats['pre_post']:>10,}")
776 |     print(f"  Unknown (skipped): {total_stats['unknown']:>10,}")
777 |     print(f"  Failed:            {total_stats['failed']:>10,}")
778 |     print(f"Pre-code reconstructed: {total_stats['pre_reconstructed']:>10,}")
779 |     print(f"Non-ASCII cleaned:   {total_stats['non_ascii_cleaned']:>10,}")
780 |     print()
781 |     print("Output files:")
782 |     for fmt in FORMATTERS:
783 |         path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f'commit_chains_{fmt}.jsonl')
784 |         size_mb = os.path.getsize(path) / (1024 * 1024)
785 |         print(f"  {fmt}: {doc_counts[fmt]:>10,} docs, {size_mb:>8.1f} MB  -> {path}")
786 | 
787 | 
788 | if __name__ == '__main__':
789 |     main()
```

```bash
  1 | #!/bin/bash
  2 | set -euo pipefail
  3 | 
  4 | # Parallel clang commit processor runner for build3
  5 | # Uses xargs -P with file-locking state management
  6 | #
  7 | # Resolves all tool paths from the active repo checkout so the script works
  8 | # regardless of where the repo is cloned.  Supports multiple concurrent
  9 | # controllers via a per-PID worker script and an exclusive controller lock.
 10 | 
 11 | WORKERS="${1:-8}"  # Default 8 parallel workers
 12 | 
 13 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 14 | # Resolve repo root from this script's location
 15 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 16 | SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
 17 | REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"
 18 | 
 19 | # Python: honour $PYTHON env, fall back to whatever is on PATH
 20 | PYTHON="${PYTHON:-python3}"
 21 | # PYTHONPATH must include the repo root so `tools.clang_indexer.*` resolves
 22 | PYTHONPATH="${REPO_ROOT}${PYTHONPATH:+:$PYTHONPATH}"
 23 | export PYTHONPATH
 24 | 
 25 | RAW_DIR="${RAW_DIR:-$HOME/adaptive_runs/build3-v9v10-20260310-relaunch/commits/raw_extracts}"
 26 | OUTPUT_BASE="${OUTPUT_BASE:-$HOME/adaptive_runs/clang_commits_v1}"
 27 | JSONL_DIR="$OUTPUT_BASE/jsonl"
 28 | PARQUET_DIR="$OUTPUT_BASE/parquet"
 29 | LOG_DIR="$OUTPUT_BASE/logs"
 30 | STATE_DIR="$OUTPUT_BASE/state"
 31 | COMPLETED_FILE="$STATE_DIR/completed.txt"
 32 | EMPTY_FILE="$STATE_DIR/empty.txt"
 33 | FAILED_FILE="$STATE_DIR/failed.txt"
 34 | LOCK_FILE="$STATE_DIR/.lock"
 35 | 
 36 | TOKENIZER="${TOKENIZER:-$REPO_ROOT/tokenizer_v3.json}"
 37 | MAX_TOKENS="${MAX_TOKENS:-4096}"
 38 | MAX_FILE_BYTES="${MAX_FILE_BYTES:-500000}"
 39 | MAX_DEP_DEPTH="${MAX_DEP_DEPTH:-5}"
 40 | FORMAT="${FORMAT:-both}"
 41 | 
 42 | PARQUET_CONVERTER="$REPO_ROOT/scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py"
 43 | 
 44 | mkdir -p "$JSONL_DIR" "$PARQUET_DIR" "$LOG_DIR" "$STATE_DIR"
 45 | touch "$COMPLETED_FILE" "$EMPTY_FILE" "$FAILED_FILE"
 46 | 
 47 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 48 | # Controller-level lock: prevent two controllers from running on the same
 49 | # OUTPUT_BASE simultaneously.  The lock is held for the lifetime of this
 50 | # script.  Workers use a separate lock (LOCK_FILE) for state-file updates.
 51 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 52 | CONTROLLER_LOCK="$STATE_DIR/.controller.lock"
 53 | exec 9>"$CONTROLLER_LOCK"
 54 | if ! flock -n 9; then
 55 |     echo "ERROR: another controller is already running on $OUTPUT_BASE" >&2
 56 |     exit 1
 57 | fi
 58 | 
 59 | # Worker script path — use PID suffix so concurrent controllers (on different
 60 | # OUTPUT_BASE dirs) never clobber each other's worker script.
 61 | WORKER_SCRIPT="$STATE_DIR/_worker.$$.sh"
 62 | trap 'rm -f "$WORKER_SCRIPT"' EXIT
 63 | cat > "$WORKER_SCRIPT" << 'WORKER_EOF'
 64 | #!/bin/bash
 65 | repo="$1"
 66 | 
 67 | RAW_FILE="$RAW_DIR/${repo}.jsonl"
 68 | JSONL_FILE="$JSONL_DIR/${repo}.jsonl"
 69 | PARQUET_FILE="$PARQUET_DIR/${repo}.parquet"
 70 | LOG_FILE="$LOG_DIR/${repo}.log"
 71 | 
 72 | # Skip if already in any state file (atomic check with flock)
 73 | (
 74 |     flock -s 200
 75 |     if grep -qxF "$repo" "$COMPLETED_FILE" "$EMPTY_FILE" "$FAILED_FILE" 2>/dev/null; then
 76 |         exit 0
 77 |     fi
 78 |     exit 1
 79 | ) 200>"$LOCK_FILE" && exit 0
 80 | 
 81 | [ -f "$RAW_FILE" ] || exit 0
 82 | 
 83 | RAW_SIZE=$(stat -c%s "$RAW_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
 84 | RAW_MB=$((RAW_SIZE / 1048576))
 85 | RAW_GB=$((RAW_SIZE / 1073741824))
 86 | 
 87 | # Dynamic timeout: 2h base, +1h per GB for big repos, max 24h
 88 | TIMEOUT=7200
 89 | if [ "$RAW_GB" -gt 1 ]; then
 90 |     TIMEOUT=$((7200 + RAW_GB * 3600))
 91 |     [ "$TIMEOUT" -gt 86400 ] && TIMEOUT=86400
 92 | fi
 93 | 
 94 | echo "[$(date '+%H:%M:%S')] [$$] $repo (${RAW_MB}MB, timeout=${TIMEOUT}s)"
 95 | 
 96 | # Step 1: Process with clang commit processor
 97 | if [ ! -s "$JSONL_FILE" ]; then
 98 |     timeout "$TIMEOUT" $PYTHON -m tools.clang_indexer.process_commits \
 99 |         --inputs "$RAW_FILE" \
100 |         --output "$JSONL_FILE.tmp" \
101 |         --max-tokens "$MAX_TOKENS" \
102 |         --max-file-bytes "$MAX_FILE_BYTES" \
103 |         --max-dep-depth "$MAX_DEP_DEPTH" \
104 |         --format "$FORMAT" \
105 |         --tokenizer-path "$TOKENIZER" \
106 |         > "$LOG_FILE" 2>&1
107 |     RC=$?
108 | 
109 |     if [ $RC -ne 0 ]; then
110 |         echo "  [$$] $repo FAILED (rc=$RC)"
111 |         (flock 200; echo "$repo" >> "$FAILED_FILE") 200>"$LOCK_FILE"
112 |         rm -f "$JSONL_FILE.tmp"
113 |         exit 0
114 |     fi
115 | 
116 |     if [ ! -s "$JSONL_FILE.tmp" ]; then
117 |         echo "  [$$] $repo EMPTY (0 docs)"
118 |         (flock 200; echo "$repo" >> "$EMPTY_FILE") 200>"$LOCK_FILE"
119 |         rm -f "$JSONL_FILE.tmp"
120 |         exit 0
121 |     fi
122 | 
123 |     mv "$JSONL_FILE.tmp" "$JSONL_FILE"
124 | fi
125 | 
126 | # Step 2: Convert to parquet
127 | if [ ! -s "$PARQUET_FILE" ]; then
128 |     $PYTHON "$PARQUET_CONVERTER" \
129 |         --size "$MAX_TOKENS" \
130 |         --input-file "$JSONL_FILE" \
131 |         --output-file "$PARQUET_FILE.tmp" \
132 |         --overflow-policy drop \
133 |         --materialize-tokenized-enriched \
134 |         --tokenizer-path "$TOKENIZER" \
135 |         >> "$LOG_FILE" 2>&1
136 |     RC=$?
137 | 
138 |     if [ $RC -ne 0 ]; then
139 |         echo "  [$$] $repo PARQUET FAILED (rc=$RC)"
140 |         (flock 200; echo "$repo" >> "$FAILED_FILE") 200>"$LOCK_FILE"
141 |         rm -f "$PARQUET_FILE.tmp"
142 |         exit 0
143 |     fi
144 | 
145 |     mv "$PARQUET_FILE.tmp" "$PARQUET_FILE"
146 | fi
147 | 
148 | echo "  [$$] $repo DONE (parquet created)"
149 | (flock 200; echo "$repo" >> "$COMPLETED_FILE") 200>"$LOCK_FILE"
150 | 
151 | # Clean up JSONL to save space
152 | rm -f "$JSONL_FILE"
153 | WORKER_EOF
154 | chmod +x "$WORKER_SCRIPT"
155 | 
156 | export PYTHON PYTHONPATH RAW_DIR OUTPUT_BASE JSONL_DIR PARQUET_DIR LOG_DIR STATE_DIR
157 | export COMPLETED_FILE EMPTY_FILE FAILED_FILE LOCK_FILE
158 | export TOKENIZER MAX_TOKENS MAX_FILE_BYTES MAX_DEP_DEPTH FORMAT PARQUET_CONVERTER
159 | 
160 | # Build sorted repo list (smallest first for quick wins early)
161 | REPO_LIST=$(ls -Sr "$RAW_DIR"/*.jsonl 2>/dev/null | xargs -I{} basename {} .jsonl)
162 | TOTAL=$(echo "$REPO_LIST" | wc -w)
163 | 
164 | echo "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S') Starting PARALLEL clang commit processing"
165 | echo "  Workers: $WORKERS"
166 | echo "  Total repos: $TOTAL"
167 | echo "  Already completed: $(wc -l < "$COMPLETED_FILE")"
168 | echo "  Already empty: $(wc -l < "$EMPTY_FILE")"
169 | echo "  Already failed: $(wc -l < "$FAILED_FILE")"
170 | echo ""
171 | 
172 | # Use xargs -P for concurrent processing
173 | echo "$REPO_LIST" | tr ' ' '\n' | xargs -P "$WORKERS" -I{} bash "$WORKER_SCRIPT" {}
174 | 
175 | echo ""
176 | echo "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S') COMPLETED"
177 | echo "  Done: $(wc -l < "$COMPLETED_FILE")"
178 | echo "  Empty: $(wc -l < "$EMPTY_FILE")"
179 | echo "  Failed: $(wc -l < "$FAILED_FILE")"
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/nanochat/scripts/eval_commit_to_task.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 26727 bytes
- Created: 2026-04-06 21:03:22

### Code

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Evaluate commit-to-task classification on enriched commit data.
  3 | 
  4 | Given enriched commit data (with changed_symbol_ids, ripple_candidates, and
  5 | commit messages), this script classifies each commit into a task category
  6 | (bugfix, feature, refactor, test) based on commit message keywords and diff
  7 | features, then evaluates classification quality.
  8 | 
  9 | For each commit:
 10 |   1. Extract features: changed files, changed symbols, diff stats, ripple info
 11 |   2. Extract ground-truth label from commit message keywords
 12 |   3. Classify using a simple keyword-based heuristic
 13 |   4. Evaluate: precision, recall, F1 per category
 14 | 
 15 | Usage:
 16 |     # From parquet files with enriched commit data
 17 |     python -m scripts.eval_commit_to_task \\
 18 |         --data_dir /path/to/enriched_commits_4k \\
 19 |         --output_json results.json
 20 | 
 21 |     # With synthetic data (for testing)
 22 |     python -m scripts.eval_commit_to_task --synthetic --output_json results.json
 23 | 
 24 |     # Limit evaluation to first N commits
 25 |     python -m scripts.eval_commit_to_task \\
 26 |         --data_dir /path/to/enriched_commits_4k \\
 27 |         --max_commits 1000 --output_json results.json
 28 | """
 29 | 
 30 | from __future__ import annotations
 31 | 
 32 | import argparse
 33 | import json
 34 | import logging
 35 | import re
 36 | import sys
 37 | from collections import Counter, defaultdict
 38 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 39 | from pathlib import Path
 40 | from typing import Any, NotRequired, TypedDict
 41 | 
 42 | logging.basicConfig(
 43 |     level=logging.INFO,
 44 |     format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s",
 45 | )
 46 | log = logging.getLogger("eval_commit_to_task")
 47 | 
 48 | _REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
 49 | if str(_REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
 50 |     sys.path.insert(0, str(_REPO_ROOT))
 51 | 
 52 | from nanochat.temporal_perf import TemporalPerfTracker  # noqa: E402
 53 | 
 54 | 
 55 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 56 | # Task categories
 57 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 58 | 
 59 | TASK_CATEGORIES = ("bugfix", "feature", "refactor", "test", "other")
 60 | 
 61 | # Keyword patterns for each category — checked against lowercased commit message.
 62 | # Order matters: first match wins.
 63 | CATEGORY_KEYWORDS: dict[str, list[re.Pattern[str]]] = {
 64 |     "bugfix": [
 65 |         re.compile(r"\bfix(?:e[ds])?\b"),
 66 |         re.compile(r"\bbug(?:fix)?\b"),
 67 |         re.compile(r"\bpatch(?:e[ds])?\b"),
 68 |         re.compile(r"\bhotfix\b"),
 69 |         re.compile(r"\bresolve[ds]?\b"),
 70 |         re.compile(r"\bcorrect(?:s|ed|ion)?\b"),
 71 |         re.compile(r"\brepair(?:s|ed)?\b"),
 72 |         re.compile(r"\bworkaround\b"),
 73 |     ],
 74 |     "feature": [
 75 |         re.compile(r"\badd(?:s|ed|ing)?\b"),
 76 |         re.compile(r"\bfeat(?:ure)?(?:s)?\b"),
 77 |         re.compile(r"\bimplement(?:s|ed|ing|ation)?\b"),
 78 |         re.compile(r"\bintroduce[ds]?\b"),
 79 |         re.compile(r"\bnew\b"),
 80 |         re.compile(r"\bsupport(?:s|ed|ing)?\b"),
 81 |         re.compile(r"\benable[ds]?\b"),
 82 |     ],
 83 |     "refactor": [
 84 |         re.compile(r"\brefactor(?:s|ed|ing)?\b"),
 85 |         re.compile(r"\brename[ds]?\b"),
 86 |         re.compile(r"\breorganize[ds]?\b"),
 87 |         re.compile(r"\bclean\s*up\b"),
 88 |         re.compile(r"\bcleanup\b"),
 89 |         re.compile(r"\bsimplif(?:y|ied|ies|ication)\b"),
 90 |         re.compile(r"\brestructure[ds]?\b"),
 91 |         re.compile(r"\bmove[ds]?\b"),
 92 |         re.compile(r"\bextract(?:s|ed|ing)?\b"),
 93 |     ],
 94 |     "test": [
 95 |         re.compile(r"\btest(?:s|ed|ing)?\b"),
 96 |         re.compile(r"\bunittest\b"),
 97 |         re.compile(r"\bunit[\s_-]?test\b"),
 98 |         re.compile(r"\bcoverage\b"),
 99 |         re.compile(r"\bassert(?:s|ed|ion)?\b"),
100 |     ],
101 | }
102 | 
103 | 
104 | def classify_commit_message(message: str) -> str:
105 |     """Classify a commit message into a task category.
106 | 
107 |     Uses keyword matching against the lowercased message.
108 |     First matching category wins. Returns 'other' if no match.
109 |     """
110 |     if not message:
111 |         return "other"
112 |     lower = message.lower()
113 |     for category, patterns in CATEGORY_KEYWORDS.items():
114 |         for pat in patterns:
115 |             if pat.search(lower):
116 |                 return category
117 |     return "other"
118 | 
119 | 
120 | def extract_keywords(message: str) -> list[str]:
121 |     """Extract task-relevant keywords from a commit message.
122 | 
123 |     Returns a list of matched keyword strings (lowercased).
124 |     """
125 |     if not message:
126 |         return []
127 |     lower = message.lower()
128 |     keywords: list[str] = []
129 |     for category, patterns in CATEGORY_KEYWORDS.items():
130 |         for pat in patterns:
131 |             m = pat.search(lower)
132 |             if m:
133 |                 keywords.append(m.group(0))
134 |     return keywords
135 | 
136 | 
137 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
138 | # Commit record
139 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
140 | 
141 | 
142 | class CategoryMetrics(TypedDict):
143 |     precision: float
144 |     recall: float
145 |     f1: float
146 |     support: int
147 |     true_positives: int
148 |     false_positives: int
149 |     false_negatives: int
150 | 
151 | 
152 | class AggregateMetrics(TypedDict):
153 |     precision: float
154 |     recall: float
155 |     f1: float
156 | 
157 | 
158 | class ClassificationMetrics(TypedDict):
159 |     per_category: dict[str, CategoryMetrics]
160 |     macro: AggregateMetrics
161 |     weighted: AggregateMetrics
162 |     accuracy: float
163 |     total_commits: int
164 |     category_distribution: dict[str, int]
165 | 
166 | 
167 | class FeatureSummaryStats(TypedDict):
168 |     mean: float
169 |     min: float
170 |     max: float
171 | 
172 | 
173 | class EvaluationReport(TypedDict):
174 |     schema_version: str
175 |     classification_metrics: ClassificationMetrics
176 |     feature_summary: dict[str, FeatureSummaryStats]
177 |     performance: NotRequired[dict[str, Any]]
178 | 
179 | 
180 | @dataclass
181 | class CommitTaskRecord:
182 |     """Enriched commit record for task classification."""
183 | 
184 |     commit_hash: str
185 |     message: str
186 |     filepath: str
187 |     changed_symbol_count: int
188 |     ripple_candidate_count: int
189 |     ripple_relation_types: list[str]
190 |     changed_chunk_count: int
191 |     diff_additions: int
192 |     diff_deletions: int
193 |     true_category: str  # derived from message keywords
194 |     predicted_category: str = ""
195 | 
196 |     @property
197 |     def diff_total(self) -> int:
198 |         return self.diff_additions + self.diff_deletions
199 | 
200 | 
201 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
202 | # Feature extraction
203 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
204 | 
205 | 
206 | def extract_commit_features(record: CommitTaskRecord) -> dict[str, int]:
207 |     """Extract numeric features from a commit record."""
208 |     relation_counts: dict[str, int] = Counter(record.ripple_relation_types)
209 |     return {
210 |         "changed_symbol_count": record.changed_symbol_count,
211 |         "ripple_candidate_count": record.ripple_candidate_count,
212 |         "changed_chunk_count": record.changed_chunk_count,
213 |         "diff_additions": record.diff_additions,
214 |         "diff_deletions": record.diff_deletions,
215 |         "diff_total": record.diff_total,
216 |         "ripple_caller_count": relation_counts.get("caller", 0),
217 |         "ripple_type_dependent_count": relation_counts.get("type_dependent", 0),
218 |         "ripple_co_member_count": relation_counts.get("co_member", 0),
219 |     }
220 | 
221 | 
222 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
223 | # Metrics: precision, recall, F1
224 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
225 | 
226 | 
227 | def compute_classification_metrics(
228 |     records: list[CommitTaskRecord],
229 | ) -> ClassificationMetrics:
230 |     """Compute per-category and overall precision, recall, F1.
231 | 
232 |     Uses the ``true_category`` and ``predicted_category`` fields on each record.
233 |     """
234 |     # Count TP, FP, FN per category
235 |     tp: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
236 |     fp: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
237 |     fn: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
238 | 
239 |     for rec in records:
240 |         true = rec.true_category
241 |         pred = rec.predicted_category
242 |         if true == pred:
243 |             tp[true] += 1
244 |         else:
245 |             fp[pred] += 1
246 |             fn[true] += 1
247 | 
248 |     per_category: dict[str, CategoryMetrics] = {}
249 |     for cat in TASK_CATEGORIES:
250 |         t = tp.get(cat, 0)
251 |         f_p = fp.get(cat, 0)
252 |         f_n = fn.get(cat, 0)
253 |         precision = t / (t + f_p) if (t + f_p) > 0 else 0.0
254 |         recall = t / (t + f_n) if (t + f_n) > 0 else 0.0
255 |         f1 = (
256 |             2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)
257 |             if (precision + recall) > 0
258 |             else 0.0
259 |         )
260 |         per_category[cat] = {
261 |             "precision": round(precision, 4),
262 |             "recall": round(recall, 4),
263 |             "f1": round(f1, 4),
264 |             "support": t + f_n,  # total true instances
265 |             "true_positives": t,
266 |             "false_positives": f_p,
267 |             "false_negatives": f_n,
268 |         }
269 | 
270 |     # Macro-averaged metrics
271 |     active_cats = [c for c in TASK_CATEGORIES if per_category[c]["support"] > 0]
272 |     if active_cats:
273 |         macro_precision = sum(per_category[c]["precision"] for c in active_cats) / len(
274 |             active_cats
275 |         )
276 |         macro_recall = sum(per_category[c]["recall"] for c in active_cats) / len(
277 |             active_cats
278 |         )
279 |         macro_f1 = sum(per_category[c]["f1"] for c in active_cats) / len(active_cats)
280 |     else:
281 |         macro_precision = macro_recall = macro_f1 = 0.0
282 | 
283 |     # Weighted-average (by support)
284 |     total_support = sum(per_category[c]["support"] for c in TASK_CATEGORIES)
285 |     if total_support > 0:
286 |         weighted_precision = sum(
287 |             per_category[c]["precision"] * per_category[c]["support"]
288 |             for c in TASK_CATEGORIES
289 |         ) / total_support
290 |         weighted_recall = sum(
291 |             per_category[c]["recall"] * per_category[c]["support"]
292 |             for c in TASK_CATEGORIES
293 |         ) / total_support
294 |         weighted_f1 = sum(
295 |             per_category[c]["f1"] * per_category[c]["support"]
296 |             for c in TASK_CATEGORIES
297 |         ) / total_support
298 |     else:
299 |         weighted_precision = weighted_recall = weighted_f1 = 0.0
300 | 
301 |     # Overall accuracy
302 |     correct = sum(1 for r in records if r.true_category == r.predicted_category)
303 |     accuracy = correct / len(records) if records else 0.0
304 | 
305 |     macro: AggregateMetrics = {
306 |         "precision": round(macro_precision, 4),
307 |         "recall": round(macro_recall, 4),
308 |         "f1": round(macro_f1, 4),
309 |     }
310 |     weighted: AggregateMetrics = {
311 |         "precision": round(weighted_precision, 4),
312 |         "recall": round(weighted_recall, 4),
313 |         "f1": round(weighted_f1, 4),
314 |     }
315 |     category_distribution = {
316 |         cat: sum(1 for r in records if r.true_category == cat)
317 |         for cat in TASK_CATEGORIES
318 |     }
319 |     return {
320 |         "per_category": per_category,
321 |         "macro": macro,
322 |         "weighted": weighted,
323 |         "accuracy": round(accuracy, 4),
324 |         "total_commits": len(records),
325 |         "category_distribution": category_distribution,
326 |     }
327 | 
328 | 
329 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
330 | # Parquet data loading
331 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
332 | 
333 | 
334 | def _extract_ripple_relations(ripple_raw: Any) -> list[str]:
335 |     """Extract relation types from ripple_candidates (list or JSON string)."""
336 |     if ripple_raw is None:
337 |         return []
338 |     if isinstance(ripple_raw, str):
339 |         try:
340 |             ripple_raw = json.loads(ripple_raw)
341 |         except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
342 |             return []
343 |     if not isinstance(ripple_raw, list):
344 |         return []
345 |     relations: list[str] = []
346 |     for entry in ripple_raw:
347 |         if not isinstance(entry, dict):
348 |             continue
349 |         for cand in entry.get("candidates", []):
350 |             if isinstance(cand, dict):
351 |                 rel = cand.get("relation", "caller")
352 |                 relations.append(rel)
353 |     return relations
354 | 
355 | 
356 | def _count_ripple_candidates(ripple_raw: Any) -> int:
357 |     """Count total ripple candidates."""
358 |     if ripple_raw is None:
359 |         return 0
360 |     if isinstance(ripple_raw, str):
361 |         try:
362 |             ripple_raw = json.loads(ripple_raw)
363 |         except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
364 |             return 0
365 |     if not isinstance(ripple_raw, list):
366 |         return 0
367 |     count = 0
368 |     for entry in ripple_raw:
369 |         if isinstance(entry, dict):
370 |             count += len(entry.get("candidates", []))
371 |     return count
372 | 
373 | 
374 | def load_commits_from_parquet(
375 |     data_dir: str,
376 |     max_commits: int | None = None,
377 | ) -> list[CommitTaskRecord]:
378 |     """Load enriched commit records from parquet shards.
379 | 
380 |     Expects parquet files with columns including:
381 |     - commit_hash, text (commit message in first line or separate column)
382 |     - changed_symbol_ids, ripple_candidates
383 |     - Optional: filepath, changed_chunk_ids, diff stats
384 |     """
385 |     try:
386 |         import pyarrow.parquet as pq
387 |     except ImportError:
388 |         log.error("pyarrow is required. Install with: pip install pyarrow")
389 |         sys.exit(1)
390 | 
391 |     data_path = Path(data_dir)
392 |     parquet_files = sorted(data_path.glob("*.parquet"))
393 |     if not parquet_files:
394 |         log.error(f"No parquet files found in {data_dir}")
395 |         sys.exit(1)
396 | 
397 |     log.info(f"Loading from {len(parquet_files)} parquet shards in {data_dir}")
398 | 
399 |     records: list[CommitTaskRecord] = []
400 |     for pf in parquet_files:
401 |         table = pq.read_table(pf)
402 |         columns = set(table.column_names)
403 | 
404 |         for i in range(len(table)):
405 |             # Commit hash
406 |             commit_hash = (
407 |                 table.column("commit_hash")[i].as_py()
408 |                 if "commit_hash" in columns
409 |                 else f"{pf.stem}_{i}"
410 |             )
411 | 
412 |             # Message: try 'message' column, then first line of 'text'
413 |             message = ""
414 |             if "message" in columns:
415 |                 message = table.column("message")[i].as_py() or ""
416 |             elif "text" in columns:
417 |                 text = table.column("text")[i].as_py() or ""
418 |                 message = text.split("\n", 1)[0] if text else ""
419 | 
420 |             # Filepath
421 |             filepath = ""
422 |             if "filepath" in columns:
423 |                 filepath = table.column("filepath")[i].as_py() or ""
424 | 
425 |             # Changed symbols
426 |             changed_symbol_count = 0
427 |             if "changed_symbol_ids" in columns:
428 |                 csids = table.column("changed_symbol_ids")[i].as_py()
429 |                 changed_symbol_count = len(csids) if csids else 0
430 | 
431 |             # Ripple candidates
432 |             ripple_raw = None
433 |             if "ripple_candidates" in columns:
434 |                 ripple_raw = table.column("ripple_candidates")[i].as_py()
435 | 
436 |             # Changed chunks
437 |             changed_chunk_count = 0
438 |             if "changed_chunk_ids" in columns:
439 |                 ccids = table.column("changed_chunk_ids")[i].as_py()
440 |                 changed_chunk_count = len(ccids) if ccids else 0
441 | 
442 |             # Diff stats (often not in parquet — default to 0)
443 |             diff_add = 0
444 |             diff_del = 0
445 |             if "diff_additions" in columns:
446 |                 diff_add = table.column("diff_additions")[i].as_py() or 0
447 |             if "diff_deletions" in columns:
448 |                 diff_del = table.column("diff_deletions")[i].as_py() or 0
449 | 
450 |             category = classify_commit_message(message)
451 | 
452 |             records.append(
453 |                 CommitTaskRecord(
454 |                     commit_hash=commit_hash,
455 |                     message=message,
456 |                     filepath=filepath,
457 |                     changed_symbol_count=changed_symbol_count,
458 |                     ripple_candidate_count=_count_ripple_candidates(ripple_raw),
459 |                     ripple_relation_types=_extract_ripple_relations(ripple_raw),
460 |                     changed_chunk_count=changed_chunk_count,
461 |                     diff_additions=diff_add,
462 |                     diff_deletions=diff_del,
463 |                     true_category=category,
464 |                 )
465 |             )
466 | 
467 |             if max_commits is not None and len(records) >= max_commits:
468 |                 return records
469 | 
470 |     return records
471 |

591 | def generate_synthetic_commits() -> list[CommitTaskRecord]:
592 |     """Generate synthetic commit records for testing."""
593 |     records: list[CommitTaskRecord] = []
594 |     for entry in SYNTHETIC_COMMITS:
595 |         category = classify_commit_message(entry["message"])
596 |         records.append(
597 |             CommitTaskRecord(
598 |                 commit_hash=entry["hash"],
599 |                 message=entry["message"],
600 |                 filepath=entry["filepath"],
601 |                 changed_symbol_count=entry["changed_symbols"],
602 |                 ripple_candidate_count=entry["ripple_count"],
603 |                 ripple_relation_types=entry["ripple_relations"],
604 |                 changed_chunk_count=entry["changed_chunks"],
605 |                 diff_additions=entry["additions"],
606 |                 diff_deletions=entry["deletions"],
607 |                 true_category=category,
608 |             )
609 |         )
610 |     return records

612 | 
613 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
614 | # Classifier (keyword-based — same as labeling, so accuracy is definitionally
615 | # perfect when labels come from the same keywords. This is intentional: the
616 | # eval substrate tests the harness infrastructure, not model accuracy.)
617 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
618 | 
619 | 
620 | def run_keyword_classifier(records: list[CommitTaskRecord]) -> list[CommitTaskRecord]:
621 |     """Classify commits using the keyword heuristic.
622 | 
623 |     Sets ``predicted_category`` on each record and returns the same list.
624 |     """
625 |     for rec in records:
626 |         rec.predicted_category = classify_commit_message(rec.message)
627 |     return records
628 | 
629 | 
630 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
631 | # Full evaluation pipeline
632 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
633 | 
634 | 
635 | def _build_feature_summary(features_list: list[dict[str, int]]) -> dict[str, FeatureSummaryStats]:
636 |     feature_summary: dict[str, FeatureSummaryStats] = {}
637 |     if not features_list:
638 |         return feature_summary
639 |     for key in features_list[0]:
640 |         values = [float(features[key]) for features in features_list]
641 |         feature_summary[key] = {
642 |             "mean": round(sum(values) / len(values), 2),
643 |             "min": min(values),
644 |             "max": max(values),
645 |         }
646 |     return feature_summary
647 | 
648 | 
649 | def _build_evaluation_report(
650 |     metrics: ClassificationMetrics,
651 |     feature_summary: dict[str, FeatureSummaryStats],
652 |     perf_report: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
653 | ) -> EvaluationReport:
654 |     report: EvaluationReport = {
655 |         "schema_version": "nanochat.commit_to_task_eval.v1",
656 |         "classification_metrics": metrics,
657 |         "feature_summary": feature_summary,
658 |     }
659 |     if perf_report is not None:
660 |         report["performance"] = perf_report
661 |     return report
662 | 
663 | 
664 | def _format_summary_lines(report: EvaluationReport) -> list[str]:
665 |     metrics = report["classification_metrics"]
666 |     lines = [
667 |         (
668 |             f"Accuracy: {metrics['accuracy']:.4f} | "
669 |             f"Macro F1: {metrics['macro']['f1']:.4f} | "
670 |             f"Weighted F1: {metrics['weighted']['f1']:.4f}"
671 |         )
672 |     ]
673 |     for category in TASK_CATEGORIES:
674 |         category_metrics = metrics["per_category"][category]
675 |         lines.append(
676 |             f"  {category:10s}: P={category_metrics['precision']:.3f} "
677 |             f"R={category_metrics['recall']:.3f} "
678 |             f"F1={category_metrics['f1']:.3f} "
679 |             f"support={category_metrics['support']}"
680 |         )
681 |     perf_report = report.get("performance")
682 |     if perf_report is not None:
683 |         throughput = perf_report.get("throughput", {})
684 |         commits_per_sec = float(throughput.get("commits_per_sec", 0.0))
685 |         wall_clock_seconds = float(perf_report.get("wall_clock_seconds", 0.0))
686 |         lines.append(
687 |             f"Throughput: {commits_per_sec:.1f} commits/sec | "
688 |             f"Wall clock: {wall_clock_seconds:.2f}s"
689 |         )
690 |     return lines
691 | 
692 | 
693 | def run_evaluation(
694 |     records: list[CommitTaskRecord],
695 |     tracker: TemporalPerfTracker | None = None,
696 | ) -> EvaluationReport:
697 |     """Run the full commit-to-task evaluation pipeline.
698 | 
699 |     Steps:
700 |       1. Extract features for each commit
701 |       2. Classify using keyword heuristic
702 |       3. Compute metrics
703 |       4. Track performance
704 | 
705 |     Returns a JSON-serializable report dict.
706 |     """
707 |     if tracker is not None:
708 |         tracker.start_run(config={"num_commits": len(records)})
709 | 
710 |     # Step 1: Feature extraction
711 |     features_list: list[dict[str, int]] = []
712 |     batch_size = max(1, len(records) // 10)  # 10 steps for tracking
713 |     step_idx = 0
714 |     for i in range(0, len(records), batch_size):
715 |         batch = records[i : i + batch_size]
716 |         for rec in batch:
717 |             features_list.append(extract_commit_features(rec))
718 |         if tracker is not None:
719 |             tracker.record_step(
720 |                 step_idx,
721 |                 {"phase": "feature_extraction", "batch_size": len(batch)},
722 |                 commits=len(batch),
723 |             )
724 |             step_idx += 1
725 | 
726 |     # Step 2: Classification
727 |     records = run_keyword_classifier(records)
728 |     if tracker is not None:
729 |         tracker.record_step(
730 |             step_idx,
731 |             {"phase": "classification", "num_classified": len(records)},
732 |             commits=len(records),
733 |         )
734 |         step_idx += 1
735 | 
736 |     # Step 3: Metrics
737 |     metrics = compute_classification_metrics(records)
738 |     if tracker is not None:
739 |         tracker.record_step(
740 |             step_idx,
741 |             {"phase": "metrics_computation"},
742 |             commits=1,
743 |         )
744 |         step_idx += 1
745 | 
746 |     # Step 4: Finalize
747 |     perf_report = tracker.finish_run() if tracker is not None else None
748 | 
749 |     feature_summary = _build_feature_summary(features_list)
750 |     return _build_evaluation_report(metrics, feature_summary, perf_report)
751 | 
752 | 
753 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
754 | # CLI
755 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
756 | 
757 | 
758 | def main() -> None:
759 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
760 |         description="Evaluate commit-to-task classification on enriched commit data"
761 |     )
762 |     parser.add_argument(
763 |         "--data_dir",
764 |         type=str,
765 |         help="Path to directory containing enriched commit parquet shards",
766 |     )
767 |     parser.add_argument(
768 |         "--synthetic",
769 |         action="store_true",
770 |         help="Use synthetic test data instead of parquet",
771 |     )
772 |     parser.add_argument(
773 |         "--max_commits",
774 |         type=int,
775 |         default=None,
776 |         help="Limit evaluation to first N commits",
777 |     )
778 |     parser.add_argument(
779 |         "--output_json",
780 |         type=str,
781 |         default=None,
782 |         help="Path to write JSON report",
783 |     )
784 |     args = parser.parse_args()
785 | 
786 |     if not args.synthetic and not args.data_dir:
787 |         parser.error("Either --data_dir or --synthetic must be specified")
788 | 
789 |     # Load data
790 |     if args.synthetic:
791 |         log.info("Using synthetic commit data")
792 |         records = generate_synthetic_commits()
793 |     else:
794 |         records = load_commits_from_parquet(args.data_dir, args.max_commits)
795 | 
796 |     log.info(f"Loaded {len(records)} commit records")
797 | 
798 |     # Run evaluation with performance tracking
799 |     tracker = TemporalPerfTracker("commit_to_task_eval")
800 |     report = run_evaluation(records, tracker=tracker)
801 | 
802 |     for line in _format_summary_lines(report):
803 |         log.info(line)
804 | 
805 |     # Save report
806 |     if args.output_json:
807 |         with open(args.output_json, "w") as f:
808 |             json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
809 |         log.info(f"Report written to {args.output_json}")
810 |     else:
811 |         print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
812 | 
813 | 
814 | if __name__ == "__main__":
815 |     main()
```

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Learned commit-to-task classification using TF-IDF + Logistic Regression.
  3 | 
  4 | Second variant of commit-to-task evaluation (vnw2.3.2.7). Adds a learned
  5 | classifier (TF-IDF on commit message + logistic regression) and compares
  6 | against the keyword baseline from eval_commit_to_task.py.
  7 | 
  8 | For each commit:
  9 |   1. Extract TF-IDF features from the commit message (and optionally diff text)
 10 |   2. Train a logistic regression classifier on a train split
 11 |   3. Evaluate on a held-out test split
 12 |   4. Compare against the keyword baseline
 13 |   5. Output a JSON report with both methods' metrics
 14 | 
 15 | Usage:
 16 |     # With synthetic data (for testing)
 17 |     python -m scripts.eval_commit_to_task_learned --synthetic --output_json results.json
 18 | 
 19 |     # From parquet files with enriched commit data
 20 |     python -m scripts.eval_commit_to_task_learned \\
 21 |         --data_dir /path/to/enriched_commits_4k \\
 22 |         --output_json results.json
 23 | 
 24 |     # Custom train/test split ratio and TF-IDF params
 25 |     python -m scripts.eval_commit_to_task_learned --synthetic \\
 26 |         --test_size 0.3 --max_features 5000 --output_json results.json
 27 | """
 28 |

 30 | 
 31 | import argparse
 32 | import json
 33 | import logging
 34 | import sys
 35 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 36 | from pathlib import Path
 37 | from typing import Any, Callable, TypedDict, cast
 38 | 
 39 | import numpy as np
 40 |

 44 |     level=logging.INFO,
 45 |     format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s",
 46 | )
 47 | log = logging.getLogger("eval_commit_to_task_learned")

 50 | if str(_REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
 51 |     sys.path.insert(0, str(_REPO_ROOT))

 53 | from scripts.eval_commit_to_task import (
 54 |     CommitTaskRecord,
 55 |     classify_commit_message,
 56 |     compute_classification_metrics,
 57 |     load_commits_from_parquet,
 58 |     run_keyword_classifier,
 59 | )
```
