 61 | # Lazy imports for sklearn — avoids import cost when only testing helpers.
 62 | TfidfVectorizer: Any | None = None
 63 | LogisticRegression: Any | None = None
 64 | train_test_split: Any | None = None
 65 | _sklearn_available = True
 66 | try:
 67 |     from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer as _TfidfVectorizer
 68 |     from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression as _LogisticRegression
 69 |     from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split as _train_test_split
 70 | except ImportError:
 71 |     _sklearn_available = False
 72 | else:
 73 |     TfidfVectorizer = _TfidfVectorizer
 74 |     LogisticRegression = _LogisticRegression
 75 |     train_test_split = _train_test_split
 76 | 
 77 | 
 78 | def _require_sklearn() -> None:
 79 |     """Raise ImportError with a helpful message if sklearn is missing."""
 80 |     if not _sklearn_available:
 81 |         raise ImportError(
 82 |             "scikit-learn is required for the learned classifier. "
 83 |             "Install with: pip install scikit-learn"
 84 |         )
 85 | 
 86 | 
 87 | def _require_sklearn_symbol(name: str, value: Any) -> Any:
 88 |     _require_sklearn()
 89 |     if value is None:
 90 |         raise ImportError(f"scikit-learn symbol {name} is unavailable")
 91 |     return value
 92 | 
 93 | 
 94 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 95 | # TF-IDF feature extraction
 96 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 97 | 
 98 | 
 99 | def build_tfidf_features(
100 |     messages: list[str],
101 |     max_features: int = 5000,
102 |     ngram_range: tuple[int, int] = (1, 2),
103 |     fit: bool = True,
104 |     vectorizer: Any = None,
105 | ) -> tuple[Any, Any]:
106 |     """Build TF-IDF feature matrix from commit messages.
107 | 
108 |     Args:
109 |         messages: List of commit message strings.
110 |         max_features: Maximum number of TF-IDF features.
111 |         ngram_range: (min_n, max_n) for n-gram extraction.
112 |         fit: If True, fit a new vectorizer. If False, use the provided one.
113 |         vectorizer: Pre-fitted vectorizer (required if fit=False).
114 | 
115 |     Returns:
116 |         (feature_matrix, vectorizer) tuple. The feature matrix is a sparse
117 |         CSR matrix of shape (n_messages, n_features).
118 |     """
119 |     vectorizer_cls = cast(Callable[..., Any], _require_sklearn_symbol("TfidfVectorizer", TfidfVectorizer))
120 |     if fit:
121 |         vectorizer = vectorizer_cls(
122 |             max_features=max_features,
123 |             ngram_range=ngram_range,
124 |             stop_words="english",
125 |             sublinear_tf=True,
126 |             dtype=np.float32,
127 |         )
128 |         X = cast(Any, vectorizer).fit_transform(messages)
129 |     else:
130 |         if vectorizer is None:
131 |             raise ValueError("vectorizer must be provided when fit=False")
132 |         X = cast(Any, vectorizer).transform(messages)
133 |     return X, vectorizer
134 | 
135 | 
136 | def extract_tfidf_feature_names(vectorizer: Any) -> list[str]:
137 |     """Get the feature names from a fitted TF-IDF vectorizer."""
138 |     return list(cast(Any, vectorizer).get_feature_names_out())
139 | 
140 | 
141 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
142 | # Logistic Regression classifier
143 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
144 | 
145 | 
146 | @dataclass
147 | class LearnedClassifierResult:
148 |     """Result from training and evaluating a learned classifier."""
149 | 
150 |     train_accuracy: float
151 |     test_accuracy: float
152 |     test_predictions: list[str]
153 |     test_true_labels: list[str]
154 |     test_indices: list[int]
155 |     feature_names: list[str]
156 |     n_train: int
157 |     n_test: int
158 |     top_features_per_class: dict[str, list[tuple[str, float]]]
159 | 
160 | 
161 | class AggregateMetrics(TypedDict):
162 |     precision: float
163 |     recall: float
164 |     f1: float
165 | 
166 | 
167 | class CategoryMetrics(TypedDict):
168 |     precision: float
169 |     recall: float
170 |     f1: float
171 |     support: int
172 |     true_positives: int
173 |     false_positives: int
174 |     false_negatives: int
175 | 
176 | 
177 | class ClassificationMetrics(TypedDict):
178 |     per_category: dict[str, CategoryMetrics]
179 |     macro: AggregateMetrics
180 |     weighted: AggregateMetrics
181 |     accuracy: float
182 |     total_commits: int
183 |     category_distribution: dict[str, int]
184 | 
185 | 
186 | class LearnedClassifierReport(TypedDict):
187 |     method: str
188 |     train_accuracy: float
189 |     test_metrics: ClassificationMetrics
190 |     n_features: int
191 |     top_features_per_class: dict[str, list[tuple[str, float]]]
192 | 
193 | 
194 | class ComparisonDelta(TypedDict):
195 |     delta_accuracy: float
196 |     delta_macro_f1: float
197 |     delta_weighted_f1: float
198 |     learned_wins: bool
199 |     summary: str
200 | 
201 | 
202 | class LearnedDatasetSummary(TypedDict):
203 |     total_commits: int
204 |     train_size: int
205 |     test_size: int
206 | 
207 | 
208 | class LearnedEvaluationReport(TypedDict):
209 |     schema_version: str
210 |     dataset: LearnedDatasetSummary
211 |     keyword_baseline: ClassificationMetrics
212 |     learned_classifier: LearnedClassifierReport
213 |     comparison: ComparisonDelta
214 | 
215 | 
216 | def train_logistic_regression(
217 |     messages: list[str],
218 |     labels: list[str],
219 |     test_size: float = 0.2,
220 |     max_features: int = 5000,
221 |     ngram_range: tuple[int, int] = (1, 2),
222 |     random_state: int = 42,
223 |     C: float = 1.0,
224 | ) -> LearnedClassifierResult:
225 |     """Train a TF-IDF + Logistic Regression classifier.
226 | 
227 |     Args:
228 |         messages: List of commit message strings.
229 |         labels: List of category labels (same length as messages).
230 |         test_size: Fraction of data for testing.
231 |         max_features: Maximum number of TF-IDF features.
232 |         ngram_range: N-gram range for TF-IDF.
233 |         random_state: Random seed for reproducibility.
234 |         C: Regularization strength for logistic regression.
235 | 
236 |     Returns:
237 |         LearnedClassifierResult with metrics and predictions.
238 |     """
239 |     train_test_split_fn = cast(Callable[..., tuple[list[int], list[int]]], _require_sklearn_symbol("train_test_split", train_test_split))
240 |     logistic_regression_cls = cast(Callable[..., Any], _require_sklearn_symbol("LogisticRegression", LogisticRegression))
241 | 
242 |     if len(messages) != len(labels):
243 |         raise ValueError(
244 |             f"messages ({len(messages)}) and labels ({len(labels)}) must have same length"
245 |         )
246 |     if len(messages) < 4:
247 |         raise ValueError(
248 |             f"Need at least 4 samples for train/test split, got {len(messages)}"
249 |         )
250 | 
251 |     # Build indices for stratified split
252 |     indices = list(range(len(messages)))
253 | 
254 |     # Try stratified split; fall back to non-stratified if a class has < 2 samples
255 |     try:
256 |         train_idx, test_idx = train_test_split_fn(
257 |             indices,
258 |             test_size=test_size,
259 |             random_state=random_state,
260 |             stratify=labels,
261 |         )
262 |     except ValueError:
263 |         # Stratification fails when some class has < 2 members
264 |         train_idx, test_idx = train_test_split_fn(
265 |             indices,
266 |             test_size=test_size,
267 |             random_state=random_state,
268 |         )
269 | 
270 |     train_msgs = [messages[i] for i in train_idx]
271 |     test_msgs = [messages[i] for i in test_idx]
272 |     train_labels = [labels[i] for i in train_idx]
273 |     test_labels = [labels[i] for i in test_idx]
274 | 
275 |     # TF-IDF features
276 |     X_train, vectorizer = build_tfidf_features(
277 |         train_msgs, max_features=max_features, ngram_range=ngram_range, fit=True
278 |     )
279 |     X_test, _ = build_tfidf_features(
280 |         test_msgs, fit=False, vectorizer=vectorizer
281 |     )
282 | 
283 |     feature_names = extract_tfidf_feature_names(vectorizer)
284 | 
285 |     # Train logistic regression
286 |     clf = logistic_regression_cls(
287 |         C=C,
288 |         max_iter=1000,
289 |         solver="lbfgs",
290 |         random_state=random_state,
291 |     )
292 |     clf.fit(X_train, train_labels)
293 | 
294 |     # Evaluate
295 |     train_preds = clf.predict(X_train)
296 |     test_preds = clf.predict(X_test)
297 | 
298 |     train_accuracy = float(np.mean(np.array(train_preds) == np.array(train_labels)))
299 |     test_accuracy = float(np.mean(np.array(test_preds) == np.array(test_labels)))
300 | 
301 |     # Top features per class
302 |     top_features: dict[str, list[tuple[str, float]]] = {}
303 |     if hasattr(clf, "coef_") and len(clf.classes_) > 1:
304 |         for i, cls_name in enumerate(clf.classes_):
305 |             if i < clf.coef_.shape[0]:
306 |                 coef_row = clf.coef_[i]
307 |                 top_k = min(10, len(feature_names))
308 |                 top_indices = np.argsort(coef_row)[-top_k:][::-1]
309 |                 top_features[cls_name] = [
310 |                     (feature_names[j], round(float(coef_row[j]), 4))
311 |                     for j in top_indices
312 |                 ]
313 | 
314 |     return LearnedClassifierResult(
315 |         train_accuracy=round(train_accuracy, 4),
316 |         test_accuracy=round(test_accuracy, 4),
317 |         test_predictions=list(test_preds),
318 |         test_true_labels=test_labels,
319 |         test_indices=test_idx,
320 |         feature_names=feature_names,
321 |         n_train=len(train_idx),
322 |         n_test=len(test_idx),
323 |         top_features_per_class=top_features,
324 |     )
325 | 
326 | 
327 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
328 | # Comparison report
329 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
330 | 
331 | 
332 | def build_comparison_report(
333 |     records: list[CommitTaskRecord],
334 |     learned_result: LearnedClassifierResult,
335 |     keyword_metrics: ClassificationMetrics,
336 | ) -> LearnedEvaluationReport:
337 |     """Build a JSON-serializable comparison report.
338 | 
339 |     Compares the keyword baseline against the learned TF-IDF + LR classifier
340 |     on the same test set.
341 | 
342 |     Args:
343 |         records: All commit records (used for metadata).
344 |         learned_result: Result from train_logistic_regression.
345 |         keyword_metrics: Metrics from compute_classification_metrics on keyword
346 |             predictions (evaluated on the same test split).
347 | 
348 |     Returns:
349 |         JSON-serializable comparison report dict.
350 |     """
351 |     # Build learned metrics using CommitTaskRecord objects for the test set
352 |     test_records: list[CommitTaskRecord] = []
353 |     for pred, true_label, idx in zip(
354 |         learned_result.test_predictions,
355 |         learned_result.test_true_labels,
356 |         learned_result.test_indices,
357 |     ):
358 |         rec = CommitTaskRecord(
359 |             commit_hash=records[idx].commit_hash if idx < len(records) else f"test_{idx}",
360 |             message=records[idx].message if idx < len(records) else "",
361 |             filepath="",
362 |             changed_symbol_count=0,
363 |             ripple_candidate_count=0,
364 |             ripple_relation_types=[],
365 |             changed_chunk_count=0,
366 |             diff_additions=0,
367 |             diff_deletions=0,
368 |             true_category=true_label,
369 |             predicted_category=pred,
370 |         )
371 |         test_records.append(rec)
372 | 
373 |     learned_metrics = compute_classification_metrics(test_records)
374 | 
375 |     # Delta calculations
376 |     delta_accuracy = round(
377 |         learned_metrics["accuracy"] - keyword_metrics["accuracy"], 4
378 |     )
379 |     delta_macro_f1 = round(
380 |         learned_metrics["macro"]["f1"] - keyword_metrics["macro"]["f1"], 4
381 |     )
382 |     delta_weighted_f1 = round(
383 |         learned_metrics["weighted"]["f1"] - keyword_metrics["weighted"]["f1"], 4
384 |     )
385 | 
386 |     report: LearnedEvaluationReport = {
387 |         "schema_version": "nanochat.commit_to_task_learned_eval.v1",
388 |         "dataset": {
389 |             "total_commits": len(records),
390 |             "train_size": learned_result.n_train,
391 |             "test_size": learned_result.n_test,
392 |         },
393 |         "keyword_baseline": keyword_metrics,
394 |         "learned_classifier": {
395 |             "method": "tfidf_logistic_regression",
396 |             "train_accuracy": learned_result.train_accuracy,
397 |             "test_metrics": learned_metrics,
398 |             "n_features": len(learned_result.feature_names),
399 |             "top_features_per_class": learned_result.top_features_per_class,
400 |         },
401 |         "comparison": {
402 |             "delta_accuracy": delta_accuracy,
403 |             "delta_macro_f1": delta_macro_f1,
404 |             "delta_weighted_f1": delta_weighted_f1,
405 |             "learned_wins": delta_macro_f1 > 0,
406 |             "summary": (
407 |                 f"Learned classifier {'outperforms' if delta_macro_f1 > 0 else 'underperforms vs'} "
408 |                 f"keyword baseline by {abs(delta_macro_f1):.4f} macro F1"
409 |             ),
410 |         },
411 |     }
412 | 
413 |     return report
414 | 
415 | 
416 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
417 | # Full evaluation pipeline
418 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
419 | 
420 | 
421 | def run_learned_evaluation(
422 |     records: list[CommitTaskRecord],
423 |     test_size: float = 0.2,
424 |     max_features: int = 5000,
425 |     ngram_range: tuple[int, int] = (1, 2),
426 |     random_state: int = 42,
427 | ) -> LearnedEvaluationReport:
428 |     """Run the full learned commit-to-task evaluation pipeline.
429 | 
430 |     Steps:
431 |       1. Extract messages and labels from records
432 |       2. Train TF-IDF + Logistic Regression on train split
433 |       3. Evaluate on test split
434 |       4. Run keyword baseline on the same test split
435 |       5. Build comparison report
436 | 
437 |     Returns a JSON-serializable report dict.
438 |     """
439 |     _require_sklearn()
440 | 
441 |     messages = [r.message for r in records]
442 |     labels = [r.true_category for r in records]
443 | 
444 |     # Step 1: Train learned classifier
445 |     learned_result = train_logistic_regression(
446 |         messages=messages,
447 |         labels=labels,
448 |         test_size=test_size,
449 |         max_features=max_features,
450 |         ngram_range=ngram_range,
451 |         random_state=random_state,
452 |     )
453 | 
454 |     # Step 2: Run keyword baseline on the SAME test split
455 |     keyword_test_records: list[CommitTaskRecord] = []
456 |     for idx in learned_result.test_indices:
457 |         rec = CommitTaskRecord(
458 |             commit_hash=records[idx].commit_hash,
459 |             message=records[idx].message,
460 |             filepath=records[idx].filepath,
461 |             changed_symbol_count=records[idx].changed_symbol_count,
462 |             ripple_candidate_count=records[idx].ripple_candidate_count,
463 |             ripple_relation_types=records[idx].ripple_relation_types,
464 |             changed_chunk_count=records[idx].changed_chunk_count,
465 |             diff_additions=records[idx].diff_additions,
466 |             diff_deletions=records[idx].diff_deletions,
467 |             true_category=records[idx].true_category,
468 |         )
469 |         keyword_test_records.append(rec)
470 | 
471 |     run_keyword_classifier(keyword_test_records)
472 |     keyword_metrics = compute_classification_metrics(keyword_test_records)
473 | 
474 |     # Step 3: Build comparison report
475 |     return build_comparison_report(records, learned_result, keyword_metrics)
476 | 
477 | 
478 | def save_report(report: LearnedEvaluationReport, output_path: str) -> None:
479 |     with open(output_path, "w") as f:
480 |         json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
481 |

 15 | /// Outputs gzip-compressed JSONL per input file.

 18 |     /// Input directory containing *_commits.jsonl files
 19 |     #[arg(short, long)]

 46 | // ── Parsed document ────────────────────────────────────────────────
 47 | struct ParsedDoc {
 48 |     repo: String,
 49 |     file_path: String,
 50 |     commit_msg: String,
 51 |     commit_hash: String,
 52 |     post_code: String,
 53 |     diff_text: String,

 62 | // ── ASCII conversion ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 63 | fn to_ascii(s: &str) -> String {
 64 |     let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
 65 |     for ch in s.chars() {
 66 |         match ch {
 67 |             '\u{2018}' | '\u{2019}' | '\u{201A}' | '\u{2039}' | '\u{203A}' => out.push('\''),
 68 |             '\u{201C}' | '\u{201D}' | '\u{201E}' | '\u{00AB}' | '\u{00BB}' => out.push('"'),
 69 |             '\u{2013}' | '\u{2014}' => out.push('-'),
 70 |             '\u{2026}' => out.push_str("..."),
 71 |             '\u{2022}' => out.push('*'),
 72 |             '\u{00A0}' => out.push(' '),
 73 |             '\u{00B7}' => out.push('.'),
 74 |             '\u{00D7}' => out.push('x'),
 75 |             '\u{2192}' => out.push_str("->"),
 76 |             '\u{2190}' => out.push_str("<-"),
 77 |             '\u{2264}' => out.push_str("<="),
 78 |             '\u{2265}' => out.push_str(">="),
 79 |             '\u{2260}' => out.push_str("!="),
 80 |             c if c.is_ascii() => out.push(c),
 81 |             _ => {} // drop non-ASCII
 82 |         }
 83 |     }
 84 |     out
 85 | }
 86 | 
 87 | // ── Parse a document ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 88 | fn parse_doc(text: &str) -> Option<ParsedDoc> {
 89 |     let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
 90 |     if lines.len() < 5 {
 91 |         return None;
 92 |     }
 93 | 
 94 |     // Find the `// Applied fix:` marker (search from end for efficiency)
 95 |     let mut applied_idx = None;
 96 |     for (i, line) in lines.iter().enumerate().rev() {
 97 |         let trimmed = line.trim();
 98 |         if trimmed.starts_with("// Applied fix")
 99 |             || trimmed.starts_with("// Applied change")
100 |             || trimmed.starts_with("// Applied patch")
101 |         {
102 |             applied_idx = Some(i);
103 |             break;
104 |         }
105 |     }
106 |     let applied_idx = applied_idx?;
107 | 
108 |     // Find end of doxygen header (first line containing `*/`)
109 |     let mut header_end = 0;
110 |     for (i, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
111 |         if line.contains("*/") {
112 |             header_end = i;
113 |             break;
114 |         }
115 |     }
116 | 
117 |     // Extract metadata from header
118 |     let mut repo = String::new();
119 |     let mut file_path = String::new();
120 |     let mut commit_msg = String::new();
121 |     let mut commit_hash = String::new();
122 |     let scan_end = if header_end > 0 {
123 |         header_end + 1
124 |     } else {
125 |         lines.len().min(20)
126 |     };
127 |     for line in &lines[..scan_end] {
128 |         let trimmed = line.trim().trim_start_matches('*').trim();
129 |         if let Some(r) = trimmed.strip_prefix("Repository:") {
130 |             repo = r.trim().to_string();
131 |         } else if let Some(f) = trimmed.strip_prefix("File:") {
132 |             file_path = f.trim().to_string();
133 |         } else if let Some(m) = trimmed.strip_prefix("Commit:") {
134 |             commit_msg = m.trim().to_string();
135 |         } else if let Some(h) = trimmed.strip_prefix("@commit") {
136 |             commit_hash = h.trim().to_string();
137 |         }
138 |     }
139 | 
140 |     // Code is between header end and Applied marker
141 |     let code_start = if header_end > 0 { header_end + 1 } else { 0 };
142 |     if code_start >= applied_idx {
143 |         return None;
144 |     }
145 | 
146 |     let post_code = lines[code_start..applied_idx].join("\n");
147 |     let post_code = post_code.trim().to_string();
148 |     if post_code.is_empty() {
149 |         return None;
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     // Diff is everything after Applied marker
153 |     let diff_text = if applied_idx + 1 < lines.len() {
154 |         lines[applied_idx + 1..].join("\n").trim().to_string()
155 |     } else {
156 |         String::new()
157 |     };
158 | 
159 |     Some(ParsedDoc {
160 |         repo: to_ascii(&repo),
161 |         file_path: to_ascii(&file_path),
162 |         commit_msg: to_ascii(&commit_msg),
163 |         commit_hash,
164 |         post_code: to_ascii(&post_code),
165 |         diff_text: to_ascii(&diff_text),
166 |     })
167 | }
168 | 
169 | // ── Parse unified diff hunk header ────────────────────────────────
170 | fn parse_hunk_header_both(line: &str) -> Option<(usize, usize, usize, usize)> {
171 |     let rest = line.strip_prefix("@@ -")?;
172 |     let plus_idx = rest.find('+')?;
173 |     let old_part = rest[..plus_idx].trim().trim_end_matches(' ');
174 |     let after_plus = &rest[plus_idx + 1..];
175 |     let at_idx = after_plus.find("@@")?;
176 |     let new_part = after_plus[..at_idx].trim();
177 | 
178 |     let parse_sc = |s: &str| -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
179 |         if let Some(comma) = s.find(',') {
180 |             Some((s[..comma].parse().ok()?, s[comma + 1..].parse().ok()?))
181 |         } else {
182 |             Some((s.parse().ok()?, 1))
183 |         }
184 |     };
185 | 
186 |     let (os, oc) = parse_sc(old_part)?;
187 |     let (ns, nc) = parse_sc(new_part)?;
188 |     Some((os, oc, ns, nc))
189 | }
190 | 
191 | // ── Reverse-apply diff to reconstruct pre-commit code ──────────────
192 | fn reverse_apply_diff(post_code: &str, diff_text: &str) -> Option<String> {
193 |     if diff_text.is_empty() {
194 |         return None;
195 |     }
196 | 
197 |     let post_lines: Vec<&str> = post_code.lines().collect();
198 |     let diff_lines: Vec<&str> = diff_text.lines().collect();
199 |     let mut result: Vec<String> = Vec::with_capacity(post_lines.len());
200 |     let mut pos: usize = 0; // 0-indexed into post_lines
201 |     let mut di = 0;
202 | 
203 |     while di < diff_lines.len() {
204 |         let dl = diff_lines[di];
205 |         if dl.starts_with("@@ ") {
206 |             if let Some((_, _, new_start, _)) = parse_hunk_header_both(dl) {
207 |                 let hunk_start_0 = if new_start > 0 { new_start - 1 } else { 0 };
208 | 
209 |                 // Copy lines before this hunk
210 |                 while pos < hunk_start_0 && pos < post_lines.len() {
211 |                     result.push(post_lines[pos].to_string());
212 |                     pos += 1;
213 |                 }
214 | 
215 |                 di += 1;
216 |                 while di < diff_lines.len()
217 |                     && !diff_lines[di].starts_with("@@ ")
218 |                     && !diff_lines[di].starts_with("diff --git")
219 |                 {
220 |                     let l = diff_lines[di];
221 |                     if let Some(s) = l.strip_prefix('-') {
222 |                         result.push(s.to_string());
223 |                     } else if l.starts_with('+') {
224 |                         pos += 1;
225 |                     } else if l.starts_with(' ') || l.is_empty() {
226 |                         if pos < post_lines.len() {
227 |                             result.push(post_lines[pos].to_string());
228 |                         }
229 |                         pos += 1;
230 |                     } else if l.starts_with('\\') {
231 |                         // "\ No newline at end of file" — skip
232 |                     } else {
233 |                         if pos < post_lines.len() {
234 |                             result.push(post_lines[pos].to_string());
235 |                         }
236 |                         pos += 1;
237 |                     }
238 |                     di += 1;
239 |                 }
240 |                 continue;
241 |             }
242 |         }
243 |         di += 1;
244 |     }
245 | 
246 |     // Copy remaining lines
247 |     while pos < post_lines.len() {
248 |         result.push(post_lines[pos].to_string());
249 |         pos += 1;
250 |     }
251 | 
252 |     if result.is_empty() || result == post_lines.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>() {
253 |         return None;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     Some(result.join("\n"))
257 | }
258 | 
259 | // ── Extract changed sections for v3 ───────────────────────────────
260 | fn extract_changes(diff_text: &str) -> Vec<ChangedSection> {
261 |     let mut sections = Vec::new();
262 |     let lines: Vec<&str> = diff_text.lines().collect();
263 |     let mut i = 0;
264 | 
265 |     while i < lines.len() {
266 |         if lines[i].starts_with("@@ ") {
267 |             i += 1;
268 |             let mut current_removed = Vec::new();
269 |             let mut current_added = Vec::new();
270 |             let mut in_change = false;
271 | 
272 |             while i < lines.len()
273 |                 && !lines[i].starts_with("@@ ")
274 |                 && !lines[i].starts_with("diff --git")
275 |             {
276 |                 let l = lines[i];
277 |                 if let Some(s) = l.strip_prefix('-') {
278 |                     if !in_change && (!current_removed.is_empty() || !current_added.is_empty()) {
279 |                         sections.push(ChangedSection {
280 |                             removed: std::mem::take(&mut current_removed),
281 |                             added: std::mem::take(&mut current_added),
282 |                         });
283 |                     }
284 |                     in_change = true;
285 |                     current_removed.push(s.to_string());
286 |                 } else if let Some(s) = l.strip_prefix('+') {
287 |                     in_change = true;
288 |                     current_added.push(s.to_string());
289 |                 } else {
290 |                     if in_change {
291 |                         if !current_removed.is_empty() || !current_added.is_empty() {
292 |                             sections.push(ChangedSection {
293 |                                 removed: std::mem::take(&mut current_removed),
294 |                                 added: std::mem::take(&mut current_added),
295 |                             });
296 |                         }
297 |                         in_change = false;
298 |                     }
299 |                 }
300 |                 i += 1;
301 |             }
302 |             if !current_removed.is_empty() || !current_added.is_empty() {
303 |                 sections.push(ChangedSection {
304 |                     removed: current_removed,
305 |                     added: current_added,
306 |                 });
307 |             }
308 |             continue;
309 |         }
310 |         i += 1;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     sections
314 | }
315 | 
316 | // ── Strip diff artifacts from code ─────────────────────────────────
317 | fn strip_diff_artifacts(code: &str) -> String {
318 |     let mut out = String::with_capacity(code.len());
319 |     for line in code.lines() {
320 |         if line.starts_with("--- a/")
321 |             || line.starts_with("+++ b/")
322 |             || line.starts_with("diff --git")
323 |             || line.starts_with("index ")
324 |         {
325 |             continue;
326 |         }
327 |         if line.starts_with("@@ ") && line.contains("@@") {
328 |             continue;
329 |         }
330 |         out.push_str(line);
331 |         out.push('\n');
332 |     }
333 |     out
334 | }
335 | 
336 | // ── Format Variant 2: Full file reconstruction ────────────────────
337 | fn format_v2_doxygen(doc: &ParsedDoc, pre_code: &str) -> String {
338 |     format!(
339 |         "/**\n * @file {file}\n * @brief Full file reconstruction showing before/after commit\n * @repository {repo}\n * @commit {msg}\n *\n * @section original Original Version (Pre-Commit)\n * Complete source file before the commit was applied.\n *\n * @section modified Modified Version (Post-Commit)\n * Complete source file after the commit was applied.\n */\n\n// === ORIGINAL VERSION (Pre-Commit) ===\n\n{pre}\n\n// === MODIFIED VERSION (Post-Commit) ===\n\n{post}\n",
340 |         file = doc.file_path,
341 |         repo = doc.repo,
342 |         msg = doc.commit_msg,
343 |         pre = strip_diff_artifacts(pre_code),
344 |         post = strip_diff_artifacts(&doc.post_code),
345 |     )
346 | }
347 | 
348 | fn format_v2_simple(doc: &ParsedDoc, pre_code: &str) -> String {
349 |     format!(
350 |         "// File: {file}\n// Repository: {repo}\n// Commit: {msg}\n//\n// Full file reconstruction showing before/after commit.\n\n// === ORIGINAL VERSION (Pre-Commit) ===\n\n{pre}\n\n// === MODIFIED VERSION (Post-Commit) ===\n\n{post}\n",
351 |         file = doc.file_path,
352 |         repo = doc.repo,
353 |         msg = doc.commit_msg,
354 |         pre = strip_diff_artifacts(pre_code),
355 |         post = strip_diff_artifacts(&doc.post_code),
356 |     )
357 | }
358 | 
359 | // ── Format Variant 3: Structured diff as C++ comments ─────────────
360 | fn format_v3_doxygen(doc: &ParsedDoc, changes: &[ChangedSection]) -> String {
361 |     let mut body = String::new();
362 |     body.push_str(&format!(
363 |         "/**\n * @file {file}\n * @brief Structured diff showing inline changes as C++ comments\n * @repository {repo}\n * @commit {msg}\n *\n * @section changes Changes Applied\n * Removed lines shown as comments, added lines as live code.\n */\n\n// === CHANGES ===\n\n",
364 |         file = doc.file_path,
365 |         repo = doc.repo,
366 |         msg = doc.commit_msg,
367 |     ));
368 | 
369 |     for (i, section) in changes.iter().enumerate() {
370 |         if i > 0 {
371 |             body.push_str("\n// ---\n\n");
372 |         }
373 |         for line in &section.removed {
374 |             body.push_str("// Removed: ");
375 |             body.push_str(line);
376 |             body.push('\n');
377 |         }
378 |         for line in &section.added {
379 |             body.push_str(line);
380 |             body.push('\n');
381 |         }
382 |     }
383 | 
384 |     body.push_str("\n// === FULL FILE (Post-Commit) ===\n\n");
385 |     body.push_str(&strip_diff_artifacts(&doc.post_code));
386 |     body.push('\n');
387 |     body
388 | }
389 | 
390 | fn format_v3_simple(doc: &ParsedDoc, changes: &[ChangedSection]) -> String {
391 |     let mut body = String::new();
392 |     body.push_str(&format!(
393 |         "// File: {file}\n// Repository: {repo}\n// Commit: {msg}\n//\n// Structured diff: removed lines as comments, added lines as live code.\n\n// === CHANGES ===\n\n",
394 |         file = doc.file_path,
395 |         repo = doc.repo,
396 |         msg = doc.commit_msg,
397 |     ));
398 | 
399 |     for (i, section) in changes.iter().enumerate() {
400 |         if i > 0 {
401 |             body.push_str("\n// ---\n\n");
402 |         }
403 |         for line in &section.removed {
404 |             body.push_str("// Removed: ");
405 |             body.push_str(line);
406 |             body.push('\n');
407 |         }
408 |         for line in &section.added {
409 |             body.push_str(line);
410 |             body.push('\n');
411 |         }
412 |     }
413 | 
414 |     body.push_str("\n// === FULL FILE (Post-Commit) ===\n\n");
415 |     body.push_str(&strip_diff_artifacts(&doc.post_code));
416 |     body.push('\n');
417 |     body
418 | }
419 | 
420 | // ── Transform result ──────────────────────────────────────────────
421 | struct TransformResult {
422 |     v2_doxygen: Option<String>,
423 |     v2_simple: Option<String>,
424 |     v3_doxygen: Option<String>,
425 |     v3_simple: Option<String>,
426 |     commit_hash: String,
427 | }
428 | 
429 | fn transform_doc(text: &str) -> Option<TransformResult> {
430 |     let doc = parse_doc(text)?;
431 | 
432 |     // v3: extract changes
433 |     let changes = if !doc.diff_text.is_empty() {
434 |         extract_changes(&doc.diff_text)
435 |     } else {
436 |         Vec::new()
437 |     };
438 | 
439 |     let v3_doxygen = if !changes.is_empty() {
440 |         Some(format_v3_doxygen(&doc, &changes))
441 |     } else {
442 |         None
443 |     };
444 |     let v3_simple = if !changes.is_empty() {
445 |         Some(format_v3_simple(&doc, &changes))
446 |     } else {
447 |         None
448 |     };
449 | 
450 |     // v2: reverse-apply diff
451 |     let pre_code = reverse_apply_diff(&doc.post_code, &doc.diff_text);
452 |     let v2_doxygen = pre_code.as_ref().map(|pre| format_v2_doxygen(&doc, pre));
453 |     let v2_simple = pre_code.as_ref().map(|pre| format_v2_simple(&doc, pre));
454 | 
455 |     if v2_doxygen.is_none() && v3_doxygen.is_none() {
456 |         return None;
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     Some(TransformResult {
460 |         v2_doxygen,
461 |         v2_simple,
462 |         v3_doxygen,
463 |         v3_simple,
464 |         commit_hash: doc.commit_hash,
465 |     })
466 | }
467 | 
468 | // ── JSON wrapper (manual for speed) ───────────────────────────────
469 | fn to_jsonl(text: &str, commit_hash: &str) -> String {
470 |     // Pre-calculate: {"text":"...","commit_hash":"..."}
471 |     let mut s = String::with_capacity(text.len() + commit_hash.len() + 40);
472 |     s.push_str("{\"text\":");
473 |     s.push('"');
474 |     for ch in text.chars() {
475 |         match ch {
476 |             '"' => s.push_str("\\\""),
477 |             '\\' => s.push_str("\\\\"),
478 |             '\n' => s.push_str("\\n"),
479 |             '\r' => s.push_str("\\r"),
480 |             '\t' => s.push_str("\\t"),
481 |             c if (c as u32) < 0x20 => {
482 |                 s.push_str(&format!("\\u{:04x}", c as u32));
483 |             }
484 |             c => s.push(c),
485 |         }
486 |     }
487 |     s.push('"');
488 |     if !commit_hash.is_empty() {
489 |         s.push_str(",\"commit_hash\":\"");
490 |         // commit_hash is a hex string, no escaping needed
491 |         s.push_str(commit_hash);
492 |         s.push('"');
493 |     }
494 |     s.push('}');
495 |     s
496 | }
497 | 
498 | // ── Batch result ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
499 | struct BatchResult {
500 |     v2d: Vec<String>,
501 |     v2s: Vec<String>,
502 |     v3d: Vec<String>,
503 |     v3s: Vec<String>,
504 |     written: u64,
505 |     skipped: u64,
506 |     parse_fail: u64,
507 | }
508 | 
509 | fn process_batch(lines: Vec<String>) -> BatchResult {
510 |     let mut r = BatchResult {
511 |         v2d: Vec::new(),
512 |         v2s: Vec::new(),
513 |         v3d: Vec::new(),
514 |         v3s: Vec::new(),
515 |         written: 0,
516 |         skipped: 0,
517 |         parse_fail: 0,
518 |     };
519 | 
520 |     for line in &lines {
521 |         if line.trim().is_empty() {
522 |             continue;
523 |         }
524 |         let (text, json_commit_hash) = match serde_json::from_str::<Value>(line) {
525 |             Ok(v) => match v.get("text").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) {
526 |                 Some(t) => {
527 |                     let ch = v
528 |                         .get("commit_hash")
529 |                         .and_then(|h| h.as_str())
530 |                         .unwrap_or("")
531 |                         .to_string();
532 |                     (t.to_string(), ch)
533 |                 }
534 |                 None => {
535 |                     r.parse_fail += 1;
536 |                     continue;
537 |                 }
538 |             },
539 |             Err(_) => {
540 |                 r.parse_fail += 1;
541 |                 continue;
542 |             }
543 |         };
544 | 
545 |         match transform_doc(&text) {
546 |             Some(result) => {
547 |                 // Prefer commit_hash from JSON field, fall back to header-parsed value
548 |                 let hash = if !json_commit_hash.is_empty() {
549 |                     &json_commit_hash
550 |                 } else {
551 |                     &result.commit_hash
552 |                 };
553 |                 if let Some(t) = result.v2_doxygen {
554 |                     r.v2d.push(to_jsonl(&t, hash));
555 |                 }
556 |                 if let Some(t) = result.v2_simple {
557 |                     r.v2s.push(to_jsonl(&t, hash));
558 |                 }
559 |                 if let Some(t) = result.v3_doxygen {
560 |                     r.v3d.push(to_jsonl(&t, hash));
561 |                 }
562 |                 if let Some(t) = result.v3_simple {
563 |                     r.v3s.push(to_jsonl(&t, hash));
564 |                 }
565 |                 r.written += 1;
566 |             }
567 |             None => {
568 |                 r.skipped += 1;
569 |             }
570 |         }
571 |     }
572 | 
573 |     r
574 | }
575 | 
576 | // ── Gzip writer helper ────────────────────────────────────────────
577 | type GzWriter = GzEncoder<BufWriter<File>>;
578 | 
579 | fn open_gz(path: &PathBuf) -> GzWriter {
580 |     let file = File::create(path).expect("Failed to create output file");
581 |     let buf = BufWriter::with_capacity(8 * 1024 * 1024, file);
582 |     GzEncoder::new(buf, Compression::fast()) // fast compression = level 1
583 | }
584 | 
585 | // ── Process one input file ─────────────────────────────────────────
586 | fn process_file(
587 |     input_path: &PathBuf,
588 |     output_dir: &PathBuf,
589 |     batch_size: usize,
590 |     file_idx: usize,
591 |     total_files: usize,
592 | ) {
593 |     let file_name = input_path.file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy();
594 |     let stem = input_path.file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();
595 |     let file_start = Instant::now();
596 | 
597 |     // Create per-file output files
598 |     let v2d_path = output_dir.join(format!("{}_v2_doxygen.jsonl.gz", stem));
599 |     let v2s_path = output_dir.join(format!("{}_v2_simple.jsonl.gz", stem));
600 |     let v3d_path = output_dir.join(format!("{}_v3_doxygen.jsonl.gz", stem));
601 |     let v3s_path = output_dir.join(format!("{}_v3_simple.jsonl.gz", stem));
602 | 
603 |     let mut w2d = open_gz(&v2d_path);
604 |     let mut w2s = open_gz(&v2s_path);
605 |     let mut w3d = open_gz(&v3d_path);
606 |     let mut w3s = open_gz(&v3s_path);
607 | 
608 |     // Stream batches from the file
609 |     let reader = BufReader::with_capacity(16 * 1024 * 1024, File::open(input_path).unwrap());
610 |     let mut current_batch: Vec<String> = Vec::with_capacity(batch_size);
611 |     let mut line_count: u64 = 0;
612 |     let mut file_written: u64 = 0;
613 |     let mut file_skipped: u64 = 0;
614 | 
615 |     let mut flush_batch = |batch: Vec<String>,
616 |                            w2d: &mut GzWriter,
617 |                            w2s: &mut GzWriter,
618 |                            w3d: &mut GzWriter,
619 |                            w3s: &mut GzWriter|
620 |      -> (u64, u64) {
621 |         if batch.is_empty() {
622 |             return (0, 0);
623 |         }
624 | 
625 |         // Split the batch into sub-batches for rayon
626 |         let sub_size = batch.len().max(1);
627 |         let sub_batches: Vec<Vec<String>> = batch
628 |             .chunks(sub_size.min(500))
629 |             .map(|c| c.to_vec())
630 |             .collect();
631 | 
632 |         let results: Vec<BatchResult> = sub_batches
633 |             .into_par_iter()
634 |             .map(|b| process_batch(b))
635 |             .collect();
636 | 
637 |         let mut written = 0u64;
638 |         let mut skipped = 0u64;
639 | 
640 |         for r in results {
641 |             for line in &r.v2d {
642 |                 w2d.write_all(line.as_bytes()).unwrap();
643 |                 w2d.write_all(b"\n").unwrap();
644 |             }
645 |             for line in &r.v2s {
646 |                 w2s.write_all(line.as_bytes()).unwrap();
647 |                 w2s.write_all(b"\n").unwrap();
648 |             }
649 |             for line in &r.v3d {
650 |                 w3d.write_all(line.as_bytes()).unwrap();
651 |                 w3d.write_all(b"\n").unwrap();
652 |             }
653 |             for line in &r.v3s {
654 |                 w3s.write_all(line.as_bytes()).unwrap();
655 |                 w3s.write_all(b"\n").unwrap();
656 |             }
657 |             written += r.written;
658 |             skipped += r.skipped;
659 |             DOCS_WRITTEN.fetch_add(r.written, Ordering::Relaxed);
660 |             DOCS_SKIPPED.fetch_add(r.skipped, Ordering::Relaxed);
661 |             PARSE_FAILURES.fetch_add(r.parse_fail, Ordering::Relaxed);
662 |         }
663 | 
664 |         (written, skipped)
665 |     };
666 | 
667 |     for line_result in reader.lines() {
668 |         match line_result {
669 |             Ok(line) => {
670 |                 if !line.trim().is_empty() {
671 |                     current_batch.push(line);
672 |                     line_count += 1;
673 |                     if current_batch.len() >= batch_size {
674 |                         let batch =
675 |                             std::mem::replace(&mut current_batch, Vec::with_capacity(batch_size));
676 |                         let (w, s) = flush_batch(batch, &mut w2d, &mut w2s, &mut w3d, &mut w3s);
677 |                         file_written += w;
678 |                         file_skipped += s;
679 |                     }
680 |                 }
681 |             }
682 |             Err(_) => continue,
683 |         }
684 |     }
685 | 
686 |     // Flush remaining
687 |     if !current_batch.is_empty() {
688 |         let (w, s) = flush_batch(current_batch, &mut w2d, &mut w2s, &mut w3d, &mut w3s);
689 |         file_written += w;
690 |         file_skipped += s;
691 |     }
692 | 
693 |     DOCS_READ.fetch_add(line_count, Ordering::Relaxed);
694 | 
695 |     // Finalize gzip streams
696 |     w2d.finish().unwrap();
697 |     w2s.finish().unwrap();
698 |     w3d.finish().unwrap();
699 |     w3s.finish().unwrap();
700 | 
701 |     let done = FILES_DONE.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed) + 1;
702 |     let elapsed = file_start.elapsed();
703 |     let total_written = DOCS_WRITTEN.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
704 |     let total_read = DOCS_READ.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
705 | 
706 |     eprintln!(
707 |         "[{}/{}] {} - {} lines, {} written, {} skipped ({:.1}s) | Total: {}/{} written",
708 |         done,
709 |         total_files,
710 |         file_name,
711 |         line_count,
712 |         file_written,
713 |         file_skipped,
714 |         elapsed.as_secs_f64(),
715 |         total_written,
716 |         total_read,
717 |     );
718 | }
719 | 
720 | fn main() {
721 |     let args = Args::parse();
722 | 
723 |     if let Some(t) = args.threads {
724 |         rayon::ThreadPoolBuilder::new()
725 |             .num_threads(t)
726 |             .build_global()
727 |             .unwrap();
728 |     }
729 | 
730 |     // Find all JSONL files
731 |     let mut input_files: Vec<PathBuf> = Vec::new();
732 |     for entry in WalkDir::new(&args.input_dir).max_depth(1) {
733 |         if let Ok(e) = entry {
734 |             let p = e.path();
735 |             if p.is_file() && p.extension().map(|x| x == "jsonl").unwrap_or(false) {
736 |                 input_files.push(p.to_path_buf());
737 |             }
738 |         }
739 |     }
740 |     input_files.sort();
741 | 
742 |     let total_files = input_files.len();
743 |     eprintln!("Found {} input files", total_files);
744 |     eprintln!(
745 |         "Threads: {}, batch_size: {}, parallel_files: {}",
746 |         args.threads.unwrap_or_else(|| rayon::current_num_threads()),
747 |         args.batch_size,
748 |         args.parallel_files,
749 |     );
750 | 
751 |     fs::create_dir_all(&args.output_dir).expect("Failed to create output dir");
752 | 
753 |     let start = Instant::now();
754 | 
755 |     // Process files in parallel using a dedicated thread pool
756 |     // (separate from rayon's global pool used within each file for batch processing)
757 |     let output_dir = &args.output_dir;
758 |     let batch_size = args.batch_size;
759 |     let parallel = args.parallel_files.min(total_files);
760 | 
761 |     // Use crossbeam scoped threads with a channel-based semaphore
762 |     let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel::<()>(parallel);
763 |     // Pre-fill with permits
764 |     for _ in 0..parallel {
765 |         tx.send(()).unwrap();
766 |     }
767 | 
768 |     std::thread::scope(|s| {
769 |         for (idx, input_path) in input_files.iter().enumerate() {
770 |             // Wait for a permit
771 |             rx.recv().unwrap();
772 |             let tx = tx.clone();
773 |             let output_dir_clone = output_dir.clone();
774 |             s.spawn(move || {
775 |                 process_file(input_path, &output_dir_clone, batch_size, idx, total_files);
776 |                 // Release permit
777 |                 let _ = tx.send(());
778 |             });
779 |         }
780 |     });
781 | 
782 |     let elapsed = start.elapsed();
783 |     eprintln!("\n=== DONE ({:.1}s) ===", elapsed.as_secs_f64());
784 |     eprintln!("Total docs read:    {}", DOCS_READ.load(Ordering::Relaxed));
785 |     eprintln!(
786 |         "Total docs written: {}",
787 |         DOCS_WRITTEN.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
788 |     );
789 |     eprintln!(
790 |         "Total docs skipped: {}",
791 |         DOCS_SKIPPED.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
792 |     );
793 |     eprintln!(
794 |         "Parse failures:     {}",
795 |         PARSE_FAILURES.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
796 |     );
797 |     eprintln!(
798 |         "Rate: {:.0} docs/sec",
799 |         DOCS_READ.load(Ordering::Relaxed) as f64 / elapsed.as_secs_f64()
800 |     );
801 | }
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/nanochat/scripts/data/clone_cpp_repos.sh

- Extension: .sh
- Language: bash
- Size: 3477 bytes
- Created: 2026-04-02 22:45:34
- Modified: 2026-04-02 22:45:34

### Code

```bash
  1 | #!/bin/bash
  2 | # Clone C++ repositories to /mnt/data20t/cpp_all/
  3 | # Run on build3: bash scripts/data/clone_cpp_repos.sh
  4 | #
  5 | # Generated 2026-03-05 from 8 parallel search agents.
  6 | # 59 new repos (not in existing cpp_raw/).
  7 | 
  8 | set -e
  9 | TARGET="/mnt/data20t/cpp_all"
 10 | mkdir -p "$TARGET"
 11 | cd "$TARGET"
 12 | 
 13 | clone() {
 14 |     local org="$1" repo="$2"
 15 |     local name=$(echo "$repo" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
 16 |     if [ -d "$name" ]; then
 17 |         echo "SKIP (exists): $name"
 18 |         return
 19 |     fi
 20 |     echo "CLONING: $org/$repo -> $name"
 21 |     git clone --depth=1 "https://github.com/$org/$repo.git" "$name" 2>&1 | tail -1 || echo "FAILED: $org/$repo"
 22 | }
 23 | 
 24 | clone_full() {
 25 |     local org="$1" repo="$2"
 26 |     local name=$(echo "$repo" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')
 27 |     if [ -d "$name" ]; then
 28 |         echo "SKIP (exists): $name"
 29 |         return
 30 |     fi
 31 |     echo "CLONING (full history): $org/$repo -> $name"
 32 |     git clone "https://github.com/$org/$repo.git" "$name" 2>&1 | tail -1 || echo "FAILED: $org/$repo"
 33 | }

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/nanochat/scripts/data/download_all_cpp_build3.sh

- Extension: .sh
- Language: bash
- Size: 10560 bytes
- Created: 2026-04-02 22:45:34
- Modified: 2026-04-02 22:45:34

### Code

```bash
  1 | #!/bin/bash
  2 | # Combined download of all C++ repos for clang-based indexing on build3
  3 | # Runs clones in parallel batches to maximize bandwidth
  4 | set -euo pipefail
  5 | 
  6 | DATA_DIR="${1:-/home/dave/data/cpp_raw}"
  7 | mkdir -p "$DATA_DIR"
  8 | cd "$DATA_DIR"
  9 | 
 10 | MAX_PARALLEL=8  # parallel git clones
 11 | 
 12 | clone_bg() {
 13 |     local dir="$1" url="$2"
 14 |     shift 2
 15 |     if [ -d "$dir" ]; then
 16 |         echo "SKIP: $dir exists"
 17 |         return 0
 18 |     fi
 19 |     echo "START: $dir"
 20 |     git clone "$@" "$url" "$dir" 2>&1 | tail -1 && echo "DONE: $dir" || echo "FAIL: $dir"
 21 | }
 22 | 
 23 | wait_for_slots() {
 24 |     while [ "$(jobs -r | wc -l)" -ge "$MAX_PARALLEL" ]; do
 25 |         sleep 2
 26 |     done
 27 | }
 28 | 
 29 | echo "=== Downloading all C++ repos to $DATA_DIR ==="
 30 | echo "Max parallel: $MAX_PARALLEL"
 31 | echo "Start time: $(date)"
 32 | 
 33 | # === V1: Core repos ===
 34 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg llvm-project   https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project.git     --depth=1 &
 35 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg linux          https://github.com/torvalds/linux.git         --depth=1 &
 36 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg boost          https://github.com/boostorg/boost.git         --depth=1 --recurse-submodules --shallow-submodules &
 37 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg fmt            https://github.com/fmtlib/fmt.git             --depth=1 &
 38 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg googletest     https://github.com/google/googletest.git      --depth=1 &
 39 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg abseil-cpp     https://github.com/abseil/abseil-cpp.git      --depth=1 &
 40 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg folly          https://github.com/facebook/folly.git         --depth=1 &
 41 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg grpc           https://github.com/grpc/grpc.git              --depth=1 &
 42 | 
 43 | # === V2: Frameworks and ML ===
 44 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg qtbase         https://github.com/qt/qtbase.git              --depth=1 &
 45 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg cuda-samples   https://github.com/NVIDIA/cuda-samples.git    --depth=1 &
 46 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg opencv         https://github.com/opencv/opencv.git          --depth=1 &
 47 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg eigen          https://gitlab.com/libeigen/eigen.git         --depth=1 &
 48 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg json           https://github.com/nlohmann/json.git          --depth=1 &

 50 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg protobuf       https://github.com/protocolbuffers/protobuf.git --depth=1 &
 51 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg CMake          https://github.com/Kitware/CMake.git          --depth=1 &
 52 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg Catch2         https://github.com/catchorg/Catch2.git        --depth=1 &
 53 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg poco           https://github.com/pocoproject/poco.git       --depth=1 &
 54 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg bitcoin        https://github.com/bitcoin/bitcoin.git        --depth=1 &
 55 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg tensorflow     https://github.com/tensorflow/tensorflow.git  --depth=1 &
 56 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg pytorch        https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch.git        --depth=1 &
 57 | 
 58 | # === V3: Compilers, databases, graphics ===
 59 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg gcc-mirror     https://github.com/gcc-mirror/gcc.git         --depth=1 &
 60 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg godot          https://github.com/godotengine/godot.git      --depth=1 &
 61 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg filament       https://github.com/google/filament.git        --depth=1 &
 62 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg imgui          https://github.com/ocornut/imgui.git          --depth=1 &
 63 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg SDL            https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL.git         --depth=1 &
 64 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg vulkan-hpp     https://github.com/KhronosGroup/Vulkan-Hpp.git --depth=1 &
 65 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg rocksdb        https://github.com/facebook/rocksdb.git       --depth=1 &

 68 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg clickhouse     https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse.git  --depth=1 &
 69 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg mariadb        https://github.com/MariaDB/server.git         --depth=1 &
 70 | wait_for_slots; clone_bg mysql          https://github.com/mysql/mysql-server.git     --depth=1 &

```python
  1 | """
  2 | Download and format competitive programming C++ datasets for nanochat training.
  3 | 
  4 | Downloads accepted C++ solutions from:
  5 |   - deepmind/code_contests  (10K problems, C++/Python/Java, Codeforces/AtCoder/etc.)
  6 |   - greengerong/leetcode    (1K LeetCode problems, function-level C++)
  7 | 
  8 | Formats them as JSONL for nanochat training, compatible with the existing
  9 | nanochat tokenizer and dataloader (text field, BOS-prefixed).
 10 | 
 11 | Usage:
 12 |   python3 -m scripts.data.download_competitive_cpp --output /path/to/output/
 13 |   python3 -m scripts.data.download_competitive_cpp --output /tmp/comp_cpp \
 14 |       --dataset code_contests --max-problems 5000
 15 | 
 16 | Output: JSONL files with {"text": "<code>"} per line.
 17 | """
 18 | 
 19 | import argparse
 20 | import json
 21 | import os
 22 | import sys
 23 | from typing import Mapping
 24 | 
 25 | 
 26 | JsonValue = str | int | float | bool | None | list["JsonValue"] | dict[str, "JsonValue"]
 27 | 
 28 | 
 29 | def download_code_contests(output_dir: str, max_problems: int | None = None,
 30 |                            min_solution_len: int = 50, max_solution_len: int = 8192) -> int:
 31 |     """
 32 |     Download deepmind/code_contests and extract accepted C++ solutions.
 33 | 
 34 |     Each problem can have many solutions; we extract the C++ ones.
 35 |     Each solution becomes one training document (full program including main()).
 36 | 
 37 |     Returns: number of documents written.
 38 |     """
 39 |     try:
 40 |         from datasets import load_dataset
 41 |     except ImportError:
 42 |         print('ERROR: pip install datasets')
 43 |         sys.exit(1)
 44 | 
 45 |     print('Loading deepmind/code_contests (this may take a while)...')
 46 |     ds = load_dataset('deepmind/code_contests', split='train', streaming=True)
 47 | 
 48 |     output_path = os.path.join(output_dir, 'code_contests_cpp.jsonl')
 49 |     count = 0
 50 |     problems_seen = 0
 51 | 
 52 |     with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
 53 |         for prob in ds:
 54 |             if not isinstance(prob, Mapping):
 55 |                 continue
 56 |             if max_problems and problems_seen >= max_problems:
 57 |                 break
 58 |             problems_seen += 1
 59 | 
 60 |             # Extract C++ solutions
 61 |             raw_solutions = prob.get('solutions', {})
 62 |             solutions = raw_solutions if isinstance(raw_solutions, Mapping) else {}
 63 |             # solutions is a dict with 'language' and 'solution' keys (both lists)
 64 |             raw_languages = solutions.get('language', [])
 65 |             raw_solution_texts = solutions.get('solution', [])
 66 |             languages = raw_languages if isinstance(raw_languages, list) else []
 67 |             solution_texts = raw_solution_texts if isinstance(raw_solution_texts, list) else []
 68 | 
 69 |             for lang, sol in zip(languages, solution_texts):
 70 |                 # Language codes in deepmind/code_contests:
 71 |                 #   1 = PYTHON (Python2/3), 2 = CPP (C++/C++14/C++17/C++20), 3 = JAVA
 72 |                 # NOTE: 1 is NOT C++ — it's Python! C++ is 2.
 73 |                 is_cpp = (lang == 2 or (isinstance(lang, str) and 'cpp' in lang.lower()))
 74 |                 if not is_cpp:
 75 |                     continue
 76 | 
 77 |                 if not isinstance(sol, str):
 78 |                     continue
 79 | 
 80 |                 sol = sol.strip()
 81 |                 if len(sol) < min_solution_len or len(sol) > max_solution_len:
 82 |                     continue
 83 | 
 84 |                 # Skip solutions with non-C++ patterns
 85 |                 if 'def ' in sol and 'int main' not in sol:
 86 |                     continue  # Python
 87 | 
 88 |                 # Write as training document
 89 |                 doc = {'text': sol + '\n'}
 90 |                 f.write(json.dumps(doc) + '\n')
 91 |                 count += 1
 92 | 
 93 |             if problems_seen % 500 == 0:
 94 |                 print(f'  {problems_seen} problems, {count} C++ solutions so far...')
 95 | 
 96 |     print(f'code_contests: {problems_seen} problems → {count} C++ solutions → {output_path}')
 97 |     return count
 98 | 
 99 | 
100 | def _strip_markdown_code_block(code: str) -> str:
101 |     """Strip markdown ```cpp ... ``` fences from code if present."""
102 |     import re
103 |     code = code.strip()
104 |     # Remove entire code block fence: ```lang\n...\n``` (with optional trailing whitespace)
105 |     m = re.match(r'^```[a-z+]*\n([\s\S]*?)```\s*$', code)
106 |     if m:
107 |         return m.group(1).strip()
108 |     # Fallback: remove opening fence only
109 |     for fence in ('```cpp\n', '```c++\n', '```\n'):
110 |         if code.startswith(fence):
111 |             code = code[len(fence):]
112 |             break
113 |     # Remove any trailing ``` with optional whitespace
114 |     code = re.sub(r'\n```\s*$', '', code)
115 |     return code.strip()
116 | 
117 | 
118 | def download_leetcode(output_dir: str, min_len: int = 30, max_len: int = 4096) -> int:
119 |     """
120 |     Download greengerong/leetcode and extract C++ solutions.
121 | 
122 |     LeetCode problems are function-level (class Solution { type solve(...) }),
123 |     directly relevant to HumanEval-style evaluation. Solutions are wrapped in
124 |     markdown code blocks which we strip.
125 | 
126 |     Returns: number of documents written.
127 |     """
128 |     try:
129 |         from datasets import load_dataset
130 |     except ImportError:
131 |         print('ERROR: pip install datasets')
132 |         sys.exit(1)
133 | 
134 |     print('Loading greengerong/leetcode...')
135 |     try:
136 |         ds = load_dataset('greengerong/leetcode', split='train')
137 |     except Exception as e:
138 |         print(f'ERROR loading greengerong/leetcode: {e}')
139 |         return 0
140 | 
141 |     output_path = os.path.join(output_dir, 'leetcode_cpp.jsonl')
142 |     count = 0
143 | 
144 |     with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
145 |         for item in ds:
146 |             if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
147 |                 continue
148 |             # Field is 'c++' in this dataset
149 |             cpp_sol = item.get('c++', '') or item.get('cpp', '') or ''
150 |             if not cpp_sol or not isinstance(cpp_sol, str):
151 |                 continue
152 | 
153 |             cpp_sol = _strip_markdown_code_block(cpp_sol)
154 |             if len(cpp_sol) < min_len or len(cpp_sol) > max_len:
155 |                 continue
156 | 
157 |             # Skip Python masquerading as C++
158 |             if 'def ' in cpp_sol and 'class Solution' not in cpp_sol:
159 |                 continue
160 | 
161 |             # Optionally prepend the problem description as a comment
162 |             title = item.get('title', '')
163 |             content = item.get('content', '')
164 |             if title:
165 |                 header = f'// {title}\n'
166 |                 if content and len(content) < 500:
167 |                     # Short description as comment
168 |                     desc_lines = content[:400].replace('\n', '\n// ')
169 |                     header += f'// {desc_lines}\n'
170 |                 doc_text = header + cpp_sol + '\n'
171 |             else:
172 |                 doc_text = cpp_sol + '\n'
173 | 
174 |             doc = {'text': doc_text}
175 |             f.write(json.dumps(doc) + '\n')
176 |             count += 1
177 | 
178 |     print(f'leetcode: {count} C++ solutions → {output_path}')
179 |     return count
180 | 
181 | 
182 | def convert_to_parquet(jsonl_path: str, output_dir: str, shard_size: int = 50_000) -> None:
183 |     """Convert JSONL to shuffled parquet shards (compatible with nanochat dataloader)."""
184 |     try:
185 |         import pyarrow as pa
186 |         import pyarrow.parquet as pq
187 |         import random
188 |     except ImportError:
189 |         print('Skipping parquet conversion: pip install pyarrow')
190 |         return
191 | 
192 |     print(f'Converting {jsonl_path} to parquet shards...')
193 |     with open(jsonl_path) as f:
194 |         docs = [json.loads(line)['text'] for line in f]
195 | 
196 |     random.shuffle(docs)
197 | 
198 |     # Validation split: last 2%
199 |     n_val = max(1, len(docs) // 50)
200 |     val_docs = docs[-n_val:]
201 |     train_docs = docs[:-n_val]
202 | 
203 |     def write_shards(docs_list, split):
204 |         shard_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, split)
205 |         os.makedirs(shard_dir, exist_ok=True)
206 |         for i in range(0, len(docs_list), shard_size):
207 |             batch = docs_list[i:i + shard_size]
208 |             table = pa.table({'text': batch})
209 |             out = os.path.join(shard_dir, f'shard_{i // shard_size:04d}.parquet')
210 |             pq.write_table(table, out)
211 | 
212 |     write_shards(train_docs, 'train')
213 |     write_shards(val_docs, 'val')
214 | 
215 |     # Write _COMPLETE sentinel
216 |     open(os.path.join(output_dir, '_COMPLETE'), 'w').close()
217 | 
218 |     total = len(docs)
219 |     print(f'  Wrote {len(train_docs)} train + {n_val} val docs ({total} total) to {output_dir}')
220 | 
221 | 
222 | def main():
223 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Download competitive programming C++ datasets')
224 |     parser.add_argument('--output', required=True,
225 |                         help='Output directory for JSONL/parquet files')
226 |     parser.add_argument('--dataset', choices=['code_contests', 'leetcode', 'all'],
227 |                         default='all', help='Which dataset(s) to download')
228 |     parser.add_argument('--max-problems', type=int, default=None,
229 |                         help='Max problems to process (for testing)')
230 |     parser.add_argument('--to-parquet', action='store_true', default=True,
231 |                         help='Also convert to parquet shards (default: True)')
232 |     parser.add_argument('--no-parquet', dest='to_parquet', action='store_false',
233 |                         help='Skip parquet conversion')
234 |     args = parser.parse_args()
235 | 
236 |     os.makedirs(args.output, exist_ok=True)
237 | 
238 |     total = 0
239 | 
240 |     if args.dataset in ('code_contests', 'all'):
241 |         n = download_code_contests(
242 |             args.output,
243 |             max_problems=args.max_problems,
244 |         )
245 |         total += n
246 |         if n > 0 and args.to_parquet:
247 |             convert_to_parquet(
248 |                 os.path.join(args.output, 'code_contests_cpp.jsonl'),
249 |                 os.path.join(args.output, 'code_contests_cpp_parquet'),
250 |             )
251 | 
252 |     if args.dataset in ('leetcode', 'all'):
253 |         n = download_leetcode(args.output)
254 |         total += n
255 |         if n > 0 and args.to_parquet:
256 |             convert_to_parquet(
257 |                 os.path.join(args.output, 'leetcode_cpp.jsonl'),
258 |                 os.path.join(args.output, 'leetcode_cpp_parquet'),
259 |             )
260 | 
261 |     print()
262 |     print(f'Total: {total} C++ solutions downloaded to {args.output}')
263 |     print()
264 |     print('Next steps:')
265 |     print('  # Upload to GCS:')
266 |     print(f'  gcloud storage cp -r {args.output}/*_parquet gs://nanochat-training-data-2026/data/')
267 |     print()
268 |     print('  # Train with competitive programming data mixed in:')

270 |     print('    --data_dir=/path/to/data/treesitter_compilable_16k \\')
271 |     print('    --extra_data_dirs=/path/to/output/code_contests_cpp_parquet \\')
272 |     print('    --streaming_data ...')

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/nanochat/scripts/download_stack_cpp.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 1961 bytes

### Code

```python
 1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
 2 | """Download The Stack v2 C++ subset from TempestTeam mirror to JSONL."""
 3 | 
 4 | import json
 5 | import time
 6 | from collections.abc import Mapping
 7 | from typing import cast
 8 | 
 9 | from datasets import load_dataset
10 | 
11 | OUTPUT = "data/stack_v2_cpp.jsonl"
12 | MAX_BYTES = 25_000_000_000  # 25GB target
13 | 
14 | print("Loading TempestTeam/dataset-the-stack-v2-dedup-sub C++ (streaming)...", flush=True)
15 | ds = load_dataset(
16 |     "TempestTeam/dataset-the-stack-v2-dedup-sub",
17 |     "C++",
18 |     split="train",
19 |     streaming=True,
20 | )
21 | 
22 | count = 0
23 | skipped = 0
24 | total_bytes = 0
25 | t0 = time.time()
26 | 
27 | with open(OUTPUT, "w") as f:
28 |     for row in ds:
29 |         row_map = cast(Mapping[str, object], row)
30 |         content_obj = row_map.get("content", "")
31 |         content = content_obj if isinstance(content_obj, str) else ""
32 |         if not content or len(content) < 100:
33 |             skipped += 1
34 |             continue
35 |         if len(content) > 1_000_000:  # Skip files > 1MB
36 |             skipped += 1
37 |             continue
38 |         # Skip vendor/generated
39 |         if row_map.get("is_vendor") or row_map.get("is_generated"):
40 |             skipped += 1
41 |             continue
42 | 
43 |         f.write(
44 |             json.dumps(
45 |                 {
46 |                     "text": content,
47 |                     "path": row_map.get("path", ""),
48 |                     "repo": row_map.get("repo_name", ""),
49 |                 }
50 |             )
51 |             + "\n"
52 |         )
53 |         total_bytes += len(content)
54 |         count += 1
55 | 
56 |         if count % 100_000 == 0:
57 |             elapsed = time.time() - t0
58 |             rate = total_bytes / elapsed / 1e6
59 |             print(
60 |                 f"  {count:,} files, {total_bytes/1e9:.2f} GB, "
61 |                 f"{rate:.1f} MB/s, skipped {skipped:,}",
62 |                 flush=True,
63 |             )
64 | 
65 |         if total_bytes >= MAX_BYTES:
66 |             break
67 | 
68 | elapsed = time.time() - t0
69 | print(
70 |     f"\nDone: {count:,} files, {total_bytes/1e9:.2f} GB, "
71 |     f"{elapsed:.0f}s, skipped {skipped:,}",
72 |     flush=True,
73 | )
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/nanochat/scripts/download_stack_v2_cpp.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 4178 bytes
- Created: 2026-04-02 22:45:34
- Modified: 2026-04-02 22:45:34

### Code

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Download The Stack v2 dedup C++ data from HuggingFace as JSONL chunks.
  3 | 
  4 | Uses huggingface_hub to download parquet files directly (bypasses datasets gating issue),
  5 | then converts to JSONL with only the 'content' field.
  6 | """
  7 | 
  8 | import json
  9 | import os
 10 | import time
 11 | from pathlib import Path
 12 | 
 13 | import pyarrow.parquet as pq
 14 | from huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download, HfApi
 15 | 
 16 | OUTPUT_DIR = Path(os.environ.get(
 17 |     "NANOCHAT_STACK_OUTPUT_DIR",
 18 |     str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "data" / "stack_v2_cpp"),
 19 | ))
 20 | OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 21 | 
 22 | DOCS_PER_FILE = 100_000
 23 | TARGET_BYTES = 20 * 1024**3  # 20 GB
 24 | 
 25 | PARQUET_FILES = [
 26 |     "data/C++/train-00000-of-00004.parquet",
 27 |     "data/C++/train-00001-of-00004.parquet",
 28 |     "data/C++/train-00002-of-00004.parquet",
 29 |     "data/C++/train-00003-of-00004.parquet",
 30 | ]
 31 | 
 32 | def main():
 33 |     token = HfApi().token
 34 |     print(f"Token available: {bool(token)}")
 35 | 
 36 |     total_bytes = 0
 37 |     total_docs = 0
 38 |     file_idx = 0
 39 |     buffer = []
 40 |     t0 = time.time()
 41 | 
 42 |     # Check for existing files to resume
 43 |     existing = sorted(OUTPUT_DIR.glob("chunk_*.jsonl"))
 44 |     if existing:
 45 |         print(f"Found {len(existing)} existing chunks, counting...")
 46 |         for ef in existing:
 47 |             with open(ef) as f:
 48 |                 for line in f:
 49 |                     doc = json.loads(line)
 50 |                     total_bytes += len(doc["content"].encode("utf-8"))
 51 |                     total_docs += 1
 52 |         file_idx = len(existing)
 53 |         print(f"Resuming from {total_docs:,} docs, {total_bytes/1e9:.2f} GB")
 54 |         if total_bytes >= TARGET_BYTES:
 55 |             print("Target already reached!")
 56 |             return
 57 | 
 58 |     docs_to_skip = total_docs
 59 |     docs_seen = 0
 60 | 
 61 |     for pf in PARQUET_FILES:
 62 |         print(f"\nDownloading {pf}...")
 63 |         local_path = hf_hub_download(
 64 |             repo_id="bigcode/the-stack-v2-dedup",
 65 |             filename=pf,
 66 |             repo_type="dataset",
 67 |             token=token,
 68 |         )
 69 |         print(f"  Cached at: {local_path}")
 70 | 
 71 |         # Read parquet in batches to avoid loading all into memory
 72 |         parquet_file = pq.ParquetFile(local_path)
 73 |         for batch in parquet_file.iter_batches(batch_size=10_000, columns=["content"]):
 74 |             for content in batch.column("content"):
 75 |                 content = content.as_py()
 76 |                 docs_seen += 1
 77 | 
 78 |                 if docs_to_skip > 0:
 79 |                     docs_to_skip -= 1
 80 |                     if docs_to_skip % 500_000 == 0 and docs_to_skip > 0:
 81 |                         print(f"  Skipping... {docs_to_skip:,} remaining")
 82 |                     continue
 83 | 
 84 |                 if not content:
 85 |                     continue
 86 | 
 87 |                 buffer.append({"content": content})
 88 |                 total_bytes += len(content.encode("utf-8"))
 89 |                 total_docs += 1
 90 | 
 91 |                 if len(buffer) >= DOCS_PER_FILE:
 92 |                     outpath = OUTPUT_DIR / f"chunk_{file_idx:04d}.jsonl"
 93 |                     with open(outpath, "w") as f:
 94 |                         for doc in buffer:
 95 |                             f.write(json.dumps(doc) + "\n")
 96 |                     elapsed = time.time() - t0
 97 |                     rate = total_bytes / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0
 98 |                     print(
 99 |                         f"  Wrote {outpath.name}: {total_docs:,} docs, "
100 |                         f"{total_bytes/1e9:.2f} GB, {rate/1e6:.1f} MB/s"
101 |                     )
102 |                     buffer = []
103 |                     file_idx += 1
104 | 
105 |                 if total_bytes >= TARGET_BYTES:
106 |                     break
107 |             if total_bytes >= TARGET_BYTES:
108 |                 break
109 |         if total_bytes >= TARGET_BYTES:
110 |             break
111 | 
112 |     # Write remaining buffer
113 |     if buffer:
114 |         outpath = OUTPUT_DIR / f"chunk_{file_idx:04d}.jsonl"
115 |         with open(outpath, "w") as f:
116 |             for doc in buffer:
117 |                 f.write(json.dumps(doc) + "\n")
118 |         print(f"Wrote {outpath.name} (final): {len(buffer):,} docs")
119 |         file_idx += 1
120 | 
121 |     elapsed = time.time() - t0
122 |     print(f"\nDone! {total_docs:,} documents, {total_bytes/1e9:.2f} GB in {elapsed:.0f}s")
123 |     print(f"Output: {file_idx} files in {OUTPUT_DIR}")
124 | 
125 | 
126 | if __name__ == "__main__":
127 |     main()
```

 49 | def _kind_to_int(kind: Any) -> int:
 50 |     if isinstance(kind, int):
 51 |         return kind
 52 |     text = str(kind)
 53 |     if text.isdigit():
 54 |         return int(text)
 55 |     return _KIND_STR_TO_INT.get(text.lower(), 0)

 58 | def _normalize_graph_edge_pairs(raw_edges: Any) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
 59 |     pairs: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
 60 |     for edge in raw_edges or []:
 61 |         if isinstance(edge, dict) and "from" in edge and "to" in edge:
 62 |             pairs.append((int(edge["from"]), int(edge["to"])))
 63 |         elif isinstance(edge, (list, tuple)) and len(edge) >= 2:
 64 |             pairs.append((int(edge[0]), int(edge[1])))
 65 |     return pairs

 68 | def _encode_batch_with_optional_char_spans(
 69 |     tokenizer,
 70 |     texts: list[str],
 71 |     *,
 72 |     prepend=None,
 73 |     append=None,
 74 |     num_threads: int = 8,
 75 | ) -> tuple[list[list[int]], list[list[tuple[int, int]]] | None]:
 76 |     def _call_tokenizer_encode(payload):
 77 |         kwargs = {}
 78 |         try:
 79 |             params = inspect.signature(tokenizer.encode).parameters
 80 |         except (TypeError, ValueError):
 81 |             params = {}
 82 |         accepts_var_kwargs = any(
 83 |             p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in params.values()
 84 |         )
 85 |         if accepts_var_kwargs or "prepend" in params:
 86 |             kwargs["prepend"] = prepend
 87 |         if accepts_var_kwargs or "append" in params:
 88 |             kwargs["append"] = append
 89 |         if accepts_var_kwargs or "num_threads" in params:
 90 |             kwargs["num_threads"] = num_threads
 91 |         return tokenizer.encode(payload, **kwargs)
 92 | 
 93 |     hf_tok = getattr(tokenizer, "_tokenizer", None) or getattr(
 94 |         tokenizer, "tokenizer", None
 95 |     )
 96 |     if hf_tok is None or not hasattr(hf_tok, "encode_batch"):
 97 |         return _call_tokenizer_encode(texts), None
 98 | 
 99 |     def _resolve_special_id(value: str | int | None) -> int | None:
100 |         if value is None or isinstance(value, int):
101 |             return value
102 |         if hasattr(tokenizer, "encode_special"):
103 |             return int(tokenizer.encode_special(value))
104 |         raise ValueError(f"Tokenizer cannot resolve special token {value!r}")
105 | 
106 |     prepend_id = _resolve_special_id(prepend)
107 |     append_id = _resolve_special_id(append)
108 | 
109 |     try:
110 |         encodings = hf_tok.encode_batch(texts, add_special_tokens=False)
111 |     except TypeError:
112 |         encodings = hf_tok.encode_batch(texts)
113 | 
114 |     token_lists: list[list[int]] = []
115 |     token_spans: list[list[tuple[int, int]]] = []
116 |     for text, enc in zip(texts, encodings):
117 |         ids = list(enc.ids)
118 |         spans = [(int(start), int(end)) for start, end in enc.offsets]
119 |         if prepend is not None:
120 |             assert prepend_id is not None
121 |             ids.insert(0, int(prepend_id))
122 |             spans.insert(0, (0, 0))
123 |         if append is not None:
124 |             assert append_id is not None
125 |             ids.append(int(append_id))
126 |             text_len = len(text)
127 |             spans.append((text_len, text_len))
128 |         token_lists.append(ids)
129 |         token_spans.append(spans)
130 |     return token_lists, token_spans
131 | 
132 | 
133 | def _extract_token_char_starts_and_valid(
134 |     token_positions: list[int] | list[tuple[int, int]],
135 | ) -> tuple[list[int], list[bool]]:
136 |     if not token_positions:
137 |         return [], []
138 |     first = token_positions[0]
139 |     if isinstance(first, tuple) and len(first) == 2:
140 |         spans = cast(list[tuple[int, int]], token_positions)
141 |         span_starts = [int(start) for start, _ in spans]
142 |         span_valid = [int(end) > int(start) for start, end in spans]
143 |         return span_starts, span_valid
144 | 
145 |     lengths = cast(list[int], token_positions)
146 |     starts: list[int] = []
147 |     valid: list[bool] = []
148 |     running = 0
149 |     for length in lengths:
150 |         n = max(int(length), 0)
151 |         starts.append(running)
152 |         valid.append(n > 0)
153 |         running += n
154 |     return starts, valid
155 | 
156 | 
157 | def _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
158 |     char_structure_ids: list[int],
159 |     text: str,
160 |     token_lengths: list[int] | list[tuple[int, int]],
161 | ) -> list[int]:
162 |     del text
163 |     if not char_structure_ids:
164 |         return [0] * len(token_lengths)
165 |     starts, valid = _extract_token_char_starts_and_valid(token_lengths)
166 |     n_struct = len(char_structure_ids)
167 |     out: list[int] = []
168 |     for start, is_valid in zip(starts, valid):
169 |         if is_valid and start < n_struct:
170 |             out.append(int(char_structure_ids[start]))
171 |         else:
172 |             out.append(0)
173 |     return out

176 | def _chunk_boundaries_to_token_offsets(
177 |     chunk_boundaries: list[dict[str, Any]],
178 |     text: str,
179 |     token_lengths: list[int] | list[tuple[int, int]],
180 | ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
181 |     del text
182 |     if not chunk_boundaries or not token_lengths:
183 |         return []
184 |     starts, _valid = _extract_token_char_starts_and_valid(token_lengths)
185 |     if not starts:
186 |         return []
187 | 
188 |     result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
189 |     for cb in chunk_boundaries:
190 |         char_start = int(cb.get("start", 0))
191 |         tok_idx = bisect.bisect_right(starts, char_start) - 1
192 |         if tok_idx < 0:
193 |             tok_idx = 0
194 |         elif tok_idx >= len(starts):
195 |             tok_idx = len(starts) - 1
196 |         result.append(
197 |             {
198 |                 "token_offset": int(tok_idx),
199 |                 "end_char": cb.get("end", cb.get("start", 0)),
200 |                 "kind": cb.get("kind", 0),
201 |                 "name": cb.get("name", ""),
202 |                 "dep_level": cb.get("dep_level", 0),
203 |             }
204 |         )
205 |     return result
206 | 
207 | 
208 | def _compute_token_dep_levels(
209 |     tok_struct_ids: list[int],
210 |     tok_chunks: list[dict[str, Any]],
211 |     num_tokens: int,
212 | ) -> list[int]:
213 |     del tok_struct_ids
214 |     if not tok_chunks:
215 |         return [0] * num_tokens
216 | 
217 |     sorted_chunks = sorted(tok_chunks, key=lambda c: int(c["token_offset"]))
218 |     out = [0] * num_tokens
219 |     chunk_idx = 0
220 |     current_dep = 0
221 |     for token_idx in range(num_tokens):
222 |         while chunk_idx < len(sorted_chunks) and int(sorted_chunks[chunk_idx]["token_offset"]) <= token_idx:
223 |             current_dep = int(sorted_chunks[chunk_idx].get("dep_level", 0))
224 |             chunk_idx += 1
225 |         out[token_idx] = current_dep
226 |     return out
227 |

229 | def _remap_token_edges(
230 |     raw_edges: list[tuple[int, int]],
231 |     index_map: dict[int, int],
232 | ) -> list[dict[str, int]]:
233 |     remapped = []
234 |     for src, dst in raw_edges:
235 |         if src in index_map and dst in index_map:
236 |             remapped.append({"from": int(index_map[src]), "to": int(index_map[dst])})
237 |     return remapped
238 |
