```python
240 | def _build_token_chunk_layout(
241 |     doc: dict[str, Any],
242 |     tok_chunks: list[dict[str, Any]],
243 |     token_count: int,
244 | ) -> dict[str, list[int] | list[dict[str, int]]]:
245 |     if not tok_chunks:
246 |         return {
247 |             TOKEN_CHUNK_STARTS_COLUMN: [],
248 |             TOKEN_CHUNK_ENDS_COLUMN: [],
249 |             TOKEN_CHUNK_KINDS_COLUMN: [],
250 |             TOKEN_CHUNK_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN: [],
251 |             TOKEN_CALL_EDGES_COLUMN: [],
252 |             TOKEN_TYPE_EDGES_COLUMN: [],
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |     chunk_entries = list(enumerate(tok_chunks))
256 |     chunk_entries.sort(key=lambda item: int(item[1].get("token_offset", 0)))
257 |     index_map = {orig_idx: new_idx for new_idx, (orig_idx, _) in enumerate(chunk_entries)}
258 | 
259 |     starts = [int(chunk.get("token_offset", 0)) for _, chunk in chunk_entries]
260 |     ends = [
261 |         starts[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(starts) else int(token_count)
262 |         for i in range(len(starts))
263 |     ]
264 |     kinds = [_kind_to_int(chunk.get("kind", 0)) for _, chunk in chunk_entries]
265 |     dep_levels = [int(chunk.get("dep_level", 0)) for _, chunk in chunk_entries]
266 | 
267 |     call_edges = _remap_token_edges(
268 |         _normalize_graph_edge_pairs(doc.get("call_edges", [])),
269 |         index_map,
270 |     )
271 |     type_edges = _remap_token_edges(
272 |         _normalize_graph_edge_pairs(doc.get("type_edges", [])),
273 |         index_map,
274 |     )
275 | 
276 |     return {
277 |         TOKEN_CHUNK_STARTS_COLUMN: starts,
278 |         TOKEN_CHUNK_ENDS_COLUMN: ends,
279 |         TOKEN_CHUNK_KINDS_COLUMN: kinds,
280 |         TOKEN_CHUNK_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN: dep_levels,
281 |         TOKEN_CALL_EDGES_COLUMN: call_edges,
282 |         TOKEN_TYPE_EDGES_COLUMN: type_edges,
283 |     }
284 |

286 | def _tokenize_optional_char_field(
287 |     doc: dict[str, Any],
288 |     keys: tuple[str, ...],
289 |     token_spans: list[tuple[int, int]],
290 | ) -> list[int]:
291 |     for key in keys:
292 |         values = doc.get(key, [])
293 |         if values:
294 |             return _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(values, "", token_spans)
295 |     return []

298 | def _changed_chunk_metadata(
299 |     token_chunk_starts: list[int],
300 |     token_chunk_ends: list[int],
301 |     change_masks: tuple[list[int], ...],
302 | ) -> tuple[list[int], list[dict[str, int]]]:
303 |     if not token_chunk_starts or not token_chunk_ends:
304 |         return [], []
305 |     changed_ids: list[int] = []
306 |     changed_spans: list[dict[str, int]] = []
307 |     for chunk_idx, (start, end) in enumerate(zip(token_chunk_starts, token_chunk_ends)):
308 |         start_i = max(int(start), 0)
309 |         end_i = max(int(end), start_i)
310 |         changed = False
311 |         for mask in change_masks:
312 |             if not mask:
313 |                 continue
314 |             bounded_end = min(end_i, len(mask))
315 |             if start_i < bounded_end and any(int(value) != 0 for value in mask[start_i:bounded_end]):
316 |                 changed = True
317 |                 break
318 |         if changed:
319 |             changed_ids.append(chunk_idx)
320 |             changed_spans.append({"start": start_i, "end": end_i})
321 |     return changed_ids, changed_spans

324 | def _platform_ids_from_doc(doc: dict[str, Any]) -> list[int]:
325 |     platform_info = doc.get("platform_info")
326 |     if not platform_info:
327 |         return []
328 |     if isinstance(platform_info, str):
329 |         try:
330 |             platform_info = json.loads(platform_info)
331 |         except json.JSONDecodeError:
332 |             return []
333 |     if not isinstance(platform_info, dict):
334 |         return []
335 | 
336 |     from cppmega_mlx.data.nanochat_pipeline.platform_vocab import platform_info_to_ids
337 | 
338 |     return platform_info_to_ids(platform_info)

341 | def materialize_tokenized_enriched_batch(
342 |     docs: list[dict[str, Any]],
343 |     tokenizer,
344 |     *,
345 |     num_threads: int = 8,
346 | ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
347 |     """Convert enriched char-level docs into token-level parquet-ready metadata.
348 | 
349 |     The returned token arrays align to `token_ids`, which already include the BOS
350 |     token at position 0.
351 |     """
352 |     if not docs:
353 |         return []
354 | 
355 |     texts = [str(doc.get("text", "")) for doc in docs]
356 |     if hasattr(tokenizer, "get_bos_token_id"):
357 |         bos_token = tokenizer.get_bos_token_id()
358 |     else:
359 |         bos_token = tokenizer.bos_token_id
360 |     token_lists, token_spans_batch = _encode_batch_with_optional_char_spans(
361 |         tokenizer,
362 |         texts,
363 |         prepend=bos_token,
364 |         num_threads=num_threads,
365 |     )
366 |     if token_spans_batch is None:
367 |         raise ValueError(
368 |             "Tokenizer did not expose per-token character spans; "
369 |             "cannot materialize token-level enriched metadata offline."
370 |         )
371 | 
372 |     out = []
373 |     for doc, token_ids, token_spans in zip(docs, token_lists, token_spans_batch):
374 |         token_ids = [int(tok) for tok in token_ids]
375 |         token_structure_ids = _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
376 |             doc.get("structure_ids", []),
377 |             "",
378 |             token_spans,
379 |         )
380 |         tok_chunks = _chunk_boundaries_to_token_offsets(
381 |             doc.get("chunk_boundaries", []),
382 |             "",
383 |             token_spans,
384 |         )
385 |         token_dep_levels = _compute_token_dep_levels(
386 |             token_structure_ids,
387 |             tok_chunks,
388 |             len(token_ids),
389 |         )
390 | 
391 |         token_change_mask_pre = _tokenize_optional_char_field(
392 |             doc,
393 |             ("change_mask_pre", TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_PRE_COLUMN),
394 |             token_spans,
395 |         )
396 |         token_change_mask_post = _tokenize_optional_char_field(
397 |             doc,
398 |             ("change_mask_post", "token_change_mask", TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_POST_COLUMN),
399 |             token_spans,
400 |         )
401 |         hunk_id_per_token = _tokenize_optional_char_field(
402 |             doc,
403 |             ("hunk_id_per_char", HUNK_ID_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN),
404 |             token_spans,
405 |         )
406 |         edit_op_per_token = _tokenize_optional_char_field(
407 |             doc,
408 |             ("edit_op_per_char", "token_edit_op", EDIT_OP_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN),
409 |             token_spans,
410 |         )
411 | 
412 |         row: dict[str, Any] = {
413 |             TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN: token_ids,
414 |             PLATFORM_IDS_COLUMN: _platform_ids_from_doc(doc),
415 |             TOKEN_STRUCTURE_IDS_COLUMN: token_structure_ids,
416 |             TOKEN_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN: token_dep_levels,
417 |             TOKEN_AST_DEPTH_COLUMN: _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
418 |                 doc.get("ast_depth", []),
419 |                 "",
420 |                 token_spans,
421 |             ),
422 |             TOKEN_SIBLING_INDEX_COLUMN: _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
423 |                 doc.get("sibling_index", []),
424 |                 "",
425 |                 token_spans,
426 |             ),
427 |             TOKEN_AST_NODE_TYPE_COLUMN: _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
428 |                 doc.get("ast_node_type", []),
429 |                 "",
430 |                 token_spans,
431 |             ),
432 |             TOKEN_SYMBOL_IDS_COLUMN: _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
433 |                 doc.get("symbol_ids", []),
434 |                 "",
435 |                 token_spans,
436 |             ),
437 |             TOKEN_CALL_TARGETS_COLUMN: _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
438 |                 doc.get("call_targets", []),
439 |                 "",
440 |                 token_spans,
441 |             ),
442 |             TOKEN_TYPE_REFS_COLUMN: _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
443 |                 doc.get("type_refs", []),
444 |                 "",
445 |                 token_spans,
446 |             ),
447 |             TOKEN_DEF_USE_COLUMN: _chars_to_tokens_structure_ids(
448 |                 doc.get("def_use", []),
449 |                 "",
450 |                 token_spans,
451 |             ),
452 |             TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_PRE_COLUMN: token_change_mask_pre,
453 |             TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_POST_COLUMN: token_change_mask_post,
454 |             HUNK_ID_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN: hunk_id_per_token,
455 |             EDIT_OP_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN: edit_op_per_token,
456 |         }
457 |         row.update(cast(dict[str, list[int]], _build_token_chunk_layout(doc, tok_chunks, len(token_ids))))
458 |         changed_chunk_ids, changed_chunk_spans = _changed_chunk_metadata(
459 |             cast(list[int], row[TOKEN_CHUNK_STARTS_COLUMN]),
460 |             cast(list[int], row[TOKEN_CHUNK_ENDS_COLUMN]),
461 |             (token_change_mask_pre, token_change_mask_post),
462 |         )
463 |         row[CHANGED_CHUNK_IDS_COLUMN] = changed_chunk_ids
464 |         row[CHANGED_CHUNK_SPANS_COLUMN] = changed_chunk_spans
465 |         out.append(row)
466 | 
467 |     return out
```

```python
  1 | """Shared schema constants for token-level enriched parquet columns."""
  2 | 
  3 | from __future__ import annotations
  4 | 
  5 | from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
  6 | from typing import SupportsInt, TypeAlias, TypedDict, TypeGuard
  7 | 
  8 | import numpy as np
  9 | 
 10 | 
 11 | IntLike: TypeAlias = SupportsInt | str | bytes | bytearray
 12 | ScalarLike: TypeAlias = int | float | str | bool
 13 | ArrayLikeSequence: TypeAlias = list[object] | tuple[object, ...] | np.ndarray
 14 | TokenValueSequence: TypeAlias = Sequence[object] | np.ndarray
 15 | 
 16 | 
 17 | class SpanMapping(TypedDict):
 18 |     start: object
 19 |     end: object
 20 | 
 21 | 
 22 | TokenMetadataRow: TypeAlias = Mapping[str, object]
 23 | SpanLike: TypeAlias = SpanMapping | Sequence[object] | np.ndarray

 25 | 
 26 | def _is_sequence(val: object) -> TypeGuard[ArrayLikeSequence]:
 27 |     """Return True for list or numpy array (sequence-like, not str)."""
 28 |     return isinstance(val, (list, tuple, np.ndarray))
 29 | 
 30 | 
 31 | def _is_token_value_sequence(val: object) -> TypeGuard[TokenValueSequence]:
 32 |     """Return True for token-aligned sequence payloads, excluding strings."""
 33 |     return isinstance(val, (list, tuple, np.ndarray))
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | def _is_non_empty_token_sequence(val: object) -> TypeGuard[TokenValueSequence]:
 37 |     return _is_token_value_sequence(val) and len(val) > 0
 38 | 
 39 | 
 40 | def _as_int(value: object) -> int | None:
 41 |     try:
 42 |         if isinstance(value, (str, bytes, bytearray, int, np.integer, np.bool_)):
 43 |             return int(value)
 44 |         if isinstance(value, float):
 45 |             return int(value)
 46 |         return None
 47 |     except (TypeError, ValueError):
 48 |         return None
 49 | 
 50 | 
 51 | def _extract_span_bounds(span: object) -> tuple[int, int] | None:
 52 |     if isinstance(span, Mapping):
 53 |         start_i = _as_int(span.get("start"))
 54 |         end_i = _as_int(span.get("end"))
 55 |     elif _is_sequence(span) and len(span) == 2:
 56 |         start_i = _as_int(span[0])
 57 |         end_i = _as_int(span[1])
 58 |     else:
 59 |         return None
 60 |     if start_i is None or end_i is None:
 61 |         return None
 62 |     return start_i, end_i
 63 | 
 64 | TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN = "token_ids"
 65 | PLATFORM_IDS_COLUMN = "platform_ids"

178 | 
179 | def has_materialized_token_metadata(
180 |     row: TokenMetadataRow,
181 |     *,
182 |     need_structure_meta: bool,
183 |     need_ast_metadata: bool,
184 |     need_chunk_relations: bool,
185 |     need_semantic_metadata: bool = False,
186 |     need_temporal_metadata: bool = False,
187 |     token_count: int | None = None,
188 |     require_token_ids: bool = True,
189 | ) -> bool:
190 |     """Return True when row-local token metadata is present and shape-valid.
191 | 
192 |     Missing or empty token metadata is treated as "not materialized" so callers
193 |     can fall back to legacy char-level derivation or omit optional tensors.
194 |     Shape-invalid non-empty metadata is rejected by returning False here; packed
195 |     row consumers may still choose to fail closed with a more specific error.
196 |     """
197 | 
198 |     if token_count is None:
199 |         token_ids = row.get(TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN)
200 |         if not _is_non_empty_token_sequence(token_ids):
201 |             return False
202 |         token_count = len(token_ids)
203 |     else:
204 |         normalized_token_count = _as_int(token_count)
205 |         if normalized_token_count is None:
206 |             return False
207 |         token_count = normalized_token_count
208 |         if token_count <= 0:
209 |             return False
210 |         if require_token_ids:
211 |             token_ids = row.get(TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN)
212 |             if not _is_token_value_sequence(token_ids) or len(token_ids) != token_count:
213 |                 return False
214 | 
215 |     if need_structure_meta:
216 |         for column in TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_STRUCTURE_COLUMNS:
217 |             values = row.get(column)
218 |             if not _is_token_value_sequence(values) or len(values) != token_count:
219 |                 return False
220 | 
221 |     if need_ast_metadata:
222 |         for column in TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_AST_COLUMNS:
223 |             values = row.get(column)
224 |             if not _is_token_value_sequence(values) or len(values) != token_count:
225 |                 return False
226 | 
227 |     if need_semantic_metadata:
228 |         for column in TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_SEMANTIC_COLUMNS:
229 |             values = row.get(column)
230 |             if not _is_token_value_sequence(values) or len(values) != token_count:
231 |                 return False
232 | 
233 |     if need_chunk_relations:
234 |         chunk_columns = []
235 |         for column in TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_CHUNK_COLUMNS:
236 |             values = row.get(column)
237 |             if not _is_token_value_sequence(values):
238 |                 return False
239 |             chunk_columns.append(values)
240 |         starts, ends, kinds, dep_levels = chunk_columns
241 |         if not (len(starts) == len(ends) == len(kinds) == len(dep_levels)):
242 |             return False
243 |         if row.get("chunk_boundaries") and len(starts) == 0:
244 |             return False
245 |         prev_start: int | None = None
246 |         prev_end: int | None = None
247 |         for start, end in zip(starts, ends):
248 |             start_i = _as_int(start)
249 |             end_i = _as_int(end)
250 |             if start_i is None or end_i is None:
251 |                 return False
252 |             if not (0 <= start_i < end_i <= token_count):
253 |                 return False
254 |             if prev_start is not None and start_i <= prev_start:
255 |                 return False
256 |             if prev_end is not None and end_i <= prev_end:
257 |                 return False
258 |             prev_start = start_i
259 |             prev_end = end_i
260 | 
261 |     if need_temporal_metadata:
262 |         for column in TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_TEMPORAL_TOKEN_COLUMNS:
263 |             values = row.get(column)
264 |             if not _is_token_value_sequence(values) or len(values) != token_count:
265 |                 return False
266 | 
267 |         changed_chunk_ids = row.get(CHANGED_CHUNK_IDS_COLUMN)
268 |         changed_chunk_spans = row.get(CHANGED_CHUNK_SPANS_COLUMN)
269 |         if not _is_token_value_sequence(changed_chunk_ids) or not _is_token_value_sequence(changed_chunk_spans):
270 |             return False
271 |         if len(changed_chunk_ids) != len(changed_chunk_spans):
272 |             return False
273 |         for span in changed_chunk_spans:
274 |             bounds = _extract_span_bounds(span)
275 |             if bounds is None:
276 |                 return False
277 |             start_i, end_i = bounds
278 |             if not (0 <= start_i < end_i <= token_count):
279 |                 return False
280 | 
281 |     return True
282 | 
283 | 
284 | __all__ = [
285 |     "TOKEN_IDS_COLUMN",
286 |     "PLATFORM_IDS_COLUMN",
287 |     "TOKEN_STRUCTURE_IDS_COLUMN",
288 |     "TOKEN_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN",
289 |     "TOKEN_AST_DEPTH_COLUMN",
290 |     "TOKEN_SIBLING_INDEX_COLUMN",
291 |     "TOKEN_AST_NODE_TYPE_COLUMN",
292 |     "TOKEN_SYMBOL_IDS_COLUMN",
293 |     "TOKEN_CALL_TARGETS_COLUMN",
294 |     "TOKEN_TYPE_REFS_COLUMN",
295 |     "TOKEN_DEF_USE_COLUMN",
296 |     "TOKEN_CHUNK_STARTS_COLUMN",
297 |     "TOKEN_CHUNK_ENDS_COLUMN",
298 |     "TOKEN_CHUNK_KINDS_COLUMN",
299 |     "TOKEN_CHUNK_DEP_LEVELS_COLUMN",
300 |     "TOKEN_CALL_EDGES_COLUMN",

302 |     "TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_PRE_COLUMN",
303 |     "TOKEN_CHANGE_MASK_POST_COLUMN",
304 |     "CHANGED_CHUNK_IDS_COLUMN",
305 |     "CHANGED_CHUNK_SPANS_COLUMN",
306 |     "HUNK_ID_PER_TOKEN_COLUMN",
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega.mlx/scripts/nanochat_data/materialize_tokenized_enriched_parquet.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 4721 bytes
- Created: 2026-05-21 01:03:48
- Modified: 2026-05-21 01:03:48

### Code

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Add token-level enriched columns to an existing enriched parquet dataset.
  3 | 
  4 | Reads local parquet shards, preserves existing columns, and writes a sibling
  5 | dataset with token_ids plus token-level enriched metadata columns materialized
  6 | offline. This is intended for A/B dataloader benchmarking against the legacy
  7 | char-level parquet path without round-tripping through JSONL again.
  8 | """
  9 | 
 10 | from __future__ import annotations
 11 | 
 12 | import argparse
 13 | import json
 14 | import os
 15 | from pathlib import Path
 16 | 
 17 | import pyarrow as pa  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
 18 | import pyarrow.parquet as pq  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
 19 | 
 20 | from cppmega_mlx.tokenizer.cpp_tokenizer import load_cppmega_tokenizer
 21 | from cppmega_mlx.data.nanochat_pipeline.tokenized_enriched import materialize_tokenized_enriched_batch
 22 | from cppmega_mlx.data.nanochat_pipeline.tokenized_enriched_schema import TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_COLUMNS
 23 | 
 24 | 
 25 | def _decode_json_like(value):
 26 |     while isinstance(value, str) and value:
 27 |         decoded = json.loads(value)
 28 |         if decoded == value:
 29 |             break
 30 |         value = decoded
 31 |     return value
 32 | 
 33 | 
 34 | def _list_parquet_files(input_dir: str) -> list[Path]:
 35 |     return sorted(
 36 |         Path(input_dir).glob("*.parquet"),
 37 |         key=lambda p: p.name,
 38 |     )
 39 | 
 40 | 
 41 | def _table_to_docs(table: pa.Table) -> list[dict]:
 42 |     columns = {name: table.column(name).to_pylist() for name in table.column_names}
 43 |     num_rows = table.num_rows
 44 |     docs = []
 45 |     for i in range(num_rows):
 46 |         row = {name: values[i] for name, values in columns.items()}
 47 |         for json_list_field in ("chunk_boundaries", "call_edges", "type_edges"):
 48 |             value = row.get(json_list_field)
 49 |             if value is None:
 50 |                 continue
 51 |             value = _decode_json_like(value)
 52 |             if isinstance(value, list):
 53 |                 value = [_decode_json_like(item) for item in value]
 54 |             row[json_list_field] = value
 55 |         for json_obj_field in ("platform_info", "language_info", "build_info"):
 56 |             value = row.get(json_obj_field)
 57 |             if value is None:
 58 |                 continue
 59 |             row[json_obj_field] = _decode_json_like(value)
 60 |         docs.append(row)
 61 |     return docs
 62 | 
 63 | 
 64 | def _merge_table_with_tokenized(
 65 |     table: pa.Table,
 66 |     tokenized_rows: list[dict],
 67 | ) -> pa.Table:
 68 |     base_columns = {name: table.column(name) for name in table.column_names}
 69 |     for column_name in TOKENIZED_ENRICHED_COLUMNS:
 70 |         base_columns[column_name] = pa.array(
 71 |             [row.get(column_name, []) for row in tokenized_rows]
 72 |         )
 73 |     return pa.table(base_columns)
 74 | 
 75 | 
 76 | def _copy_metadata_files(input_dir: str, output_dir: str) -> None:
 77 |     for name in ("_COMPLETE",):
 78 |         src = Path(input_dir) / name
 79 |         if src.exists():
 80 |             Path(output_dir, name).write_text(src.read_text())
 81 | 
 82 | 
 83 | def main() -> None:
 84 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
 85 |     parser.add_argument("--input-dir", required=True)
 86 |     parser.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True)
 87 |     parser.add_argument(
 88 |         "--max-files",
 89 |         type=int,
 90 |         default=0,
 91 |         help="Only process the first N parquet shards (0 = all).",
 92 |     )
 93 |     parser.add_argument(
 94 |         "--tokenizer-path",
 95 |         default="",
 96 |         help="Optional tokenizer.json path used for token materialization.",
 97 |     )
 98 |     parser.add_argument(
 99 |         "--row-group-size",
100 |         type=int,
101 |         default=1024,
102 |         help="Row group size for rewritten output shards.",
103 |     )
104 |     args = parser.parse_args()
105 | 
106 |     os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
107 |     parquet_files = _list_parquet_files(args.input_dir)
108 |     if args.max_files > 0:
109 |         parquet_files = parquet_files[: args.max_files]
110 |     if not parquet_files:
111 |         raise FileNotFoundError(f"No parquet files found under {args.input_dir}")
112 | 
113 |     tokenizer_path = args.tokenizer_path
114 |     if not tokenizer_path:
115 |         raise ValueError("--tokenizer-path is required for parquet materialization")
116 |     tokenizer = load_cppmega_tokenizer(tokenizer_path)
117 | 
118 |     for idx, src_path in enumerate(parquet_files, 1):
119 |         dst_path = Path(args.output_dir) / src_path.name
120 |         print(f"[{idx}/{len(parquet_files)}] {src_path.name}")
121 |         table = pq.read_table(src_path)
122 |         docs = _table_to_docs(table)
123 |         tokenized_rows = materialize_tokenized_enriched_batch(docs, tokenizer)
124 |         merged = _merge_table_with_tokenized(table, tokenized_rows)
125 |         pq.write_table(
126 |             merged,
127 |             dst_path,
128 |             row_group_size=args.row_group_size,
129 |             compression="snappy",
130 |         )
131 | 
132 |     _copy_metadata_files(args.input_dir, args.output_dir)
133 |     print(f"Wrote tokenized parquet dataset to {args.output_dir}")
134 | 
135 | 
136 | if __name__ == "__main__":
137 |     main()
```

```bash
 1 | #!/bin/bash
 2 | # megacpp master data prep dispatcher.
 3 | #
 4 | # Runs download -> tokenize -> format -> cache -> verify to produce the
 5 | # Megatron-format ``clang_semantic_4k_v10`` dataset used by NAM56R training
 6 | # (see scripts/remote_smoke_h200_dsa_9_4_m.sh).
 7 | #
 8 | # See docs/data_preparation.md for full details.
 9 | #
10 | # Usage:
11 | #   bash prepare_data.sh                # run all stages
12 | #   bash prepare_data.sh download       # just clone source repos
13 | #   bash prepare_data.sh tokenize       # requires MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT
14 | #   bash prepare_data.sh format         # parquet -> Megatron .bin/.idx
15 | #   bash prepare_data.sh cache          # warm/validate index cache
16 | #   bash prepare_data.sh verify         # sanity-check final dataset
17 | #
18 | # Env knobs (all optional):
19 | #   MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT    default: /home/dave/cppmega-root/data
20 | #   MEGACPP_DATASET_NAME default: clang_semantic_4k_v10
21 | #   MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT  (required for 'tokenize' stage)
22 | 
23 | set -euo pipefail
24 | 
25 | HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
26 | PY="${PYTHON:-python3}"
27 | 
28 | STAGE="${1:-all}"
29 | 
30 | run_download() {
31 |     bash "$HERE/prepare_download_megacpp.sh"
32 | }
33 | run_tokenize() {
34 |     "$PY" "$HERE/prepare_tokenize_megacpp.py"
35 | }
36 | run_format() {
37 |     "$PY" "$HERE/prepare_format_megacpp.py"
38 | }
39 | run_cache() {
40 |     "$PY" "$HERE/prepare_cache_megacpp.py"
41 | }
42 | run_verify() {
43 |     "$PY" "$HERE/verify_dataset_megacpp.py"
44 | }
45 | 
46 | case "$STAGE" in
47 |     download) run_download ;;
48 |     tokenize) run_tokenize ;;
49 |     format)   run_format ;;
50 |     cache)    run_cache ;;
51 |     verify)   run_verify ;;
52 |     all)
53 |         run_download
54 |         run_tokenize
55 |         run_format
56 |         run_cache
57 |         run_verify
58 |         ;;
59 |     *)
60 |         echo "usage: $0 {download|tokenize|format|cache|verify|all}" >&2
61 |         exit 2
62 |         ;;
63 | esac
```

### Code

```bash
 1 | #!/bin/bash
 2 | # megacpp data prep — Stage 1: Download raw C/C++ source corpora.
 3 | #
 4 | # Adopted from nanochat/scripts/data/download_cpp_sources.sh.
 5 | # Clones a fixed set of C/C++ repositories used as the megacpp training corpus
 6 | # foundation. No credentials required — all sources are public GitHub repos.
 7 | #
 8 | # Sibling stages: see scripts/data/prepare_data.sh.
 9 | #
10 | # Usage:
11 | #   bash prepare_download_megacpp.sh [DATA_DIR]
12 | #
13 | # Default DATA_DIR: ${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT:-/home/dave/cppmega-root/data}/cpp_raw
14 | #
15 | # Output: ${DATA_DIR} containing shallow clones of each repo.
16 | set -euo pipefail
17 | 
18 | DEFAULT_ROOT="${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT:-/home/dave/cppmega-root/data}"
19 | DATA_DIR="${1:-${DEFAULT_ROOT}/cpp_raw}"
20 | mkdir -p "$DATA_DIR"
21 | cd "$DATA_DIR"
22 | 
23 | echo "=== megacpp: downloading C/C++ sources to $DATA_DIR ==="
24 | 
25 | clone_shallow() {
26 |     local name="$1"
27 |     local url="$2"
28 |     local ref="${3:-}"
29 |     if [ -d "$name" ]; then
30 |         echo "--- $name already exists, skipping ---"
31 |         return 0
32 |     fi
```

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """megacpp data prep — Stage 2: Tokenize raw C/C++ source into parquet shards.
  3 | 
  4 | This stage runs the Clang semantic indexer over the raw corpus, emits JSONL
  5 | with per-chunk token IDs, and streams it into parquet shards of the form
  6 | ``shard_NNNNN.parquet`` plus an optional ``val_shard.parquet``.
  7 | 
  8 | DEPENDENCY — nanochat source tree required
  9 | -------------------------------------------
 10 | The semantic indexer and the hybrid C++ BPE tokenizer live in the upstream
 11 | nanochat repo and are too heavyweight to vendor verbatim here:
 12 | 
 13 |   * ``nanochat/tools/clang_indexer/index_project.py`` — libclang-based
 14 |     cross-file semantic indexer (emits enriched JSONL).
 15 |   * ``nanochat/nanochat/cpp_tokenizer.py`` — hybrid fixed-vocab + BPE
 16 |     tokenizer (131072 tokens) backed by ``nanochat/tokenizer.json``.
 17 |   * ``nanochat/scripts/data/clang_enriched_to_4k_parquet.py`` — 4K-budgeted
 18 |     parquet converter; imports several ``nanochat.*`` modules.
 19 |   * ``nanochat/scripts/data/stream_jsonl_to_parquet.py`` — simple streaming
 20 |     variant (no nanochat.* deps) — a standalone copy is shipped alongside as
 21 |     ``prepare_stream_jsonl_to_parquet_megacpp.py``.
 22 | 
 23 | To run the full tokenize stage, clone nanochat next to cppmega:
 24 | 
 25 |     git clone <nanochat-url> /path/to/nanochat
 26 |     export MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT=/path/to/nanochat
 27 | 
 28 | Then this script dispatches to nanochat's ``run_clang_pipeline.sh`` with the
 29 | megacpp output layout. If ``MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT`` is not set, the script
 30 | exits with a clear error pointing here.
 31 |

 33 |

 36 |       parquet/
 37 |         clang_semantic_4k_v10/

 68 |             "  The tokenize stage depends on the nanochat repo (clang indexer +\n"
 69 |             "  cpp_tokenizer). Clone nanochat and point this env var at it:\n"

 71 |             "  See docs/data_preparation.md for details."
 72 |         )

 75 |         sys.exit(f"ERROR: MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT={p} does not exist")
 76 |     pipeline = p / "scripts" / "data" / "run_clang_pipeline.sh"
 77 |     if not pipeline.is_file():
 78 |         sys.exit(f"ERROR: {pipeline} not found — is MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT correct?")
 79 |     return p
 80 | 
 81 | 
 82 | def _copy_tokenizer(nanochat_root: Path, data_root: Path) -> None:
 83 |     src = nanochat_root / "tokenizer.json"
 84 |     if not src.is_file():
 85 |         sys.exit(f"ERROR: tokenizer artifact not found at {src}")
 86 |     dst_dir = data_root / "tokenizer"
 87 |     dst_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 88 |     dst = dst_dir / "tokenizer.json"
 89 |     if not dst.exists() or dst.stat().st_mtime < src.stat().st_mtime:
 90 |         shutil.copy2(src, dst)
 91 |         print(f"[megacpp] copied tokenizer: {src} -> {dst}")
 92 |     else:
 93 |         print(f"[megacpp] tokenizer already up to date at {dst}")
 94 | 
 95 | 
 96 | def main() -> int:

 98 |     parser.add_argument(
 99 |         "--data-root",
100 |         default=DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT,
101 |         help=f"megacpp data root (default: {DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT})",
102 |     )
103 |     parser.add_argument(

105 |         default=DEFAULT_DATASET_NAME,
106 |         help=f"output parquet dataset subdir (default: {DEFAULT_DATASET_NAME})",
107 |     )

142 |     env = os.environ.copy()

146 |     #   $OUTPUT_DIR/cpp_clang_crossfile_16k.jsonl  (default 16k label)
147 |     #   $OUTPUT_DIR/parquet/cpp_clang_crossfile_16k/

150 |     staging = data_root / "staging" / args.dataset_name
151 |     staging.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
152 | 
153 |     cmd = [
154 |         "bash",
155 |         str(pipeline),
156 |         str(raw_dir),
157 |         str(staging),
158 |         str(args.workers),
159 |     ]
160 |     print("[megacpp] running nanochat clang pipeline:")
161 |     print("   ", " ".join(cmd))
162 |     ret = subprocess.call(cmd, env=env)
163 |     if ret != 0:
164 |         sys.exit(f"ERROR: nanochat clang pipeline exited with {ret}")
165 | 
166 |     # Publish: move/symlink staging/parquet/* into data/parquet/<dataset_name>/
167 |     produced = staging / "parquet"
168 |     if produced.is_dir():
169 |         # Find the single sub-dataset dir
170 |         subs = [p for p in produced.iterdir() if p.is_dir()]
171 |         if len(subs) != 1:
172 |             sys.exit(f"ERROR: expected 1 sub-dataset dir in {produced}, got {subs}")
173 |         src = subs[0]
174 |         for shard in src.iterdir():
175 |             dst = parquet_root / shard.name
176 |             if dst.exists():
177 |                 dst.unlink()
178 |             shutil.move(str(shard), str(dst))
179 |         print(f"[megacpp] published parquet shards to {parquet_root}")
180 |     else:
181 |         sys.exit(f"ERROR: pipeline did not produce {produced}")
182 | 
183 |     print(f"[megacpp] stage 2 done: {parquet_root}")
184 |     return 0
185 | 
186 | 
187 | if __name__ == "__main__":
188 |     raise SystemExit(main())
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega/scripts/data/prepare_stream_jsonl_to_parquet_megacpp.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 5578 bytes
- Created: 2026-04-30 11:25:17
- Modified: 2026-04-30 11:25:17

### Code

```python
  1 | """megacpp data prep helper — stream a growing JSONL into parquet shards.
  2 | 
  3 | Adopted verbatim from nanochat/scripts/data/stream_jsonl_to_parquet.py (no
  4 | ``nanochat.*`` imports — self-contained). Renamed with the megacpp suffix and
  5 | default paths aligned with ``MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT``.
  6 | 
  7 | Reads from a JSONL file that is still being written to, and produces parquet
  8 | shards as documents accumulate. Watches for the JSONL writer to finish (no new
  9 | data for 60s + file not growing), then writes the final validation shard and a
 10 | ``_COMPLETE`` sentinel.
 11 | 
 12 | Usage:
 13 |     python prepare_stream_jsonl_to_parquet_megacpp.py \
 14 |         --input  /path/to/cpp_chunked_all.jsonl \
 15 |         --parquet_dir /path/to/parquet/clang_semantic_4k_v10 \
 16 |         --rows_per_file 50000
 17 | """
 18 | import argparse
 19 | import json
 20 | import os
 21 | import random
 22 | import time
 23 | 
 24 | import pyarrow as pa
 25 | import pyarrow.parquet as pq
 26 | 
 27 | 
 28 | def main():
 29 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 30 |     parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input JSONL (may still be growing)")
 31 |     parser.add_argument("--parquet_dir", required=True, help="Output parquet directory")
 32 |     parser.add_argument("--rows_per_file", type=int, default=50000)
 33 |     parser.add_argument("--val_fraction", type=float, default=0.01)
 34 |     parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
 35 |     args = parser.parse_args()
 36 | 
 37 |     os.makedirs(args.parquet_dir, exist_ok=True)
 38 |     rng = random.Random(args.seed)
 39 | 
 40 |     shard_idx = 0
 41 |     batch = []
 42 |     total_docs = 0
 43 |     stale_count = 0
 44 |     last_pos = 0
 45 | 
 46 |     print(f"[megacpp_stream_pq] Watching {args.input} -> {args.parquet_dir}")
 47 |     print(f"[megacpp_stream_pq] Rows per shard: {args.rows_per_file}")
 48 | 
 49 |     while not os.path.exists(args.input):
 50 |         print(f"[megacpp_stream_pq] Waiting for {args.input}...")
 51 |         time.sleep(10)
 52 | 
 53 |     with open(args.input) as f:
 54 |         while True:
 55 |             line = f.readline()
 56 |             if not line:
 57 |                 current_size = os.path.getsize(args.input)
 58 |                 if current_size == last_pos:
 59 |                     stale_count += 1
 60 |                 else:
 61 |                     stale_count = 0
 62 |                     last_pos = current_size
 63 |                 if stale_count >= 4:
 64 |                     print("[megacpp_stream_pq] Input file stable for 60s, finishing up")
 65 |                     break
 66 |                 time.sleep(15)
 67 |                 continue
 68 | 
 69 |             stale_count = 0
 70 |             line = line.strip()
 71 |             if not line:
 72 |                 continue
 73 | 
 74 |             text = json.loads(line)["text"]
 75 |             batch.append(text)
 76 |             total_docs += 1
 77 | 
 78 |             if len(batch) >= args.rows_per_file:
 79 |                 rng.shuffle(batch)
 80 |                 table = pa.table({"text": batch})
 81 |                 path = os.path.join(args.parquet_dir, f"shard_{shard_idx:05d}.parquet")
 82 |                 pq.write_table(table, path, row_group_size=1024)
 83 |                 try:
 84 |                     pf = pq.ParquetFile(path)
 85 |                     assert pf.num_row_groups > 0, f"Empty parquet: {path}"
 86 |                     pf.read_row_group(0)
 87 |                 except Exception as e:
 88 |                     print(f"[ERROR] Corrupt parquet written, removing: {path} - {e}")
 89 |                     os.remove(path)
 90 |                     raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to write valid parquet: {path}") from e
 91 |                 print(
 92 |                     f"[megacpp_stream_pq] Written shard_{shard_idx:05d}.parquet "
 93 |                     f"({len(batch)} docs, {total_docs:,} total)"
 94 |                 )
 95 |                 shard_idx += 1
 96 |                 batch = []
 97 | 
 98 |     val_count = max(1, int(total_docs * args.val_fraction))
 99 |     if len(batch) > val_count:
100 |         train_part = batch[:-val_count]
101 |         val_part = batch[-val_count:]
102 |     else:
103 |         train_part = batch
104 |         val_part = []
105 | 
106 |     if train_part:
107 |         rng.shuffle(train_part)
108 |         table = pa.table({"text": train_part})
109 |         path = os.path.join(args.parquet_dir, f"shard_{shard_idx:05d}.parquet")
110 |         pq.write_table(table, path, row_group_size=1024)
111 |         try:
112 |             pf = pq.ParquetFile(path)
113 |             assert pf.num_row_groups > 0, f"Empty parquet: {path}"
114 |             pf.read_row_group(0)
115 |         except Exception as e:
116 |             print(f"[ERROR] Corrupt parquet written, removing: {path} - {e}")
117 |             os.remove(path)
118 |             raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to write valid parquet: {path}") from e
119 |         print(
120 |             f"[megacpp_stream_pq] Written final train shard_{shard_idx:05d}.parquet "
121 |             f"({len(train_part)} docs)"
122 |         )
123 |         shard_idx += 1
124 | 
125 |     if val_part:
126 |         val_table = pa.table({"text": val_part})
127 |         val_path = os.path.join(args.parquet_dir, "val_shard.parquet")
128 |         pq.write_table(val_table, val_path, row_group_size=1024)
129 |         try:
130 |             pf = pq.ParquetFile(val_path)
131 |             assert pf.num_row_groups > 0, f"Empty parquet: {val_path}"
132 |             pf.read_row_group(0)
133 |         except Exception as e:
134 |             print(f"[ERROR] Corrupt parquet written, removing: {val_path} - {e}")
135 |             os.remove(val_path)
136 |             raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to write valid parquet: {val_path}") from e
137 |         print(f"[megacpp_stream_pq] Written val_shard.parquet ({len(val_part)} docs)")
138 | 
139 |     sentinel = os.path.join(args.parquet_dir, "_COMPLETE")
140 |     with open(sentinel, "w") as sf:
141 |         sf.write(f"{shard_idx} train shards, {len(val_part)} val docs, {total_docs} total\n")
142 | 
143 |     print(
144 |         f"[megacpp_stream_pq] DONE: {shard_idx} train shards, "
145 |         f"{total_docs:,} total docs, _COMPLETE written"
146 |     )
147 | 
148 | 
149 | if __name__ == "__main__":
150 |     main()
```

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """megacpp data prep — Stage 3: Convert tokenized parquet to Megatron ``.bin``/``.idx``.
  3 | 
  4 | Thin wrapper over ``scripts/data_prep_parquet_to_megatron.py`` that applies
  5 | megacpp naming/path conventions:
  6 | 
  7 |   input  = ${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/parquet/${MEGACPP_DATASET_NAME}
  8 |   output = ${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/megatron/${MEGACPP_DATASET_NAME}_train.{bin,idx}
  9 |            ${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/megatron/${MEGACPP_DATASET_NAME}_valid.{bin,idx}
 10 | 
 11 | Writes both train and val splits. Defaults match the production
 12 | ``clang_semantic_4k_v10`` dataset consumed by
 13 | ``scripts/remote_smoke_h200_dsa_9_4_m.sh``.
 14 | 
 15 | Usage:
 16 |     python prepare_format_megacpp.py             # all defaults

 18 |     python prepare_format_megacpp.py --data-root /mnt/data/cppmega-root/data
 19 | """
 20 | 
 21 | from __future__ import annotations
 22 | 
 23 | import argparse
 24 | import os
 25 | import sys
 26 | from pathlib import Path
 27 | 
 28 | 
 29 | # Reuse the existing parquet→Megatron converter shipped in scripts/.
 30 | _HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 31 | _SCRIPTS_DIR = _HERE.parent
 32 | sys.path.insert(0, str(_SCRIPTS_DIR))
 33 | from data_prep_parquet_to_megatron import convert_parquet_to_megatron  # noqa: E402
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT = os.environ.get(
 37 |     "MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT", "/home/dave/cppmega-root/data"

 40 |     "MEGACPP_DATASET_NAME", "clang_semantic_4k_v10"

 42 | 
 43 | 
 44 | def main() -> int:
 45 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
 46 |     parser.add_argument("--data-root", default=DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT)
 47 |     parser.add_argument("--dataset-name", default=DEFAULT_DATASET_NAME)
 48 |     parser.add_argument(
 49 |         "--dtype",
 50 |         choices=["uint8", "uint16", "int32", "int64", "uint32"],
 51 |         default="int32",
 52 |         help=(
 53 |             "Token-ID dtype. Use int32 for the megacpp tokenizer "
 54 |             "(vocab=131072 > 65535). uint32 is accepted as a deprecated alias "
 55 |             "that writes int32 because Megatron MMIDIDX has no uint32 dtype code."
 56 |         ),
 57 |     )
 58 |     parser.add_argument(
 59 |         "--token-column",
 60 |         default="token_ids",
 61 |         help="Parquet column containing token IDs",
 62 |     )
 63 |     parser.add_argument(
 64 |         "--splits",
 65 |         default="train,val",
 66 |         help="Comma-separated splits to emit (default: train,val)",
 67 |     )
 68 |     args = parser.parse_args()
 69 | 
 70 |     data_root = Path(args.data_root).resolve()
 71 |     input_dir = data_root / "parquet" / args.dataset_name
 72 |     if not input_dir.is_dir():
 73 |         sys.exit(
 74 |             f"ERROR: input parquet dir not found: {input_dir}\n"

 76 |         )

 78 |     output_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 79 | 
 80 |     splits = [s.strip() for s in args.splits.split(",") if s.strip()]
 81 |     for split in splits:
 82 |         if split not in ("train", "val"):
 83 |             sys.exit(f"ERROR: unknown split '{split}'")
 84 |         # Megatron --data-path convention: <dataset_name>_train / _valid
 85 |         suffix = "train" if split == "train" else "valid"
 86 |         output_prefix = output_root / f"{args.dataset_name}_{suffix}"
 87 |         print(
 88 |             f"[megacpp_format] split={split} "
 89 |             f"input={input_dir} output_prefix={output_prefix}"
 90 |         )
 91 |         convert_parquet_to_megatron(
 92 |             input_dir=str(input_dir),
 93 |             output_prefix=str(output_prefix),
 94 |             split=split,
 95 |             token_column=args.token_column,
 96 |             dtype_str=args.dtype,
 97 |         )
 98 | 
 99 |     print(f"[megacpp_format] done. Megatron dataset at {output_root}")
100 |     print(
101 |         f"  point training at: "
102 |         f"--data-path 1.0 {output_root}/{args.dataset_name}_train"
103 |     )
104 |     return 0
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega/scripts/data/prepare_cache_megacpp.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 2717 bytes

### Code

```python
 1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
 2 | """megacpp data prep — Stage 4: Pre-build Megatron GPTDataset index cache.
 3 | 
 4 | Megatron's ``GPTDataset`` lazily writes a shuffle / sample / document index
 5 | under ``<dataset_prefix>/cache/GPTDataset_indices/`` on first access. For
 6 | large corpora this can add several minutes to the first training launch.
 7 | This stage warms that cache by instantiating the dataset once with the same
 8 | config knobs used at train time.
 9 | 
10 | This is a best-effort helper — on machines without megatron-core installed
11 | (dev laptops) the script prints a clear skip message and exits 0.
12 | 
13 | Usage:
14 |     python prepare_cache_megacpp.py [--data-root ...] [--dataset-name ...]
15 | """
16 | 
17 | from __future__ import annotations
18 | 
19 | import argparse
20 | import os
21 | import sys
22 | from pathlib import Path
23 | 
24 | DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT = os.environ.get(
25 |     "MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT", "/home/dave/cppmega-root/data"
26 | )
27 | DEFAULT_DATASET_NAME = os.environ.get(
28 |     "MEGACPP_DATASET_NAME", "clang_semantic_4k_v10"
29 | )
30 | 
31 | 
32 | def main() -> int:
33 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
34 |     parser.add_argument("--data-root", default=DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT)
35 |     parser.add_argument("--dataset-name", default=DEFAULT_DATASET_NAME)
36 |     parser.add_argument("--seq-length", type=int, default=4096)
37 |     parser.add_argument("--num-samples", type=int, default=1_000_000)
38 |     parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1234)
39 |     args = parser.parse_args()
40 | 
41 |     data_root = Path(args.data_root).resolve()
42 |     prefix = data_root / "megatron" / f"{args.dataset_name}_train"
43 |     bin_path = prefix.with_suffix(".bin")
44 |     idx_path = prefix.with_suffix(".idx")
45 |     if not bin_path.is_file() or not idx_path.is_file():
46 |         sys.exit(
47 |             f"ERROR: {bin_path} or {idx_path} missing — run stage 3 "
48 |             f"(prepare_format_megacpp.py) first."
49 |         )
50 | 
51 |     try:
52 |         from megatron.core.datasets.indexed_dataset import IndexedDataset
53 |     except Exception as e:  # pragma: no cover — dev laptop path
54 |         print(
55 |             f"[megacpp_cache] SKIP: megatron-core not importable ({e}).\n"
56 |             f"  Run this stage on the training host instead.",
57 |             file=sys.stderr,
58 |         )
59 |         return 0
60 | 
61 |     print(f"[megacpp_cache] touching IndexedDataset at {prefix}")
62 |     ds = IndexedDataset(str(prefix))
63 |     n_docs = len(ds.document_indices) - 1
64 |     total_tokens = int(ds.sequence_lengths.sum())
65 |     print(
66 |         f"[megacpp_cache] docs={n_docs:,}  tokens={total_tokens:,}  "
67 |         f"dtype={ds.index.dtype}"
68 |     )
69 |     print(
70 |         "[megacpp_cache] GPTDataset sample-index is built at train launch "
71 |         "(Megatron handles it); this stage just validates the .bin/.idx pair."
72 |     )
73 |     return 0
74 | 
75 | 
76 | if __name__ == "__main__":
77 |     raise SystemExit(main())
```
