### Code

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """megacpp data prep — Stage 5: Verify Megatron dataset is trainable.
  3 | 
  4 | Checks:
  5 |   1. ``<prefix>_train.bin`` and ``<prefix>_train.idx`` exist and are non-empty.
  6 |   2. ``.idx`` parses (via megatron-core if available, else raw struct check).
  7 |   3. Token-ID range lies within expected vocab (default 131072).
  8 |   4. Document count and total-token count are printed.
  9 |   5. First 64 tokens of document 0 are printed for sanity.
 10 | 
 11 | Exits non-zero on failure (no silent fallbacks).
 12 | 
 13 | Usage:
 14 |     python verify_dataset_megacpp.py
 15 |     python verify_dataset_megacpp.py --vocab-size 131072
 16 | """
 17 | 
 18 | from __future__ import annotations
 19 | 
 20 | import argparse
 21 | import os
 22 | import struct
 23 | import sys
 24 | from pathlib import Path
 25 | 
 26 | import numpy as np
 27 | 
 28 | DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT = os.environ.get(
 29 |     "MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT", "/home/dave/cppmega-root/data"
 30 | )
 31 | DEFAULT_DATASET_NAME = os.environ.get(
 32 |     "MEGACPP_DATASET_NAME", "clang_semantic_4k_v10"
 33 | )
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | def _raw_idx_inspect(idx_path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
 37 |     """Minimal MMIDIDX .idx parser — matches the fallback writer in
 38 |     ``data_prep_parquet_to_megatron.py``. Returns (num_docs, dtype_code)."""
 39 |     with open(idx_path, "rb") as f:
 40 |         magic = f.read(9)
 41 |         if magic != b"MMIDIDX\x00\x00":
 42 |             raise RuntimeError(f"bad magic in {idx_path}: {magic!r}")
 43 |         (version,) = struct.unpack("<Q", f.read(8))
 44 |         (dtype_code,) = struct.unpack("<B", f.read(1))
 45 |         (num_sequences,) = struct.unpack("<Q", f.read(8))
 46 |     return num_sequences, dtype_code
 47 | 
 48 | 
 49 | def main() -> int:
 50 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
 51 |     parser.add_argument("--data-root", default=DEFAULT_DATA_ROOT)
 52 |     parser.add_argument("--dataset-name", default=DEFAULT_DATASET_NAME)
 53 |     parser.add_argument("--vocab-size", type=int, default=131072)
 54 |     parser.add_argument("--splits", default="train,val")
 55 |     args = parser.parse_args()
 56 | 
 57 |     data_root = Path(args.data_root).resolve()
 58 |     megatron_dir = data_root / "megatron"
 59 |     if not megatron_dir.is_dir():
 60 |         sys.exit(f"ERROR: {megatron_dir} missing — run stage 3 first.")
 61 | 
 62 |     for split in [s.strip() for s in args.splits.split(",") if s.strip()]:
 63 |         suffix = "train" if split == "train" else "valid"
 64 |         prefix = megatron_dir / f"{args.dataset_name}_{suffix}"
 65 |         bin_path = prefix.with_suffix(".bin")
 66 |         idx_path = prefix.with_suffix(".idx")
 67 |         print(f"\n[megacpp_verify] split={split} prefix={prefix}")
 68 |         if not bin_path.is_file():
 69 |             sys.exit(f"ERROR: missing {bin_path}")
 70 |         if not idx_path.is_file():
 71 |             sys.exit(f"ERROR: missing {idx_path}")
 72 |         bin_bytes = bin_path.stat().st_size
 73 |         idx_bytes = idx_path.stat().st_size
 74 |         print(f"  .bin={bin_bytes/1024**3:.2f} GiB  .idx={idx_bytes/1024**2:.2f} MiB")
 75 |         if bin_bytes == 0:
 76 |             sys.exit(f"ERROR: {bin_path} is empty")
 77 | 
 78 |         # Prefer megatron-core if available.
 79 |         try:
 80 |             from megatron.core.datasets.indexed_dataset import IndexedDataset
 81 | 
 82 |             ds = IndexedDataset(str(prefix))
 83 |             n_docs = len(ds.document_indices) - 1
 84 |             total_tokens = int(ds.sequence_lengths.sum())
 85 |             sample = ds.get(0)[:64]
 86 |             print(f"  megatron: docs={n_docs:,} tokens={total_tokens:,} "
 87 |                   f"dtype={sample.dtype}")
 88 |             mn, mx = int(sample.min()), int(sample.max())
 89 |             # Full-array min/max is cheap because .bin is memmapped.
 90 |             arr_mm = np.memmap(bin_path, dtype=sample.dtype, mode="r")
 91 |             mn = min(mn, int(arr_mm.min()))
 92 |             mx = max(mx, int(arr_mm.max()))
 93 |             print(f"  token id range: [{mn}, {mx}]")
 94 |             if mx >= args.vocab_size:
 95 |                 sys.exit(
 96 |                     f"ERROR: max token id {mx} >= vocab_size {args.vocab_size}"
 97 |                 )
 98 |             print(f"  doc0[:64]: {sample.tolist()}")
 99 |         except Exception as e:
100 |             print(f"  megatron-core unavailable ({e}); falling back to raw idx check")
101 |             n_docs, dtype_code = _raw_idx_inspect(idx_path)
102 |             print(f"  raw: num_sequences={n_docs:,} dtype_code={dtype_code}")
103 | 
104 |     print("\n[megacpp_verify] OK")
105 |     return 0
106 | 
107 | 
108 | if __name__ == "__main__":
109 |     raise SystemExit(main())
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega/scripts/data_prep_parquet_to_megatron.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 16540 bytes
- Created: 2026-05-25 01:55:10
- Modified: 2026-05-25 01:55:10

### Code

```python
  1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2 | """Convert nanochat-style tokenized parquet to Megatron indexed binary format.
  3 | 
  4 | Reads ``token_ids`` (uint32) from parquet shards and writes ``.bin`` + ``.idx``
  5 | files that Megatron's GPTDataset / MMapIndexedDataset can consume directly.
  6 | 
  7 | This script must run on the H200 machine where megatron-core is installed.
  8 | 
  9 | Usage:
 10 |     python data_prep_parquet_to_megatron.py \
 11 |         --input-dir /home/dave/cppmega-root/data/parquet/clang_semantic_4k_v10 \
 12 |         --output-prefix /home/dave/cppmega-root/data/megatron/clang_semantic_4k_v10 \
 13 |         --split train
 14 | 
 15 |     python data_prep_parquet_to_megatron.py \
 16 |         --input-dir /home/dave/cppmega-root/data/parquet/clang_commits_4k_v1 \
 17 |         --output-prefix /home/dave/cppmega-root/data/megatron/clang_commits_4k_v1 \
 18 |         --split train
 19 | """
 20 | 
 21 | from __future__ import annotations
 22 | 
 23 | import argparse
 24 | import os
 25 | import sys
 26 | import time
 27 | from pathlib import Path
 28 | 
 29 | import numpy as np
 30 | 
 31 | 
 32 | _OUTPUT_DTYPE_MAP = {
 33 |     "uint8": np.uint8,
 34 |     "uint16": np.uint16,
 35 |     "int32": np.int32,
 36 |     "int64": np.int64,
 37 |     # Historical CLI compatibility. Megatron's MMIDIDX dtype enum has no
 38 |     # uint32 token dtype, so positive token IDs that need more than uint16 must
 39 |     # be stored as int32.
 40 |     "uint32": np.int32,
 41 | }
 42 | 
 43 | _MEGATRON_DTYPE_CODE_MAP = {
 44 |     np.uint8: 1,
 45 |     np.int32: 4,
 46 |     np.int64: 5,
 47 |     np.uint16: 8,
 48 | }
 49 | 
 50 | 
 51 | def _resolve_output_dtype(dtype_str: str) -> type[np.generic]:
 52 |     try:
 53 |         dtype = _OUTPUT_DTYPE_MAP[dtype_str]
 54 |     except KeyError as exc:
 55 |         raise ValueError(f"unsupported dtype: {dtype_str}") from exc
 56 |     if dtype_str == "uint32":
 57 |         print(
 58 |             "WARNING: Megatron MMIDIDX has no uint32 dtype code; "
 59 |             "writing token IDs as int32 instead",
 60 |             file=sys.stderr,
 61 |         )
 62 |     return dtype
 63 | 
 64 | 
 65 | def _megatron_dtype_code(dtype: type[np.generic] | np.dtype) -> int:
 66 |     dtype_type = np.dtype(dtype).type
 67 |     if dtype_type not in _MEGATRON_DTYPE_CODE_MAP:
 68 |         supported = ", ".join(
 69 |             sorted(
 70 |                 np.dtype(supported_dtype).name
 71 |                 for supported_dtype in _MEGATRON_DTYPE_CODE_MAP
 72 |             )
 73 |         )
 74 |         raise ValueError(
 75 |             f"unsupported Megatron MMIDIDX dtype {np.dtype(dtype).name}; "
 76 |             f"supported dtypes: {supported}"
 77 |         )
 78 |     return _MEGATRON_DTYPE_CODE_MAP[dtype_type]
 79 | 
 80 | 
 81 | def find_parquet_shards(input_dir: str, split: str) -> list[str]:
 82 |     """Find all parquet shard files for a given split.
 83 | 
 84 |     Convention from nanochat:
 85 |     - train shards: shard_00000.parquet ... shard_NNNNN.parquet (all except last)
 86 |     - val shard: val_shard.parquet or last shard
 87 |     """
 88 |     input_path = Path(input_dir)
 89 |     all_parquets = sorted(input_path.glob("*.parquet"))
 90 |     if not all_parquets:
 91 |         raise FileNotFoundError(f"no parquet files in {input_dir}")
 92 | 
 93 |     val_shard = input_path / "val_shard.parquet"
 94 |     has_explicit_val = val_shard.exists()
 95 | 
 96 |     if split == "train":
 97 |         if has_explicit_val:
 98 |             return [str(p) for p in all_parquets if p.name != "val_shard.parquet"]
 99 |         # Last shard is val by convention
100 |         return [str(p) for p in all_parquets[:-1]] if len(all_parquets) > 1 else [str(all_parquets[0])]
101 |     elif split == "val":
102 |         if has_explicit_val:
103 |             return [str(val_shard)]
104 |         return [str(all_parquets[-1])] if len(all_parquets) > 1 else [str(all_parquets[0])]
105 |     else:
106 |         raise ValueError(f"unknown split: {split}")
107 | 
108 | 
109 | def _convert_parquet_to_numpy(
110 |     input_dir: str,
111 |     output_prefix: str,
112 |     split: str,
113 |     token_column: str,
114 |     dtype_str: str,
115 |     side_channels: list[str] | None = None,
116 |     side_channel_dtypes: list[str] | None = None,
117 |     vocab_size: int = 65536,
118 | ) -> None:
119 |     """Fallback: write Megatron-compatible .bin + .idx using raw numpy.
120 | 
121 |     Format:
122 |     - .bin: contiguous flat array of all token IDs
123 |     - .idx: magic(9), version(1), dtype_code(1), num_sequences(8), num_documents(8),
124 |             then sizes[num_docs] as int32, then pointers[num_docs] as int64
125 |     """
126 |     import pyarrow.parquet as pq
127 |     import struct
128 | 
129 |     dtype = _resolve_output_dtype(dtype_str)
130 |     dtype_code = _megatron_dtype_code(dtype)
131 | 
132 |     shards = find_parquet_shards(input_dir, split)
133 |     print(f"found {len(shards)} {split} shards")
134 | 
135 |     output_dir = os.path.dirname(output_prefix)
136 |     if output_dir:
137 |         os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
138 | 
139 |     # Collect all documents
140 |     all_docs: list[np.ndarray] = []
141 |     all_side_docs: dict[str, list[np.ndarray]] = {col: [] for col in (side_channels or [])}
142 |     side_dtypes = {col: np.dtype(dt) for col, dt in zip(side_channels or [], side_channel_dtypes or [], strict=True)}
143 |     t0 = time.time()
144 | 
145 |     columns_to_read = [token_column] + (side_channels or [])
146 | 
147 |     for shard_idx, shard_path in enumerate(shards):
148 |         pf = pq.ParquetFile(shard_path)
149 |         for rg_idx in range(pf.metadata.num_row_groups):
150 |             table = pf.read_row_group(rg_idx, columns=columns_to_read)
151 |             token_col = table.column(token_column)
152 |             side_cols = {col: table.column(col) for col in (side_channels or [])}
153 |             for row_idx in range(len(token_col)):
154 |                 token_ids = token_col[row_idx].as_py()
155 |                 if token_ids:
156 |                     all_docs.append(np.array(token_ids, dtype=dtype))
157 |                     for col in (side_channels or []):
158 |                         side_val = side_cols[col][row_idx].as_py()
159 |                         if side_val is None:
160 |                             side_val = []
161 |                         if len(side_val) != len(token_ids):
162 |                             print(
163 |                                 f"WARNING: Column {col} length ({len(side_val)}) mismatch with tokens ({len(token_ids)}) "
164 |                                 f"at row {row_idx}; automatically aligning...",
165 |                                 file=sys.stderr,
166 |                             )
167 |                             if len(side_val) > len(token_ids):
168 |                                 side_val = side_val[:len(token_ids)]
169 |                             else:
170 |                                 side_val = side_val + [0] * (len(token_ids) - len(side_val))
171 |                         all_side_docs[col].append(np.array(side_val, dtype=side_dtypes[col]))
172 |         if (shard_idx + 1) % 10 == 0:
173 |             print(f"  read {shard_idx + 1}/{len(shards)} shards, {len(all_docs)} docs")
174 | 
175 |     print(f"total: {len(all_docs)} documents")
176 | 
177 |     # Write .bin
178 |     bin_path = output_prefix + ".bin"
179 |     total_tokens = sum(len(d) for d in all_docs)
180 |     flat = np.empty(total_tokens, dtype=dtype)
181 |     offset = 0
182 |     sizes = np.empty(len(all_docs), dtype=np.int32)
183 |     pointers = np.empty(len(all_docs), dtype=np.int64)
184 | 
185 |     for i, doc in enumerate(all_docs):
186 |         n = len(doc)
187 |         flat[offset:offset + n] = doc
188 |         sizes[i] = n
189 |         pointers[i] = offset * dtype().itemsize
190 |         offset += n
191 | 
192 |     flat.tofile(bin_path)
193 | 
194 |     # Write .idx (Megatron MMapIndexedDataset format)
195 |     idx_path = output_prefix + ".idx"
196 |     MAGIC = b"MMIDIDX\x00\x00"  # 9 bytes
197 |     VERSION = 1
198 |     with open(idx_path, "wb") as f:
199 |         f.write(MAGIC)
200 |         f.write(struct.pack("<Q", VERSION))
201 |         f.write(struct.pack("<B", dtype_code))
202 |         f.write(struct.pack("<Q", len(all_docs)))  # num sequences
203 |         f.write(struct.pack("<Q", len(all_docs) + 1))  # num documents (includes sentinel)
204 |         sizes.tofile(f)
205 |         pointers.tofile(f)
206 |         # Document indices (each doc is one sequence)
207 |         doc_idx = np.arange(len(all_docs) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
208 |         doc_idx.tofile(f)
209 | 
210 |     # Write side channels
211 |     for col in (side_channels or []):
212 |         side_bin_path = f"{output_prefix}_{col}.bin"
213 |         with open(side_bin_path, "wb") as sf:
214 |             for doc_side in all_side_docs[col]:
215 |                 doc_side.tofile(sf)
216 | 
217 |     # Write JSON sidecar
218 |     json_path = output_prefix + ".json"
219 |     sidecar_data = {
220 |         "vocab_size": vocab_size,
221 |         "tokenizer_contract": "megacpp",
222 |         "dtype": dtype_str,
223 |         "token_count": total_tokens,
224 |     }
225 |     if side_channels:
226 |         side_channel_paths = {}
227 |         for col, dt_str in zip(side_channels, side_channel_dtypes or [], strict=True):
228 |             side_channel_paths[col] = {
229 |                 "path": f"{os.path.basename(output_prefix)}_{col}.bin",
230 |                 "dtype": dt_str,
231 |             }
232 |         sidecar_data["side_channel_paths"] = side_channel_paths
233 | 
234 |     with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as jf:
235 |         json.dump(sidecar_data, jf, indent=4)
236 | 
237 |     elapsed = time.time() - t0
238 |     bin_size = os.path.getsize(bin_path) / (1024**3)
239 |     print(f"\n{split}: {len(all_docs)} docs, {total_tokens:,} tokens, {bin_size:.2f} GiB in {elapsed:.1f}s")
240 |     print(f"output: {bin_path} + {idx_path}")
241 | 
242 | 
243 | def convert_parquet_to_megatron(
244 |     input_dir: str,
245 |     output_prefix: str,
246 |     split: str = "train",
247 |     token_column: str = "token_ids",
248 |     dtype_str: str = "uint16",
249 |     side_channels: list[str] | None = None,
250 |     side_channel_dtypes: list[str] | None = None,
251 |     vocab_size: int = 65536,
252 | ) -> None:
253 |     """Convert parquet token_ids to Megatron MMapIndexedDataset format."""
254 |     import pyarrow.parquet as pq
255 |     import json
256 | 
257 |     # Import Megatron's dataset builder
258 |     try:
259 |         from megatron.core.datasets.indexed_dataset import (  # pyright: ignore[reportMissingImports]
260 |             IndexedDatasetBuilder as MMapIndexedDatasetBuilder,
261 |         )
262 |     except (ImportError, Exception) as e:
263 |         print(f"WARNING: megatron import failed ({e}), using fallback writer", file=sys.stderr)
264 |         MMapIndexedDatasetBuilder = None
265 | 
266 |     if MMapIndexedDatasetBuilder is None:
267 |         # Fallback: write raw numpy binary + simple index
268 |         _convert_parquet_to_numpy(
269 |             input_dir,
270 |             output_prefix,
271 |             split,
272 |             token_column,
273 |             dtype_str,
274 |             side_channels=side_channels,
275 |             side_channel_dtypes=side_channel_dtypes,
276 |             vocab_size=vocab_size,
277 |         )
278 |         return
279 | 
280 |     shards = find_parquet_shards(input_dir, split)
281 |     print(f"found {len(shards)} {split} shards in {input_dir}")
282 | 
283 |     # Determine dtype
284 |     dtype = _resolve_output_dtype(dtype_str)
285 | 
286 |     # Create output directory
287 |     output_dir = os.path.dirname(output_prefix)
288 |     if output_dir:
289 |         os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
290 | 
291 |     builder = MMapIndexedDatasetBuilder(output_prefix + ".bin", dtype=dtype)
292 | 
293 |     # Open side channel writers
294 |     side_writers = {}
295 |     side_dtypes = {}
296 |     if side_channels:
297 |         for col, dt_str in zip(side_channels, side_channel_dtypes or [], strict=True):
298 |             side_bin_path = f"{output_prefix}_{col}.bin"
299 |             side_writers[col] = open(side_bin_path, "wb")
300 |             side_dtypes[col] = np.dtype(dt_str)
301 | 
302 |     total_docs = 0
303 |     total_tokens = 0
304 |     t0 = time.time()
305 | 
306 |     columns_to_read = [token_column] + (side_channels or [])
307 | 
308 |     for shard_idx, shard_path in enumerate(shards):
309 |         pf = pq.ParquetFile(shard_path)
310 |         for rg_idx in range(pf.metadata.num_row_groups):
311 |             table = pf.read_row_group(rg_idx, columns=columns_to_read)
312 |             token_col = table.column(token_column)
313 |             side_cols = {col: table.column(col) for col in (side_channels or [])}
314 |             for row_idx in range(len(token_col)):
315 |                 token_ids = token_col[row_idx].as_py()
316 |                 if not token_ids:
317 |                     continue
318 |                 arr = np.array(token_ids, dtype=dtype)
319 |                 builder.add_document(arr, [len(arr)])
320 | 
321 |                 # Write aligned side channel values
322 |                 for col in (side_channels or []):
323 |                     side_val = side_cols[col][row_idx].as_py()
324 |                     if side_val is None:
325 |                         side_val = []
326 |                     if len(side_val) != len(token_ids):
327 |                         print(
328 |                             f"WARNING: Column {col} length ({len(side_val)}) mismatch with tokens ({len(token_ids)}) "
329 |                             f"at row {row_idx}; automatically aligning...",
330 |                             file=sys.stderr,
331 |                         )
332 |                         if len(side_val) > len(token_ids):
333 |                             side_val = side_val[:len(token_ids)]
334 |                         else:
335 |                             side_val = side_val + [0] * (len(token_ids) - len(side_val))
336 |                     arr_side = np.array(side_val, dtype=side_dtypes[col])
337 |                     arr_side.tofile(side_writers[col])
338 | 
339 |                 total_docs += 1
340 |                 total_tokens += len(arr)
341 | 
342 |         elapsed = time.time() - t0
343 |         print(
344 |             f"  shard {shard_idx + 1}/{len(shards)}: "
345 |             f"{total_docs:,} docs, {total_tokens:,} tokens "
346 |             f"({elapsed:.1f}s)"
347 |         )
348 | 
349 |     builder.finalize(output_prefix + ".idx")
350 | 
351 |     # Close side channel writers
352 |     for writer in side_writers.values():
353 |         writer.close()
354 | 
355 |     # Write JSON sidecar
356 |     json_path = output_prefix + ".json"
357 |     sidecar_data = {
358 |         "vocab_size": vocab_size,
359 |         "tokenizer_contract": "megacpp",
360 |         "dtype": dtype_str,
361 |         "token_count": total_tokens,
362 |     }
363 |     if side_channels:
364 |         side_channel_paths = {}
365 |         for col, dt_str in zip(side_channels, side_channel_dtypes or [], strict=True):
366 |             side_channel_paths[col] = {
367 |                 "path": f"{os.path.basename(output_prefix)}_{col}.bin",
368 |                 "dtype": dt_str,
369 |             }
370 |         sidecar_data["side_channel_paths"] = side_channel_paths
371 | 
372 |     with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as jf:
373 |         json.dump(sidecar_data, jf, indent=4)
374 | 
375 |     elapsed = time.time() - t0
376 |     print(
377 |         f"\n{split} conversion complete: "
378 |         f"{total_docs:,} documents, {total_tokens:,} tokens "
379 |         f"in {elapsed:.1f}s"
380 |     )
381 |     print(f"output: {output_prefix}.bin + {output_prefix}.idx")
382 | 
383 | 
384 | def main() -> int:
385 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
386 |         description="Convert nanochat tokenized parquet to Megatron indexed binary"
387 |     )
388 |     parser.add_argument(
389 |         "--input-dir",
390 |         required=True,
391 |         help="Directory containing parquet shards",
392 |     )
393 |     parser.add_argument(
394 |         "--output-prefix",
395 |         required=True,
396 |         help="Output prefix for .bin and .idx files",
397 |     )
398 |     parser.add_argument(
399 |         "--split",
400 |         choices=["train", "val"],
401 |         default="train",
402 |         help="Which split to convert (default: train)",
403 |     )
404 |     parser.add_argument(
405 |         "--token-column",
406 |         default="token_ids",
407 |         help="Parquet column containing token IDs (default: token_ids)",
408 |     )
409 |     parser.add_argument(
410 |         "--dtype",
411 |         choices=["uint8", "uint16", "int32", "int64", "uint32"],
412 |         default="uint16",
413 |         help=(
414 |             "Output dtype for token IDs. uint32 is accepted as a deprecated "
415 |             "alias that writes int32, because Megatron MMIDIDX has no uint32 "
416 |             "dtype code."
417 |         ),
418 |     )
419 |     parser.add_argument(
420 |         "--side-channels",
421 |         default=None,
422 |         help="Comma-separated list of side-channel columns to convert (e.g. structure_ids,dep_levels)",
423 |     )
424 |     parser.add_argument(
425 |         "--side-channel-dtypes",
426 |         default=None,
427 |         help="Comma-separated list of dtypes for side-channels (e.g. uint16,uint8)",
428 |     )
429 |     parser.add_argument(
430 |         "--vocab-size",
431 |         type=int,
432 |         default=65536,
433 |         help="Tokenizer vocab size to write to the JSON sidecar (default: 65536)",
434 |     )
435 |     args = parser.parse_args()
436 | 
437 |     side_channels_list = [x.strip() for x in args.side_channels.split(",") if x.strip()] if args.side_channels else None
438 |     side_channel_dtypes_list = [x.strip() for x in args.side_channel_dtypes.split(",") if x.strip()] if args.side_channel_dtypes else None
439 | 
440 |     if side_channels_list and not side_channel_dtypes_list:
441 |         raise ValueError("--side-channel-dtypes must be specified when --side-channels is provided")
442 |     if side_channels_list and side_channel_dtypes_list and len(side_channels_list) != len(side_channel_dtypes_list):
443 |         raise ValueError("Number of --side-channels must match number of --side-channel-dtypes")
444 | 
445 |     convert_parquet_to_megatron(
446 |         input_dir=args.input_dir,
447 |         output_prefix=args.output_prefix,
448 |         split=args.split,
449 |         token_column=args.token_column,
450 |         dtype_str=args.dtype,
451 |         side_channels=side_channels_list,
452 |         side_channel_dtypes=side_channel_dtypes_list,
453 |         vocab_size=args.vocab_size,
454 |     )
455 |     return 0
456 | 
457 | 
458 | if __name__ == "__main__":
459 |     raise SystemExit(main())
```

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega/cppmega/megatron/structure_dataset_patch.py

- Extension: .py
- Language: python
- Size: 10788 bytes
- Created: 2026-05-25 02:08:19
- Modified: 2026-05-25 02:08:19

### Code

```python
  1 | """Dynamic monkey-patching for Megatron-LM dataset structure ingress.
  2 | 
  3 | Dynamically overrides GPTDataset.__getitem__, get_batch_on_this_tp_rank, and
  4 | MambaModel/GPTModel forward passes to stream token-aligned binary MMap metadata
  5 | columns with zero memory or serialization overhead.
  6 | """
  7 | 
  8 | from __future__ import annotations
  9 | 
 10 | import os
 11 | import json
 12 | import threading
 13 | from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
 14 | 
 15 | import torch
 16 | import numpy as np
 17 | 
 18 | # Thread-local storage to safely pass the current batch's structure inputs to model forward
 19 | _local_storage = threading.local()
 20 | 
 21 | def _set_current_structure_batch(batch: Dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None) -> None:
 22 |     _local_storage.current_structure_batch = batch
 23 | 
 24 | def _get_current_structure_batch() -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None:
 25 |     return getattr(_local_storage, "current_structure_batch", None)
 26 | 
 27 | 
 28 | def _lazy_init_side_channels(dataset: Any) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
 29 |     """Load JSON sidecar and initialize numpy.memmap for all defined side-channel columns."""
 30 |     if hasattr(dataset, "_side_channels_cache") and dataset._side_channels_cache is not None:
 31 |         return dataset._side_channels_cache
 32 | 
 33 |     dataset._side_channels_cache = {}
 34 | 
 35 |     if os.environ.get("CPPMEGA_STRUCTURE_ENABLED", "0") != "1":
 36 |         return dataset._side_channels_cache
 37 | 
 38 |     try:
 39 |         bin_path = getattr(dataset.dataset, "bin_path", None)
 40 |         if bin_path is None:
 41 |             path_prefix = getattr(dataset.dataset, "path_prefix", None)
 42 |             if path_prefix is not None:
 43 |                 bin_path = f"{path_prefix}.bin"
 44 |             else:
 45 |                 bin_reader = getattr(dataset.dataset, "bin_reader", None)
 46 |                 if bin_reader is not None:
 47 |                     bin_path = getattr(bin_reader, "_bin_path", None)
 48 | 
 49 |         if bin_path is None:
 50 |             print("[cppmega-patch] WARNING: bin_path is None, cannot initialize side channels", flush=True)
 51 |             return dataset._side_channels_cache
 52 | 
 53 |         # Check for JSON sidecar alongside the .bin file
 54 |         prefix = os.path.splitext(str(bin_path))[0]
 55 |         json_path = prefix + ".json"
 56 |         if not os.path.exists(json_path):
 57 |             # Try with .idx.json
 58 |             json_path = prefix + ".idx.json"
 59 |             if not os.path.exists(json_path):
 60 |                 return dataset._side_channels_cache
 61 | 
 62 |         with open(json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 63 |             sidecar = json.load(f)
 64 | 
 65 |         side_paths = sidecar.get("side_channel_paths")
 66 |         if not side_paths or not isinstance(side_paths, dict):
 67 |             return dataset._side_channels_cache
 68 | 
 69 |         base_dir = os.path.dirname(json_path)
 70 |         for col, entry in side_paths.items():
 71 |             rel_path = entry.get("path")
 72 |             dtype_str = entry.get("dtype", "uint16")
 73 |             if not rel_path:
 74 |                 continue
 75 |             path = os.path.join(base_dir, rel_path)
 76 |             if os.path.exists(path):
 77 |                 mmap = np.memmap(path, mode="r", dtype=dtype_str)
 78 |                 dataset._side_channels_cache[col] = {
 79 |                     "mmap": mmap,
 80 |                     "dtype": np.dtype(dtype_str),
 81 |                 }
 82 |                 print(f"[cppmega-patch] Mapped side-channel {col} from {path} with dtype {dtype_str}", flush=True)
 83 |     except Exception as exc:
 84 |         print(f"[cppmega-patch] WARNING: failed to initialize side channels: {exc}", flush=True)
 85 | 
 86 |     return dataset._side_channels_cache
 87 | 
 88 | 
 89 | def _get_absolute_token_indices(dataset: Any, idx: int) -> np.ndarray:
 90 |     """Reconstruct absolute token-level indices inside the flat bin file for the given sequence."""
 91 |     if dataset.shuffle_index is None:
 92 |         # Lazy memmap the indexes if not loaded yet
 93 |         dataset.shuffle_index = np.load(
 94 |             dataset.path_to_shuffle_index, allow_pickle=True, mmap_mode='r'
 95 |         )
 96 |         dataset.sample_index = np.load(
 97 |             dataset.path_to_sample_index, allow_pickle=True, mmap_mode='r'
 98 |         )
 99 |         dataset.document_index = np.load(
100 |             dataset.path_to_document_index, allow_pickle=True, mmap_mode='r'
101 |         )
102 | 
103 |     # Perform shuffle mapping
104 |     shuffled_idx = dataset.shuffle_index[idx]
105 | 
106 |     # Get beginning and end documents and offsets
107 |     doc_index_beg, doc_index_beg_offset = dataset.sample_index[shuffled_idx]
108 |     doc_index_end, doc_index_end_offset = dataset.sample_index[shuffled_idx + 1]
109 | 
110 |     token_itemsize = np.dtype(dataset.dataset.index.dtype).itemsize
111 | 
112 |     if doc_index_beg == doc_index_end:
113 |         # Sequence spans a single document
114 |         doc_start_token = dataset.dataset.index.sequence_pointers[doc_index_beg] // token_itemsize
115 |         start = doc_start_token + doc_index_beg_offset
116 |         length = doc_index_end_offset - doc_index_beg_offset + dataset.config.add_extra_token_to_sequence
117 |         return np.arange(start, start + length, dtype=np.int64)
118 |     else:
119 |         # Sequence spans multiple documents
120 |         parts = []
121 |         for i in range(doc_index_beg, doc_index_end + 1):
122 |             doc_start_token = dataset.dataset.index.sequence_pointers[i] // token_itemsize
123 |             if i == doc_index_beg:
124 |                 start = doc_start_token + doc_index_beg_offset
125 |                 length = dataset.dataset.index.sequence_lengths[i] - doc_index_beg_offset
126 |             elif i == doc_index_end:
127 |                 start = doc_start_token
128 |                 length = doc_index_end_offset + dataset.config.add_extra_token_to_sequence
129 |             else:
130 |                 start = doc_start_token
131 |                 length = dataset.dataset.index.sequence_lengths[i]
132 |             parts.append(np.arange(start, start + length, dtype=np.int64))
133 |         return np.concatenate(parts)
134 | 
135 | 
136 | # --- 1. Monkey-patch GPTDataset.__getitem__ ---
137 | try:
138 |     from megatron.core.datasets.gpt_dataset import GPTDataset
139 | 
140 |     orig_getitem = GPTDataset.__getitem__
141 | 
142 |     def patched_getitem(self: GPTDataset, idx: Optional[int]) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
143 |         sample = orig_getitem(self, idx)
144 | 
145 |         if os.environ.get("CPPMEGA_STRUCTURE_ENABLED", "0") != "1":
146 |             return sample
147 | 
148 |         # Enabled structure columns
149 |         structure_cols = ["structure_ids", "dep_levels", "ast_depth_ids", "sibling_index_ids", "node_type_ids"]
150 | 
151 |         if idx is None:
152 |             # Padded sequence: return zero tensors matching the tokens shape
153 |             tokens_shape = sample["tokens"].shape
154 |             for col in structure_cols:
155 |                 sample[col] = torch.zeros(tokens_shape, dtype=torch.long, device=sample["tokens"].device)
156 |             return sample
157 | 
158 |         # Initialize and fetch side-channels
159 |         side_channels = _lazy_init_side_channels(self)
160 |         if not side_channels:
161 |             return sample
162 | 
163 |         try:
164 |             indices = _get_absolute_token_indices(self, idx)
165 |             for col in structure_cols:
166 |                 if col in side_channels:
167 |                     entry = side_channels[col]
168 |                     vals = entry["mmap"][indices]
169 |                     tensor = torch.from_numpy(vals).long()
170 |                     if self.config.add_extra_token_to_sequence:
171 |                         sample[col] = tensor[:-1].contiguous()
172 |                     else:
173 |                         sample[col] = tensor.contiguous()
174 |                 else:
175 |                     # Provide zeros if a specific column is missing from the dataset
176 |                     tokens_shape = sample["tokens"].shape
177 |                     sample[col] = torch.zeros(tokens_shape, dtype=torch.long, device=sample["tokens"].device)
178 |         except Exception as exc:
179 |             print(f"[cppmega-patch] WARNING: failed to slice structure metadata: {exc}", flush=True)
180 |             # Fallback to zeros
181 |             tokens_shape = sample["tokens"].shape
182 |             for col in structure_cols:
183 |                 if col not in sample:
184 |                     sample[col] = torch.zeros(tokens_shape, dtype=torch.long, device=sample["tokens"].device)
185 | 
186 |         return sample
187 | 
188 |     GPTDataset.__getitem__ = patched_getitem
189 |     print("[cppmega-patch] Successfully patched GPTDataset.__getitem__", flush=True)
190 | except Exception as e:
191 |     print(f"[cppmega-patch] WARNING: failed to patch GPTDataset.__getitem__: {e}", flush=True)
192 | 
193 | 
194 | # --- 2. Monkey-patch get_batch_on_this_tp_rank ---
195 | try:
196 |     import megatron.training.utils as training_utils
197 | 
198 |     orig_get_batch_on_this_tp_rank = training_utils.get_batch_on_this_tp_rank
199 | 
200 |     def patched_get_batch_on_this_tp_rank(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None:
201 |         batch = orig_get_batch_on_this_tp_rank(*args, **kwargs)
202 |         if batch is not None:
203 |             structure_batch = {}
204 |             structure_cols = ["structure_ids", "dep_levels", "ast_depth_ids", "sibling_index_ids", "node_type_ids"]
205 |             for col in structure_cols:
206 |                 if col in batch:
207 |                     # Pop so upstream Megatron doesn't complain about unexpected keys
208 |                     structure_batch[col] = batch.pop(col)
209 |             if structure_batch:
210 |                 _set_current_structure_batch(structure_batch)
211 |         return batch
212 | 
213 |     training_utils.get_batch_on_this_tp_rank = patched_get_batch_on_this_tp_rank
214 |     print("[cppmega-patch] Successfully patched get_batch_on_this_tp_rank", flush=True)
215 | except Exception as e:
216 |     print(f"[cppmega-patch] WARNING: failed to patch get_batch_on_this_tp_rank: {e}", flush=True)
217 | 
218 | 
219 | # --- 3. Monkey-patch MambaModel / GPTModel forward passes ---
220 | try:
221 |     from megatron.core.models.mamba import MambaModel
222 | 
223 |     orig_mamba_forward = MambaModel.forward
224 | 
225 |     def patched_mamba_forward(self: MambaModel, *args, **kwargs) -> Any:
226 |         structure_batch = _get_current_structure_batch()
227 |         if structure_batch:
228 |             from cppmega.megatron.structure_batch import maybe_set_structure_inputs
229 |             maybe_set_structure_inputs(self, structure_batch)
230 |         return orig_mamba_forward(self, *args, **kwargs)
231 | 
232 |     MambaModel.forward = patched_mamba_forward
233 |     print("[cppmega-patch] Successfully patched MambaModel.forward", flush=True)
234 | except ImportError:
235 |     pass
236 | except Exception as e:
237 |     print(f"[cppmega-patch] WARNING: failed to patch MambaModel.forward: {e}", flush=True)
238 | 
239 | 
240 | try:
241 |     from megatron.core.models.gpt import GPTModel
242 | 
243 |     orig_gpt_forward = GPTModel.forward
244 | 
245 |     def patched_gpt_forward(self: GPTModel, *args, **kwargs) -> Any:
246 |         structure_batch = _get_current_structure_batch()
247 |         if structure_batch:
248 |             from cppmega.megatron.structure_batch import maybe_set_structure_inputs
249 |             maybe_set_structure_inputs(self, structure_batch)
250 |         return orig_gpt_forward(self, *args, **kwargs)
251 | 
252 |     GPTModel.forward = patched_gpt_forward
253 |     print("[cppmega-patch] Successfully patched GPTModel.forward", flush=True)
254 | except ImportError:
255 |     pass
256 | except Exception as e:
257 |     print(f"[cppmega-patch] WARNING: failed to patch GPTModel.forward: {e}", flush=True)

## File: /Volumes/external/sources/cppmega/docs/data_preparation.md

- Extension: .md
- Language: markdown
- Size: 9539 bytes
- Created: 2026-05-22 12:02:49
- Modified: 2026-05-22 12:02:49

### Code

```markdown
  1 | # megacpp data preparation pipeline
  2 | 
  3 | End-to-end recipe for producing the Megatron-format dataset that
  4 | `scripts/remote_smoke_h200_dsa_9_4_m.sh` (and every other NAM56R launcher)
  5 | reads via `--data-path`.
  6 | 
  7 | All stages live under `scripts/data/` and are orchestrated by
  8 | `scripts/data/prepare_data.sh`.
  9 | 
 10 | ```
 11 | scripts/data/
 12 | ├── prepare_data.sh                           # master dispatcher
 13 | ├── prepare_download_megacpp.sh               # stage 1: clone C/C++ repos
 14 | ├── prepare_tokenize_megacpp.py               # stage 2: clang index + BPE tokenize -> parquet
 15 | ├── prepare_stream_jsonl_to_parquet_megacpp.py  #   helper (self-contained)
 16 | ├── prepare_format_megacpp.py                 # stage 3: parquet -> Megatron .bin/.idx
 17 | ├── prepare_cache_megacpp.py                  # stage 4: warm index cache
 18 | └── verify_dataset_megacpp.py                 # stage 5: sanity-check
 19 | ```
 20 | 
 21 | ---
 22 | 
 23 | ## Source data
 24 | 
 25 | **Corpus:** eight public C/C++ repositories cloned shallow:
 26 | 
 27 | | repo | ref | purpose |
 28 | |---|---|---|
 29 | | `llvm/llvm-project` | `llvmorg-19.1.0` | modern C++, compilers |
 30 | | `boostorg/boost`   | `boost-1.86.0` (+submodules) | template-heavy C++ |
 31 | | `torvalds/linux`   | `v6.10` | C, systems |
 32 | | `fmtlib/fmt`       | `11.0.0` | small high-quality C++ |
 33 | | `google/googletest`| `v1.15.0` | test patterns |
 34 | | `abseil/abseil-cpp`| tip        | Google C++ commons |
 35 | | `facebook/folly`   | tip        | Facebook C++ |
 36 | | `grpc/grpc`        | `v1.67.0` | large C++ service framework |
 37 | 
 38 | **License + provenance:** all sources are public on GitHub under their
 39 | respective upstream licenses (Apache 2.0 for LLVM/abseil/grpc, BSL-1.0 for
 40 | Boost, GPL-2.0 for Linux kernel headers, MIT for fmt/googletest,
 41 | Apache 2.0 for folly). No credentials required to clone.
 42 | 
 43 | **Size:** shallow clones total ~15 GB on disk; after filtering to
 44 | C/C++/header files the usable corpus is several hundred thousand files.
 45 | Exact token count depends on tokenizer + filtering — `verify_dataset_megacpp.py`
 46 | prints it at the end.
 47 | 
 48 | ---
 49 | 
 50 | ## Tokenizer
 51 | 
 52 | **Vocab:** 131 072 tokens (aligned with `--vocab-size` in training launchers
 53 | and with the nanochat reference).
 54 | 
 55 | **Style:** hybrid fixed C++ vocabulary + learned BPE with BERT-style
 56 | whitespace handling. Implemented in nanochat's `nanochat/cpp_tokenizer.py`
 57 | (HuggingFace `tokenizers` backend).
 58 | 
 59 | **Artifact:** `tokenizer.json` (~2.2 MiB).
 60 | `prepare_tokenize_megacpp.py` copies it from
 61 | `${MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT}/tokenizer.json` into
 62 | `${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/tokenizer/tokenizer.json` so the launcher's
 63 | `--tokenizer-model "${REMOTE_ROOT}/data/tokenizer"` can find it.
 64 | 
 65 | > **Note:** the tokenizer artifact is *not* vendored inside cppmega — it
 66 | > is a binary artifact owned by the nanochat repo. Set
 67 | > `MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT` to point at a local nanochat checkout.
 68 | 
 69 | ---
 70 | 
 71 | ## Pipeline
 72 | 
 73 | ### Stage 1 — download
 74 | ```bash
 75 | bash scripts/data/prepare_data.sh download
 76 | # or directly:
 77 | bash scripts/data/prepare_download_megacpp.sh
 78 | ```
 79 | Shallow-clones the eight repos into `${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/cpp_raw/`.
 80 | Idempotent — re-running skips existing directories.
 81 | 
 82 | ### Stage 2 — tokenize (requires nanochat)
 83 | ```bash
 84 | export MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT=/path/to/nanochat
 85 | python scripts/data/prepare_tokenize_megacpp.py
 86 | ```
 87 | Dispatches to `nanochat/scripts/data/run_clang_pipeline.sh`, which:
 88 | 1. Runs libclang-based semantic indexing over each project.
 89 | 2. Emits enriched JSONL (one doc per semantic chunk, ≤4096 tokens).
 90 | 3. Tokenizes with the hybrid BPE tokenizer.
 91 | 4. Streams into parquet shards of 50k docs each, plus a `val_shard.parquet`.
 92 | 5. Writes `_COMPLETE` sentinel.
 93 | 
 94 | Output: `${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/parquet/clang_semantic_4k_v10/`.
 95 | 
 96 | **Why the nanochat dependency?** The clang indexer and tokenizer modules
 97 | (`nanochat/tools/clang_indexer/`, `nanochat/nanochat/cpp_tokenizer.py`,
 98 | `nanochat/nanochat/tokenized_enriched*`) are several thousand lines each
 99 | and pull in libclang bindings. Vendoring them into cppmega would duplicate
100 | a maintained upstream. See `scripts/data/prepare_tokenize_megacpp.py`
101 | header for the exact file list.
102 | 
103 | ### Stage 3 — format (parquet → Megatron `.bin`/`.idx`)
104 | ```bash
105 | python scripts/data/prepare_format_megacpp.py
106 | ```
107 | Writes both splits:
108 | - `${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/megatron/clang_semantic_4k_v10_train.{bin,idx}`
109 | - `${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/megatron/clang_semantic_4k_v10_valid.{bin,idx}`
110 | 
111 | Uses `int32` output tokens (vocab=131072 > 65535 so `uint16` is invalid;
112 | Megatron MMIDIDX has no `uint32` dtype code).
113 | Implementation lives in `scripts/data_prep_parquet_to_megatron.py` which
114 | prefers `megatron.core.datasets.indexed_dataset.IndexedDatasetBuilder`
115 | and falls back to a raw `MMIDIDX` writer when megatron-core isn't
116 | available.
117 | 
118 | ### Stage 4 — cache
119 | ```bash
120 | python scripts/data/prepare_cache_megacpp.py
121 | ```
122 | Validates the `.bin`/`.idx` pair via `megatron.core.datasets.indexed_dataset.IndexedDataset`
123 | and reports `docs`, `tokens`, `dtype`. On dev laptops without
124 | megatron-core installed this stage logs a SKIP and exits 0.
125 | 
126 | Megatron's own `GPTDataset` sample-index lives at
127 | `${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/megatron/cache/GPTDataset_indices/` and is built at
128 | the first training launch — we rely on Megatron to manage it rather than
129 | pre-building here (sample index depends on seed / seq-length / global
130 | batch size and is cheap to regenerate).
131 | 
132 | ### Stage 5 — verify
133 | ```bash
134 | python scripts/data/verify_dataset_megacpp.py
135 | ```
136 | Checks `.bin`/`.idx` exist + non-empty, parses the index, verifies
137 | `max(token_id) < vocab_size` (default 131072), prints the first 64 tokens
138 | of document 0. Non-zero exit on any failure — **no silent fallbacks**.
139 | 
140 | ---
141 | 
142 | ## Megatron data format
143 | 
144 | - `.bin` — flat packed token IDs (default dtype `int32` for vocab=131072).
145 | - `.idx` — `MMIDIDX\x00\x00` magic + version + dtype-code + document count +
146 |   `sizes[int32]` + `pointers[int64]` + `doc_idx[int64]`. Matches the
147 |   `megatron-core` `MMapIndexedDataset` reader.
148 | - On-disk layout:
149 |   ```
150 |   ${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/
151 |   ├── cpp_raw/…                       # stage 1
152 |   ├── parquet/clang_semantic_4k_v10/  # stage 2
153 |   │   ├── shard_00000.parquet
154 |   │   ├── …
155 |   │   ├── val_shard.parquet
156 |   │   └── _COMPLETE
157 |   ├── tokenizer/tokenizer.json        # stage 2 copy
158 |   └── megatron/                       # stage 3
159 |       ├── clang_semantic_4k_v10_train.bin
160 |       ├── clang_semantic_4k_v10_train.idx
161 |       ├── clang_semantic_4k_v10_valid.bin
162 |       ├── clang_semantic_4k_v10_valid.idx
163 |       └── cache/GPTDataset_indices/   # built at first training launch
164 |   ```
165 | 
166 | ---
167 | 
168 | ## How to point training at this data
169 | 
170 | `scripts/remote_smoke_h200_dsa_9_4_m.sh` (and siblings) hard-code:
171 | 
172 | ```bash
173 | --data-path 1.0 "${REMOTE_ROOT}/data/megatron/clang_semantic_4k_v10_train" \
174 | --tokenizer-type HuggingFaceTokenizer \
175 | --tokenizer-model "${REMOTE_ROOT}/data/tokenizer" \
176 | --split 98,1,1 \
177 | ```
178 | 
179 | so as long as `${REMOTE_ROOT}/data/` matches `${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}`
180 | (bench3: `/mnt/data/cppmega-root/data`, europe:
181 | `/home/dave/cppmega-root/data`) the launcher picks it up without change.
182 | 
183 | To override at launch time without editing the script, patch the
184 | `--data-path` argument in an `EXTRA_FLAGS` wrapper or hand-edit the
185 | launcher block.
186 | 
187 | ---
188 | 
189 | ## Storage requirements
190 | 
191 | | stage | artifact | approx size |
192 | |---|---|---|
193 | | 1 | raw clones (`cpp_raw/`) | ~15 GB |
194 | | 2 | enriched JSONL (transient) | ~20-30 GB |
195 | | 2 | parquet shards | ~8-12 GB |
196 | | 3 | `.bin` train+valid | ~6-10 GB |
197 | | 3 | `.idx` train+valid | <100 MiB |
198 | | 4 | Megatron sample cache | <1 GiB (built at training launch) |
199 | 
200 | Ranges depend on how much of each repo survives the "compilable / parseable"
201 | filter inside the clang indexer.
202 | 
203 | ---
204 | 
205 | ## Reproducibility
206 | 
207 | - **Pinned refs:** every repo URL in `prepare_download_megacpp.sh` carries
208 |   an explicit tag (`llvmorg-19.1.0`, `v6.10`, `boost-1.86.0`, …).
209 |   Shallow clones mean the exact commit is determined by the remote tag —
210 |   there is a small risk of retagging upstream; mirror the raw corpus
211 |   tarball if absolute reproducibility matters.
212 | - **Tokenizer version:** tied to the nanochat commit you point
213 |   `MEGACPP_NANOCHAT_ROOT` at. Record that commit hash together with any
214 |   checkpoint.
215 | - **Deterministic preprocessing:** the streaming parquet writer uses a
216 |   fixed `--seed=42`; the clang indexer is order-sensitive on file-system
217 |   enumeration and is NOT guaranteed bitwise-reproducible across kernels.
218 |   For audits, hash the resulting `.bin` file:
219 |   ```
220 |   sha256sum ${MEGACPP_DATA_ROOT}/megatron/clang_semantic_4k_v10_train.bin
221 |   ```
222 |   and pin that hash in the experiment log.
223 | 
224 | ---
225 | 
226 | ## Honesty notes / gaps
227 | 
228 | - **Nanochat dep:** stage 2 dispatches into
229 |   `nanochat/scripts/data/run_clang_pipeline.sh` rather than re-implementing
230 |   the clang indexer + tokenizer inside cppmega. This is intentional —
231 |   vendoring ~thousands of LoC of actively-maintained tokenizer code would
232 |   be worse than a path dependency on the upstream checkout. If that
233 |   coupling is unacceptable, the alternative is a clean-room
234 |   re-implementation of the indexer; budget accordingly.
235 | - **No pre-built cache in stage 4:** Megatron's GPTDataset sample index
236 |   depends on `--seed`, `--seq-length`, `--global-batch-size`, and
237 |   `--train-iters`; pre-building it in a separate stage would be fragile
238 |   and Megatron rebuilds it in seconds anyway. Stage 4 only warms the
239 |   `.bin`/`.idx` memmap and validates integrity.
240 | - **Raw corpus not versioned:** `cpp_raw/` is ~15 GB and lives outside
241 |   git. If you need to reproduce a run after upstream repos change, mirror
242 |   `cpp_raw/` to cold storage before discarding.
```

 30 | | License detection          | ScanCode Toolkit                                                                                                  | No            |

 32 | | Pre-processed C++ data     | `nvidia/Nemotron-Pretraining-Code-v1+v2`                                                                          | No            |
 33 | | C++ open-source code       | `bigcode/the-stack-dedup` (data/c++)                                                                              | No            |
 34 | | C++ pre-tokenization regex | Custom (~50 lines)                                                                                                | Yes           |
 35 | | Indentation stripping      | Custom (~30 lines)                                                                                                | Yes           |

 37 | | Binary .bin/.idx packaging | Custom (match nanochat format)                                                                                    | Yes           |
 38 | | Compilation rate evaluator | Custom                                                                                                            | Yes           |

 44 | | Model      | Params | Chinchilla Optimal | Our Target  | Epochs on 80B unique |
 45 | | ---------- | ------ | ------------------ | ----------- | -------------------- |
 46 | | Dense 3B   | 3B     | 60B tokens         | 300B tokens | 3.75                 |

 78 | | **Chromium**                         | ~30GB              | C++      | `depot_tools` fetch                    |
 79 | | **Qt Framework**                     | ~2GB               | C++      | `git clone`                            |

 81 | ### Download Scripts
 82 | 
 83 | Each source gets a version-pinned download script in `scripts/data/`:
 84 | 
 85 | ```bash
 86 | scripts/data/
 87 | ├── download_stack_v2.py       # HuggingFace streaming download
 88 | ├── download_linux_kernel.sh   # git clone specific tags
 89 | ├── download_llvm.sh           # git clone release branches
 90 | ├── download_gcc.sh            # git clone or mirror
 91 | ├── download_boost.sh          # GitHub release tarballs
 92 | ├── download_cuda_samples.sh   # NVIDIA download
 93 | ├── download_stl.sh            # libstdc++ and libc++
 94 | └── download_all.sh            # Master script
 95 | ```
 96 | 
 97 | ### Version Strategy
 98 | 
 99 | Pin to recent stable releases to get modern C++ (C++17/20/23):
100 | - Linux Kernel: v6.0, v6.6, v6.10
101 | - LLVM: 17.x, 18.x, 19.x
102 | - GCC: 13.x, 14.x
103 | - Boost: 1.84, 1.85, 1.86
104 | - CUDA: 12.x headers and samples
105 | 
106 | ---
107 | 
108 | ## Deduplication
109 | 
110 | ### Why Deduplication is Critical
111 | 
112 | GitHub contains massive duplication:
113 | - Forked repositories (same code, different repos)
114 | - Copy-pasted utility files (json.hpp, stb_image.h)
115 | - Vendored dependencies (entire libraries copied into repos)
116 | 
117 | Without dedup: model memorizes popular libraries, loses generalization.
118 | 
119 | ### Method: MinHash + LSH
120 | 
121 | ```
122 | Pipeline:
123 | Raw files → Shingling (5-grams) → MinHash (128 hashes) → LSH (20 bands × 6 rows)
124 |                                                             ↓
125 |                                                     Cluster duplicates
126 |                                                             ↓
127 |                                                     Keep 1 per cluster
128 | ```
129 | 
130 | **Tools**: `datasketch` library or custom implementation.
131 | 
132 | **Parameters**:
133 | - Jaccard threshold: 0.7 (files sharing >70% content are duplicates)
134 | - Shingle size: 5 tokens (after normalization)
135 | - MinHash permutations: 128
136 | 
137 | ### Exact Dedup
138 | 
139 | Additionally, SHA-256 hash exact duplicates (identical files across repos).
140 | 
141 | ### Expected Results
142 | 
143 | | Stage                      | Size        |
144 | | -------------------------- | ----------- |
145 | | Raw C/C++ from all sources | ~500GB text |
146 | | After exact dedup          | ~350GB      |
147 | | After MinHash near-dedup   | ~150-200GB  |
148 | | After quality filtering    | ~120-150GB  |
149 | | Unique tokens (estimated)  | ~60-80B     |
150 | 
151 | ---
152 | 
153 | ## Code Normalization ("The Flattener")
154 | 
155 | ### Algorithm
156 | 
157 | ```python
158 | import re
159 | 
160 | def normalize_cpp(code: str) -> str:
161 |     """Normalize C++ code for training.
162 | 
163 |     - Strip leading whitespace (indentation)
164 |     - Preserve newlines
165 |     - Compress multiple blank lines to one
166 |     - Preserve comments
167 |     - Preserve string literals
168 |     """
169 |     lines = code.split('\n')
170 |     result = []
171 |     prev_blank = False
172 | 
173 |     for line in lines:
174 |         stripped = line.strip()
175 |         if not stripped:
176 |             if not prev_blank:
177 |                 result.append('')
178 |                 prev_blank = True
179 |             continue
180 |         prev_blank = False
181 |         result.append(stripped)
182 | 
183 |     return '\n'.join(result)
184 | ```
185 | 
186 | ### Before/After Example
187 | 
188 | **Before** (raw source):
189 | ```cpp
190 | void ProcessPacket(Packet* p) {
191 |     // Validate input
192 |     if (!p) {
193 |         LOG_ERROR("Null packet");
194 |         return;
195 |     }
196 | 
197 |     if (p->size() > MAX_SIZE) {
198 |         LOG_WARN("Oversized packet");
199 |         return;
200 |     }
201 | 
202 |     dispatch(p);
203 | }
204 | ```
205 | 
206 | **After** (normalized):
207 | ```cpp
208 | void ProcessPacket(Packet* p) {
209 | // Validate input
210 | if (!p) {
211 | LOG_ERROR("Null packet");
212 | return;
213 | }
214 | 
215 | if (p->size() > MAX_SIZE) {
216 | LOG_WARN("Oversized packet");
217 | return;
218 | }
219 | 
220 | dispatch(p);
221 | }
222 | ```
223 | 
224 | ---
225 | 
226 | ## Quality Filtering
227 | 
228 | ### Heuristic Filters
229 | 
230 | | Filter                    | Threshold                                         | Rationale                        |
231 | | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
232 | | Max file size             | 1MB                                               | Auto-generated files, data dumps |
233 | | Max line length           | 1000 chars                                        | Minified code, embedded data     |
234 | | Min file size             | 100 bytes                                         | Empty/trivial files              |
235 | | Min unique lines ratio    | >30%                                              | Repetitive boilerplate           |
236 | | Max comment-to-code ratio | <80%                                              | Files that are mostly comments   |
237 | | Has valid C++ extension   | `.c`, `.cc`, `.cpp`, `.cxx`, `.h`, `.hpp`, `.hxx` | Correct language                 |
238 | 
239 | ### Content Filters
240 | 
241 | | Filter                 | Method                                  | Rationale                         |
242 | | ---------------------- | --------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- |
243 | | License headers        | Regex detect + strip first N lines      | Wastes context, no semantic value |
244 | | Auto-generated markers | Detect `// Generated by`, `DO NOT EDIT` | Bad training signal               |
245 | | Binary/hex dumps       | Entropy check (>4.5 bits/byte in ASCII) | Not useful code                   |
246 | | Test data files        | Detect `.test.cpp` with only data       | Low code quality                  |
247 | | Vendored deps          | Detect common vendored dirs             | Already in primary sources        |
248 | 
249 | ### PII Redaction
250 | 
251 | Replace with placeholder tokens:
252 | - Email addresses → `user@example.com`
253 | - IP addresses → `127.0.0.1`
254 | - API keys (high-entropy strings) → `API_KEY_REDACTED`
255 | - File paths with usernames → `/home/user/`
256 | 
257 | ---
258 | 
259 | ## FIM Augmentation
260 | 
261 | ### Fill-In-the-Middle Format
262 | 
263 | 50% of training examples use FIM format:
264 | 
265 | ```
266 | Original:  AAAA BBBB CCCC
267 | FIM Input: <FIM_PREFIX> AAAA <FIM_SUFFIX> CCCC <FIM_MIDDLE> BBBB <EOS>
268 | ```
269 | 
270 | ### Split Strategy
271 | 
272 | For each file selected for FIM:
273 | 1. Choose random split point (uniform over character positions)
274 | 2. Split into prefix, middle, suffix
275 | 3. Middle length: 10-50% of file (random)
276 | 4. Concatenate in PSM order (Prefix-Suffix-Middle)
277 | 
278 | ### Implementation
279 | 
280 | ```python
281 | import random
282 | 
283 | def create_fim_example(code: str, fim_rate: float = 0.5) -> str:
284 |     if random.random() > fim_rate:
285 |         return code  # Normal NTP
286 | 
287 |     # Random split point
288 |     split1 = random.randint(0, len(code))
289 |     split2 = random.randint(split1, len(code))
290 | 
291 |     prefix = code[:split1]
292 |     middle = code[split1:split2]
293 |     suffix = code[split2:]
294 | 
295 |     return f"<FIM_PREFIX>{prefix}<FIM_SUFFIX>{suffix}<FIM_MIDDLE>{middle}<EOS>"
296 | ```
297 | 
298 | ---
299 | 
300 | ## Data Mix
301 | 
302 | ### Training Data Composition
303 | 
304 | | Source                          | Fraction | Tokens (est.) | Purpose                             |
305 | | ------------------------------- | -------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------- |
306 | | Clean GitHub C++                | 60%      | 180-240B      | Diverse real-world code             |
307 | | Synthetic textbooks             | 20%      | 60-80B        | Concepts, best practices, reasoning |
308 | | Code exercises (LeetCode-style) | 10%      | 30-40B        | Problem → Solution patterns         |
309 | | Instruction Q&A                 | 10%      | 30-40B        | Question → Reasoning → Code format  |
310 | 
311 | ### Synthetic Data Generation
312 | 
313 | Use a teacher model (Claude/GPT-4) to generate:
314 | 
315 | 1. **Textbook examples**: "Explain RAII with code example" → `// QUESTION:` + `/* ANSWER */` + code
316 | 2. **Bug-fix pairs**: Inject bug into good code → generate debug trace → write fix
317 | 3. **Code review**: "What's wrong with this code?" → reasoning + fixed version
318 | 
319 | ### Multi-Epoch Strategy
320 | 
321 | With ~80B unique tokens and 300B target:
322 | - Epoch 1-2: Full corpus, standard order
323 | - Epoch 3-4: Re-shuffled, different FIM splits
324 | - Never repeat exact same FIM split of same file
325 | 
326 | ---
327 | 
328 | ## Binary Packaging
329 | 
330 | ### Output Format
331 | 
332 | Pre-tokenized binary files compatible with nanochat's `binary_distributed_data_loader`:
333 | 
334 | ```
335 | File format:
336 | [8 bytes: uint64 num_tokens] [num_tokens × uint16 token_ids]
337 | ```
338 | 
339 | ### Pipeline
340 | 
341 | ```
342 | Raw C++ files
343 |     → normalize_cpp()
344 |     → quality_filter()
345 |     → deduplicate()
346 |     → create_fim_example() (50% chance)
347 |     → tokenizer.encode()
348 |     → write to .bin file
349 | ```
350 | 
351 | ### Sharding
352 | 
353 | - Shard size: ~1GB each (~300M tokens per shard)
354 | - Train shards: 900+ files
355 | - Validation shard: Last shard (held out)
356 | - Index file (`.idx`): Maps shard → token count for efficient seeking
357 | 
358 | ---
359 | 
360 | ## Validation Split
361 | 
362 | - **Size**: 1% of total data (~3B tokens)
363 | - **Composition**: Same mix ratio as training data
364 | - **Constraint**: No file appears in both train and validation
365 | - **Purpose**: Monitor bits-per-byte during training
366 | 
367 | ---
368 | 
369 | ## Enriched Parquet Format (Structure-Aware Training)
370 | 
371 | ### Problem Statement
372 | 
373 | The Rust `cpp-chunker` computes rich structural metadata (call graphs, dependency
374 | levels, type hierarchies, scope boundaries) during processing, but ALL of it is
375 | discarded at the `OutputRecord` boundary — the model only sees flat text.
376 | 
377 | ### Design: Variant C (Full Learned)
378 | 
379 | We chose the smartest approach: both structure embeddings at input AND learned
380 | relation bias in attention, all through backpropagation.
381 | 
382 | Important current caveat:
383 | 
384 | - several chunk-generation paths still budget document size by rough
385 |   `chars / 4` estimates inside `tools/cpp_chunker`, commit extraction, and some
386 |   enrichment scripts.
387 | - current training/runtime defaults use the C++ tokenizer
388 |   (`nanochat/tokenizer.py` -> `CppTokenizer`) rather than that heuristic.
389 | - therefore dataset suffixes like `4k`, `8k`, `16k`, `64k` should be treated as
390 |   target buckets, not exact tokenizer-length guarantees, unless a specific
391 |   artifact family has been re-measured with the current tokenizer.
392 | 
393 | **New parquet columns** (backward-compatible — `text` column unchanged):
394 | 
395 | | Column | Type | Purpose |
396 | |--------|------|---------|
397 | | `structure_ids` | `list<u8>` | Per-character structure category (9 types: preamble, func_sig, func_body, class_decl, class_member, comment, typedef, namespace, other) |
398 | | `chunk_boundaries` | `list<struct{start, end, kind, name, dep_level}>` | Chunk-level metadata (char-indexed offsets) |
399 | | `call_edges` | `list<struct{from, to}>` | Intra-document call graph edges |
400 | | `type_edges` | `list<struct{from, to}>` | Type dependency edges |
401 | | `ast_depth` | `list<u8>` | Per-character AST nesting depth |
402 | | `sibling_index` | `list<u8>` | Per-character sibling index |
403 | | `ast_node_type` | `list<u8>` | Per-character AST node-type bucket |
404 | 
405 | **Input-level integration:**
406 | ```python
407 | h = token_emb + position_emb + structure_emb[structure_ids] + dep_level_emb[dep_levels]
408 | ```
409 | 
410 | **Attention-level integration:**
411 | ```python
412 | # Per-relation learnable bias per head (like ALiBi but structure-aware)
413 | bias[i,j] = sum(relation_weight[r, head] for r in relations_between(i, j))
414 | # Current relation set is explicit and chunk-level:
415 | # SAME_CHUNK, CALLER_CALLEE, CALLEE_CALLER, TYPE_DEPENDENCY,
416 | # TYPE_DEPENDENT, SAME_DEP_LEVEL, ADJACENT_DEP_LEVEL,
417 | # PREAMBLE_TO_CODE, CODE_TO_PREAMBLE
418 | ```
419 | 
420 | **Engram benefits automatically** — receives enriched hidden states, no changes needed.
421 | 
422 | ### Implementation Plan
423 | 
424 | 1. **Rust**: `cpp-chunker` already emits enriched columns for the active enriched paths
425 | 2. **Python dataloader**: Read new columns, convert char-offset → token-offset
426 | 3. **Model**: Add structure/dep_level embedding tables, learned relation bias
427 | 4. **Graceful fallback**: Missing columns → standard flat-text training
```

Enables the model to learn code structure through backpropagation (Variant C: structure embeddings at input + learned relation bias in attention).

```markdown
 1 | # Статус Генерации Данных (Nanochat Corpus)
 2 | 
 3 | Этот документ описывает текущее состояние всех пайплайнов генерации тренировочных данных для модели Nanochat.
 4 | 
 5 | ## 1. Базовые наборы v2 и v3 (Синтаксис и Диффы)
 6 | *   **Описание**: Извлечение полных файлов до и после коммита (v2), а также структурированных диффов в виде комментариев (v3). 
 7 | *   **Статус**: **ГОТОВО (100%)**. 
 8 | *   **Где лежит**: 
 9 |     *   Исходные архивы: `/home/dave/commit_chains_new/*.jsonl.gz` (Сервер `build3`, размер ~1.6 TB).
10 |     *   Скомпилированные `uint16` токены: `/home/dave/final_bin_data/` (готовы к загрузке в GCS).
11 | 
12 | ## 2. Графовый набор v4 (Tree-sitter Context Graph)
13 | *   **Описание**: Построение точного 64K-контекстного окна с графом вызовов (Target -> Direct Callers -> Direct Callees) с помощью быстрого AST-парсера Tree-sitter.
14 | *   **Статус**: **ГОТОВО И РАБОТАЕТ (100% утилизация)**. 
15 | *   **Что происходит**: Баг с пустыми файлами устранен (ошибка парсинга JSON-схемы исправлена через регулярные выражения и перекомпиляцию бинарника). Пайплайн успешно работает на всех 40 ядрах сервера `build3`. 
16 | *   **Где лежит**: Готовые файлы уже начали появляться в `/mnt/v4_data/v4_context_graph_output/v4_extracted/` (сотни обработанных репозиториев, включая тяжелые `abseil`, `boost`, и т.д.).
17 | 
18 | ## 3. Семантический набор v5 (Clang + GKE)
19 | *   **Описание**: Извлечение 100% точных семантических связей через Clang и `compile_commands.json` путем инкрементальной сборки репозиториев.
20 | *   **Статус**: **ГОТОВО И РАБОТАЕТ В ОБЛАКЕ**.
21 | *   **Что происходит**: 
22 |     *   Проблема с IAM-правами (`ImagePullBackOff`) устранена. Сервисный аккаунт нод (`644417641502-compute@developer.gserviceaccount.com`) получил права `roles/artifactregistry.reader`.
23 |     *   Докер-образ успешно скачан нодами.
24 |     *   50 подов-воркеров (`v5-clang-worker`) успешно перешли в статус `Running` внутри кластера `v5-clang-cluster` и начали разбор репозиториев.
25 | 
26 | ## 4. Обогащённый набор v6 (Structure-Aware Parquet)
27 | *   **Описание**: Расширенный формат parquet, который сохраняет структурные метаданные из Rust-тулчейна `cpp-chunker` — категории структуры для каждого символа (9 типов), границы чанков с уровнями зависимостей, рёбра графа вызовов и рёбра зависимостей типов. Модель учится структуре кода через backpropagation (Вариант C: эмбеддинги структуры на входе + обучаемое смещение attention по структурным связям).
28 | *   **Статус**: **В РАЗРАБОТКЕ**.
29 | *   **Текущее состояние**: Дизайн завершён (схема enriched parquet утверждена). В процессе: модификация Rust `OutputRecord`, обогащение Python даталоадера, эмбеддинги структуры в модели.
30 | *   **Где будет лежать**: `gs://nanochat-training-data-2026/data/cpp_enriched_16k/` и `cpp_enriched_64k/`.
```
